                      utayaṇaṉ carittirac curukkam
                     eḻutiyavar: u.vē. cāminātaiyar

   tirucciṟṟampalam
       Source:
       utayaṇaṉ carittirac curukkam
       {peruṅkataiyait taḻuviya vacaṉam}
       ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātaiyar
       urimai pativu
       *makaḷ kāriyālayam, mayilāppūr - ceṉṉai
       iraṇṭām patippu - jaṉavari 1948
       Printed at: The Madras Law Journal Press, Mylapore, Madras
       --------
       mukavurai
       koṅku nāṭṭilirunta vēḷirkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa koṅku vēḷir eṉparāl pala
       nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu tamiḻil akaval pākkaḷāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa
       peruṅkatai yeṉṉum nūliṉ kataippōkkait taḻuvi eḻuti ataṟku aṅkamāka
       ataṉōṭu cērttup patippikkappeṟṟa ivvacaṉa nūlaic cila aṉparkaḷ
       virumpiyapaṭi taṉiyākavum veḷiyiṭalāyiṟṟu.
       kurukulattil piṟantavaṉum keḷacāmpi nakarattu aracaṉum
       catāṉikaṉuṭaiya putalvaṉumāṉa utayaṇaṉ eṉpavaṉ piṟantatu mutal avaṉ
       tuṟavu pūṇṭamai iṟutiyākavuḷḷa varalāṟukaḷ itaṉ pāl virivākak
       kāṇalākum.
       paṇṭaikkālattu aracāṭci muṟaiyaiyum nāṭukaḷiṉ amaitikaḷaiyum,
       nītikaḷaiyum, ciṟpam mutaliya palavakaik kalaikaḷiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷaiyum
       itu kāṟum aṟiyappaṭāta iṉṉum ettaṉaiyō ariya periya viṣayaṅkaḷaiyum
       aṟintu koḷvataṟkut takka karuviyākum itu.
       ikkataiyiṉ campantamāka vaṭamoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟil iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷa
       nūlkaḷ pala vaḻaṅkappaṭiṉum kuṇāṭṭiyar eṉṉum mahākaviyāl paicāca
       pāṣaiyil mutalil iyaṟṟap peṟṟa piruhat kataiyai mutal nūlākakkoṇṭu
       kaṅkanāṭṭu aracaṉākiya turnītaṉ iyaṟṟiyatākat teriyavarum oru
       vaṭanūlē koṅku vēḷir iyaṟṟiya peruṅkataikku mutal nūlāka iruttal
       vēṇṭumeṉpatu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karuttu.
       innūl uñcaik kāṇṭam, ilāvāṇa kāṇṭam, makata kāṇṭam, vattava kāṇṭam,
       naravāṇa kāṇṭam, tuṟavuk kāṇṭam eṉṉum āṟu pakutikaḷai uṭaiyatu.
       paṭippavarkaḷ mūlattiṉ iṉimaiyai oruvāṟu aṟintu iṉpuṟumporuṭṭu
       mukkiyamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil cila cila mūlappakutikaḷ aṅkaṅkē
       cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
       kiṭaitta peruṅkataik kaiyeḻuttup piratiyil, makata kāṇṭattiṉ
       muṟpakutiyum, naravāṇa kāṇṭattiṉ piṟpakutiyum, tuṟavuk kāṇṭamum
       illāmaiyāl antap pakutikaḷukkuriya kataiyai utitōtaya kāvyam
       mutaliya vēṟu nūlkaḷiliruntu aṟintu eḻuti itu pūrtticeyyap peṟṟatu.
       arumpataṅkaḷukku uraiyum, apitāṉa viḷakkamum paṭippavarkaḷukku
       upayōkamāka irukkumeṉṟeṇṇi itaṉpāl cērkkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
       "citamparam" iṅṅaṉam,
       20-8-26 vē. cāminātaiyar.
       ---------------
                        _________________________________

                      utayaṇaṉ carittirac curukkam
                           1. uñcaik kāṇṭam

       camputtīviṟ cutarcaṉameṉṉum malaiyiṉ teṟkēyuḷḷatāṉa
       pāratavaruṭattiluḷḷa 1taruma kaṇṭattil 2vattavanāṭṭil 3 keḷacāmpi
       nakarattil kurukulattiṟ piṟantu ataṉai viḷakkiya catāṉikaṉeṉṉum
       aracaṉ cētināṭṭiluḷḷa vaicāli nakarattaracaṉum 4ēyarkulattava
       ṉumākiya 5cēṭaṉeṉpaṉuṭaiya putalvi mirukāpati eṉpavaḷai maṇañceytu
       illaṟattai iṉitu naṭatti aracāṭci ceytuvantāṉ. avaṉ, aracarkkuriya
       kuṇa vicēṭaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉātaliṉ, mantiraccuṟṟattārum ēṉaiyārum
       mikka iṉpamuṟṟu avaṉ kuṭainiḻalil vāḻvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam cila
       āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.
       caraṟkālattil oru nāḷ mēkan tōṉṟuvataiyum aḻivataiyum kaṇṭapōtu
       vairākkiyam piṟantamaiyāl, mēṟkūṟiya cēṭakarājaṉ, ñālaṅ kāvalai
       nañceṉa veṟuttut taṉ putalvaruḷ patiṉmarkaḷuḷ mūttavaṉāṉa
       taṉamittiraṉai aḻaittu, "iṉit tavañ ceyyac celvēṉ; nī inta
       irācciyapārattai oppukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟāṉ. avaṉ, "yāṉum tavañ
       ceytaṟku varuvēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟi maṟuppa, cēṭakaṉ, avaṉ tampikaḷ
       eṇmarait taṉittaṉiyē aḻait taḻaittuc colla avarkaḷum mūttavaṉ
       kūṟiyataiyē kūṟi maṟuttu viṭṭaṉar. appāl avaṉ pattām piḷḷaiyākiya
       vikkiraṉai arukaḻaittu mikavum vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟit taṉ aracāṭciyai
       avaṉiṭam opputtuviṭṭuc ceṉṟu vipulameṉṉum malaiyoṉṟiṉ cāralil
       āliṅkāṉattil tavañ ceytukoṇ ṭirunta srītarareṉṉum muṉivaraic
       caraṇamaṭaintu upatēcam peṟṟut tavañ ceyvāṉāyiṉaṉ.

   --------
       1. āriya kaṇṭameṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. 2. vatsatēcam.
       3. kōcampiyeṉa innūluḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭum. 4.hēhayar.
       5. utitōtaya kāviyattil ippeyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

   tantai mutaliyavarkaḷuṭaiya pirivāṟṟāmal varuttamuṟṟa vikkiraṉ,
   nikaḻntavaṟṟaic catāṉikaṉukkum taṉ taṅkai mirukāpatikkum tūtuvar
   mūlamākat terivittu viṭṭut tantaiyiṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyiṉpaṭi aracāṭci ceytu
   koṇṭiruntāṉ. ic ceytiyai aṟinta catāṉikaṉ viyantāṉ; mirukāpati mikka
   varuttattai yaṭaintu mūrccaiyuṟṟāḷ. piṉpu kaṇavaṉ palavāṟu tēṟṟat tēṟi
   avaḷ oruvāṟu āṟutaluṟ ṟiruntāḷ. iruvarum iṅṅaṉam cila nāḷ vāḻntu
   vantārkaḷ. cila kālam ceṉṟapiṉ mirukāpati karuppamuṟṟāḷ. akkaruppattil
   puṇṇiya puruṭaṉākiya tēvaṉoruvaṉ vantu taṅkiṉāṉ. avaḷukkuvēṇṭiya
   poruḷkaḷai varuvittaḷittum virumpiya iṭaṅkaḷukku aḻaittuc ceṉṟum
   avaḷatu mayaṟkai (macakkai)yaip pōkki aracaṉ avaḷaip pātukāttu vantāṉ.
   karuppam nirampiyatu. akkālattil oru nāḷ iravil mēlmāṭattuḷḷa
   nilāmuṟṟattil tāṉ irunta iṭaṅkaḷaiyellām cātiliṅka mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ
   civanta niṟamuḷḷaṉavākac ceyvittuc civanta malarmālaikaḷaiyum civanta
   aṇikalaṅkaḷaiyum tāṉ aṇintukoṇṭu taṉṉaic cūḻntuḷḷa tōḻiyarkaḷaiyum
   aṅṅaṉam aṇintukoḷḷac ceytuviṭṭu avarkaḷiṉ iṭaiyē oru mañcattiṉ mēl
   civanta paṭṭāṭai yoṉṟaip pōrttukkoṇṭu avaḷ ayarntu nittirai ceytāḷ.
   appoḻutu ākāya vaḻiyē ceṉṟatum palvaṉmai yuṭaiyatumāṉa 1carapap paṟavai
   yoṉṟu īram pularāta tacaittokuti yeṉṟu niṉaittu avaḷai mañcattuṭaṉ
   tūkkikkoṇṭu paṟantu ceṉṟu vipulakiriyiṟ cēṭaka muṉivaruṭaiya
   paḷḷiṉarukē mella vaittu uṇṇat toṭaṅkukaiyil, avaḷ viḻittukkoṇṭāḷ.
   ataṉaik kaṇṭa appaṟavai, "itu tacaittokutiyaṉṟu. uyirōṭu kūṭiya oru
   peṇvaṭivam" eṉṟu niṉaittu uṇṇāmal tiṭīreṉ ṟeḻuntu taṉṉiṭam
   ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. appāl avaḷ nāṟpuṟattaiyum pārttut tāṉ vēṟiṭam
   vantiruttalai yaṟintu tikaittu añci varuntuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. piṉpu accattālum
   ākāya vaḻiyē vanta varuttattālum karuppa vētaṉai atikarikkappeṟṟuc
   cūriyōtaya kālattil ellāk kōḷkaḷum nalvaḻi nōkkat tirumaṇi viḷakkam
   ticainiṉ ṟaḻala ōr āṇ kuḻantaiyaip peṟṟāḷ.
   -------
       1. kaṇṭappuḷḷeṉṟum kaṇṭapēraṇṭameṉṟum karuṭap paṟavai yeṉṟum vēṟu
       nūlkaḷ kūṟum.

   "porukaṭaṟ parutipōlap poṉṉaṉāṉ piṟanta pōḻtē
       maruḷuṭaiya māta ruṟṟa mammarnōy maṟaintatu" (cīva.304)

   piṉpu avaḷ mikka viruppattōṭu akkuḻantaiyait taḻuvi yeṭuttu antac
   camayattuḷḷa taṉ nilaimaiyai niṉaintu palavāṟu pulampiṉāḷ. appoḻutu
   nīrāṭutaṟ poruṭṭu iyalpāka anta vaḻiyē avviṭam vanta
   cēṭakamuṉivaruṭaiya kātiṟku avaḷuṭaiya pulampaloli eṭṭiyatu. uṭaṉē avar
   vantu kaṇṭu avaḷait tammuṭaiya makaḷeṉ ṟaṟinta taṉṟi nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyum
   tammuṭaiya ñāṉattā laṟintukoṇṭu, "añcātē" eṉṟu apaya maḷittuk
   kuḻantaiyuṭaṉ avaḷai aḻaittuc ceṉṟu tammuṭaiya paḷḷiyiṉ orupāl irukkac
   ceytu ātarittu varuvārāyiṉar. avarait taṉ tantaiyeṉṟaṟinta mirukāpati
   oruvakaiyāka āṟutaluṟṟiruntāḷ. aṅkē tavañ ceytukoṇṭirunta
   paramaviruṭikaḷ pallōrukkut talaivarum, tarumattaiyum viratattaiyum
   tāṅkiyarumākiya piramacuntara muṉivarum avar maṉaivi paḻippil kaṟpiṟ
   paramacuntariyum cēṭaka muṉivaruṭaiya viruppattiṉpaṭi mirukāpatiyaik
   kuḻantaiyuṭaṉ pātukāttu vantamaiyiṉ avaḷ ciṟitum kavalaiyiṉṟi
   yiruntaṉaḷ.
   cūriyōtaya kālattiṟ piṟantamaiyiṉ avaṉ utayaṇaṉeṉṉum peyar peṟutaṟku
   uriyaṉeṉṟu cēṭakamuṉivar nāmakaraṇañ ceytataṉṟi avaṉuṭaiya varalāṟu
   anēkaruṭaiya varalāṟukaḷukku iṭamāka viḷaṅkumeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar:
   "atirā ñālat taracuvīṟ ṟirunta
       kataiyuraik kellāṅ kāraṇa mātaliṟ
       putaiyiru ḷakaṟṟum poṅkoḷi maṇṭilam
       utaya mivartara vutittōṉ maṟṟivaṉ
       utayaṇa ṉākeṉap peyarmutaṟ koḷīi" (2.11:79-83)

   utayaṇaṉ, piṟaipōl vaḷarntu mēṟkūṟiya piramacuntara muṉivarkkup
   paramacuntaripāṟ ṟōṉṟiya yūkiyuṭaṉ naṭpuṟṟu virumpi viḷaiyāṭip piṉ
   ammuṉivariṭattum cēṭaka muṉivariṭattum aracarkkuriya palavakaik
   kalaikḷaiyum vēṟu kalaikaḷaiyum avaṉuṭaṉ kacaṭaṟak kaṟṟu iyaṟkai
   aṟivālum ceyaṟkai aṟivālum mēmpaṭṭu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. avarkaḷuṭaiya ākkattaik
   kaṇṭa muṉivarkaḷ mikavum pārāṭṭi āci kūṟiṉārkaḷ.
   appāl oru nāḷ piramacuntaramuṉivar yāṉaiyiṉ kōpattai aṭakki
   vayappaṭutti āḷutaṟkuriya mantiramoṉṟai utayaṇaṉukku upatēcittataṉṟi
   icaip payiṟci paṇṇuvittu, muṉpu intiraṉāl tamakkuk kiṭaittiruntatum
   yāṉai mutaliyavaṟṟai vayamākkuvataṟkuriyatumākiya kōṭapatiyeṉṉum yāḻai
   avaṉukkuk kaṟpittu ataṉai avaṉ vācittu varukaiyiṟ kēṭṭa kāṭṭu yāṉaikaḷ
   mutaliyaṉavum paṟavaikaḷum piṟavuyirkaḷum mikunta uvakaiyuṭaṉ vantu
   avaṉukku vēṇṭiya ēvalkaḷ ceytalaik kaṇṭu viyantu anta yāḻai avaṉukkē
   koṭuttu, "nī inta vittaiyil mikka āṟṟalai aṭaivāyāka" eṉṟu āci kūṟiṉar.
   kūṟiyavar, "ivaṉ uṉakku aṭaikkalam" eṉṟu taṉ putalvaṉākiya yūkiyaiyum
   avaṉpāl oppittaṉar. "kaṭantu ḷōrkaḷuṅ kaṭapparō makkaṇmēṟ kātal." piṉpu
   utayaṇaṉ yūkiyai uyirttuṇaiyākak koṇṭu ellā vakaiyilum ciṟappuṟṟu
   viḷaṅkiṉāṉ.
   orunāḷ, tāṉ ceṉṟa kāṭṭil pala piṭikaḷ cūḻap peṟṟatum patiṉāyiram
   yāṉaikaḷiṉ palamuḷḷatumāṉa oru teyvayāṉai niṉṟatu. ataṉaik kaṇṭa
   utayaṇaṉ mantirat tiyāṉattōṭu kōṭapatiyai vācittāṉ. vācikkavē aḵtu
   avaṉarukē vantu mikka aṉpu pārāṭṭi avaṉ ēviya toḻilkaḷaic ceytatu. ataṉ
   nilaiyaik kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ ataṉ mēlēṟit taṉṉiṭamaṭaintāṉ. aḵtu
   avaṉaiviṭṭup piriyāmal avaṉukkuriya paṇikaḷaic ceytuvantatu. cila
   nāṭkaḷ ceṉṟa piṉpu oru nāḷ iravil anta yāṉai avaṉ kaṉavil tōṉṟi, "eṉmēṟ
   pākarai yēṟṟiṉālum tōṟkayiṟṟāl eṉṉaik kaṭṭiṉālum yāṉ uṇṇutaṟku muṉṉamē
   nī uṇṭālum uṉṉaiviṭṭu nīṅkiviṭuvēṉ" eṉṟatu. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ uṭaṉē
   viḻitteḻuntu atumutal mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu tavaṟukaḷum uṇṭākātapaṭi
   cākkirataiyāka naṭantu vantāṉ. teyva yāṉaiyum avaṉai viṭṭu nīṅkāmaṟ
   paṇipurintu oḻukiyatu.
   ippaṭi yirukkuṅ kālattil aracāṭciyil veṟuppuṟṟut tuṟavaṟam pūṇutaṟku
   virumpiya vikkiramaṉ taṉakkuc cantatiyillāmaiyiṉāl taṉ irācciyattaip
   pātukāttaṟku yārum illaiyēyeṉṟu tuṉpamuṟṟup piṉ taṉ tantaiyākiya cēṭaka
   muṉivarpāṟ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅki mutalil taṉ karuttait terivittu appāl
   avaruṭaiya upatēca moḻikaḷai viruppattuṭaṉ kēṭṭuk koṇṭiruntapoḻutu,
   iyalpākavē avviṭam vanta utayaṇaṉaik kaṉṭu, oḷiyālum tōṟṟap polivālum
   ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiya avaṉai naṉku matittu, "ivaṉ yār?" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ.
   kēṭkavē muṉivar, "kuru kulattaracaṉākiya catāṉikaṉ tēviyum uṉ
   taṅkaiyumākiya mirukāpatiyiṉ putalvaṉ ivaṉ; ūḻviṉai vayattāl avaḷ iṅkē
   vantu avaṉaip peṟṟaṉaḷ. kalvi kēḷvikaḷil avaṉ ciṟantavaṉāyiṉaṉ. uriya
   pirāyam vantuviṭṭamaiyāl ivaṉukku iṉi aṟivikka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aṟivittuk
   kaucāmpi nakarkku aṉuppa eṇṇiyirukkiṟēṉ" eṉṟaṉar. uṭaṉē vikkiraṉ
   mikavum uvantu, "ivaṉai aṭiyēṉukku aḷittāl ēyarkulattiṟku uriya
   urimaiyai ivaṉpāl oppittu ivaṉai aracaṉākkiviṭṭut tuṟavu pūṇṭu tavañ
   ceytu uyvēṉ" eṉṟu mutalil avaraiyum appāl mirukāpatiyaiyum irantu
   avarkaḷuṭaiya aṉumati peṟṟu yūkiyōṭu utayaṇaṉaiyum mirukāpatiyaiyum
   vaicāli nakarukku aḻaittuc ceṉṟu aṅkēyuḷḷa mantirikaḷukkum cēṉait
   talaivarkaḷukkum maṟṟavarkaḷukkum terivittut taṉ aracāṭciyai avaṉpāl
   oppittuviṭṭut taṉ eṇṇattai niṟaivēṟṟik koḷḷutaṟkut tavavaṉañ ceṉṟāṉ.
   mēṟkūṟiya teyva yāṉai utayaṇaṉuṭaṉē vaicālikku vantu vaḻakkappaṭiyē
   utayaṇaṉukkuc ceytaṟkuriya ēvalkaḷaic ceytukoṇṭu avaṉuṭaiya yāḻicaiyai
   nukarntu annakaril iruntatu.
   appāl utayaṇaṉ yūkiyait taṉṉuṭaiya mutal mantiriyākki aracāṭci
   ceytukoṇṭu vaicāli nakarattiliruntāṉ. appōtu avaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya vīrattālum
   yūkiyiṉ utaviyālum teyva yāṉaiyiṉ upakārattālum pala pakaivarkaḷai
   veṉṟu aṭakki avarkaḷuṭaiya nāṭukaḷait taṉvayamākki vāḻntu vantāṉ.
   nilāmuṟṟattil uṟaṅkiya tēviyaik kāṇāmaṟ catāṉikaṉ tiṭukkiṭṭu
   nikaḻntatai aṟiyāmal, pala iṭaṅkkaḷukkum taṉ parivāraṅkaḷai aṉuppit
   tēṭuvittuk koṇṭē kālaṅ kaḻittuvantāṉ. varukaiyil oru nāḷ avaṉ mukkāla
   uṇarciyuḷḷa suvrutareṉṉum muṉivarai yaṭaintu taṉ kuṟaiyait terivittu
   varuntiṉāṉ. avar mirukāpatiyaic carapap puḷ eṭuttuc ceṉṟu vipulakiriyil
   vaittatu mutal utayaṇaṉ vaicāliyil aracāṭci ceytukoṇ ṭiruppatu
   iṟutiyākavuḷḷa ceytikaḷaiyellām viḷaṅkak kūṟiyataṉṟic catāṉikaṉuṭaiya
   piṟkālac ceytikaḷaiyum terivittaṉar. uṭaṉē catāṉikaṉ vaicāli nakarattai
   yaṭaintu mirukāpatiyaik kaṇṭu avaḷuṭaiya kuṇavicēṭaṅkaḷaip pārāṭṭip
   pukaḻntaṉaṉ. piṉpu avaḷ vāyilāka utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu avaṉāl
   upacarikkappeṟṟuc cila nāḷ aṅkē yiruntu, appāl avaṉai annakarilēyē
   aracāṭci ceytukoṇ ṭirukkumpaṭi colliviṭṭuttāṉ tēviyuṭaṉ keḷacāmpiyai
   yaṭaintu muṉpōla aracu purintu vantāṉ. appōtu mirukāpati iraṇṭu
   putalvarkaḷaip peṟṟāḷ. avarkaḷ muṟaiyē piṅkalaṉ, kaṭakaṉ eṉa
   vaḻaṅkappeṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kalvi kēḷvikaḷiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   utayaṇaṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē keḷacāmpikku vantu tantaiyuṭaṉ cila
   kālamiruntuviṭṭuc celvatuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam cila āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟa piṉ,
   catāṉikaṉukkut taṉ aracāṭciyil veṟuppum tuṟavil viruppamum uṇṭāyiṉa.
   ataṉāl, utayaṇaṉai varuvittu avaṉpāl aracāṭciyai oppittuviṭṭut tāṉ
   eṇṇiyavāṟē tavañ ceytaṟku vaṉañ ceṉṟāṉ.
   appāl utayaṇaṉ, keḷacāmpi, vaicāliyeṉṉum iraṇṭu irācciyaṅkaḷaiyum
   keḷacāmpiyilē yiruntu koṇṭu cevvaṉē cila nāḷ āṇṭu piṉ
   vaicāliyilēyiruntu aracu puriyumpaṭi yūkiyai niyamittuviṭṭut tāṉ
   kaucāmpiyai maṭṭum āḷuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. yūkiyaiyallāmaṟ kalviyaṟiviṟ
   ciṟantavarkaḷum puṟattē ceṉṟu ulāvi varum uyir pōṉṟavarkaḷumāṉa
   vayantakaṉ, urumaṇṇuvā, iṭavakaṉeṉṟu vēṟu mūṉṟu mantirikaḷum
   utayaṇaṉukku uṇṭu. avarkaḷum vēṟu cila tōḻarum avaṉukku aṅkē utavi
   purintu vantārkaḷ. mēṟkūṟiya teyva yāṉaiyum avaṉukku vēṇṭiya paṇikaḷ
   purintu aṅkē utavi ceytuvantatu. appōtu utayaṇaṉ pala pakaivarkaḷai
   veṉṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya iṭaṅkaḷaik kaikkoṇṭu mika mēmpāṭuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ.
   kalviyālūm, vīrattālum, āṭci muṟaiyālum, piratāpattālum, utayaṇaṉ
   ciṟantavaṉeṉṟa pukaḻ eṅkum paraviyatu.
   iṅṅaṉa mirukkaiyil oru nāḷ utayaṇaṉ, cēṉaiyaiyum teyva yāṉaiyaiyum ūril
   niṟuttiviṭṭu urumaṇṇuvā mutaliya cila tōḻarōṭum, cila vīrarōṭum maṭṭuñ
   ceṉṟu oru kāṭṭil vēṭṭaiyāṭik kaḷaittu mayaṅki nīr vēṭkaiyuṟṟu
   varuntiṉaṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷum appaṭiyē varuttamaṭaintārkaḷ. piṉ ellōrum,
   "intak kāṭṭil ētēṉum teyvamirukkumāyiṉ iraṅki nammuṭaiya tākattai atu
   pōkkiyaruḷum" eṉṟu niccayittuk koṇṭavarkaḷāki aṅkē oru puracamaratti
   ṉiḻalilē cōrntu kiṭantārkaḷ. kiṭakkaiyil, veṇṭukil uṭuttiya vaṇṇam mika
   melliya mēṟ pōrvaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉum araiyiṟ kaccaṇintavaṉum cantaṉam
   pūciya mārpai yuṭaiyavaṉum oḷiviṭṭu vīcukiṉṟa mēṉiyai yuṭaiyavaṉum
   piṟaivaṭam viḷaṅkukiṉṟa kaḻuttai yuṭaiyavaṉum ceṅkaḻunīr mālaiyaic
   cūṭiya kuñciyai yuṭaiyavaṉum āyiram itaḻai yuṭaiya centāmarai malar
   pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉumākiya mika aḻakiya ōriḷaiñaṉ aṅkē vantu
   avarkaḷai nōkki, "nīvir yāvir? mikunta iḷaippai yaṭaintirukkiṟīrkaḷē;
   nērnta tuṉpam yātu?" eṉṟu viṉāviṉaṉ. avarkaḷ tamakku appōtuḷḷa nīr
   vēṭkait tuṉpattaik kūṟiṉar. avaṉ uṭaṉē iṉiya naṉṉīraik koṇarntu
   avarkaḷuṭaiya tākattai nīkkiṉaṉ. nīkkiya piṉ vēṟoṉṟaiyum etirpārāmal
   avaṉ cellat toṭaṅkiyapoḻutu avarkaḷ avaṉuṭaiya upakārattai mikavum
   pārāṭṭi avaṉai nōkki, "itu pōṉṟa tuṉpam iṉi eṅkaḷukku ētēṉum
   uṇṭākumāyiṉ, ummai niṉaittup pōkkik koḷḷak karutukiṉṟōm; nīr iṉṉāreṉṟu
   emakkut terivikka vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉar. kēṭkavē avaṉ, "nāṉ
   kupēraṉukkut toṇṭu ceyyum iyakkarkaḷil oruvaṉ. eṉ peyer nañcukaṉeṉpatu;
   uṅkaḷuṭaiya iṉiya naṭpiṉaṉāka eṉṉaik karutuka; nīṅkaḷ entac camayattil
   niṉaittālum uṭaṉē nāṉ vantu num kuṟaiyait tīrttaṟkuriya
   kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ; eṉṉai niṉaittaṟkuriya mantiram iḵtu" eṉru colli am
   mantirattai urumaṇṇuvāvukku upatēcittuviṭṭut taṉṉiṭañ ceṉṟaṉaṉ; avarkaḷ
   avaṉuṭaiya pērutaviyai maṟavātavarkaḷāki avaṉaip pārāṭṭik koṇṭu nakaram
   vantu cērntārkaḷ.
   piṉpu oru nāḷ nāṭaka araṅkil āṭiya āṭaṉ makaḷiruṭaiya kūttilum pāṭṭilum
   maṉattaic celutti mayaṅkip pōyp paci mikutiyāl utayaṇaṉ mēṟkūṟiya teyva
   yāṉaikku vaḻakkampōlē uṇavaḷittalai maṟantu tāṉ munti yuṇṭāṉ.
   ataṉaiyaṟinta anta yāṉai,"utayaṇaṉ eṉakku vākkaḷittatai maṟantuviṭṭu
   uṇṭatu taṉ puṇṇiyak kuṟaivālētāṉ" eṉṟeṇṇi uṭaṉē taṉṉiṭam ceṉṟuviṭṭatu.
   piṉpu kōṭapatiyai vācikkumpōtu kēṭpataṟku vaḻakkappaṭiyē teyva yāṉai
   vārāmaiyai aṟinta utayaṇaṉ tāṉ ceyta piḻaiyai niṉaintu mikavum
   varuntiṉaṉ; ataṉai aṟinta mantirimār, "anta yāṉaiyai eppaṭiyāvatu tēṭit
   taruvōm" eṉṟu vākkaḷittaṉar. aṅṅaṉam vākkaḷittum varuttan
   taṇiyātavaṉākik kavalaik kaṭalil aḻuntiya utayaṇaṉ anēka iṭaṅkaḷukkut
   taṉ paricaṉaṅkaḷ palarai aṉuppi ataṉait tēṭac ceytaṉaṉ. ceytum aḵtu
   akappaṭāmaiyāl yāḻuṅ kaiyumākat tāṉē kāṭukaḷukku nāḷtōṟuñ ceṉṟu ceṉṟu
   ataṉait tēṭit tirivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   avanti nāṭṭil uccaiṉi nakarattil 1piraccōtaṉaṉeṉṉuñ cakkaravaratti,
   vīram mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟantu pala aracarkaḷum tiṟai koṭukka aracāṭci
   ceytu ceṅkōl celutti vantāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya patiṉāyiran tēvimārkaḷil
   mutaṉmai vāynta patumakārikai yeṉpavaḷpāl avaṉukku intiraṉuṭaiya
   aruḷiṉāṟ peṇ orutti utittaṉaḷ. ataṉāl avaḷukku vācavatattai yeṉṟu
   peyariṭappaṭṭatu. avaḷ mikka aḻakuṭaiyavaḷ:
   ------
       1. pirattiyōtaṉaṉ.
       ----
       "yāṟṟaṟa laṉṉa kūntal yāṟṟuc
       cuḻiyeṉak kiṭanta kuḻinavil koppūḻ;
       villeṉak kiṭanta puruvam villiṉ
       ampeṉak kiṭanta ceṅkaṭai maḻaikkaṇ;
       piṟaiyeṉac cuṭaruñ ciṟunutal piṟaiyiṉ
       niṟaiyeṉat tōṉṟuṅ kaṟaiyil vāṇmukam;
       kiḷiyeṉa miḻaṟṟuṅ kiḷavi kiḷiyiṉ
       oḷipeṟu vāyi ṉaṉṉa voḷḷukir;
       vēyeṉat tiraṇṭa meṉṟōḷ vēyiṉ
       viḷaṅkumut taṉṉa tuḷaṅkoḷi muṟuval;
       kāntaṇmuki ḻaṉṉa melviral kāntaṭ
       pūntuṭup paṉṉa puṉaivaḷai muṉkai" (4.11:64-79)

   avaḷ maṟṟa ellāvakaiyilum ciṟantavaḷ; vēṟuḷḷa paṭṭat tēviyarkaḷiṭam
   piraccōtaṉaṉukku utitta piḷḷaikaḷum peṇkaḷum mikap palar. piḷḷaikaḷuḷḷē
   mikac ciṟantavarkaḷ pālakaṉ, pālakumaraṉ, kōpālakaṉ eṉpār. mikka
   vīriyamuṭaiyatum mikap periyatumāṉa oru paṭṭattu yāṉai avaṉukku uṇṭu.
   ataṉ peyar naḷakiri yeṉpatu. avaṉukku mantirikaḷ patiṉāṟāyiravar.
   antac cakkaravartti oru nāḷ, "ellā aracarkaḷum kappam
   celuttivarukiṟārkaḷā?" eṉṟu vicāritta poḻutu kaṇakkar, "vattava
   nāṭṭaracaṉum vaicāli nakarattaracaṉum vaḻakkappaṭi celuttavillai" eṉṟu
   terivittaṉar.ataṟkuriya kāraṇattai viṉāviyataṉṟi avarkaḷaip piṭittu
   varumpaṭi ceytu vicārikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aracaṉ mantirikaḷai nōkkik kūṟa,
   avarkaḷuḷḷē mutaṉmai vāntavaṉum nītinūṟ payiṟciyilum tantirattilum
   mikkavaṉumākiya cālaṅkāyaṉeṉpavaṉ, "utayaṇaṉukkut teyvayāṉaiyoṉṟum
   parāk kiramacālikaḷāṉa mantirikaḷum tuṇaiyāka iruttaliṉ avaṉ ippoḻutu
   vellappaṭāṉ. ippōtu vaicāli nakarattaracaṉāka iruppavaṉum
   utayaṇaṉuṭaiya naṇpaṉumākiya yūkiyum vellutaṟkariyaṉ. avaṉum utayaṇaṉum
   cērāmalirukkumpaṭi mutaliṟ ceyya vēṇṭum. yūkiyuṭaṉ pōr ceytaṟkākac
   caturaṅka cēṉaikaḷaiyum, takka vīrarkaḷaiyum nām vaicālikku aṉuppiṉāl,
   yūki taṉ cēṉaikaḷōṭu vantu nammuṭaiya cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ pōr ceyvāṉ; avaṉ
   vantu utayaṇaṉukku utavi ceyya iṭamirātu. iyalpākavē ellā nalamum
   vāynta utayaṇaṉ taṉakkup paṇi purintuvanta teyva yāṉaiyaip
   pirintamaiyāl ippōtu ataṉait tēṭikkoṇṭu yāḻuṅ kaiyumākak kāṭukaḷil
   alaikiṟāṉ. ataṉālum, yūki vantu utavuvataṟku iṭa millāmaiyālum
   utayaṇaṉai nām piṭittukkoṇṭu varuvataṟkut takka camayam itu. yām māya
   yāṉai yoṉṟai nirumittu aṉuppi mayakki utayaṇaṉaip piṭittuvarac
   ceyvatutāṉ takka upāyam" eṉṟāṉ. kēṭṭa piraccōtaṉaṉ uṭaṉē yūkiyuṭaṉ pōr
   ceyvataṟkākac cila vīrarkaḷaic cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ vaicālikku aṉuppiṉaṉ.
   ciṟanta ciṟpikaḷaik koṇṭu arakku mutaliyavaṟṟālum, maraṅkaḷālum mikap
   peritāka entirayāṉai yoṉṟaic ceyvittu āyutapāṇikaḷāṉa toṇṇūṟṟāṟu
   pōrvīrarkaḷai ataṉ vayiṟṟil irukkumpaṭi ceytu ataṉ tutikkaiyuḷḷum,
   nāṉku kālkaḷuḷḷum pala vakaiyāṉa āyutaṅkaḷai vaippittu aṉuppiyataṉṟip
   patiṉāṟāyirañ caturaṅkap paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ mēṟkūṟiya cālaṅkāyaṉaiyum oru
   cēṉait talaivaṉaiyum ataṉpiṉ keḷacāmpikku aṉuppiṉāṉ.
   piraccōtaṉaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭiyē ceṉṟu cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ vaicāli nakarattaic
   cūḻntukoṇṭa vīrar mutaliyavarkaḷukkum yūkikkum perum pōr nikaḻntatu.
   utayaṇaṉ tēṭikkoṇ ṭirukkum kāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil nīlamalai pōṉṟa anta entira
   yāṉaiyāṉatu mēkam pōṟ piḷiṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu ulāviyatu. cālaṅ kāyaṉ piṟar
   ayātapaṭi perum paṭaiyōṭu ceṉṟu ataṉ piṉṉē vēṟiṭattu oḷittiruntāṉ. anta
   vañca yāṉai taṉṉai uyiruḷḷa yāṉaipōṟ kāṭṭip pala piṭikaḷ cūḻa akkāṭṭil
   ulāvukaiyil ataṉaik kaṇṭa kāṭṭu vēṭarkaḷ muṉṉam kāṇāmaṟpōṉa teyva
   yāṉaiyeṉṟu niṉaittu makiḻntu uṭaṉē ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉukkut terivittārkaḷ.
   appōtu utayaṇaṉ makiḻvuṟṟu avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya cammāṉaṅkaḷaic
   ceytuviṭṭu yāḻuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭāṉ. pakaippaṭai varumeṉṟu vayavaṉeṉṉum
   paṟavaiyiṉ oliyāl teriyavantatu. piṉṉum pala tīya nimittaṅkaḷ
   kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. uṭaṉvanta vayantakaṉ taṭuttāṉ.
   utayaṇaṉ avaṟṟaic ciṟitum uṭkoḷḷāmal viraintu kāṭu ceṉṟu anta yāṉaiyaik
   kaṇṭu taṉakkup paḻakkamuḷḷa teyva yāṉaiyākavē niṉaintu muḻu matiyaik
   kaṇṭa cakōrampōl makiḻntu taṉṉuṭaṉ vanta cēṉaikaḷai veku tūrattiṟkappāl
   niṟuttiviṭṭut tāṉmaṭṭum ataṉarukiṟ ceṉṟu 1iṉiya irāka moṉṟai yāḻil
   vācittāṉ. appōtu atu yāḻicaiyil mikka viruppa muḷḷatupōlvantu avaṉ
   etirē niṉṟatu. avaṉ makiḻvaṭaintu meymmaṟantu niṉṟāṉ; niṉṟavuṭaṉ
   uḷḷirunta pōrvīrarkaḷ mika viraivāka ataṉ poṟikaḷaik kaḻaṟṟikkoṇṭu
   tiṭīreṉṟu veḷiyē vantu koṭiya coṟkaḷaic collikkoṇṭu pōr ceyyat
   toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. itu mikac ciṟiyavarkaḷuṭaiya ceyaleṉṟu eṇṇip puṉmuṟuval
   ceytukoṇṭu utayaṇaṉ kampīramāka niṉṟāṉ. niṟkaiyil, ainnūṟu
   pōrvīrarkaḷōṭu piṉṉē niṉṟa vayantakaṉāl antat toṇṇūṟṟaṟuvarum
   kollappaṭṭārkaḷ. maṟaintu cēṉaiyuṭaṉ piṉṉē orupālirunta cālaṅkāyaṉ
   mikunta kōpaṅkoṇṭu pala vīrarkaḷōṭu vantu etirttup pōr ceytu utayaṇaṉāl
   tōlviyuṟṟāṉ;
   --------
       1. māḷavarākameṉṟum takkarākameṉṟum vēṟu nūlkaḷ kūṟum.

   cēṉātipatiyum māṇṭāṉ. cālaṅkāyaṉai veṭṭuvataṟkut taṉ vāḷai uyarttiya
   utayaṇaṉ, mantiriyaik kollalākāteṉṟa nūl vitiyai yuṭkoṇṭu avaṉaik
   kollāmal viṭṭuviṭṭu avvāḷāṟ palyāṉaikaḷai veṭṭi vīḻttip piṉpu oru
   yāṉaiyai veṭṭat toṭaṅkiyapoḻutu ataṉ kompiṟ paṭṭu avvāḷ oṭintu pōyiṟṟu.
   āyutamiṉṟit taṉiyē niṉṟa utayaṇaṉaip piṭittukkoḷḷutaṟkuc cālaṅkāyaṉ
   mutaliyōr cūḻntukoṇṭārkaḷ. appoḻutu utayaṇaṉ nikaḻntavaṟṟai ōr
   ōlaiyileḻuti ataṉai yūkiyiṭam cērppikkumpaṭi vayantakaṉiṭam
   koṭuttaṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ avarkaḷ vacamāyiṉaṉ.
   appāl, oru matayāṉaiyait tāmarait taṇṭiṉ nūlāṟ kaṭṭuvatu pōlat taṉ
   mēlāṭaiyāṟ cālaṅkāyaṉ utayaṇaṉaik kaṭṭi oru tēriṉ mēlēṟṟik koṇṭu
   uccaiṉi ceṉṟu piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam terivittu avaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭiyē
   1iruṇmayamākiya oru peruñ ciṟaiyiṟ kālkaḷil vilaṅkiṭṭu mikat
   tuṉpamuṟumpaṭi avaṉai vaittuviṭṭāṉ. iḵtivvāṟāka;
   vayantakaṉ viraintu ceṉṟu vaicāliyiṉ puṟattē perum pōr nikaḻttikkoṇṭu
   niṉṟa yūkiyaik kaṇṭu nikaḻntavaṟṟaik kūṟi utayaṇaṉatu tirumukattai
   avaṉiṭaṅkoṭuttāṉ. ataṉaip pirittup paṭittaṟinta yūki mutalil mikunta
   tuṉpamuṟṟut taṉ talaivaṉatu nilaimaikku varunti, appāl mikka ciṉam
   koṇṭu2 "irumpai eli viḻuṅkiṟṟu" eṉṟataṟku, "kuḻantaiyaip paruntu
   koṇṭupōyiṟṟu" eṉṟatu pōla ulakil vañcakac ceyal ceytavaṉai
   vañcakacaceyal ceytē.
   -----
       1 "vēḻa vēṭṭattu vīḻa nūṟi yaruñciṟaip paḷḷi yāppoṭu pukka
       peruñciṟaip paḷḷip pēriruḷ" {1.15:132-4) eṉap peruṅkataiyilum
       "vāṉṟaḷai viṭīiya"(15:63) eṉa maṇimēkalai yilum, vārār vaṉamulai
       vācava tattaiyeṉ ṟārāṉuñ collap paṭuvā ḷavaḷuntaṉ pērāya mellā
       moḻiyap perunteruvē,kārār taṭantōṭ ṭaṉaikkālaṉ piṉ pōṉāḷ, ūrā
       rikaḻntiṭap paṭṭāḷē?" (ciṟiya tirumaṭal 66-8) eṉat tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār
       vākkilum kāṇappaṭutalāl, utayaṇaṉirunta ciṟaiccālai mikka
       iruṇmayamāka iruntateṉṟum avaṉ kālil vilaṅku iṭappaṭ ṭirunta teṉṟum
       terikiṉṟaṉa.
       2. utitōtaya kāviyam.

   vellavēṇṭumeṉṟu taṉṉuḷḷē niccayittukkoṇṭu aṅkē cūḻntu niṉṟa naṇparkaḷai
   nōkki, "kaḷḷa yāṉaiyāl mayakki emmaracaṉaip piṭittut taṉṉakarkku
   varuvitta piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya nakarattai yāṉ oru yāṉaiyālēyē aḻippittu
   utayaṇaṉai mīṭpatallāmal uñcai nakarattait tīkkiraiyākkiyum
   avaṉaikkoṇṭu vācavatattaiyaip pirittup piraccōtaṉaṉ aṟiyāmal valittuk
   koṇṭuvarac ceytum avaṉukku avaḷai maṇañ ceyvippēṉ" eṉṟu capatañ
   ceytuviṭṭu vaicāli nakarattaip pātukāttu varumpaṭi taṉ cuṟṟattārkaḷai
   niyamittāṉ. keḷacāmpi nakarattaip pātukākkum vaṇṇam piṅkala
   kaṭakarukkuc colli aṉuppiṉāṉ.
   avantināṭṭiṉ ellaippuṟattatākiya puṭpaka nakarattil utayaṇaṉuṭaiya
   cuṟṟattārkaḷai irukkac ceytu cēṉaikaḷōṭē aṅkiruntu avarkaḷaip
   pātukāppatuṭaṉ piṉpu ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai appoḻu tap poḻutu ālōcittuc
   ceytu varumpaṭi colli iṭavakaṉai anta nakarukku aṉuppiṉāṉ. cayanti
   nakarattil urumaṇṇuvāvai irukkac ceytāṉ; iṅṅaṉam iṉṉum ceyya
   vēṇṭiyavaṟaic ceytu muṭitta yūki, "utayaṇaṉuṭaiya piriviṉukku āṟṟāmal
   varuntiya yūki, iṟantu pōyviṭṭāṉ" eṉṟa ceytiyai ellā nāṭukaḷilum
   nakaraṅkaḷilum paravac ceytuviṭṭu oruvarukkun teriyātapaṭi tāṉ maṟaintu
   niṉṟu, oru piṇattait taṉṉuṭaiya piṇamākap piṟar niṉaikkumpaṭi ceytu
   ataṉait taṉṉuṭaiya aṇikala mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ puṉaintu ataic cuṭuvikkumpaṭi
   ceytuviṭṭut tāṉ māṟuvēṭam pūṇṭu appaṭiyē vēṟu vēṭam pūṇṭa pala
   vīrarkaḷōṭum vayantakaṉ mutaliya tōḻarkaḷōṭum kūṉar, kuṟaḷar, cittar
   mutaliyavarkaḷōṭum uñcaiyai yaṭaintu ataṉ puṟattēyuḷḷa taṉiyiṭamoṉṟil
   iruntu mēl nikaḻtta vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai ellōrukkum terivittuviṭṭup
   piraccōtaṉaṉatu araṇmaṉaiyilum akanakarattilum, puṟanakarattilum,
   ayalūrkaḷilum piṟar aṟiyātapaṭi avvavviṭattiṟkēṟṟa vēlaikaḷaip peṟṟu
   iruntu varum vaṇṇam colli avarkaḷai aṉuppiviṭṭuc ceṉṟu tāṉ anta nakarp
   puṟattēyuḷḷa mākaḷa vaṉattē yirukkum kāḷi kōyilil iruntu mēl āka
   vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai mutalil ārāyntāṉ.
   yūki iṟantuviṭṭā ṉeṉpataik kēṭṭa piraccōtaṉaṉ mika makiḻntu pōrukkuc
   ceṉṟa cēṉaikaḷai vaicāliyiṉiṉṟum varuvittuviṭṭuk kavalai nīṅki,
   utayaṇaṉuṭaiya nāṭukaḷiṟ cila pakutikaḷaiyum valintu kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ.
   piṅkala kaṭakar yūki, iṟantāṉeṉṟa ceytiyaik kēḷviyuṟṟu varuntip pulampi
   mayāṉa maṭaintu aṅkēyuḷḷa cāmpalil avaṉuṭaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ
   mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu mikat tuṉpuṟṟuk keḷacāmpi nakarkku mīṇṭaṉar.
   appāl yūki uccaiṉiyiṉ puṟattēyuḷḷa1 ōr ūrai yaṭaintu aṅkē
   oruvarumillāta pāḻvīṭoṉṟil vayantakaṉ mutaliya cila aṉparkaḷuṭaṉ iruntu
   ālōcittu vēṟu pāṣaikaḷai avarkaḷukkuk kaṟpittuk
   kaṭaivīti mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil viyāpāra mutaliyavaṟṟaic ceyyumpaṭi
   avarkaḷai aṉuppiṉāṉ. vayantakaṉ piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya kumārarkaḷōṭu cērntu
   kalvi kaṟpāṉāki avarkaḷukku ēval ceytukoṇṭu miruntāṉ.
   ---------
       1 avvūrp peyar pākameṉṟu terikiṟatu; utitōtaya kāviyam, utayaṇa.

   yūkiyāl aṉuppappaṭṭa vīrarkaḷiṟ cilar ūmai vēṭamum cilar ceviṭar
   vēṭamum tarittuk koṇṭu araṇmaṉai mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil tāmtām ceytaṟkuriya
   vēlaikaḷaip peṟṟu avaṟṟai naṉku ceypavarkaḷāki appoḻu tappoḻutu aṅkē
   nikaḻum ceytikaḷaiyum āṇṭuḷḷār ceyyum ālōcaṉaikaḷaiyum mella aṟintu
   vantu yūkikkuc colavārāyiṉar. maṟṟaiyōriṟ palar piṟaraṟiyāmal aṅkaṅkē
   ceṉṟu tāṅkaḷ avvav viṭaṅkaḷil aṟivaṉavaṟṟai yūkikku aṟivittum avaṉ
   kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟait tām aṟintukoṇṭum tattam iṭam celvārkaḷ. pirintavarkaḷ
   oṉṟu cērvataṟkakum cērntārkaḷ pirintu celvataṟkum cila kuṟippolikaḷum
   kuṟippukkaḷum avaṉāl aṟivikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
   avarkaḷ appaṭiyirukkaiyil cētināṭṭiṉ ayalnāṭāṉa pāñcāla tēcattu
   aracaṉākiya āruṇiyeṉpavaṉ, ēyar kulattavarkkum, taṉ kulattavarkkumuḷḷa
   paramparai vairattāl akkula vurimai pūṇṭa utayaṇaṉpāṟ pakaimai
   koṇṭavaṉātaliṉ, avaṉ ciṟaippaṭṭatai aṟintu uṭaṉē cēṉaikaḷṭaṉ
   puṟappaṭṭuppōy kaucāmpi nakarattaik kaippaṟṟi ataṉaiyē taṉṉuṭaiya
   irācatāṉiyākak koṇṭu āḷat toṭaṅki viṭṭāṉ. avaṉukku añcip piṅkalaṉum
   kaṭakaṉum cila cēṉaikaḷōṭu ayaliṭañ ceṉṟu maṟaintiruppārāyiṉar.
   uccaiṉi nakaril iṅṅaṉam caṅkētamāṉa cila ēṟpāṭukaḷaic ceytukoṇṭu
   puṟattē irunta yūki, oru nāḷ mikunta nōyuṭaiyavaṉ pōlavum teyvāvēcaṅ
   koṇṭavaṉ pōlavum taṉṉaik kāṭṭi nīrk karakam oṉṟait talaiyil ēntikkoṇṭum
   kaiyait taṭṭikkoṇṭum pāṭikkoṇṭum nakara vītiyiṟ ceṉṟu, " tēva
   lōkattiliruntu oru tēvaṉ innakaril vantirukkiṟāṉ. avaṉ eṉṉuṭaiya
   talaivaṉ: yāṉ avaṉaip piṉtoṭarntu vantēṉ;1 aintalai nākam oṉṟai ivvūr
   aracaṉ vaittirukkiṟāṉ. aḵtu evviṭattē yuḷḷatu?" eṉṟu mayakkamum
   teḷivumuḷḷa pala vakaiyāṉa vārttaikaḷaiccollikkoṇṭē2 pala caṉaṅkaḷ
   viyantum payan tum iraṅkiyum taṉṉaic cūḻntuvara naṭittukkoṇṭum utayaṇaṉ
   irukkum ciṟaiccālaiyiṉ pakkattai yaṭaintāṉ. aṭaintavaṉ utayaṇaṉum
   tāṉumē kaṟṟatum piṟar aṟiyātumāṉa oru pāṭṭaip pāṭiṉāṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ
   yūki vantirukkiṟāṉeṉṟu terintu makiḻntu kavalai tīrntu tāṉ aṅkiruttalai
   vēyṅkuḻalai ūti avaṉukkut terivittuviṭṭuc ciṟai kāppāḷariṭañ collik
   koṇṭu vēṭikkai pārppavaṉ pōlap puṟattē vantāṉ. yūkiyum avaṉuṭaṉ vanta
   vīrarkaḷum utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu āṟutaluṟṟārkaḷ. piṉpu yūki piṟar
   taṉṉaippēyeṉṟu niṉaikkumpaṭi naṭittukkoṇṭu tāṉ irukkum iṭañ ceṉṟāṉ.
   -------
       1 'aintalai nākam’ eṉṟatu, tōḻar nālvarkkum utayaṇaṉukkum
       uyiroṉṟāka iruttalpaṟṟi; "aintalai yaraviṉ cīṟṟat tāraḻal
       kuḷikkaluṟṟār" (cīvaka cintāmaṇi, 746) "puṟṟiṉi luṟaiyum poṟivari
       yaintalaip paṟṟaru nākam paṟṟivan tiṉitā, vuṟṟavin nakarat tuṭciṟai
       vaittār" (utayaṇa kumāra kāviyam, 1:76), "uṭampāṟiraṇṭiṟ kuyiroṉ
       ṟeṉa" (tiruviḷaiyāṭal, 46:13) eṉpavai aṟiyaṟ pālaṉa.
       2 "koṭikkō campik kōmaka ṉākiya, vaṭittērt tāṉai vattavaṉ ṟaṉṉai,
       vañcañ ceytuḻi vāṉṟaḷai viṭīiya, uñcaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya yūki yantaṇaṉ,
       uruvuk kovvāvuṟu nōykaṇṭu, parivuṟu mākkaḷiṟ ṟāmpari veyti"
       maṇimēkalai, 15: 61-6.

   yūki mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu vēṭam pūṇṭu caṉaṅkaḷ aiyuṟutaṟkiṭamāṉa cila
   vārttaikaḷaic collikkoṇṭu piṉṉum cila nāḷ vītikaḷil ulāvi varukaiyil,
   ciṟaiccālai vēlaikkārarkaḷiṟ kūrtta puttiyaiyuṭaiya oruvaṉ pirac
   cōtaṉaṉiṭañ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅki, "aracarkkaracē! cila tiṉaṅkaḷākappala
   caṉaṅkaḷ taṉṉaic cūḻntuvara nōyāḷi oruvaṉ viṉōta vēṭam pūṇṭu āṭikkoṇṭum
   pāṭik koṇṭum cilavaṟṟaic collikkoṇṭum vītiyil varukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ pāṭṭaik
   kēṭkuñ camayaṅkaḷil utayaṇaṉ vēykuḻal ūtukiṉṟāṉ. ivaṟṟait terivikka
   vantēṉ; pātukāttaruḷka" eṉṟāṉ. eṉṟapoḻutu, aracaṉ arukilirunta
   mantirikaḷai nōkka, avarkaḷiṟ cālaṅkāyaṉ, "aracē, avaṉ yūkiyāka
   irukkalāmeṉṟu nāṉ aiyuṟukiṟēṉ: avaṉ iṟantirukkamāṭṭāṉ. avaṉ mārpil1
   yāṉaik kompu kuttiyatāl uṇṭākiya taḻumpu oṉṟu uṇṭu. ataṉai eppaṭiyēṉum
   aṟintu varumpaṭi ceytal naṉṟu" eṉṟu terivittāṉ. ataṉaik kēṭṭa aracaṉ
   ataippaṟṟi naṉku ārāyntu atai aṟintukoḷḷutaṟku vēṇṭiya muyaṟciyaic
   ceyvippāṉāyiṉaṉ. tāṉ kēḷātavaṉ pōla avaṟṟai ellām aṟintukoṇ
   ṭiruntavaṉum ceviṭaṉ vēṭam pūṇṭavaṉumākiya (yūkiyiṉ oṟṟaṉ) oruvaṉ uṭaṉē
   vantu nikaḻntavaṟṟai yūkiyiṉiṭam terivittāṉ. ap pāl yūki taṉ mārpiluḷḷa
   taḻumpai ayalār oru varum terintukoḷḷātapaṭi kantaikaḷāl maṟaittuk
   koṇṭu vaḻakkampōlē vītiyiṟ pōy varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   -------
       1 "valintumēṟ ceṉṟa kaliṅkat taracaṉ, kuñcara maruppiṟ kuṟiyiṭap
       paṭṭuc, ceñcāntu meḻukiya cēṭupaṭu celvattu, mārpiṉatu vaṉappun
       tōḷiṉatu tiraṭciyum" peruṅkatai,1.45: 20-23.

   ippaṭic cila tiṉaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. piṉpu oru nāḷ yūki, "iṉi utayaṇaṉaic
   ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittaṟku uriya upāyam yātu?" eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ ārāyntu pēy
   vaṭivaṅ koṇṭu naḷḷiravil, piṟar aṟiyāmal nakarattuḷḷē ceṉṟu, kuṇañ
   citaintu keṭṭu mayaṅkit tirintu piṟarai varuttumpaṭi nḷakirikku
   akiṟpukai yūṭṭiyum oruvakai maruntaik koṭuttum mataveṟiyaiyum
   kōpattaiyum palattaiyum uṇṭākki ataṉarukiṟ ceṉṟu, "innakarattai aḻittu
   aracaṉ mutaliyōrkaḷukkuk kavalai uṇṭākumpaṭi ceytu utayaṇaṉaic
   ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkal uṉṉuṭaiya kaṭaṉ" eṉṟu colli uccāṭaṉa mantiram
   oṉṟai ataṉ ceviyil ōtiṉāṉ. ōtiyavuṭaṉ atu mikunta kōpamuṟṟu mataveṟi
   koṇṭu kaṭṭuttaṟiyai muṟittut taṉṉaik kaṭṭiya caṅkiliyaiyum aṟuttu
   viṭṭup peru muḻakkam ceytukoṇṭu cuḻaṉṟu ōṭi ōṭi ūrai aḻikkat
   toṭaṅkiṟṟu. atu kaṇṭa yūki uṭaṉē tāṉirunta ūrai aṭaintāṉ.
   paḻakiya pākarkaḷ atṉai aṭakkuvataṟku mikka muyaṟci paṇṇavum anta yāṉai
   aṭaṅkāmal avarkaḷ ellāraiyum koṉṟuviṭṭatu. nakarattār yāvarum
   naṭuṅkuvarāyiṉar; ic ceytiyaik kēḷviyuṟṟu,
   "nāvāy kaviḻtta nāykaṉ pōla
       ōvā vavalamoṭu kāvalaṉ kalaṅki"(2.9:46-7)

   mikka kavalaiyai yaṭaintu, araṇmaṉaiyiṉ cūḷikaiyil ēṟi niṉṟu pārttāṉ.
   anta yāṉai, māḷikaikaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum kōpura mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum taṉ
   kompukaḷāṟ kutti aḻittalaiyum, caṉaṅkaḷ kollappaṭutalaiyuṅ kaṇṭāṉ. anta
   yāṉai ainnūṟu vīrarkaḷaik koṉṟuviṭṭu matiṟpuṟam pōntu aṟunūṟṟaimpatu
   cērikaḷaiyum iraṇṭāyirat teṇṇūṟu pāṭikaḷaiyum pala uyyāṉaṅkaḷaiyum
   aḻittatu. nakaram nilai kulaintatu, ataṉāl kavalaik kaṭalil aḻuntiya
   piraccōtaṉaṉ, ittuṉpattai nīkkuvataṟkuriya upāyam yātu?" eṉṟu
   kēṭṭapoḻutu, "inta yāṉaiyiṉ kōpattai aṭakkutaṟ kuriya karuvi
   ciṟaiccālaiyil irukkum utayaṇaṉum avaṉ kaiyiluḷḷa yāḻumē. ātalāl,
   avaṉai yāḻuṭaṉ ivviṭam aṉuppiṉ inta yāṉaiyai aṭakki viṭuvāṉ" eṉṟu
   cālaṅkāyaṉ kūṟiṉaṉ. piraccōtaṉaṉ, "vella muṭiyāmal vañcaṉaiyāl māya
   yāṉaiyaik kāṭṭip piṭippittu varuvittuc ciṟaiyil vaittataṉṟi utayaṇaṉai
   mataṅkoṇṭa yāṉaiyiṉ muṉpum aṉuppiṉōmeṉṟa paḻi namakku varumē" eṉṟu
   taṉṉuḷ eṇṇi, "atu ceytaṟku eṉ maṉam tuṇintilatu" eṉṟu maṟuttāṉ.
   cālaṅkāyaṉ, "intiraṉuṭaiya yāṉaiyāka iruntālum avaṉuṭaiya yāḻicaikku
   vacamāki aṭaṅki naṭakkumēyaṉṟi yātum ceyyātu. ātalāl avaṉai naḷakiriyiṉ
   muṉpu aṉuppalām. illaiyāyiṉ iṉi eṉṉa eṉṉa tīṅku viḷaiyumō!" eṉṟu
   vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟa aracaṉ, "ic ceytiyai utayaṇaṉiṭam terivittu nīyē
   avaṉai aḻaittu varuka" eṉṟu colla, avaṉ, "vañcaṉaiyāṟ piṭittu
   vantēṉātaliṉ, avaṉiṭam cellutaṟku accamum nāṇamum uṭaiyēṉ" eṉṟu
   maṟuttāṉ.
   piṉpu civētaṉeṉṉum mantiriyai aṉuppa avaṉ ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu
   palapaṭap pārāṭṭi, 'aracar kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi iṅkē vantēṉ. ippōtu ivvūrukku
   naḷakiri yeṉṉum yāṉaiyāl nērntirukkiṟa perun tuṉpattai nīkkutal
   ummuṭaiya kaṭamai. ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum nīkki ummaik keḷacāmpi nakarukku
   aṉupputal tammuṭaiya kaṭamaiyeṉṟu avar terivikkaccoṉṉār; ātalāl, aruḷ
   ceyya vēṇṭum; maṉattil yātoṉṟaiyum vaittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭām" eṉṟu pala muṟai
   vaṇakkattuṭaṉ colli uṭaṉpaṭuvittu mikka mariyātaiyōṭu mella
   aḻaittukkoṇṭu veḷiyē vantu avaṉaic civikaiyil ēṟṟuvittuk kaṇṭōr
   iṉpuṟumpaṭi aḻaittuc ceṉṟu, pala paruntukaḷum kaḻukukaḷum mēlē
   vaṭṭamiṭak kīḻē mikunta kōpāvēcattōṭu ulāvit tiriyum anta naḷakiriyiṉ
   muṉpu iṟakkuvittu niṟuttiṉāṉ. utayaṇaṉ, mantirattiṉ makimaiyāl mutalil
   ataṉai acaivaṟac ceytuviṭṭu aṅkē niṉṟu mikunta karuṇaiyuṭaṉ pāṭi
   yāḻaiyum vācittu arukiṟ cella, kēṭṭa yāṉai uṭaṉē ciṉan taṉintu
   1āṇaiyācāṟ kaṭiyuṟai ceyyum māṇi pōla mikavum aṭaṅki avaṉarukil vantu
   aṉpuṭaṉ avaṉai vaṇaṅki avaṉuṭaiya ēval kēṭṭu niṉṟatu. kaṇṭōr mikavum
   viyantārkaḷ. utayaṇaṉ, vaṇaṅki niṟkum ataṉ maruppiṉ mītu aṭivaittēṟi
   ataṉ piṭariyil iruntukoṇṭu celutta, yātōr iṭai yūṟuñ ceyyāmalē aḵtu
   ellā vītikaḷilum ulāviyatu. ulāvavē, kaṇṭavarkaḷ,
   -------
       1. "pirintāṟ putalvar vantu peṟṟatan tantai pātam,parintanaṟ
       kātalālē tām paṇintiṭuvatēpōl, iruntutaṟ paṇinta yāṉai yeḻiṉmarup
       paṭivait tēṟip, peruntakai yēval kūṟap peruṅkaiyāṟ ṟōṭṭi koṇṭāṉ"
       (utayaṇa kumāra kāviyam, 1:91) eṉa in nikaḻccikku vēṟu uvamai
       kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
       -------
       "maṉṉavaṉ vāḻka vattavaṉ vāḻka
       olikeḻu nakarat tuṟupiṇi nīkkiya
       valikeḻu taṭakkai vayavaṉ vāḻkeṉap
       pūttūy vītitō ṟēttiṉa retirkoḷa." (2.9:62-5).

   piraccōtaṉaṉ itaṉaik kēṭṭu makiḻntu tēvimārōṭum pulimuka māṭattu vantu
   utayaṇaṉaik kāṇutaṟku virumpa, utayaṇaṉ ataṉai yaṟintu yāṉaiyaic
   celuttikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu avaṉ muṉṉē niṟuttiṉaṉ. an nikaḻcciyaik kaṇṭa
   piraccōtaṉaṉ mikunta makiḻcci yaṭaintu utayaṇaṉai nōkki, "nī
   puṇṇiyavāṉ; tūymai yamainta kulattiṟ piṟantavaṉ; kuṇak kaṭal; mikka
   parākkiramacāli; mantira nūlil valiyai; uṉ nilaiyai yaṟiyāmal yāṉ
   uṉakku mikka tavaṟu ceytuviṭṭēṉ; poṟuttiṭuka" eṉṟu colli vilai
   varampillāta āramoṉṟai aṇintukoḷḷumpaṭi avaṉpāl aṉuppiṉāṉ. utayaṇaṉ
   ataṉai ēṟṟuk koṇṭu avaṉ virumpiyapaṭi etirē vantāṉ.
   piraccōtaṉaṉ, utayaṇaṉaiyum, yāṉai avaṉukku aṭaṅki avaṉ ēviya
   toḻilkaḷaic ceytukoṇṭu niṟṟalaiyum kaṇṭu makiḻntu, "uṉṉuṭaiya arumaic
   ceyalkaḷaik kaṇṭu makiḻntēṉ. nī kaṟṟa vittaikaḷ yāvai? avaṟṟai
   aṟintukoḷḷa eṉ maṉam avāvukiṉṟatu" eṉṟu colla, 1utayaṇaṉ tāṉ kaṟṟa
   kalaikaḷ iṉṉavai yeṉṟu avaṟṟai virittu viḷaṅkac colliyavuṭaṉ, "inta
   iḷamaiyil ivvaḷavu vittaikaḷ ivaṉpāl niṟaintiruppataṟkuk kāraṇam ivaṉ
   paḻam piṟappiṟ ceyta perun tavamē yākum" eṉṟu taṉ uḷḷē viyantu aṉpuṟṟa
   piraccōtaṉaṉ, "iṅkē emmāl iyaṉṟa aḷavu uṉakku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaic ceyyā
   niṟpōm. keḷacāmpi nakarkku uṉṉai aṉuppumpoḻutu uṉ tampiyar vantu uṉṉai
   vaḻipaṭuvārkaḷākuka" eṉṟu colli matippup peṟṟa irācakumārarkaḷ cilarai
   avaṉpuṭaiyē niṉṟu kuṟṟēval ceyyumpaṭi niyamittuviṭṭu maṉaṅkalantu
   pēcikkoṇṭiruntāṉ. irunta avaṉ piṉṉum utayaṇaṉai nōkki, "inta yāṉai taṉ
   nilaimai tirintataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa?" eṉṟu kēṭpa, utayaṇaṉ, ataṉaik
   kūṟuvataṟkut talaiyeṭuttup piraccōtaṉaṉaip pārtta poḻutu, avaṉ piṉṉē
   tāymārkaḷiṉ iṭaiyē niṉṟa vācavatattaiyaik kaṇṭāṉ. kāṇavē oruvar
   mukattai oruvar nōkki, mataṅkoṇṭa kaḷiṟum piṭi yum pākarkaḷukku
   aṭaṅkāmal oṉṟai yoṉṟu uṟṟu nōkki, iṉpuṟṟāṟpōl iṉpuṟṟu vēṟoṉṟaiyum
   aṟiyārāki oruvar uḷḷattil oruvar māṟip pukuntu niṉṟaṉar.
   ---------
       1. kiṭaitta peruṅkatai yēṭṭup piratiyil ituvaraiyiluḷḷa kataiyiṉ
       mūlap pakuti yillai.

   appāl, iṭam aracaṉ muṉṉilai yātalāl, utayaṇaṉ taṉ maṉattai oruvāṟu
   tēṟṟik koṇṭu, naḷakiri taṉmai tirintataṟkuriya kāraṇattait takka
   ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅka virittuk kūṟiṉaṉ. ap pōtu pākarkaḷum capaiyil
   uḷḷavarkaḷum avaṉ tērcciyai aṟintu kaivitirttup pukaḻntārkaḷ. aracaṉ
   ayalil niṉṟa paricaṉaṅkaḷai aṉuppiviṭṭuk kaṇakkar mutaliyavarkaḷai
   aḻaittut tāṉeṇṇiyavaṟṟait taṉ kaiyilirunta ēṭṭil varaintu ataṉai
   avarkaḷukkuk kāṭṭik kuṟippittu "ivaṟṟai yāṉaic cēriyiluḷḷa araṇmaṉaiyil
   viraivil āyattañ ceytuviṭṭu vantu terivittiṭuka" eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ.
   avarkaḷum appaṭiyē ceṉṟu
   "paṉmaṇi viḷakkum paḷḷik kaṭṭilum
       poṉṉi ṉaṭaippaiyum pūraṇa kalacamum
       kavariyuṅ kaṭamuṅ katirmut tāramum
       nikariṉ māṇkala nitiyoṭu niṟainta
       āriyac ceppum yavaṉamañ cikaiyum
       poṉcey pēḻaiyoṭu poṟittāḻ nīkki
       naṉkaṉam paṭuttu nakumalar parappi
       viraiviri yāḷar puraivuṟap puṇartta
       paṇṭam putaitta vaṇṭupaṭu vaḷanakar
       maṭaiyaru makaḷiru mallaru mamaiccarum
       kaṭaiyaruṅ kaṇakkaruṅ kāpparu muḷappaṭa
       iṟaiviṉai tiriyāp paḻaviṉai yāḷarai
       vaḻimuṟai marapiṟṟan toḻiṉmuṟai niṟīi (1-32:72-84)

   ellām niṟaivēṟiṉa" eṉṟu paṇintu viṇṇappañ ceytārkaḷ. aracaṉ utayaṇaṉai
   nōkki, "iḵtu uṉṉuṭaiya nakar; yāṅkaḷ yāvarum uṉakkuriyavarkaḷē.
   emmuṭaiya poruḷkaḷellām uṉṉuṭaiyaṉavē. ātalāl iṉi uṉṉuṭaiya māḷikai
   ceṉṟu iḷaippāṟuka" eṉru muka malarntu aṉuppi avaṉ piṉṉarc cellumpaṭi
   ūrtiyaiyum celuttiviṭṭut tāṉ araṇmaṉaiyai aṭaintāṉ. utayaṇaṉ, kaṇṭōr
   yāvarum eḻuntu eḻuntu taṉṉaik kaitoḻutu pukaḻntu mikap pārāṭṭumpaṭi
   vītivaḻiyē ceṉṟu kuñcaraccēriyai aṭaintu naḷakiriyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅkit
   taṉakku amaittirunta māḷikaiyai aṭaintāṉ.
   piṉṉar avaṉ, ammāḷikaiyil uḷḷa pala vāyilkaḷaiyum kaṭantu ceṉṟu aṅkaṅkē
   amaintuḷḷa,
   "antak kēṇiyu mentirak kiṇaṟum
       taṇpūṅ kāvun talaittōṉ ṟaruviya
       veṇcutaik kuṉṟoṭu vēṇṭuva piṟavum
       iḷaiyōrk kiyaṟṟiya viḷaiyāṭ ṭiṭatta
       cittirap pūmi vittaka nōkki" (1.33:2-7)

   viyantu, "turiyōtaṉātiyar pāṇṭavarkaḷai akappaṭuttaṟkuc ceyta arakku
   māḷikaiyaippōlap pirac cōtaṉaṉ nammai akappaṭuttaṟku vañcaṉaiyāka
   amaitta iṭamākavum ivviṭam iruttal kūṭum. itaṉ uṇmaiyai ārāyntē teḷiya
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu eṇṇi uṭaṉirukkum vēlaikkārarkaḷuṭaṉ ovvōriṭattum ceṉṟu
   ceṉṟu ammāḷikaiyilum ataṉ cuṟṟuppuṟattilumuḷḷa,
   muṭṭu muṭukku miṭṭiṭai kaḻiyum
       karappaṟai vītiyuṅ kaḷḷap pūmiyum
       marattiṉu maṇṇiṉu matiyōr puṇarkkum
       entira maruṅki ṉiḻukka miṉmaiyai (1.33:16-9)

   ārāyntu ārāyntu pārttup pārttup atiṟ ciṟitum tīṅkillai yeṉṟaṟintu tēṟi
   ataṉpāluḷḷa cōlaiyiṉ vaḷattaik kaṇṭu aṅkuḷḷa iraṭṭaikaḷāṉa paṟavaikaḷum
   māṉ mutaliya mirukaṅkaḷum tammuḷ oṉṟai yoṉṟu aṉpuṭaṉ pārāṭṭutalai
   yaṟintu " matarari neṭuṅkaṇ vēṟkaṭai kāṉṟa, puḷḷi vempaṉi karanta
   kaḷvitaṉ, kārikai yuṇṭaveṉ pēricai yāṇmai, ceṟunar muṉṉarc ciṟumai
   yiṉṟip peṟuvēṉ kol" eṉṟu vācavatattaiyai niṉaintu niṉaintu
   kāmaparavacaṉākik kavalaiyuṟṟāṉ. uṟukaiyil, 1ulakaiyellām āṇṭa
   cakkaravartti taṉṉilaimai vēṟupaṭṭuk kuṟaintatu pōlac cūriyaṉ
   attamittatu. uṭukkaḷ viḷaṅkiṉa;
   --------
       1. piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya perumaiyiṉ kuṟaivu iṅkē kūṟiya cūriyāstamaya
       varuṇaṉaiyāṟ kuṟippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
       ----------
       "mulaippāṟ kālattu muṭi muṟai yeytik
       kuṭaivīṟ ṟirunta kuḻavi pōl" (1.33: 52-3)

   piṟai utittatu. appōtu utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyiṉ niṉaivāl varunti
   mayaṅkuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   pulimuka māṭattiliruntu tāymāruṭaṉ ceṉṟa vācavatattai tōḻimārkaḷuṭaṉ
   kaṉṉimāṭattaic cārnta pūñcōlaiyiṉ oru pakkattai yaṭaintu utayaṇaṉai
   niṉaintu niṉaintu mayaṅki, "araciḷaṅ kumaraṉaic cuṟṟattiṉiṉṟum pirittu
   varuvittuc ciṟaippaṭuttiyataṉṟit taṉiyaṉeṉṟu niṉaiyāmal naḷakiriyai
   aṭakkuvataṟku yāḻoṭu celkaveṉṟuṅ kūṟi avaṉ ataṉai aṭakkuñ ceyalai uṟṟu
   nōkkikkoṇṭē niṉṟa aracaṉ kaṇkaḷtām marattāl iyaṉṟaṉavō!" eṉṟu tantaiyai
   veṟuttup poṟiyaṟṟa pāvai pōlac ceyalaṟṟu niṟaiyaḻintu, "kāmaṉeṉṉu
   nāmattai maṟaittu vattavaṉeṉṉum naṟpeyar koṇṭu yāṉaiyaip piṇittataṉṟi
   eṉ uḷḷattaiyum piṇitta kaḷvaṉai iṉṉum kāṇavum peṟuvēṉō" eṉṟu pulampi
   avaṉ pōlak kāmaparavacaiyāki uruvam vēṟupaṭṭaṉaḷ. avaḷaip pirintu
   taṉittaṉiyē pūkkoytal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu vēṟiṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟa
   tōḻimārkaḷellōrum vantu avaḷuṭaiya vēṟupāṭṭai yaṟintu, "tattaiyē! inta
   nilaimai uṉakku vantataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa?" eṉṟu viṉāvi viṉāvi oṉṟum
   aṟiyātavarkaḷāki avaḷai mella aḻaittuc ceṉṟu malaraṇaiyiṟ paṭukkac
   ceytaṉar. avaḷ atiṟ kiṭantu imai poruntāḷāy mikat tuṉpuṟṟaṉaḷ.
   iṅṅaṉam iruvar neñcamum anta irāmuṟṟum ippaṭiyē nilai kulaintu
   varuntiṉa; appōtu 1"ellām,viṭintatu viṭintateṉṉa viṭintatu kaṅkuṟ
   kālam." utayaṇaṉaiyē niṉaintukoṇ ṭirunta piraccōtaṉaṉ tuyiluṇarn
   teḻuntu, kaṇkaḷukku maiyeḻutip pacuvait taricittuk kālaikkaṭaṉai
   muṭittukkoṇṭu makaṭūut tuṟanta mācaṟu paṭivattaiyuṭaiya tuṟavikaḷai
   vaṇaṅkip periyōr mantirittu aḷitta tīrttattai uṭkoṇṭu pērattāṇiyil
   mālai toṭarnta maṅkalap pantariṉ kīḻē virinūlantaṇar irukkum veṇmaṇai
   cūḻnta ācaṉattē iruntu kēṭka vēṇṭiya tarumaṅkaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum
   aṟiñarpāl kēṭṭāṉ.
   ---------
       1. tirukkuṟṟālappurāṇam, 12, 72.

   kēṭṭapiṉ, aṅkē vantavarkaḷai aṉuppiviṭṭu araṇmaṉai ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉōṭu
   taṉiyē pēca virumpi, avaṉai viraintu aḻaittu varukaveṉṟu uḻaiyarkaḷai
   aṉuppiṉāṉ. avarkaḷ ōṭic ceṉṟu terivikkavē utayaṇaṉ, 'iḻaiyaṇi irumpiṭi
   yerutta mēṟik kaṭaiyaṇi yāvaṇam kaitoḻumpaṭi’ vantu araṇmaṉai vāyilil
   niṟka; ataṉai yaṟinta aracaṉ, tarumaṇal muṟṟattut tāṉ etirceṉṟu mikka
   upacārattuṭaṉ aḻaittukkoṇṭu uḷḷē ceṉṟu maṇiyampala moṉṟil iraṭṭait
   tavicil avaṉai mutalil irukkacceytu tāṉum atil uṭaṉiruntu, "iḵtu
   uṉṉuṭaiya iṭameṉṟu iṉi nāṉ colluvatu mikai" eṉpatu pōṉṟa upacāra
   vārttaikaḷ palavaṟṟaic colli, mukavāca mutaliyavaṟṟai yaḷittu, "iṉi
   urimai pūṇṭu eṇṇiyavāṟu nī naṭappāyāka" eṉṟu colli avaṉai aṅkē irukkac
   ceytuviṭṭu, "utayaṇaṉai enta vakaiyālāvatu cila kālam ivviṭattilēyē
   irukkumpaṭi ceyya vēṇṭum" eṉkiṟa eṇṇattōṭu, tāṉ araṇmaṉaik kuḷḷē ceṉṟu
   civētaṉai varuvittu avaṉai nōkki, "nī utayaṇaṉiṭañ ceṉṟu,
   'irācakumārarkku āyutavittai mutaliya palavakaik kalaikaḷaiyum
   vācavatattaikku vīṇaiyaiyum payiṟṟuvikka vēṇṭum; iḵtu emmuṭaiya kuṟai’
   eṉṟu yām virumpuvatākac colli eppaṭiyēṉum avaṉai uṭaṉpaṭuvittu varuka"
   eṉṟu colla avaṉum appaṭiyē ceṉṟu aracaṉ colliyavaṟṟai utayaṇaṉukku
   mikka vaṇakkattuṭaṉ terivittaṉaṉ.
   appoḻutu utayaṇaṉ neṭunēram yōcittu, "aracaṉ kūṟiyavaṇṇam it toḻilaic
   ceytal namakkuc ciṟumaiyeṉṟu nāṇiṉāl, virumpiya kaṉṉiyaip peṟutaṟku
   vēṟu viḻiyillāmaiyiṉ yām piḻaittaṟku iṭamillai; aṅṅaṉam
   kaṟpikkumiṭattup pulimukamāṭattil aṉṟu kaṇṭa kaṉṉiyārai yām kāṇutal
   kūṭum: kāṇiṉ nām uytaṟku vaḻi uḷatām: namakku
   'uyirkeṭa varuvaḻi yoḻukkaṅ koḷḷār
       ceyiraṟu kēḷvi tērntuṇarn tōr’ (1.34: 88-9);

   eṉṉa ciṟumai variṉum iṉi aracaṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaic ceyvatē naṉṟu" eṉṟu
   teḷintu, "virumpiya vaṇṇam ceyvēṉeṉpataic collumiṉ" eṉṟu colla, civētaṉ
   ceṉṟu aracaṉukku utayaṇaṉ uṭaṉpaṭṭatait terivittaṉaṉ. appāl aracaṉ taṉ
   tēviyar palar peṟṟa makaḷiruḷ petumpaip paruvattu makaḷirkaḷ yāvarum
   varuvārkaḷāka eṉṟu colli viṭuppa, cevilit tāyār mutaliyōr,
   "paḷikkaṟaip pūmiyum panteṟi kaḷattum
       maṇikkayiṟ ṟūcaṉ maṟaliya viṭattum
       koymmalark kāvum poykaik karaiyum
       antak kēṇiyum vantupeyar kūvi (1.34: 115-18)

   muttiṉarum, uttiyarum, mummaṇik kāciṉarum, kacciṉarum, kaṇṇiyarum,
   veḷvaḷaiyiṉarum, cilkalattiyaṉṟa aṇiyiṉarallatu palkalañ cērā melleṉ
   yākkaiyarumākiya avarkaḷai aḻaittukkoṇṭu vara, avarkaḷ pālparantaṉṉa
   pañcu mellaṇaiyiṟ cēkkai meliyac cemmāntirunta muṭikeḻu tantai muṉṉart
   tōṉṟi vaṇaṅki niṉṟaṉar. niṉṟavarkaḷai yellām aracaṉ muṟaiyē nōkki
   avarkaḷuḷḷē,
   "oṇmaiyu niṟaiyum ōṅkiya voḷiyum
       peṇmaiyum perumaiyum piṟavu muṭaimaiyiṟ
       pāciḻai yāyattup paiyeṉa niṉṟa
       vācava tattai valla ḷākeṉa" (1.34: 151-4)

   eṇṇi, pantum, kiḷiyum, pacumpoṟ ṟūtaiyum, kantiyaṉ mayilum, karantuṟai
   pūvaiyum, kaḻaṅkum, kaccum, muṟṟilum pavaḻap pāvaiyum, paḷikkuk
   kiḷikkūṭumākiya ivaṟṟil virumpiyavaṟṟai avarkaḷukku aḷittu, "nīṅkaḷ itu
   varaiyiṟ kaṟṟa,
   aṭicil viṉaiyum yāḻiṉ ṟuṟaiyum
       kaṭimalarc cippamuṅ karantuṟai kaṇakkum
       vaṭṭikai varaippum vākkiṉ vikaṟpamum
       kaṟṟavai yellāṅ kāṭṭumi ṉemakku" (1.34:166-9)

   eṉa uraittu oruvakaiyāka makiḻvittu avarkaḷai yellām aṉuppiviṭṭu,
   ayalil niṉṟavarkaḷai nōkki, "iṉi vācavatattai utayaṇakumaraṉpāl yāḻ
   kaṟṟuk koḷḷat toṭaṅkuvāḷ; ataṟku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai viraivil amaimiṉ"
   eṉṟaṉaṉ. ataṉaik kēṭṭa mutaṟ peruntēvi taṉ makaḷukku ippayiṟci mikka
   māṭcimaiyait taruvataṟkuriyatu eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ makiḻntāḷ. maṟṟa tēviyar
   mikap pukaḻntārkaḷ. cakkaravarttiyiṉ makaḷukku yāḻ kaṟpittaṟkuriya
   ācāṉāka varupavaṉ perum puṇṇiyamuṭaiyōṉ eṉṟu aṭiyavarum āyattārum
   viyantārkaḷ. vattavarperumāṉukkuriya vīṇai viccai tattaikkē uriyateṉṟu
   muṟṟattē niṉṟvarkaḷ tammuḷ mellap pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ.
   appoḻutu ceviliyarkaḷ pūkkaḷ mutaliyavai vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa poṉ akalkaḷai
   aṅkē alaṅkāramāka vaittu icaikkuriya teyvaṅkaḷai aḻaittut tutittu
   vācvatattaiyaic cantaṉa nṟunīrāl āṭṭi alaṅkarippārāy yāṉaittantattāṟ
   ceyta maṅkalac cīppāṟ kūntalai vārip poṉṉāṇāṟ kaṭṭi muṭittup poṟ
   ciṉṉaṅkaḷai ataṉmēlē citaṟi, neṟṟiyiṟ cuṭṭiyaiyum muttaṇikalaṅkaḷaiyuñ
   cērtti, muṉṉamirunta ōlaiyaiyum kuḻaiyaiyum nīkkik kātukaḷiṟ
   kaṭippiṇaikaḷai yaṇintu, cantaṉak kuḻampāl tōḷkaḷil pūṅkoṭi oṉṟai
   eḻuti, mārpil muttavaḷḷiyaiyum mummaṇiyaiyum poṟṟōraiyaiyum pūṭṭi,
   iṭaiyiṟ kaṭṭiya maṭṭañ ceyta paṭṭuṭaimītu mēkalaiyaic cērtti, piṉṉum
   aṇiya vēṇṭiya melliya āparaṇaṅkaḷai yaṇintu, naṭaip peruntaviciṉmītu
   avaḷai mella naṭatti varuvārāyiṉar. appōtu mikavum aḻakiya nāṉku
   ālavaṭṭaṅkaḷ pakkattē kaṉṉiyarkaḷāl acaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaḷ taṉ tāymārōṭu
   melleṉa vantu poṟṟakaṭu potinta cuvarkaḷai yuṭaiyatum yāṉaik kom
   pālākiya tūṇkaḷai yuṭaiyatum mēṟkaṭṭi kaṭṭiyatumākiya kītacālaiyiṉ
   vētiyiṉmītu cevvarakku vaḻitta pūmiyiṉ mēlē cuṟṟattōṭu pukuntu melliya
   palavakaiyāṉa malar parappiya taṉiyiṭam oṉṟil tirai maṟaiyil niṉṟaṉaḷ.
   appōtu tāymārkaḷ, "yāḻaip pūcittaṟkum ataṉaik kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkuvataṟkum
   uriya nalla vēḷai vantuviṭṭatu. itaṉai utayaṇaṉukkut terivikka vēṇṭum"
   eṉṟu coṉṉārkaḷ. uṭaṉē cila makaḷir ōṭic ceṉṟu terivikkavē avaṉ vantu
   aṅkē amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa melliya ācaṉattiṉmēl vīṟṟiruntaṉaṉ.
   vācavatattaiyai aḻaittu vantu mēṟ kūṟiya tiraimaṟaivilēyē vēṟoru
   tavicil irukkac ceytaṉar.
   appōtu "yāḻ kaṟṟukkoḷḷa iṅkē vantiruppāḷ eṉ maṉattai muṉpu kavarnta peṇ
   maṇiyō, illaḷō?" eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ eṇṇik koṇṭiruntāṉ. "namakku yāḻaik
   kaṟpittaṟku iṅkē vantiruppavaṉ aṉṟu naḷakiriyai aṭakki ataṉai ūrntu
   vantu pulimuka māṭattiṉ etirē niṉṟu nam maṉattaik kavarntu ceṉṟa āṭavar
   ciṅkamō, vēṟu yārō?" eṉṟu vācavatattaiyum eṇṇik koṇṭiruntāḷ.antac
   camayattil tōḻimār, "āciriyaṉukkuk ceyya vēṇṭiya vaḻipāṭu ituvākum"
   eṉṟu colli, vācavatattaiyiṉ kaiyaik kuvikkac ceytu tiraiyait
   tiṟantaṉar. tiṟantapoḻutu, oruvarai oruvar kaṇṭu iruvarum kaḷittu aiyam
   nīṅki iṉpuṟṟaṉar;1 "cempulap peynnīrpōla aṉpuṭai neñcan tāmkalantaṉa"2
   "pirintavar kūṭiṉāṟ pēcavum vēṇṭumō?" appāl, tāymārum kaikkōṟ cilatarum
   kaṉṉiyarum puṟattē pātukāttu niṉṟaṉar. appōtu avaṉ yāḻai aḷittuk
   kaṟpiyāniṟka, avaḷ ataṉai ēntik kaṟpāḷāyiṉaḷ.
   ---------
       1. kuṟuntokai, 40 2. kamparāmāyaṇam, mitilai. 37.

   iṅṅaṉam avaṉ nāṭōṟum vantu vācavatattaikku yāḻaip payiṟṟuvikkaiyil
   annakarattuḷḷa vampa mākkaḷil oru cārār, "pakaivar kuṟuka voṇṇāta
   antappuratti liruttaṟkuriya taṉmakaḷ oruttikku yāḻ kaṟpikkumpaṭi
   ayalāṉākiya araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ oruvaṉai ārāyāmal teḷintu ācāṉāka
   niyamitta nam pētai maṉṉaṉ ataṉāl viḷaiyun tīṅkaic cila kālañ ceṉṟa
   piṉṉarāvatu naṉṟākat terintukoḷvāṉ" eṉṟaṉar. ippaṭiyē maṟṟaiyōrum
   tattamakkut tōṟṟiya karuttukkaḷait tōṉṟiyavāṟu viraivākac colliviṭṭu,
   appāl accamuṟṟu ayaliṭaṅkaḷaip pārttukkoṇṭu, "iḵtu aracaṉpāl nikaḻuñ
   ceytiyātaliṉ, yām yātoṉṟaiyum collalākātu" eṉṟu vāyaṭaṅkiṉar.
   tantaiyiṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi irācakumārarkaḷ utayaṇaṉpāl vilvittai
   mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum yāṉaiyēṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum pōr ceyyum vitaṅkaḷ
   palavaṟṟaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum muṟaiyē nāṭōṟum kaṟṟuvaruvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam
   cila nāṭkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.
   uṭuppaṉa, pūcuvaṉa, aṇivaṉa, uṇpaṉavaṟṟaiyum, piṟavaṟṟaiyum uriya
   kālattil takkavarkaḷ mukamākat tavaṟiṉṟi utayaṇakumaraṉukku nāṭōṟum
   aracaṉatu kaṭṭaḷaiyāl aṉuppikkoṇṭē varum pālakumāraṉ, taṉṉuṭaṉ kalvi
   payilum vayantakaṉiṭamāka oru nāḷ avaṟṟai aṉuppiṉaṉ. aṅṅaṉam
   aṉuppappaṭu vanta vayantakaṉaik kaṇṭu utayaṇaṉ, 'ivar eṉakku muṉpu
   paḻakkamuḷḷavar pōlkiṉṟār’ eṉṟu ayalil niṉṟavarkaḷai nōkkik kūṟa,
   vayantakaṉ maṟuttaṉaṉ. "ivar ceppamuṭaiyavarāka iruttaliṉ eṉakku aṉuppa
   vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai ivarpāl aṉuppi varuka" eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ colliviṭuttamaiyāl
   aṉṟu mutal pāla kumaraṉ aṉuppa vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai vayantakaṉiṭamākavē
   aṉuppuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. aṅṅaṉam aṉuppivarukaiyil taṉṉuṭaiya tōḻar, vīrar
   mutaliyōr vēṟu vaṭivamum vēṟu peyaruṅ koṇṭu ovvoru vēlaiyaip peṟṟu
   annakarattiṉ akattum puṟattum iruttalaiyum avarkaḷ ceytuvarum ariya
   ceyalkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṟkellāṅ kāraṇamāṉa yūkiyiṉ iṭaiviṭāta muyaṟciyaiyum
   vayantakaṉ mukamāka utayaṇaṉ aṟintukoṇṭāṉ.
   puṟattēyuḷḷa avaṉuṭaiya tōḻar mutaliyōrkaḷum utayaṇaṉuṭaiya nilaimaiyai
   ovvoru nāḷum vayantakaṉāl aṟintu, "eṇṇiyavaṟṟai eppaṭiyum niṟai
   vēṟṟuvōm" eṉṟu āṟutaluṟṟu mikka ūkkam uṭaiyavarkaḷāy iruntu
   varuvārāyiṉar.
   ippaṭiyirukkaiyil, vācavatattaiyiṉ pārvaiyāl utayaṇaṉ mikka tuṉpamuṟṟu
   maṉa vēṟupāṭṭaiyum uṭal vēṟupāṭṭaiyum aṭaintu naṉaviluṅ kaṉavilum
   avaḷaiyē niṉaittu niṉaittu araṟṟumpaṭiyāṉa kāma nōy atikarikkap peṟṟu,
   "tīmukat tiṭṭa meḻukiṟ ṟēmpiyum
       tāymukat tiyātta kaṉṟiṟ pulampiyum" (1.35:52-3)

   varuntuvāṉāki oru nāḷ, "ivaḷ kāraṇamāka uṇṭāṉa namatu vēṟupāṭṭaip
   paḻakiyavar kāṇiṉ aiyuṟutal kūṭum; piṉpu itaṉai maṟaippataṟkum
   vaḻiyirātu; ātalāl, itaṉai vēṟu vakaiyāl maṟaittal vēṇṭum" eṉa eṇṇi
   vayantaṉōṭu ārāyntu, "ayalāṉ oruvaṉiṭam ācaikoṇṭu avaṉiṭañ ceṉṟuvarum
   iyalpuḷḷa parattai oruttiyai eppaṭiyēṉum aḻaittuvā" eṉṟu colla,
   vayantakaṉ, avaṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyiṉpaṭi puṟattē ceṉṟu nakarattār aṟiyumāṟu,
   "muṉṉam veṟikoṇṭa naḷakiriyiṉmītu utayaṇaṉ ulāviyapoḻutu avaṉai virumpi
   avaṉ maṉattaik kavarntu avaṉai araṟṟikkoṇ ṭirukkumpaṭi ceyta narumatai
   eṉpavaḷ eṅkē yuḷḷāḷ?" eṉṟu kēṭpa ūrār, "attaṉmaiyai yuṭaiyāḷ
   parattaiyar cēriyil araṅ kiyal makaḷirkku āṭalaik kaṟpikkum talaikkōṟ
   peṇṭiruḷ mutalvaḷākiya oruttiyiṉ makaḷ narumatai yeṉpāḷ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.
   vayantakaṉ kainniṟaiya māṟṟuyarnta nūṟṟoru kaḻañcu poṉṉai ēntik koṇṭu
   maṉṟaṅkaḷilum maṟukukaḷilum tāṉ cellum ceyti paravumpaṭi ceytu tērmītu
   ceṉṟu, tamakkuriya yāḻ mutaliya aṟupattu nāṉku kalaikaḷil
   payiṉṟavarkaḷum oṉṟumutal āyiratteṭṭu iṟutiyākiya poṟ paricaṅkaḷaip
   peṟupavarkaḷum paḷḷi maruṅkiṟ paṭiṟiṉṟi oḻukupavarkaḷumākiya celvap
   parattaiyaruṭaiya cēriyai aṭaintu vicārittukkoṇṭu narumataiyiṉ vīṭṭai
   aṭaintaṉaṉ, appoḻutu avaṉ varavai aṟinta tāyk kiḻavi mikka
   mariyātaiyōṭu varavēṟṟu avaṉai ōr ātaṉattil irutti avaṉ vanta ceytiyai
   aṟintu, "eṉ makaḷ utayaṇaṉ maṉattil uḷḷāḷeṉṟāṉ avaḷ ellārilum mikap
   perumai peṟṟavaḷē; avaḷaip peṟṟa nāṉum pākkiyacāliyē; avaḷaip poruḷāka
   niṉaintu ippōtu poṉṉai yaṉuppiyavaṉ tāṉ vantu pātukākkavillaiyē" eṉṟu
   colliviṭṭu avaṉ vanta nōkkattai narumataikkuc colliyaṉuppa avaḷ,1 "eṉṉa
   koṭuttālum nāṉ ayalāṉ vīṭṭukkuc cellēṉ" eṉṟu maṟuttaṉaḷ.
   -----
       1 iḵtu oru cārār koḷkai.

   arukilirunta uṟaviṉar, "ivaḷai valintu piṭittuc celvāyāka" eṉṟu kūṟavē
   vayantakaṉ avaḷai appaṭiyē ceytu tērilēṟṟi vīti vaḻiyē cellumpoḻutu
   avaḷ, "eṉṉaik kollutaṟku vattavaṉpāl ivaṉ koṇṭu celluvataik kūṭṭattārē
   kāṇpīrāka" eṉṟu araṟṟa, avarkaḷ, "takka talaivaṉpāl aḻaittuc cellu
   kaiyil iṅṅaṉam araṟṟukiṉṟa ivaḷ aṟiyāmaiyaiyuṭaiyavaḷō?" eṉṟum,
   "uṭaṉpāṭillātavaḷait taṉṉuṭaiya atikārattāl valintu aḻaittuvarac
   ceytamaiyāl vattavaṉ kāma viruntiṉaṉ; avaṉ peṇṭirkaḷpāl tavaṟu ceytaluṅ
   kūṭum" eṉṟum, "naṭanavil maṅkaiyaruṭaiya nalattiṟku avaṉ viruntiṉaṉ
   pōlum" eṉṟum colvārāyiṉar. vayantakaṉ, utayaṇaṉatu kāma vēṟupāṭṭiṉai
   ivvāṟu nakarattār yārum aṟiyumpaṭi ceytavaṉāki avaḷaiaḻaittuc ceṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉpāl aṉṟiravu muṟṟum irutti maṟu nāṭ kālaiyil avaḷai
   aṉuppiviṭṭaṉaṉ. avviru varum oruvarai oruvar virumpiṉārallar.
   vācavatattai pāl taṉakkuḷḷa viruppattaiyum ataṉāluṇṭākiya taṉ
   vēṟupāṭṭaiyum maṟaittaṟku utayaṇaṉ ceyvitta ic ceyal
   narumataiyiṉpāluḷḷa viruppattāl avaṉ nikaḻttiyatākavē nakar muḻuvatum
   paraviyatu.
   itaṉai aṟinta oṟṟarkaḷ viraintōṭic ceṉṟu cevviyaṟintu piraccōtaṉaṉukku
   uṇarttiṉar. kēṭṭa aracaṉ icceyalil veṟuppuṟṟālum piṟaraip paḻiyāta
   iyalpiṉaṉātalāl, "utayaṇaṉ iṅṅaṉam oḻukutal irājakumāraṉākiya avaṉatu
   iḷamaikku iyalpē" eṉṟu puṉṉakaiyuṭaṉ colliviṭṭu,
   "cāmarai iraṭṭaiyun tamaṉiyak kuṭaiyum
       māmaṇi yaṭaippaiyu maruppiya lūrtiyum
       paintoṭi yāyamum paṭṭamu muṭaiyōr
       aimpati ṉāyira raraṅkiyaṉ makaḷiruḷ (1-34: 21-4)

   utayaṇaṉ taṉṉai virumpumpaṭi nalviṉaip payaṉ vāykkappeṟṟa narumataikku
   ivai uriyaṉa" eṉṟu pala āparaṇap pēḻaikaḷaiyum, marattāṟ
   ceyvikkappaṭāmal yāṉait tantattālum poṉṉālum maṇiyālum
   ceyvikkappaṭṭatum, mukattil muttumālaikaḷaiyum kaḻuttiṟ poṉṉālākiya
   kiṇkiṇi mālaikalaiyum aṇinta uyarnta pāṇṭi lerutukaḷ pūṭṭiyatumākiya
   oru vaṇṭiyaiyum narumataikku aṉuppac ceytataṉṟi, "iṉṟu mutal avaḷ
   nīrviḷaiyāṭṭu, tiruviḻā mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkāka nakarppuṟattē cellutalaiyum,
   araṇ maṉaiyil vantu āṭutalaiyum tavirvāḷāka" eṉṟum kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ.
   vācavatattai utayaṇaṉpāṟ kaṟṟu yāḻ vittaiyil mikkat tērcci yaṭaintu
   viḷaṅki varutalaik kēḷviyuṟṟa ēṉai aracarkaḷ avaḷai maṇañ ceytukoḷḷa
   virumpi oruvarukkut teriyāmal oruvar mikavum uyarvuḷḷa
   poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷai yāṉaikaḷiṉ mēlēṟṟi mikunta vaṇakkattuṭaṉ aṉuppiya
   tallāmaṟ piraccōtaṉaṉukkut tattam karuttaiyum colliviṭuttārkaḷ. avaṉum
   avaṟṟai aṅkīkarittu avarkaḷ kuṟippai yaṟintum taṉ karuttai
   veḷiyiṭātavaṉāki yiruntāṉ. ic ceytiyait taṉ cevilikaḷuḷ oruttiyākiya
   cāṅkiyat tāyāl aṟinta vācavatattai poṟiyaṟṟa putumarap pāvaipōlē
   viḻuntu naṭuṅki vimmi, "aracarkaḷ eṉakkākap poṟparicam aṉuppiyatum
   avaṟṟait tantai aṅkīkarittatum uṇmaiyāyiṉ uyirai viṭuvēṉ; ataṉ
   eppaṭiyāvatu tantai taṉ karuttai muṟṟuvittuk koṇṭu vāḻka" eṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉpāluḷḷa aṉpiṉ mikutiyāṟ palavāṟu araṟṟik kaṇṇīrt tuḷikaḷai
   vīḻtti mayaṅkik kalaṅkip pulampuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. an nikaḻcciyaik kaṇṭa
   cevili, taṉ maṭimītu avaḷai vaittukkoṇṭu kaṇṇīrait tuṭaittu avaḷ
   tuṉpattai māṟṟi, "eṉ pāvaiyē! piṟa aracaṉukku uṉṉai maṇañ ceyvikka
   aracaṉ tuṇivāṉō? aṅṅaṉam tuṇivāṉāyiṉ nāmellām aracaṉai viṭṭup pirintu
   ceṉṟu tavañ ceyvōm; uṉṉuṭaiya eṇṇattai aṟintukoḷḷutaṟkākavē nāṉ ip poyc
   ceytiyaic coṉṉēṉ" eṉṟu avaḷuṭaiya kūntalaiyum neṟṟiyaiyum
   kaiyāltaivantu, uṉ tantaiyiṉ karuttai yāṉ aṟivēṉ; kēṭpāyāka;
   iḷamaiyum vaṉappu milloṭu varavum
       vaḷamaiyun taṟukaṇum varampil kalviyum
       tēcat tamaitiyu mācil cūḻcciyō
       ṭeṇvakai niṟainta naṉmakaṟ kallatu
       makaṭkoṭai nērār matiyō rātaliṉ’ (1.36:89-93)

   ikkuṇaṅkaḷ niṟainta utayaṇaṉukkē uṉṉai maṇañ ceyvikka eṇṇi
   yirukkiṉṟaṉaṉ; itaṉait teḷivāyāka" eṉṟu tēṟṟa vācavatattai
   talaiyeṭuttuk kaṇṇīrait tuṭaittukkoṇṭu varuttam nīṅkiṉaḷ. cevili,
   "tattaiyē! āyattārōṭu noṭikaḷaip pēcikkoṇṭē yiruntamaiyiṉ nēṟṟiravu
   muḻuvatum nī tūṅkavillai; utayaṇaṉ varumvaraiyil tūṅkuvāyāka" eṉṟu
   colli avaḷaip paṭukkac ceytu kāñcaṉa mālaiyai aḻaittu, "kāñcaṉamālāy!
   yāṉ varum varaiyil yāraiyum iṅkē pukaviṭātē" eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭuk kītacālai
   ceṉṟu iyalpākavē yāḻ kaṟpittaṟku aṅku vanta utayaṇaṉait taṉiyiṭattiṟku
   aḻaittuc ceṉṟu irukkac ceytu, nikaḻum ceytikaḷaik kaṇṇālum ceviyālum
   aṟintu uṭaṉē ceṉṟu veḷippaṭuttuvōr avviṭattil illai yeṉpatai yaṟintu
   utayaṇaṉai nōkki,
   "maṇṇakaṅ kāvalaṉ māperuntēvi
       tiruvayiṟ ṟiyaṉṟa peruviṟaṟ polivē!
       iṉaiyai yāvatu memmaṉōr viṉai" (1.36:144-6)

   eṉṟu pārāṭṭit taṉ aṉpaip pulappaṭutti, "araciḷañ ciṅkamē, eṉ varalāṟṟai
   aṟivāyāka: nāṉ keḷacāmpi nakariluḷḷa oru pārppaṉi. kaṇavaṉ iḷamaiyil
   eṉṉaip pirintu uṉ tantaiyiṉ avaikkaḷattaic cārntu aṅkē cila tiṉaṅkaḷ
   taṅki appaṭiyē vēṟiṭañ ceṉṟu tirumpi vārāmaiyāl; kaṟpiṉ varampaik
   kaṭantu oḻukiṉēṉ. eṉ ceykēṉ! ataṉai yaṟinta aṟaṅkūṟavaiyattār
   akkuṟṟattiṟkāka maṇaṟ kuṭattōṭu kaṭṭi yamuṉaiyāṟṟiṉiṭaiyē eṉṉai
   vīḻttiviṭumpaṭi vitittaṉar. vitikkavē oru pulaiyaṉ avar kūṟiyavāṟu
   kaṭṭic ceṅkaṟpoṭiyai eṉmēlē tūvip pampaip paṟaiyai aṟaintu
   nakarattārkku eṉ kuṟṟattait terivittukkoṇṭē ceṉṟu tōṇiyoṉṟilēṟṟi
   yamuṉai yāṟṟiṉiṭaiyē ceṉṟa poḻutu, yāṉaiyiṉmēl ēṟic ceṉṟu anta āṟṟil
   tōḻarkaḷuṭaṉ viḷaiyāṭikkoṇ ṭirunta nī kēḷviyuṟṟu eṉṉai,
   aḻaittuvarumpaṭi ēvalāḷarai aṉuppiṉai. appōtu appulaiyaṉ karaikku
   arukēyuḷḷa iṭattiṟ koṇarntu mika viraivākat tōṇiyiṉiṉṟum iṟakkiya
   poḻutu tuṭuppu eṉṉuṭaiya neṟṟiyiṟ paṭṭatu; ataṉāluṇṭākiya taḻumpu
   ituvākum; kāṇpāyāka. nī piṟpāṭu nikaḻntatai vicārittut terintukoṇṭu,
   'ikkuṟṟattiṟkāka ivaḷukku vititta ittaṇṭam mika atikamē. itaṟkut takka
   cikṣaiyaic colluka’ eṉṟu ayal niṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷaik kēṭṭa poḻutu oru
   kuṟikōḷāḷaṉ "tām mēṟkoṇṭa tavattai iṭaiyē kaiviṭṭārkaḷ ceytaṟkuṟiya
   viratattai ivaḷ kaippaṟṟuvāḷāka’ eṉṟaṉaṉ. nī avaṉ kūṟṟai aṅkīkariyāmaṟ
   piṉṉum viṉāviyapōtu aṅkē niṉṟa kōcikaṉeṉṟa peyaruḷḷa cēṉaik kaṇi
   makaṉoruvaṉ, 'maṇampurintavaṉ muṟṟuṅ kaiviṭṭup puṟattē ceṉṟamaiyāl
   varampaik kaṭanta ivaḷukkut tavamē uriyatu; kolluvittal tavaṟākum;
   iṉṉuṅ kūṟuvēṉ. yāṉaiyiṉ niḻal taṉmēṟ paṭumpaṭi niṉṟamaiyāl ivvantaṇi
   tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa tavattuṟaiyiṟ cila kālam oḻuki appāl atiṉiṉṟum nīṅki
   ōraracaṉuṭaiya pātukāppiṟ ṟaṅki avaṉuṭaiya antappura makaḷirkku aṟam
   ceviyaṟivuṟuttum talaiviyāka viḷaṅkuvāḷ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ" eṉṟaṉaḷ.
   ivaṟṟai yellāṅkēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ tāṉ avaḷai viṭuvittapiṉ nikaḻntataiyuṅ
   kēṭka virumpiṉaṉ. virumpavē avaḷ, "nī viṭaiyaḷittapiṉ avviṭam nīṅkic
   ceṉṟu kaṅkaik karaiyai yaṭaintu viyāpārañ ceyyuṅ kūṭṭattāruṭaṉ vaṭakkē
   yuḷḷa tēyattiṟ ceṉṟu peruvaḻi yoṉṟaṟku ayalatākiya ōraraca marattiṉ
   kīḻēyiruntēṉ. irunta kālattil,
   'pūtiyu maṇṇum pottak kaṭṭu
       māṉuri maṭiyu mantirak kalappaiyum
       kāṉoṭu maṇaiyuṅ kaṭṭuṟut tiyātta
       kūṟai veḷḷuṟik kuṇṭikaik kāviṉar
       tarum tarukkar taṟpuṟañ cūḻap
       paripu melinta paṭivap paṇṭitaṉ
       cāṅkiya nuṉittavōr cāṟayar muṉivaṉai
       āṅketirp paṭṭāṅ kavaṉoṭum pōki’ (1.36: 225-32)

   avaṉiṭam kalvi payilvōruṭaṉiruntu cāṅkiya matak koḷkaiyai aṟintu
   ataṉaiyē mēṟkoṇṭu maṟṟac camayanū luṇarccikaḷaiyum naṉku peṟṟu avaṟṟaip
   piṟarkku viḷaṅkac collum vaṉmaiyaiyumuṭaiyēṉāki imayamalaiyil iraṇṭu
   varuṭam avaṉuṭaṉ taṅkiṉēṉ. avvāciriyaṉ kaṉṉiyākumariyil nīrāṭaniṉaintu
   māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ yāttiraiyākap puṟappaṭṭut teṟkē vantu in nakarattaic
   cārnta kālattil orācciramattil taṅki iḷaippāṟiṉaṉ. yāṉum uṭaṉ vantu
   aṅkē taṅkiṉēṉ. piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya avaikkaḷattil aṟu vakaic camayattōrum
   avaṉuṭaiya vēṇṭukōḷāl tammuḷ vātañ ceykiṉṟārkaḷeṉṟa ceytiyaik kēṭṭu mēṟ
   kūṟiya āciriyaṉ aṅkē ceṉṟu vātam nikaḻtti maṟṟac camayattavarai veṉṟu
   cāṅkiya camayamē ciṟanta teṉṟu tāpittāṉ. kēṭṭa āṇṭuḷḷōr accamayattai
   mēṟkoṇṭārkaḷ. aracaṉ avvāciriyaṉai naṉku matittut taṉṉuṭa ṉirukkumpaṭi
   virumpiṉamaiyāl avaṉ māṇākkaruṭaṉ iṅkē iruntaṉaṉ. yāṉum iruntēṉ.
   irukkaiyil aracaṉ tēvi eṉpāl aṟaṅ kēṭṭalai virumpi eṉṉait taṉ
   pāṅkiyākakkoṇṭu pātukāttu vantāḷ. ak kālattil iḷaṅ kuḻantaiyāka irunta
   vācavatattai aṭikkaṭi eṉṉiṭam vantu viḷaiyāṭakkaṇṭa naṟṟāy avaḷukkuc
   cevilittāyāka irukkumpaṭi kēṭṭukkoṇṭaimaiyāl appaṭiyē iruntuvarukiṉṟēṉ.
   itu niṟka; takāta iṭattil maṉattaic celuttāta nī narumatai yeṉṉuṅ
   kaṇikai oruttiyai virumpi avaḷai varuvittāyeṉṟu ūrār kūṟum paḻimoḻi
   mikka tuṉpattai uṇṭupaṇṇukiṉṟatu. ataṟkuk kāraṇam yātu?" eṉṟu
   viṉaviṉāḷ.
   utayaṇaṉ, "paḻamaiyāḷareṉṟu niṉaintu, pakaivar pāluḷḷavarai nampi
   uṇmaik karuttai veḷiyiṭutal tavaṟēyāyiṉum ivaḷaiyaṉṟi ippōtu iṅkē
   nammuṭaiya kuṟaiyai nīkkuvōrillai" eṉṟu tuṇintu narumataiyait taṉpāl
   varuvittataṟkuriya kāraṇattait terivittaṉaṉ. avaḷ kēṭṭu makiḻntu
   avaṉpāl vācavatattaikkuḷḷa vēṇavāvaiyum uṟutiyaiyum viḷaṅka virittu
   avaṉukkuk kūṟiyatallāmal, vācavatattaiyai maṇañceytaṟkuriya
   naṉmuyaṟcikaḷai mēṟkoḷvāyāka; niṉṉuṭaiya maṇam taṉakku nērātāyiṉ
   vācavatattaiyāṉavaḷ taṉakku nērnta paḻikkañci, ’maṇkeḻu maṭantāy
   maṟaiviṭantā’ eṉṟu vēṇṭip pūmikkuḷḷē oḷittut taṉ pukaḻai eṅkum viḷaṅkac
   ceyta cītātēvipōla enta vakaiyālāvatu taṉṉuyirai viṭviṭuvāḷeṉṟu
   añcukiṉṟēṉ. nāṉ avaḷiṭam pōyc colli viṭukkumaḷavum iṅkē iruppāyāka"
   eṉṟu avaṉiṭam colliviṭṭu vācavatattaiyai yaṭaintu utayaṇaṉpāl
   nikaḻntavaṟṟai avaḷukkuk kūṟi avaḷ mayakkattait tīrttaṉaḷ. tīrtta piṉpu
   kāñcaṉamālaiyai nōkki, "tattai kaṟṟaṟkuriya nēram āyviṭṭamaiyāl,
   taṉṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaikkuc cellalāmeṉṟu nī utayaṇaṉiṭam colli varuka"
   eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. avaḷ aṅṅaṉamē ceṉṟu terivikka utayaṇaṉ, "vācavatattai yāḻ
   vittaiyai muṟṟak kaṟṟaṉaḷ. araca kumārarkaḷum yāṉaiyēṟṟa
   mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum vilvittai mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum nīti nūlkaḷaiyuṅ kaṟṟuk
   karaikaṇṭaṉar. viraivil araṅkēṟṟuvikka aracarpiraṉ eṇṇiyirukkiṉṟaṉaṉ.
   ātalāl vīṇai vācittalai naṉṟākap payiṉṟu vaittukkoḷka veṉṟu
   vācavatattaikkuc colvāyāka" eṉṟu kāñcaṉamālaiyiṭam colliviṭṭu, avaṉ
   kōvalar cūḻntu pātukāttu varak kuṟumpuḻai vaḻiyē pōyt taṉṉiṭattaic
   cārntāṉ.
   mutalil maintarkaḷai araṅkēṟṟuvikka eṇṇiya aracaṉ maṟunāṭ kālaiyil
   aiyāyiram irācakumārar mutaliyavarkaḷ taṉṉaic cūḻntuvara avaikkaḷattai
   aṭaintu cantaṉap pīṭikaiyiluḷḷa cārvaṇaiyiṉkaṇ vīṟṟiruntāṉ. appoḻutu,
   nakarattiluḷḷa aṟiñar mutaliyōr vantu tattamakku amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa
   ācaṉattil iruntārkaḷ. aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi irācakumārarkaḷ vantu tām
   kaṟṟukkoṇṭa palavakaip paṭaikkalak kalvikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum
   taṉittaṉiyē naṉku araṅkēṟṟiṉar. kaṇṭu capaiyōr mika viyantārkaḷ.
   appaṭiyē viyanta aracaṉ, "taṉakkum emakkumuḷḷa virōtattaip pārāṭṭamalum
   kapaṭamillāmalum nam makkaḷaik kaṟpitta utayaṇaṉukku yām ceytaṟkuriya
   kaimmāṟu yātuḷatu?
   oṉpatiṉ kōṭi yoṇporuḷ koṭuppiṉum
       paṇpeṉak koṇṭavaṉ paṇṭañ ceyyāṉ
       naṅkuṭit talaimai yiṅkivaṟ kiyaṟṟi
       nāmivaṉ kuṭaikkīḻk kāmuṟak kalantivaṉ
       vēṇṭiyatu ceyyu māṇpala tilam." (1.37: 64-8).

   eṉṟu mikap pārāṭṭi, "kaucāmpi nakarkku viraivil ivaṉai aṉuppi maṇam
   purintukoṇṭu kavalaiyiṉṟi vāḻumpaṭi ceyvatutāṉ namatu kaṭaṉ" eṉṟu
   piṟarōṭu pēcikkoṇṭē iruntu mukamaṉkaḷaik kūṟi avaṉai irukkaikku
   aṉuppiviṭṭu antappuram ceṉṟu maintarkaḷ kalviyāl nirampiyataiyum
   araṅkēṟṟiyataiyum tēvikkuk kūṟiṉaṉ. avaḷ kēṭṭu, "tattaiyiṉ kalviyaiyum
   nalla tiṉam oṉṟil avaiyār aṟiyumpaṭi ceyvikka vēṇṭum" eṉṟu terivittāḷ.
   ataṉi utayaṇaṉukku aṟivikka avaṉum uṭaṉ paṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ colli viṭuppak
   kēṭṭu aṟiyum vaṉmaiyuḷḷa palavakai icaippayiṟciyāḷarum vantu
   avaikkaḷattil nāṟpuṟattum nirampiṉar. tōḻimāruṭaṉ vācavatattai avaikkaṇ
   vantu vitippaṭi yāḻ vācittaṉaḷ; piṉpu pāṭiṉaḷ. kēṭṭavar mutalil
   vattavaṉaip pukaḻntu piṉpu piraccōtaṉaṉai nōkki,
   "vattava nāṭaṉ vāymaiyiṟ ṟarukkum
       koṟṟa vīṇaiyuṅ koṭuṅkuḻai koṇṭaṉaḷ
       iraikeḻu kumararu mēṉai viccait
       tuṟaineṟi pōkiya tuṇiviṉa rāyiṉar
       tēyāt tiruva nīyun tēriṉ
       nilaṅkoṭai muṉiyāy kalaṅkoṭai kaṭavāy
       vēḷviyiṟ ṟiriyāy kēḷviyiṟ piriyāy
       iṉaiyōr tāṇiḻaṟ ṟaṅkiya nāṭē
       vayira velpaṭai vāṉava riṟaivaṉ
       āyiraṅ kuñcarat taṇṇal kākkum
       mīmicai yulakiṉun tītikan taṉṟu" (1.37:137-47).

   eṉṟu koṇṭāṭiṉārkaḷ. vācavatattai vantu tantaiyai vaṇaṅkiṉaḷ. avaṉ
   mikavum pārāṭṭi avaḷai naṟṟāypā laṉuppiṉāṉ. naṟṟāyum maṟṟait tāyarum
   avaḷait taḻuvik koṇṭāṭiṉar.
   piraccōtaṉaṉ kaṟṟaṟivāḷar cuṟṟiya ācaṉattēyiruntu utayaṇaṉai aḻaittu,
   "tām piḻaittiruttaṟku virumpiya ulakattār,
   maramutal cāya maruntukoṇ ṭāaṅku
       naṅkuṭi valittal vēṇṭiya nampi
       taṉkuṭi keṭutta takavi lāḷaṉēṉ" (1.37: 188-90).

   eṉṟu colli varunti āruṇiyai veṉṟu utayaṇaṉaik keḷacāmpi nakarattiṟku
   aṉuppi muṭicūṭṭat tuṇintu mantirikaḷuṭaṉ ālōcittaṉaṉ. aṅṅaṉam ceyvittal
   naṉṟeṉṟu avarkaḷ coṉṉārkaḷ. piṉpu, "kōcampiyum uñcēṉaiyum muṉpuḷḷa
   pakaimai yoḻintu poṉṉum nellum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa vākuka" eṉṟu palarum
   aṟiyumpaṭi muracaṟaivittu,
   "muṉyā ṉivaṉai murukkalum vēṇṭiṉēṉ
       piṉyā ṉivaṉaip perukkalu muṟṟaṉeṉ
       emara ṉāyi ṉiṟaikoṭut takalka
       amara ṉāyi ṉamaivoṭu niṟka (1.37:192-202).

   utayaṇaṉukkup pōrt tuṇaiyāka eṉṉuṭaiya kumāraṉ pālakaṉum varuvāṉ" eṉṟa
   kaṭuñcoṟkaḷai amaitteḻutiya ōlaiyoṉṟai oru tūtaṉ mūlamāka āruṇikku
   aṉuppiviṭṭu utayaṇaṉukku varicaikaḷ palavaṟṟai aḷittu, "cēṉaikaḷum
   cēṉait talaivarkaḷum pālakaṉum nāḷai utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ puṟappaṭuka" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭuviṭṭup pōyiṉaṉ.
   itaṉaik kēḷviyuṟṟa yūki, "utayaṇa kumāraṉ pālakaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷōṭu
   keḷacāmpikku ippōtu celvāṉāyiṉ muṉṉamē nām ceyta capatam niṟaivēṟāmaṟ
   pōm. ātalāl, itaṉai ippoḻutu naṭai peṟāmaṟ ceyvittu nammuṭaiya
   capatattai niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu" eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ
   niccayittukkoṇṭu, kuṟi collupavaḷākiya pākīrati eṉpaḷai varuvittu, "nī
   āvēcamuṟṟavaḷ pōla nakarattuḷḷē ceṉṟu araṇmaṉaiyi luḷḷārum
   puṟattuḷḷārum aṟiyumpaṭi kuṟicolli ellōrukkum mikka kavalaiyai uṇṭākki
   viraivil nīrviḻā naṭakkumpaṭi ceyka" eṉṟataṉṟic colla-vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaiyum
   aṟivuṟutti avaḷai aṉuppiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē avaḷ teyvāvēcamuṭaiyavaḷ pōlak
   kaiyai vīci āṭik koṇṭu ceṉṟu nakariluḷḷa iru pālārum kēṭkumpaṭi, "ceṉṟa
   yāṇṭil nīr viḻāvai in nakarattār ceyyāmal niṟutti viṭṭamaiyāṟ
   kōpaṅkoṇṭu muṉpu naḷakiriyai veṟikoḷvittu ūrai aḻikkumpaṭi ceyta teyvam
   yāṉ; ippoḻutum av viḻāvai nīṅkaḷ ceyyīrāyiṉ,
   piṇakkuṟai paṭuttup piḷiṟupu cīṟiya
       iṉṟuñ ceṉṟiyāṉ kuñcaram pukuval" (1.37:249-50)

   eṉṟu colla, paṉṟiyāl mikavum tuṉpuṟṟa nāy cōṟṟup pāṉaiyaik kaṇṭapoḻutum
   ataṉaip paṉṟiyeṉṟu niṉaittu mika añcuvatupōla ūrār poyyā vēcaṅ
   koṇṭavaḷatu kūṟṟukkum añciṉavarāki aracaṉukku ataṉaic colli viṭuttaṉar.
   kēṭṭa avaṉ nīr viḻāvai naṭattumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. cēṉait talaivaṉ,
   'nālai nīrāṭṭaṇi’ eṉṟu muracaṟaivittu ūrārellārkkum aṟivittāṉ.
   aṟivittavuṭaṉ iyalpākavē nīr viḻāvil viruppamuḷḷa nakarattār,
   tōṇiyu maramun tuṟainā vāyum
       nīriyaṉ māṭamu nīntiyaṟ puṇaiyum
       cuṇṇamuñ cuṭṭuñ cuvainaṟun tēṟalum
       ceṇṇac civikaiyun tērum vaiyamum
       kaṇṇār piṭikaiyuṅ kaṭṭamai yūrtiyum
       paṇṇirum piṭiyum paṇṇuvaṉar maṟalip (1.37:266-71)

   puṟappaṭuvārāyiṉar. ic ceytiyai utayaṇaṉukkut terivikkumpaṭi aracaṉ
   nūlaṟivāḷar nālvarai yaṉuppiṉaṉ. ellāc cēriyilum caṅkaṅkaḷum kompu
   mutaliyavaikaḷum muḻaṅkiṉa. palavakaik koṭikaḷum acaintaṉa.
   ārva makaḷiru māykaḻaṉ maintarum
       vīra kumararum virumpuvaṉa rēṟiya
       māvuṅ kaḷiṟu maruppiya lūrtiyum
       kālirum piṭiyuṅ kaṭuṅkāṟ piṭikaiyum
       tēru mākkaḷun teruvakat teṭutta
       eḻutukaḷ cūḻntu’ (1.38:9-14.

   eḻumpic cūriyaṉuṭaiya kiraṇaṅkaḷai maṟaittaṉa. yāṉaikaḷil varum nakara
   nampiyarum irācakumārarum, "cellutaṟku vaḻiyillai; yāṉaikaḷ
   varukiṉṟaṉa; vaḻivilakumiṉ; aracaṉāṇai iḵtu" eṉṟu collavum, iḷaiñar,
   "aracaṉ āṇaikku yām añcōm; ipputu nīrāṭṭu emakkē uriyatu" eṉṟu kūṟi
   neruṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   ’vaiya niraiyum vayappiṭi yoḻukkum
       kaipuṉai civikaiyuṅ kaccaṇi māṭamum
       ceṟṟupu ceṟintavai mokkuka ḷāka
       makkaṭ peruṅkaṭaṉ maṭaitiṟan tatupōl
       etticai maruṅkiṉu mivarntu’ (1.38: 42-6)

   ellōrum palavakaic ciṟappukkaḷuṭaṉ nakarattiṉ vāyilaik kaṭantu 1āṟṟi
   ṉiru karaiyaiyum oru yōcaṉai yaḷavuḷḷatākiya poykaiyiṉ nāṟkaraiyaiyum
   aṭaintu aṅkaṅkē yuḷḷa acōkampoḻil mutaliya tōṭṭaṅkaḷil maravaṭikaḷtōṟum
   paṭamāṭaṅkaḷ amaittup palavakaippaṭṭa nukarporuḷkaḷuṭaṉ taṉittaṉiyē
   taṅkiṉar. pala paṇṭaṅkaḷ viṟkappaṭum kaṭaikaḷ aṅkaṅkē amaikkap peṟṟaṉa.
   aracaṉ vārāmaiyāl yārum viḻācceyyat toṭaṅkavillai; ac ceytiyait
   tūtarkaḷ piraccōtaṉaṉukku aṟivittaṉar. avaṉ urimai makaḷirōṭu puṟappaṭa
   niṉaintu, "matampaṭṭa kaḷiṟukaḷai nakar puṟattēyuḷḷa cōlai kaḷiṉpāṟ
   cērttiṭuka;
   -----------
       1 āṟu - cippirānati; utitōtaya kāviyam
       ---------
       vēntupiḻait takaṉṟa viṉaiva rāyiṉum
       cērntōrt tappiya ceṟuna rāyiṉum
       kalaṅkavarn takaṉṟa kaḷva rāyiṉum
       nilampeyarn tuṟaita ṉeṭuntakai vēṇṭāṉ
       tokutan tīṇṭik kiḷaiña rākip
       pukutan tīkavip puṉalāṭ ṭakattu" (1.38: 94-9)

   eṉṟu muracaṟaivittu yāvarkkum terivikkac ceytu taṉ meykāppāḷaraiyaṉṟi
   maṟṟa vīrar yāvarum anta viḻā naṭaipeṟum irupattoru nāṭkaḷilum
   āyutaṅkaḷaik kaikkoḷḷātoḻika veṉṟum kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ;
   'tāḻpuṉaṟ ṟāraiyun tamarōṭu tarukkum
       nāḻikait tūmpum naṟumalarp pantum
       cuṇṇa vaṭṭuñ cuḻinīrk kōṭumeṉ
       ṟeṇṇiya piṟavum’ (1.38:105-8).

   ākiya tēviyarkkuriya nīrviḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ yāṉaikaḷiṉ mītu ceṉṟaṉa.
   piraccōtaṉaṉ, aracarkaḷ kaikoṭuppak 1kaṭikaiyāraṅ kaḻuttil miṉṉa
   aṇṇāntu ceṉṟu āyiram piṭikaḷiṉiṭaiyē alaṅkarikkappaṭṭu niṉṟa
   naḷakiriyiṉmītu ēṟic ceṉṟaṉaṉ. mantirikaḷ mutaliyōr tattam ūrtikaḷilēṟi
   avaṉaic cūḻntu ceṉṟaṉar. urimai makaḷirum tattamakkamainta ūrtikaḷiṟ
   ceṉṟārkaḷ.
   ---------
       1 nāḻikaiyai aṟintukoḷḷutaṟkuriya karuvi (kaṭiyāram).
       -------
       'kaṭaippakac ceppē kavari kuñcam
       aṭaippaic cuṟṟamō ṭaṉṉavai piṟavum
       aṇikalap pēḻaiyu māṭai vaṭṭiyum
       maṇicey vaḷḷamu matumakiḻ kuṭamum
       pūppey ceppum pukaiyaki laṟaiyum
       cīppiṭu cikkamuñ cempoṟ kalacamum
       kāppiyak kōcamuṅ kaṭṭilum paḷḷiyum
       cuṭṭik kalaṉuñ cuṇṇakak kuṟṟiyum
       vaṭṭikaip palakaiyum varumulaik kaccum
       muṭṭiṉai vaṭṭu mukakkaṇ ṇāṭiyum
       nakkirap palakaiyu naṟuñcān tammiyum
       kaḻuttiṭu kaḻaṅkuṅ kavaṟuṅ kaṇṇiyum
       pantum pāvaiyum paiṅkiḷik kūṭum
       yāḻuṅ kuḻalu maricciṟu paṟaiyum
       tāḻa muḻavamun taṇṇumaik karuviyum’ (1.38:161-75)

   ākiya poruḷkaḷ avarkaḷ puṭaiyē kūṉar mutaliyōrkaḷāṟ koṇṭu
   cellappaṭṭaṉa. tēviyarkaḷ vācavatattai vārātatu kaṇṭu ēvaṉ
   makaḷiriṭamākac colli yaṉuppiviṭṭu avaḷatu varavaik katirviraṟ
   kaviyaluṭ kaṇṇiṇai piṟaḻa nōkkikkoṇṭu vaḻiyiṟ kāttiruntaṉar.
   ceṉṟavarkaḷ, cēṉāpati makaḷai alaṅkarittukkoṇ ṭirunta vācavatattaiyiṭañ
   colla, avaḷ tōḻiyarkku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai yaḷittu avarkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉaḷ.
   avaḷaik kaṇṭa naṟṟāy, "muṟṟat tuṟanta muṉivarkaḷaiyum nilaikulaiyac
   ceyyum, ivaḷ kaṇkaḷ" eṉṟu kūṟi iṉpuṟṟu avaḷuṭaiya pāṅkimāraiyum
   uyirttōḻiyākiya kāñcaṉamālaiyaiyum pārttu, "ivaḷ umataṭaikkalam" eṉṟu
   kūṟi oppittuviṭṭuc cella, vācavatattai oru vaiyattilēṟiṉaḷ. atiṟ
   pūṭṭiya kāḷaikaḷiṉ kuḷampukaḷ vaḻukkak kaṇṭa pākaṉ avaṟṟai aṉuppiviṭṭu
   mallar palar tāṅkum oru māṭac civikaiyiṉmītu avaḷai ēṟṟiṉaṉ. avaḷ
   poykaik karaiyai yaṭaintāḷ.
   aracaṉ, piṭikaḷuṭ ciṟantatum ilakkaṇa mikkatum maṅkalakaramāṉa
   yākkaiyai yuṭaiyatum vīriyamuṭaiyatum neṭun tūrañ cellum
   vaṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyatum mattaka mālaiyai yaṇintu maṇamakaḷ pōla
   viḷaṅkuvatum nikarillātatumāṉa pattirāpati yeṉṉum peṇyāṉaiyaic civētaṉ
   mukamāka utayaṇaṉukku aṉuppi ataṉai ūrntu varumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭut tāṉ
   mēṟkūriya poykaikkaraiyiṟ ceṉṟu taṅkiṉāṉ. utayaṇaṉum aracaṉuṭaiya
   viruppattiṉpaṭi anta yāṉaiyai ūrntu vayantakaṉōṭu aṅkē ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   nālvakaip paṭaikaḷum tammaic cūḻa aracakumārarkaḷ vantu tamakku amainta
   iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkiṉārkaḷ. civappuk koṭikaḷai eṅkum piṭikkac ceytu
   parivāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ nakara nampiyarkaḷ tamakkuriya iṭaṅkaḷil vantu
   amarntārkaḷ.
   nīrāṭutaṟ kuriya naṟkālattai maṅkalak kaṇikaḷ kūṟiṉar. aracaṉ irupatu
   yāṉaikaḷaiyum eṇpatu puravikaḷaiyum aimpatu tērkaḷaiyum nūṟāyirattoru
   kaḻañcu mācaiyaiyum tāṉam ceytāṉ; takkaṇai yaḷittāṉ; periyōrkaḷai
   vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ; iraṇiya karppam naṭattiṉāṉ; srīkōyilukkup poṟpū āyirañ
   cērppittāṉ; piṉṉum ceyya vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic ceytāṉ; neṟkatir cūṭṭi
   veṇṭukiliṭṭa vicaya muracattai muṉṉē yaṉuppiṉāṉ; nīrāṭap puṟappaṭumpaṭi
   ēṉaiyōrkkuk kūṟiyataṉṟi virumpiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu āṭumiṉeṉṟu irāca
   kumārarkaḷukkum nīrviḻāviṉ kāṭcikaḷai aṅkaṅkē ceṉṟu kaṇṭu nīrāṭi
   varukaveṉṟu utayaṇaṉukkum colli yaṉuppiviṭṭup piṉṉum palavakaippaṭṭa
   tāṉaṅkaḷ purintu nīrāṭiṉaṉ. uṭaṉvantōr,
   ’tuṟaituṟai tōṟu muṟaiviṭam peṟāar
       taṇpuṉa lāṭṭiṉ ṟakkaṇai yēṟkum
       anta ṇāḷa raṭaika virainteṉap
       payiṉū lāḷaraip payirvaṉar kūuyk
       kavarvaṉar pōlak kātali ṉuyttu
       naṉkala mēṟṟi nāḷaṇi yaṇintu
       maṅkala vēḷviyuṇ makaḷī vōrum
       cempoṟ puricai veṇcutai māṭat
       tuḻaikkalam parappi yuruviya makaḷirō
       ṭiḻaikkala niṟīi yillī vōrum
       ālaik karumpu mariyuṟu cennelum
       pāḷaik kamukiṉ paṭuvuñ cuṭṭip
       puyalcē ṇīṅkiṉum pūvaḷaṅ kuṉṟā
       vayalun tōṭṭamum vaḻaṅku vōrum
       nākucū ṉīṅkiya cētāt tokuttuk
       kuḷampuṅ kōṭum viḷaṅku poṉṉuṟīit
       taḷaiyun tāmpu maḷaikaṭai mattum
       kaḻuvuṅ kalaṉum vaḻuvila piṟavum
       paimpoṉi ṉiyaṉṟavai pāṟpaṭa vakuttuk
       kuṉṟāk kōṭi koṭuttuvap pōrum
       iṉṉavai piṟavu meṉṉōrk kāyiṉum
       uṭaiyavai tavāak koṭaipuri paṭivamō
       ṭilameṉ mākakaḷai yiravoḻip pavarpōṟ
       kalaṅkoṭai pūṇṭa kaiya rāki
       veṇṭukil pūṭṭiya vēḻak kuḻaviyum
       oṇpaṭai yaṇinta vaṇparip paraviyum
       uṇṭiyu muṭaiyuṅ koṇṭakañ ceṟitta
       paṇṭiyu mūrtiyuṅ koṇṭaṉa ruḻitan
       tantaṇar cālaiyu maruntavar paḷḷiyum
       tantaṟa maruṅkiṟ ṟalaivaip pōrum
       nilampey vōru nitipey vōrum
       kaḷiṟupey vōrum paripey vōrum’ (1.39:50-81)

   ākit tattamakku iyaṉṟa tāṉaṅkaḷaic ceytaṉar. nīrāṭṭu viḻavu iṅṅaṉam
   mikka ciṟappuṭaṉ naṭai peṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
   nakaramāntar aṅkaṅkēyuḷḷa1 palavakaiyāṉa kāṭcikaḷait tām kaṇṭu
   piṟarkkuṅ kāṭṭi iṉpuṟṟu,
   "paṇivi ṉalviṉaip payaṉuṇ ṭāyiṉ
       maṇimuṭi paṉṉa ṉaṇiyuñ cēṉaiyuḷ
       ēḻumaip piṟappu meytukam yām" (1.40: 386-8)

   eṉṟu iṉpuṟṟuk kūṟiṉar.
   ----------
       1. intappakuti mika virivākavum aḻakākavum itaṉ mutaṉūliṟ
       kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu; pakkam, 60-71.

   poykaiyum poykaik karaiyum palavakaic ciṟappukkaḷālum palavakai
   ōcaikaḷālum viḷaṅkutaluṟṟaṉa.
   araca kumararum pētai makaḷir mutaliya 2ēḻu paruva makaḷirum nīrāṭi
   makiḻntaṉar. appāl, kāñcaṉa mālaiyum maṟṟat tōḻimārum vācavatattaiyai,
   veyilaḻal kaviyātu viyalaka varaippiṉ
       uyiraḻal kavikku muyarccit tākip
       pūntā raṇinta vēntal veṇkuṭai
       vēntaṉ makaḷē viraiyā teṉmarum
       paṇṭai makaḷir paṭimaiyiṟ piḻaiyātu
       taṇṭan tūkkit talaippuṉal viḻaviṉaik
       koṇṭuvan tāṭuṅ koḻumalart taṭaṅkaṭ
       poṅkumalark kōtāy pōṟṟeṉ pōrum
       niṉṉai yuvakkuniṉ perumā ṉēntiya
       veṉvēl kaṭukkum veṉmai nōkkattup
       poṉṉē pōṟṟi polikeṉ pōrum’ (1.42:44.54)

   āki mikka upacārattuṭaṉ aḻaittuc ceṉṟu vitippaṭi nīrāṭṭi
   alaṅkarippā-rāyiṉar.
   ----------
   2. intap pakuti, maṟṟa enta nūliluṅ kāṇappaṭāta iṉpamuṭaiyatāy itaṉ
   mutaṉūliṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu; pakkam, 72-6.
   antaṇa makaḷirum, araca makaḷirum, cēṉāpati makaḷum, tiṇai makaḷirum,
   kāviti makaḷirum, peruṅkuṭi makaḷirum,
   nīrttalaik koṇṭa neṭumperun tuṟaivayiṟ
       pōrtalaik koṇṭu poṅkupu maṟalik
       koṅkalar kōtai koṇṭupuṟat tōcciyum
       añceñ cānta mākat teṟintum
       naṟunīrc civiṟip porinī rekkiyum
       mukiḻviraṟ ṟārai mukanēr viṭṭum
       matimaru ṭirumukat tetirnīr tūviyum
       potipūm panti ṉetirnī reṟintum
       civanta kaṇṇiṉar viyanta nutaliṉar
       aviḻnta kūntalar nekiḻnta vāṭaiyar
       ocinta maruṅkula racainta tōḷiṉar
       nalkūr perumpuṉal koḷka veṉṟutam
       celva mellāñ cērttiṟait taruḷi
       iḷaiyā viruppiṟṟam viḷaiyāṭṭu muṉaiik
       kayampā ṭaviyap puṟaṅkarai pōntu’ (1.42:184.98)

   kūntal ularttip pūmālaikaḷai muṭittu,
   'paiṅkūṟ pātirip pōtupirit taṉṉa
       aṅkō cikamum vāṅkac cātarum
       koṅkār kōṅkiṉ koymmala raṉṉa
       paiṅkōḻk kaliṅkamum paṭṭut tūcum
       nīlamu marattamum vāliḻai vaṭṭamum
       kōlamoṭu puṇarnta vēṟuvē ṟiyaṟkai
       nūliṉu mulaṇṭiṉu nāriṉu miyaṉṟaṉa
       yāvai yāvai yavaiyavai maṟṟavai
       mēvaṉa mēvaṉa kāmuṟa vaṇintu’ (1.42:20-12)

   piṉ ēṟṟa āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum aṇintukoṇṭaṉar.
   antac camayattil, utayaṇaṉ taṉṉaic ciṟaip paṭuttiya piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya
   celva mikutiyum aṅkē nīrāṭuvōruṭaiya iṉpa vicēṭamum taṉ maṉattai
   varutta, "pattirāpatiyiṉ mēlē ēṟiya intap pakalilāvatu nam eṇṇattai
   muṭikka vēṇṭum" eṉṟa karuttuḷḷavaṉākip piraccōtaṉaṉai vañcittu
   varuttuvataṟkuriya upāyam yāteṉṉum yōcaṉaiyōṭē antap piṭiyiṉmēl
   iruntaṉaṉ.
   iruntapoḻutu, utayaṇaṉ kuṟittatai aṟinta vayantakaṉ, pirayāṇattukkuc
   cātaṉamāṉavaṟṟaic cēkarittu vaittukkoṇṭu avaṉai nōkki, "uṉakkut
   terivikkumpaṭi yūki colliviṭuttavaṟṟai viṇṇappañ ceyvēṉ; kēṭṭuk
   kaikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum; utayaṇaṉ ippōtu piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya pātukāppil
   uḷḷāṉāyiṉum pāmpaippōlac camayam pārttup pakaiyai vellutal aracarkkuk
   kaṭamaiyātalālum, tāṉ pattirāpatiyiṉ mēl ēṟiyiruttalālum, vācavatattai
   taṉiyē nīrāṭutalālum kaippaṟṟi avaḷai anta yāṉaiyiṉ mēlēṟṟic
   cellutaṟkuriya camayam ituvēyām; muṉṉamē eṉṉāl niyamikkappaṭṭuḷḷa cila
   kaḷḷa makaḷir viraivil an nakarattait tīkku iraiyākkutal kūṭum; aṅkē
   pukai tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ ciṟitum pāṇittaliṉṟi vācavatattaiyaip piṭiyiṉ mītu
   ēṟṟic celvāṉāka; ēṟṟiyavuṭaṉ taṭukka vanta pirac cōtaṉuṭaiya
   vīrarkaḷai, nāṟṟicaikaḷilum maṟaintu tiriyum nam vīrar, 'vāḻka
   utayaṇaṉ' eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭum aṅkaṅkē marappontu mutaliyavaṟṟil oḷittu
   vaittirukkum āyutaṅkaḷai veḷippaṭaiyākat tāṅkikkoṇṭum pōr ceytu
   tolaittu viṭuvārkaḷ. atu paṟṟiya kavalai taṉakkuc ciṟitum vēṇṭām.
   pakaiyaracaṉuṭaiya nāṭṭiṉ aka ellaiyiṉaḷavākiya1 ainnūṟaiyum kaṭantu
   cellutaṟku ippiṭikku vaṉmai yillaiyāyiṉum nāṉūṟellai yaḷavu
   ceṉṟu vīḻntālum vīḻka, ataṉāl namakkut tuṉpamillai. appāluḷḷa nāṭṭiṉ
   pakuti vēṭar mutaliyōrai yuṭaiyatātalāl cellutaṟ kariyatu. āṉālum tāṉ
   vācavatattai mutaliyavarkaḷuṭaṉ aṅkē vantāṟ pātukāttuk koṇṭupōy
   viṭumpaṭi avviṭamuḷḷa vēṭarkaḷiṉ talaivaṉum eṉṉuṭaiya naṇpaṉumākiya
   oruvaṉiṭam muṉṉamē collivaittirukkiṟēṉ.
   ------
       1. iṅkē ainnūṟeṉṟatu, kātam, yōcaṉai, kalviyūki, ampuvīḻellai,
       kurōcam, viṟkiṭai mutaliya nīṭṭalaḷavai kaḷuḷ iṉṉateṉṟu
       viḷaṅkavillai.

   ātalāl, avaṉ pātukāppāṉ. avviṭattiṟ ciṟitu tuṉpam nēriṉum ataṉaip
   parikarittukkoṇṭu appāluḷḷa ellai nūṟaiyum kaṭantuviṭuka. pakaivarai
   vellum āṟṟalaiyuṭaiya nammavar aṅkē uḷḷār. aṅkē taṅkuka. yāṉ eṇṇit
   tuṇivatu entak kāriyamāyiṉum tītiṉṟi niṟaivēṟu meṉpatai itaṉ mukamākat
   teḷika. yūkiyeṉṉum peyar eṅkum paravumpaṭi māṉ kūṭṭattiṟ puli
   pukuntāṟpōlap pakaivarkaḷuṭaiya vīrarai vāḷāl vīḻttiviṭṭu viraivil
   taṉṉiṭam vantu cēruvēṉ. yāṉ colliyavaṇṇam ceyyāviṭṭāl aracāṭciyait tāṉ
   iḻantuviṭutal niccayam eṉpavaiyē" eṉṟu colla utayaṇaṉ, "avaṉum yāṉum
   uyirōṭiruppiṉ vāṉulakattaiyum vaṇaṅkac ceyvōm; maṟṟavai mika eḷiyaṉa"
   eṉṟu collip puṉ muṟuvaluṭaṉ aṅkaṅkē nikaḻuṅ kāṭcikaḷaip pārppataṟkuc
   celpavaṉpōlē pattirāpatiyai naṭattikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu vācavatattai āṭum perun
   tuṟaiyai aṭaivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   appoḻutu nīruṭai mēkamum kāṟṟum kalantu mikak kaṭumaiyākat tōṉṟiṉa.
   ataṉaik kaṇṭa yūki kaṉāviṟ kaṇṭa poruḷaik kaiyiṟ peṟṟa vaṟiyavaṉpōla
   mika makiḻntu aṅkē maṟaintu tiriyum vīrarkaḷ taṉ karuttai
   aṟintukoḷḷumpaṭi muraca moṉṟaik koṭṭiṉaṉ. aḵtu iṭiyaippōl muḻaṅkiṟṟu.
   ataṉaik kēṭṭa avaṉ vīrarkaḷ mikka ūkkamuṭaiyavarkaḷākit tattam
   vāṭpaṭaikaḷait tāṅki, "utayaṇa kumāraṉum yūkiyum vāḻka" eṉṟu muḻakkañ
   ceypavarkaḷāy uṟaiyiṉiṉṟum vāḷai uruvi eṭuttukkoṇṭu āṇṭuḷḷārōṭu pōr
   ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar.
   uḷ nakarattil yūkiyiṉāl vaikkappaṭṭirunta kaḷḷa makaḷir taṅkaḷ
   iruppiṭaṅkaḷai viṭṭup puṟappaṭṭu vīṭukaḷ tōṟum neruppu vaippārāyiṉar.
   'aintalai yutti yaravunā ṇāka
       mantara villi ṉantaṇaṉ viṭṭa
       tīvā yampu tiritaru nakariṉ
       ōvā teḻu maṭaṅ kuṭkuvara' {1,43: 119-22)

   eṅkum tīkkoḻuntu neṭuntūratti luḷḷavarkaḷum aṟintukoḷḷumpaṭi mika ōṅkit
   tōṉṟiyatu. āṟṟaṅ karaiyilum poykaik karaiyilumuḷḷa makaḷirkaḷ ataṉaik
   kaṇṭu, "cakkaravartti illāta camayam pārttup pakaivarkaḷ nakarattiṟ
