   pukuntu tuṉpam viḷaikkiṉṟārkaḷō? naḷakiriyeṉṉum yāṉai muṉpōlak kuṇañ
   citaintu mata veṟiyāl vītitōṟuñ ceṉṟu ceṉṟu tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟatō? yātun
   teriyavillaiyē!" eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu nilaikulaintu tattam kuḻantaikaḷaiyum
   piṟaraiyum tammaiyum pātukāttaṟku aṟiyārāyp paravaca muṟṟu ōṭi ōṭip
   palavāṟu tirivārāyiṉar. uccaiṉiyil
   'naṟuney payanta naṉṉakar muttī
       maṟumaik keṇṇiya mayalaṟu kiricai
       antaṇar cēriyu maruntavar paḷḷiyum
       veṇcutai māṭamum vēntaṉ kōyilum
       teyvat tāṉamō ṭavvaḻi yoḷiya' (1.43; 173-7).

   ēṉaiya iṭaṅkaḷ tīkkiraiyāyiṉa. piṉṉum,
   'paṭalaṇi vāyiṉ maṭalaṇi vēyuḷ
       iṭaiyaṟa villā virukkaiyiṟ polinta
       paṉṉā ṟāyiram pāṭik koṭṭilum
       munnū ṟāyira muṭṭikaic cēriyum
       ainnū ṟāyiraṅ kammavā layamum
       cēṉai vēntaṉ ciṟappiṉō ṭirunta
       tāṉaic cēriyun talaikkoṇ ṭōṭik
       kāṉat tīyiṟ kaṭukupu ticaippa' (1.43: 197-204)

   maṟṟa māṭaṅkaḷum erintupōyiṉa. ataṉaik kaṇṭaaracamaṅkaiyarkaḷ palarum
   pala vakaiyāka alaintu tuṉpuṟṟaṉar.
   appōtu piraccōtaṉaṉ vēṟoṉṟuñ ceyya aṟiyātavaṉāki, "yāṉaikaḷai
   nāṟpuṟattum araṇāka niṟutti ataṉ iṭaiyē nammuṭaiya urimai makaḷirai
   irukkumpaṭi ceyka" eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. aṅkē niṉṟa yāṉaikaḷuṭ cila mēka
   muḻakkattaip pakaimaiyaiyuṭaiya yāṉai muḻakkameṉṟu karuti varampu
   kaṭantu ōṭic ceṉṟu kūṭṭaṅkaḷai mitikkat toṭaṅkiṉa.
   "nakari muḻakkiṉu mikaiyeḻu tīyiṉum
       aḷavi lārppiṉu maruntaḷai parintu
       kaṭaleṉa vatirntu kāreṉat tōṉṟi
       viṭaleruñ cīṟṟamoṭu vēṟupaṭa nōkkik
       kaḷameṉak karutik kaṉaṉṟa vuḷḷamoṭu
       naḷakirik kūṟṟa nakara muḻakka
       etireḻu vōrai yatira nūṟi
       vattavar kōmāṉ vayavar tiritara," (1.44:86-93)

   aṅkirunta piraccōtaṉaṉ taṉ ēvaliḷaiyarai nōkki, "taṉ māṇākkiyākiya
   vācavatattaiyai eppaṭiyēṉum pātukāttal taṉ kaṭaṉeṉṟu yāṉ kūṟiyatāka
   utayaṇaṉukku viraintu ceṉṟu collumiṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. ataṉai avarkaḷ
   utayaṇaṉiṭam terivikkavē avaṉ, " vācavatattaiyaip pātukāttal eṉṉuṭaiya
   kaṭamai. atupaṟṟic ciṟitum kavalaiyaṭaiyā toḻika" eṉṟu aracaṉukkuc
   colli aṉuppiviṭṭup pattirā patiyaic celuttikkoṇṭu vācavatattaiyirunta
   tuṟaiyai aṭaintāṉ. aṭaintavaṉai, aṅkē irunta kāvalāḷar mutaliyōr kaṇṭu
   viraintu ceṉṟu, "neruppu paṟṟi erikiṉṟamaiyāl, ippoḻutu nakarattiṉuḷḷē
   cellal emmāl iyaluvataṉṟu; peruṅkāṟṟāṟ poṟikaḷ citaintu pōṉamaiyiṉ,
   ūrti vakaikaḷum ippōtu utavā; ātalāl, um māṇākkiyākiya vācavatattaiyai
   ip piṭiyiṉmītu ēṟṟikkoṇṭu pātukāttataruḷal vēṇṭum" eṉṟu
   paṇinturaittaṉar. maṟṟaiyōrum aṅṅaṉamē vēṇṭik kūṟiṉar. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ
   taṉ maṉattiluḷḷa pēruvakaiyaic ciṟitum veḷiyiṭātu aṭakkikkoṇṭu, avarkaḷ
   kūṟuvataik kēṭṭut taṉṉai aṭaivatil mikka ārvattōṭu niṟkum
   vācavatattaiyaik kāñcaṉamālai ēṟṟa avaṉ taṉ kuṭaṅkaiyāl taḻuvip piṭi
   mītēṟṟi, "muṭiyā āḷviṉai muṭittaṉa miṉṟu" eṉṟu makiḻvuṟṟāṉ.
   appiṭiyiṉmēl avaḷuṭaṉ kāñcaṉamālaiyum ēṟiṉaḷ. ēṟiyavaḷ taṉ kaiyilirunta
   kōṭapatiyai vayantakaṉiṭaṅ koṭuttuviṭṭup piṭiyiṉ vēkattāl naṭuṅkum
   vācavatattaiyaip pātukāttukkoṇṭiruntāḷ. appoḻutu vācavatattaiyaip
   pārttaṟku varupavaḷ pōlavē cāṅkiyattāy aṅkē vantaṉaḷ; yūkiyiṉ
   aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷai avaḷ terintu koṇṭu avaṉai aṭaintu yōcittu mēl nikaḻtta
   vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic ceyvittaṟku eṇṇiya utayaṇaṉ muṉṉamē tāṉ eḻuti
   vaittirunta ōlaiyai avaḷiṭam piṟaraṟiyāmaṟ koṭuppittu avaḷai
   mantaṇamāka avaṉpāṟ cellumpaṭi kuṟippittaṉaṉ. avaḷum akkuṟip paṟintu
   vācavatattaiyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmaṟ ceṉṟaṉaḷ.
   utayaṇaṉ piṭiyai nōkki, " uṅkaḷ cātiyiṭattuḷḷa aṉpāl yāṉ aṭainta ciṟait
   tuṉpattai muṉpu naḷakiri tīrt taḷittatu; ippoḻutu ivaḷ kaṇṇāl uṇṭākiya
   tuṉpattai ivviruḷil viraintu ceṉṟu tīrttaḷippatu uṉṉuṭaiya
   kaṭaṉākum"eṉṟu ataṉ ceviyiṟ kūṟi ataṉaic celuttiṉaṉ.. celuttukaiyil,
   ampaiyum villaiyun tāṅkiya kaiyaṉāki aṅkē varākaṉeṉṉum vīraṉ vantaṉaṉ.
   avaṉaik kaṇṭu, " varāka! nī añcātē; iṅkē cūḻntu niṟpōrkaḷvarkaḷākavum
   iruttal kūṭum. ātalāl uṉ kaiyiluḷḷa villaiyum ampaiyum eṉṉiṭaṅ
   koṭuttuviṭṭu eṉ kaiyiluḷḷa vāḷai nī tāṅkikkoṇṭu ip piṭiyiṉ mītu ēṟik
   kavalaiyiṉṟi uṭaṉvaruvāyāka" eṉṟu kapaṭamākac colla, varākaṉ taṉ
   kaiyiluḷḷa villaiyum ampaiyum utayaṇaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttuviṭṭup piṭiyiṉ
   mītēṟutaṟku ataṉarukiṟ ceṉṟaṉaṉ. avaṉ ēṟutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭāmaṟ
   pattirāpatiyai utayaṇaṉ ati vēkamākac celuttikkoṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. aṅkē niṉṟa
   kāvalarkaḷ peritum maṉaṅ kalaṅki, "vattavaṉuṭaiya karuttai nām aṟintu
   koḷḷāmal irantu vācavatattaiyai ivaṉ piṭiyiṉmēl ēṟṟiviṭṭōmē! ivaṉ taṉ
   nāṭṭai nōkkic cellukiṉṟaṉaṉē! iṉik kūṟṟa vāṇaiyeṅ koṟṟavaṉ tirumuṉ yātu
   collic celvēm?" eṉṟu eṇṇi accamum nāṇam uṟṟār.
   utayaṇaṉ vīrarkaḷil oru cārār ampukaḷaiyum viṟkaḷaiyum kaiyilēnti
   etirppōraip peṟātavarkaḷākit tam aracaṉuṭaiya veṟṟiyaip pārāṭṭik koṇṭu,
   "em talaivaṉukkup piḻai ceytōr viṇṇulakai aṭaivataṉṟi maṇṇulakil
   iruttaṟku uriyarallar" eṉṟu collikkoṇṭē piṭiyiṉ piṉ ceṉṟaṉar. vēṟoru
   cārār, pōr ceytaṟku varupavar yārēṉu muḷarō eṉṟu iraiyai virumpik
   kaṭalil ulāvit tiriyum cuṟākkaḷaip pōṉṟu aṅkaṅkē tēṭit tirintu
   cuḻaṉṟukoṇṭu tiṭṭiviṭameṉṉum pāmpaippōlavē neruppai umiḻuṅ kaṇkaḷai
   uṭaiyavarkaḷāki, etirppōrkaḷai veṭṭi vīḻttupavarkaḷāyp piṭitta vāḷar
   maṭitta vāyarāy naṭuṅkuvittut tirivārāyiṉar.
   piṉṉum yūkiyāl aṅkaṅkē iruttappaṭṭa ainnūṟ ṟaimpattaintu vīrarkaḷ
   veḷippaṭṭuk kēṭakattaiyum vāḷaiyu muṭaiyavarkaḷāki, piṭiyait taṭuttaṟku
   ataṉpiṉ cellum vīrarkaḷai muṉ ceṉṟu veṭṭi vīḻttikkoṇṭum kūṟṟuṟai
   ulakiṉuḷ uṟaikuvi rāyiṉ viraintu kuṟukumiṉ eṉṟu collikkoṇṭum
   tirintaṉar.
   antac camayattil yūki, iyalpāṉa uruvamuṭaiyavaṉāki mayāṉattiliruntu
   peruntavañ ceytu tāṉ peṟṟatum karuṅ kallaip piḷantālum vaṭuppaṭāta
   vāyai yuṭaiyatumākiya vāḷaik kaiyilēnti ataṉai nōkki, "vāṭpaṭaiyē!
   aruccuṉaṉ centīk kaṭavuḷukkuk kāṇṭava vaṉattai muṟkālattil uṇavāka
   arutti ataṉ paciyait tīrttatupōla uṉ paciyai iṉṟu tīrttuviṭuvēṉ;
   pātukākka vēṇṭum" eṉṟu colli etirttavarkaḷai eḷitiṟ koṉṟukoṇṭu ati
   vēkamāka utayaṇaṉai aṭaintu pattirāpatiyai valam vantu pala muṟai
   tutittu, "pattirāpatī! uṉ cātiyiluḷḷa piriyattāl em talaivaṉ aṭainta
   varuttattait tīrttaṟku nī viraintu ceṉṟu iṭaiyēyuḷḷa kāṭṭil taṅki
   viṭāmal emmuṭaiya nāṭṭai yaṭaika. uṉakku itu peruṅkaṭaṉākum" eṉṟu
   ompaṭai colli ataṉ kātil mantiram oṉṟai upatēcittu aracaṉai nōkki,
   "viraintu celka; cayamuṇṭākuka" eṉṟaṉaṉ.
   utayaṇaṉ, cāṅkiyat tāyiṉuṭaiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum iyalpaiyum utaviyaiyum
   avaḷ kaiviṭutaṟkariyaḷ eṉpataiyum viḷaṅkap poṟitta ōlai oṉṟai
   yūkiyiṉiṭam koṭuppittu viṭṭu, taṉṉuṭaiya vīrarkaḷiṉ veṟṟiyum
   yūkiyiṉuṭaiya ālōcaṉaiyiṉ mēmpāṭum avaṟṟāluṇṭākiya iṉpamum taṉṉuḷḷattai
   nirappat taṉakkākavē imaiyavar nirumittut tantāṟpōṉṟa antap piṭiyai
   vaṭa kīḻt ticaiyiṉvaḻiyē celuttat toṭaṅkukaiyil, mēṟkūṟiya
   varākaṉeṉpavaṉ uñcai vīrarkaḷiṉ uyiraip pātukāppavaṉākip piṉtoṭarntu
   aṅkē vantāṉ. avaṉai nōkki utayaṇaṉ, "nī avantiyar perumāṉuṭaiya
   tāṇiḻalai yaṭaintu, 'pātukāttiṭuka’ eṉṟu vācavatattaiyai eṉpāl aṉpuṭaṉ
   oppittamaiyāl yāṉ ivaḷaik koṇṭu eṉṉāṭu cellutaṟkut tuṇintēṉ; itaṉai
   avarukkuk kūṟuvatuṭaṉ eṉ vaṇakkattaiyum terivikka vēṇṭum" eṉṟu
   pāloṭutēṉ kalantāṟpōṉṟa collaic colla, varākaṉ ataṉaik kēṭṭu mayaṅkip
   piṭiyiṉ piṉṉē mīṭṭum ceṉṟu niṉṟaṉaṉ. uṭaṉaḻaittuc cellāmaṟ ceyti colli
   varākaṉaimaṭṭum tantaipāl viṭuttamai terinta vācavatattai mayaṅki
   veytuyirttut tuṉpuṟṟuk kāñcaṉamālaiyai nōkki, "kāñcaṉamālāy, eṉṉaik
   kaippaṟṟikkoṇṭu kīḻē iṟaṅkuka" eṉṟu colluvāḷāyt tantai mutaliyōriṉ
   pirivāṟṟātavaḷ pōlavum accamuṟṟavaḷ pōlavum pulampuvāḷāyiṉāḷ. ataṉaik
   kaṇṭa kāñcaṉamālai kaikuvittukkoṇṭu tāṉ eṇṇiyatai muṭittu viḷaṅkum
   utayaṇaṉai nōkki, "aracē! tāṉ ūcalāṭiṉum pāṅkiyar kuravai yāṭiṉum mika
   añcukiṉṟa eṅ kōmakaḷ naṭuṅkumpaṭi,
   eṟivaḷi puraiyu mirumpiṭi kaṭaiip
       piṉvaḻip paṭarumem perumpaṭai pēṇāy
       eṉvalit taṉaiyō iṟaiva" (1.46:185-7)

   eṉru colli varuntiṉaḷ. uṭaṉē utayaṇaṉ, "nīvir iruvarum mayaṅkā toḻika.
   aracaṉ ivaḷai eṉṉōṭu cērttu aṉuppiyatai aṟiyāta vīrarkaḷ pōr ceytaṟkut
   tuṇintu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. yāṉ ivarkaḷai oru poruḷāka eṇṇēṉ. ivarkaḷai
   vellutal eṉakku mika eḷiya kāriyamākum; añcaṟka" eṉṟaṉaṉ. kāñcaṉamālai
   vācavatattaiyait teruṭṭi avaḷ naṭukkattait tīrttaṉaḷ. avaḷum naṭukkoḻin
   tiruntāḷ. appāl utayaṇaṉ taṉ nāṭṭai nōkkip piṭiyaic celuttiṉaṉ. atu
   kaṇṭōr yāvarum, "utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyai aracaṉiṭattāvatu
   irācamātēviyiṭattāvatu koṇṭu cellāmal taṉ nāṭu nōkkic cellukiṉṟaṉaṉē!
   iṉi eṉṉa nērumō!’ eṉṟu pakaṟ kālattiṟ cūriyaṉai iḻantavarkaḷ pōlē
   mikavum añciṉar. cevilit tāyarum pāṅkimārum maṟṟaiyōrum avaḷuṭaiya
   ceyalkaḷai niṉaintu collic colli aṅkaṅkē palavakaiyākap
   pulampuvārāyiṉar.
   cāṅkiyattāy, etirttōrai veṉṟukoṇṭu tirikiṉṟa yūkiyai oṟṟiyaṭaintaṉaḷ.
   aṭaiyavē utayaṇaṉ koṭutta ōlaiyāl avviruvarum oruvaraiyoruvar naṉku
   aṟintukoṇṭu mēl āka vēṇṭiya vaṟṟai uṇarntu cātakaṉeṉṉum kuyavaṉuṭaiya
   maṉaiyil maṟaintiruntaṉar.
   nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷai aracaṉukku aṟivittaṟ kañci nakarattār iṉṉatu
   ceyvateṉ ṟaṟiyāmal taṅkaḷ kaiyaip picaintukoṇṭu aṅkumiṅkum
   tirivārāyiṉar.
   utayaṇaṉāl viṭukkappaṭṭa varākaṉ mīṇṭu vantu aracaṉuṭaiya paṭamāṭak
   kōyiliṉ mutal vāyililuḷḷa tōraṇa kampattiṉ aṭiyai aṭaintu vāyil
   kāvalaṉaik kaṇṭu, "aracar perumāṉukkuc cila ceytikaḷ aṭiyēṉ vaṇaṅkit
   terivikka vēṇṭuva tuṇṭu; ataṟkuc camayamaṟintu viraintu varuka" eṉṟu
   colli niṉṟāṉ.
   niṟkaiyil uḷḷē aracaṉai nōkkic cōtiṭa ṉoruvaṉ, "kāṟṟum mēkamum kalanta
   in nimitta māṉatu ūrukku mutalil tuṉpattait tantu piṉpu umakku
   iṉpattait tarum" eṉṟu colla aracaṉ, "aḵteṉṉa?" eṉru viṉāviyapoḻutu,
   vāyilkāvalaṉ vantu iṟaiñciṉaṉ. aracaṉ, "vanta kāriyattaic col" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭa avaṉ mīṭṭum vaṇaṅki, "num maṭittiyum emperumāṉumākiya
   vācavatattaikku aṭittoṇṭu ceypavarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉum valvil iḷaiñaṉumākiya
   varākaṉeṉpavaṉ, tāṉ vantu iṅkē cilavaṟṟait terivittuk koḷvataṟkuc
   camayam peṟa vēṇṭip puṟattē niṟkiṉṟāṉ; pallāṇṭu vāḻka" eṉru iṟaiñciṉaṉ.
   aracaṉ, "avaṉai viraivil varaviṭuka" eṉru colla avaṉ varākaṉukkut
   terivittaṉaṉ. uṭaṉē varākaṉ uḷḷē vantu, ulāvu maṇṭapattil ulāvāmal
   muḷaikkōṟ peruntirai cūḻnta vaṭṭattiṉiṭaiyē ciṅkātaṉattiṟ civanta
   mēlāṭaiyāl tāṇmutalaik kaṭṭik koṇṭu oru tāmaraip pūvaik kaiyilēntik
   katiravaṉ pōlavē vīṟṟirunta aracarpirāṉai eḻukōl ellaikku appāl niṉṟu
   mikka naṭukkattuṭaṉ vaṇaṅka, aracaṉ avaṉatu accat tōṟṟattaik kaṇṭu avaṉ
   karuti vanta kāriyam mikavum kaṭumaiyāka iruttal vēṇṭu meṉ ṟeṇṇi avaṉai
   irukōl ellaiyuḷ varumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭu, "vācavatattaipāl nikaḻnta
   putiya nikaḻcci ētēṉum uṇṭō?" eṉṟu viṉāviṉaṉ. viṉāvavē, avaṉ
   yātoṉṟaiyum colla iyalātavaṉāki mīṭṭum iṟaiñciṉaṉ. tāṉ mīṭṭum mīṭṭuṅ
   kēṭkavum avaṉ viṭai kūṟāmaṟ piṉṉum piṉṉum mayaṅkutalaik kaṇṭu,
   "nikaḻntatai añcāmaṟ col" eṉṟu avaṉ uyirkku apayaṅkoṭuttaṉaṉ.
   appōtu varākaṉ tīyiṟpaṭṭa meḻukupōl urukiya mukattaṉāki,
   :aracarpirāṉuṭaiya āṇaiyālō vēṟoṉṟālō viḷaṅkavillai; kāṟṟālum
   maḻaiyālum tīyālum puṟa nakarum aka nakarum aṇukutaṟ kariyaṉavāyiṉa;
   aṅkaṅkē palar pōr ceytaṟkup puṟappaṭṭaṉar. an nikaḻccikaḷaik kaṇṭu
   mikavum añci vācavatattaiyai iṅkē aḻaittu varuvataṟku ēṟṟa araṇ
   kāṇappaṭāmaiyiṉ, niṉ āṟṟalāṇai; yāṅkaḷ ceyalaṟṟōmāki iṉic ceyya
   vēṇṭuvatu yāteṉṟu karuti muṉṉam naḷakiriyai aṭakkiya utayakumajṉaik
   kuṟaiyirantu avaṉuṭaṉ pattirāpatiyiṉmēlē ēṟṟiṉam; ēṟṟiyavuṭaṉ avaṉ
   pattirātipatiyaik kāṟṟaippōlavē celuttikkoṇṭu, emmaip pārāmalum in
   nakarattai aṭaiyāmalum taṉ nakaram cellat toṭaṅkiṉaṉ. māyiru ñālattu
   maṉṉuyiruṇṇum kūṟṟaip pōṉṟavarākiya nammuṭaiya vīrar āyirat
   taiññūṟṟuvar añcāmaṟ ceṉṟu taṭuttaṉar. taṭukkavum avaṉuṭaiya vīrar
   vantu pōr ceytu nammavarkaḷaik koṉṟu vīḻtti veṟṟipeṟṟut tiriyalāyiṉar.
   ap poḻutum nāṉ añci nillāmal pattirātipatiyiṉ piṉṉē viraintu
   ceṉṟapoḻutu utayaṇaṉ eṉṉai nōkki, 'nammaracarpirāṉ vācavatattaiyai
   eṉṉiṭam oppittu aṉuppiṉamaiyāl nāṉ ivaḷuṭaṉ eṉṉiṭañ celkiṉṟēṉ; eṉ
   vaṇakkattai avarukkuc colla vēṇṭum' eṉṟu ticainōkki iṟaiñcik kūṟip
   piṭiyai naṭattikkoṇṭu celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. terivittaṟku vantēṉ; icceyal
   māyampōlum, kāvala! aruḷ purika" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ.
   kēṭṭavuṭaṉ piraccōtaṉaṉukkuk kaṇkaḷ tīyaippōlē mikac civantaṉa. cūḻntu
   niṉṟa uṟutic cuṟṟattārkaḷaic cuṭupavaṉ pōlappārttu arukēyiḷḷa tūṇaip
   puṭaittu pakaicciṅkattiṉ koṭuṅkuralaik kēṭṭa ciṅkavēṟṟaippōlē peru
   muḻakkañ ceytu, koṭiyaṇi tērum kutiraiyum yāṉaiyum vaṭivē liḷaiyarum
   valviraintōṭi, ippupuṇṇiya viḻaviṟ camayam pārttu oṟṟi nammuṭaiya
   cōrvai yaṟintu vācavatattaiyaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭu nammai ikaḻntu ceṉṟa
   pakaivaṉaip piṭittuk koṇṭu viraivil varuka" eṉṟu vīrarkaḷukkuk
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ; avarkaḷum appaṭiyē ceṉṟaṉar. appōtu aṅkē pala
   mantirimāruṭaṉirunta cālaṅkāyaṉ, " utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyaip piṭittuc
   ceṉṟatu kuṟṟamākātu; ālōcittup pārkku miṭattu,
   'kulattiṉuṅ kuṇattiṉu nalattaku naṇpiṉum
       nilattiṉi ṉiṉṉoṭu nikarkkuna ṉātaliṉ
       mēlvakai vitiyiṉ viḻumiyōr vakutta
       pālvakai yallatu paḻikkuna rillai.' (1,47:137.40)

   ātalāl, ippōtu ciṉan taṇital nalamākum" eṉṟu collak kōpan taṇinta
   piraccōtaṉaṉ, " ic ceytiyai maṟṟaiyōr terivittaṟku muṉṉatāka nāmē
   paṭṭattēvikkut terivikka vēṇṭum" eṉ ṟeṇṇi muracaṟaikiṉṟa
   vaḷḷuvamutumakaṉai varuvittu. "ip puṉalāṭu viḻāviṇ kaṇ, mālaiyil
   maḻaittuḷiyil nakarattukkuc cellutal naṉṟaṉṟu; ātalāl ippōtu cella
   vēṇṭām; kālaiyiṟ celluka; iḵtu aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷai yeṉṟu nīrttuṟai
   nakarattār aṟiyumpaṭi muracaṟaivittut terivittiṭuka" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. avaṉum av vaṇṇañ ceytaṉaṉ. appāl aracaṉ mutaṟ
   peruntēviyai aṭaintu aṅkē paḷḷikoḷḷāṉāki avaḷai nōkki, "vācavatattaiyai
   utayaṇaṉukku maṇañ ceyvittu avaṉuṭaiya nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppa eṉ maṉam
   virumpiyatu; uṉakku itu uṭaṉpāṭākumō?" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ. avaḷ,
   "naḷakiriyai aṭakki namakku muṉ koṇarntu niṟuttiya aṉṟē avaṉuṭaiya
   iḷamaik kōlamum piṟavum avaṉ vācavatattaikku uriyavaṉeṉṟu niṉaikkumpaṭi
   ceytatu; eṉakku ac ceyalil yātoru veṟuppu millai; viruppamē" eṉṟu
   uvantu kūṟiṉaḷ. ataṉaik kēṭṭu uṭkoṇṭa aracaṉ, neṟuppil kāynta
   nārācattaik kātiṟ ceṟittatupōla vācavatattaiyiṉ pirivuc ceytiyaip piṉpu
   kūṟiṉaṉ; kūṟiyavuṭaṉ avaḷ,
   "icaikoḷ cīṟiyā ḻiṉṉicai kēṭṭa
       acuṇa naṉmā vannilaik kaṇṇē
       paṟaiyoli kēṭṭuttaṉ paṭimaṟan tatupōl (1.47; 241-3)

   mūrccittup piṉpu pulampuvāḷāyiṉaḷ; aracaṉ takka kāraṇaṅkaḷaik kūṟit
   tēṟṟiṉaṉ. avaḷ ciṟitu teḷintiruntāḷ; maṟṟai antappuramakaḷir tattaiyiṉ
   pirivāṟṟāmaiyāṟ palavāṟu pulampuvārāyiṉar. poykaik karaik kaṇ
   viḻāviṟkāka amaikkappaṭṭa ap putiya nakarattuḷḷa makaḷir palarum
   vācavatattaiyaip pirintamaiyāl tiṅkaḷai yiḻanta viṇmīṉkaḷaippōlap
   polivillātavarkaḷāy vilāvittuk koṇṭiruntaṉar.
   muṉṉam utayaṇaṉaip piṭittu varumpaṭi piraccōtaṉaṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭa
   pōrvīrar palar nālvakaip paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟu kaṭal puṭaipeyarntāṟpōlavē
   muṉpum piṉpum pakkaṅkaḷilum vantu taṭukkaiyil aṅkaṅkē yūkiyāl
   vaikkappaṭṭup patuṅkiyirunta vīrarkaḷ avarkaḷai veṉṟu mīḷacceytārkaḷ.
   pattirāpati uñcēṉaikku appāl iraṇṭu kātatūram cellutaṟku muṉ cūriyaṉ
   astamittāṉ; mati tōṉṟiyatu. piṭi viraintu ceṉṟatu ataṉ aṭiyōcaikkum
   vēkattiṟkum vācavatattai añciṉaḷ; avaḷai nōkkik kāñcaṉai, "uṉ tantai
   vitippaṭi maṇañ ceytu koṭukkavillai eṉkiṟa kuṟaiyai yaṉṟi vēṟu kuṟai
   yātum ilatu; nī virumpiya aṇimuṭi yaṇṇal valiya vantu uṉṉai aṭuttāṉ;
   ātalāl, nī kavalai aṭaiyātē; piṭiyiṉ vēkattāl cantira maṇṭilamum
   nakṣattiraṅkaḷum viṇṇil oru nilaiyāka irāmal cuḻaluvaṉapōlat
   tōṉṟāniṉṟaṉa; malaikaḷum maraṅkaḷum piṟavum mukattait tākkuvataṟku
   viraintu varuvaṉapōlavē maṇṇiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; nī oṉ ṟaiyum
   pārāmaliruppāyāka'' eṉṟu avaḷatu nutal vērvaiyait tuṭaittuk kūṭṭamākac
   celluṅ karuṭap paṟavaikaḷuṭaiya ciṟakukaḷiṉ oliyaip pōlavē timmeṉa
   olikkum piṭiyiṉ aṭiyōcai kēḷātapaṭi avaḷ kātiṟ pañciyaic ceṟittu
   avaḷait taḻuvikkoṇ ṭiruntāḷ. irunta kālattiṟ piṭiyāṉatu,
   'pāḷaik kamukum paṉaiyum paḻakkiya
       vāḻaik kāṉamum vārkulait teṅkum
       palavum payiṉu milaivaḷar māvum
       puṉṉaiyuñ ceruntiyum poṉṉiṇar ñāḻalum
       iṉṉavai piṟavu miṭaiyaṟa viṉṟi
       iyaṟṟap paṭṭavai yerikatir vilakkip
       pakaliruḷ payakkum paṭimat tāki
       akala mamainta vayirmaṇa laṭukkattuk
       kāṟōy kaṇaikkatirc cāṟōy cāli
       varampaṇi koṇṭa nirampaṇi neṭuviṭai
       uḻava roṭiyuṅ kaḷamar kampalum
       vaḷavaya liṭaiyiṭaik kaḷaikaḷai kaṭaiciyar
       patalai yariyal pācilaip parukiya
       matalaik kiḷiyiṉ maḻalaip pāṭalum
       taṇṇumai yoliyun taṭārik kampalum
       maṇṇamai muḻaviṉ vayava rārppum
       maṭaivāy tiruttu maḷḷar cummaiyum
       iṭaiyaṟa viṉṟi yiraiyāṟu taḻīi
       vayaṟpulac cīṟū rayaṟpulat taṇuki
       marutan taḻīiya ' (1.48:151-70)

   ammarutattiṇaiyaik kaṭantu viraintu ceṉṟatu. cellukaiyil, vayantakaṉ, "
   iṅkē tōṉṟum aruṭṭa nakarattār itaṉ ōcaiyaik kēḷātapaṭi in nakarattiṉ
   valappakkamāka uḷḷa peruvaḻiyil itaṉaic celuttutal naṉṟu" eṉṟu
   terivittaṉaṉ; utayaṇaṉ avvāṟu celutta, appiṭi,
   'iṟaṅkukura liṟaṭi yiṟuṅkaṭai nīṭik
       kavaikkatir varakuṅ kārpayi leḷḷum
       pukarppū vavaraiyum poṅkukulaip payaṟum
       uḻuntuṅ koḷḷuṅ koḻuntupaṭu caṇāyum
       tōraiyun tuvaraiyu māyavum piṟavum
       aṭakka lākā viṭaṟkaru viḷaiyuṭ
       kollai payiṉṟu vallai yōṅkiya
       varaiyi ṉarukā maraiyā maṭappiṇai
       ceruttaṟ ṟīmpāl cetumpupaṭap piliṟṟi
       veṇpū mucuṇṭaip paiṅkuḻai mēyac
       ciṟupiṇai taḻīya tirimarup piralai
       ceṟiyilaik kāyā ciṟupuṟat tuṟaippat
       taṭavunilaik koṉṟaiyoṭu piṭavutalaip piṇaṅkiya' (1.49;104-16)

   mullaittiṇaiyaiyum,
   'īramil kuṟava ritaṇmicaip pottiya
       ārat tuṇiyōṭu kārakil kaḻumiya
       koḷḷik kūreri veḷḷi viḷakkiṟ
       kavarimā ṉēṟu kaṇpaṭai koḷḷum
       takaraṅ kaviṉiya taṇvarac cāral
       kaṟaiyu nākamu muṟaiyiru vēriyum
       varukkaiyu māvum vaḻaiyum vāḻaiyum
       maruppiṭai nivanta vāciṉi maramum
       peruñceṇ pakamum piṇṭiyum pirampum
       karukāṟ kuṟiñciyuṅ kaṭināḷ vēṅkaiyum
       cuḷḷiyuñ cūralum vaḷḷiyu maralum
       vālveḷ vacampum vaḷḷitaḻk kāntaḷum
       pālveṇ kōṭṭamum paṇiccaiyun tilakamum
       vēyum vetiramum veṭciyuṅ kuḷaviyum
       āypūn tillaiyu maṇimā rōṭamum
       āramuñ cantu makilun tamālamum
       ērila vaṅkamu mēlamu miruppaiyum’ (1.50:17.33

   ceṟinta kuṟiñcittiṇaiyaiyum,
   viñcaiyam perumalai neñcakam piḷantu
       kalluṭ piṟanta kaḻuvāk katirmaṇi
       maṇṇuṭ piṟanta mācaṟu pacumpoṉ
       vēyuṭ piṟanta vāykatir muttam
       vetiriṟ piṟanta potiyavi ḻarunel
       maruppiṉuṭ piṟanta maṇṇā muttam
       varaiyiṟ piṟanta vayiramoṭu varaṉṟi
       …. …. …. …. …. …. …. …. …
       muṭantāṭ pālāviṉ kuṭampurai yamiḻtamum
       neṭuntāṇ māvi ṉeypaṭu kaṉiyum
       karuntāḷ vāḻaip paruṅkulaip paḻaṉum
       peruntēṉ ṟoṭaiyalum viraintukoṇ ṭaḷaii
       naṟavañ cāraṟ kuṟavar parīiya
       aivaṉa nelluṅ kaivaḷar karumpum
       karuntiṉaik kuralum peruntiṉaip piṟaṅkalum
       pāvai yiñciyuṅ kūvaic cuṇṇamum
       nākat talliyu mēlat tiṇarum
       kaṭcā lēkamu muṭkā ḻakilum
       kuṅkumat tātum paiṅkaṟip paḻaṉum
       kiḻaṅku mañcaḷuṅ koḻuṅkāṟ ṟakaramum
       kaṭuppaṭu kaṉiyuṅ kāḻttip piliyum
       ciṟṟilai nellic ciṟukāyt tuṇarum
       arakkiṉ kōlō ṭaṉṉavai piṟavum
       oruppaṭut toḻiyātu viruppiṉēnti
       malaiyiṟ piṟanta māṇpuṟu peruṅkalam
       nilaivayiṉ vāḻnarkkut talaivayi ṉuykkum
       pakarvilai māntariṉ nukarporu ḷaṭakkip
       paṉmalaip piṟanta taṇṇiṟa varuviya
       amalai yaruṅkala maṭakkupu taḻīit
       taṉṉiṟ kūṭṭiya tāṉaic celvamō
       ṭirukarai maruṅkiṉum perukupu taḻīit
       aṇikkuruk kattiyu matiralu maṉukki
       maṇiccaiyu mayilai mauvalu mayakki
       ñāḻalum puṉṉaiyum vīḻa nūkkik
       kuruntuṅ koṉṟaiyum varunta vaṇakkit
       taṭavum piṭavun tāḻac cāyttu
       muḷavu murukku muruṅka voṟṟi
       māvu marutum vēraṟap puyttuc
       cēvuṅ kuravuñ ciṉaipiḷan taḷaintu
       naṟaiyu nākamu muṟainaṭu murukki
       vaḻaiyum vāḻaiyuṅ kaḻaiyuṅ kālkīṇ
       ṭālu maracuṅ kāloṭu tuḷakkip
       puṉku nāvalum puraḷa veṟṟik
       koṅkār kōṭalōṭu koyyal kuḻaii
       aṉiccamu macōkamu maṭara valaittut*
       paṉiccaiyum payiṉum paṟiyap pāyntu
       vaḷḷiyu varalun taṉvaḻi vaṇakkip
       puḷḷi māṉum pulvāyt tokutiyum
       āmā viṉamun tāmā ṟōṭi' (1,51:8-56)

   varum narumataiyāṟṟaiyuṅ kaṭantu cellumpoḻutu, vayantakaṉ viṇmīṉkaḷaip
   pārttuk kāla vellaiyai aḷantu kūṟiṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ viraivākap piṭiyai
   naṭattiya poḻutu, arattiṉ nuti pōṉṟa parukkaik kaṟkaḷālākiya
   murampukaḷaiyuṭaiya pālai nilam kāṇap paṭṭatu. "eyiṉar atiluḷḷa
   turkkātēviyiṉ kaṟciṟaik kōṭṭattiṉ orucār oṭuṅki maṟaintiruntu koṇṭu av
   vaḻiyē cellum vaṇikar tiraḷaiyum maṟṟavarkaḷaiyum varuttip poruḷkaḷaip
   paṟittukkoḷḷum naṭukal uḻalaiyum, iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭampukaḷai avarkaḷ
   taḻaikaḷāl mūṭi avaṟṟiṉ mēṟ kuvikkappaṭṭirukkum kaṟkuviyalkaḷai uṭaiya
   iṭaṅkaḷum, ciṟu tūṟukaḷ piṇaṅki irukkum iṭaṅ kaḷum, kollappaṭṭa
   āṭkaḷaic cummā pōkaṭṭuviṭum kuḻikaḷum, ivai pōṉṟa piṟavum mikkatāyt
   taṭpak kālattum veppam nīṅkātatāy,
   'ōmaiyu muḻiñcilu mulavaiyu mukāyum
       kaṭuvun tāṉṟiyuṅ koṭumuṭ ṭoṭariyum
       aravu maracu māru māttiyum
       iravu miṇṭuṅ kuravuṅ kōṅkum
       taṇakkum palācuṅ kaṇaikkāṉ ñemaiyum
       īṅkaiyu milavun tēṅkāy nelliyum
       vākaiyum piṟavum' (1.51: 37-44)

   ākiya maraṅkaḷai uṭaiyatāyt tōṉṟum ippālait tiṇaip peruvaḻiyiṟ pakaliṟ
   cellutal iyaluvataṉṟu; iravilēyē cella vēṇṭum" eṉṟu vayantakaṉ
   colla, utayaṇaṉ piṭiyai viraivāka naṭattiṉaṉ. naṭattiyapoḻutu
   avaṉuṭaiya yāḻ kaṭṭaviḻntu vīḻntu 1mūṅkiṟkāṭṭil oru mūṅkiliṟ cikkik
   koṇṭatu.
   ---------
       1 ikkāṭṭiṉ peyar pañcavaṉameṉpatu; utitōtaya kāviyam.

   ataṉai yaṟinta vayantakaṉ, "niṉ yāḻ kīḻē viḻuntatu; piṭiyai niṟuttuka"
   eṉṉa, utayaṇaṉ, "piṭi eṇṇūṟu viṟkiṭait tūram vantuviṭṭatu; iṉi nammāṟ
   ceyyaṟpālatu yātoṉṟum illai; tanta teyvam tāṉē tarum" eṉṟu collip
   paṟantu celvatupōlat tōṉṟumpaṭi piṭiyai viraivāka naṭattiṉaṉ. naṭattavē
   kālavēkameṉṉum nōy kaṉaṟṟa appiṭi mellac ceṉṟu piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya
   nāṭṭiṉ ellaiyākiya ainnūṟṟiṉuḷ melivuṟuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉai aṟinta
   utayaṇaṉ vayantakaṉiṭam, "ippiṭi nōyāl melivuṟṟatu; iṉi vīḻntuviṭum;
   kāñcaṉaiyuṭaṉ nī piṉpakkattu vaḻiyē iṟaṅkuvāyāka" eṉṟu kūṟit tāṉ
   vācavatattaiyait taḻuvik koṇṭāṉ;
   "umaiyoṭu puṇarnta vimaiyā nāṭṭattuk
       kaṇṇaṇaṅ kaviroḷik kaṭavuḷ pōla" (1.53;15-6)

   ataṉ talaippakkattu vaḻiyē mella iṟaṅkukaiyil appiṭi cāyāmaṟ kālaip
   parappikoṇṭu, "nāṉ ceyta kuṟṟattaip poṟuttaruḷka" eṉṟu utayaṇaṉai
   vaṇaṅkuvatupōlat taṉ talai avaṉ kāliṟpaṭumpaṭi vīḻntatu. utayaṇaṉ atu
   vīḻnta iyalpaiyum atu nōkkiya ticaiyaiyum nimitta nūlvaḻiyē ārāyntu
   pārttu, mutalil taṉakkut tuṉpam uṇṭāku meṉṟum appāl at tuṉpam nīṅkap
   peṟṟut taṉ iṭattait tāṉ aṭaital kūṭum eṉṟum terintukoṇṭāṉ. piṉpu
   ataṉuṭaiya maṇikaḷ, purōcaik kayiṟu, tavicu, kaṭittakam eṉpavaṟṟaik
   kaḷaintu vēṟiṭattē vaittāṉ. vaittavaṉ, "itaṉ uyir viraivil nīṅkum" eṉṟu
   vayantakaṉukkuc colliviṭṭut tāṉ ataṉ mattakattataic cārntu uṭampu
   kuḷirumpaṭi ataṉait taivantu, iṟutik kālattiṟ collutaṟkuriya
   uṟutiyākiya ōmpaṭai moḻikaḷaik kūṟi, naṟkatiyai aṭaivikka eṇṇip 1pañca
   mantirattai ataṉ ceviyil upatēcittuviṭṭu, 'ippiṟappil eṉṉuṭaiya
   tuyarattait tīrtta piṭiyē! varupiṟappil eṉṉōṭu uṭaṉvāḻvāyāka" eṉṟu
   kaṇṇīrttuḷiyuṭaṉ collit tāṅka muṭiyāta maṉakkavalai uṭaiyavaṉāki
   vayantakaṉai nōkki, " ivviruvaraiyum tuyilac ceytu pātukāttiṭuka" eṉṟu
   collivillaiyum ampaiyum avaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttuviṭṭuk kēṭakattaiyum
   vāḷaiyum tāṉ kaikkoṇṭu yāṉaikku nīrkkaṭaṉ kaḻittaṟporuṭṭu aṅkē nīrnilai
   uḷatō eṉṟu tēṭit tiripavaṉ ōr ampuvīḻ tūrattil oru puṭkaraṇiyaik
   kaṇṭaṉaṉ.
   -------
       1pañca namaskārameṉa vaḻaṅkum.

   appoḻutu veḷḷi muḷaittut tōṉṟiyatu; tōṉṟavē utayaṇaṉ vāḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai
   ataṉ karaiyil vaittuviṭṭu atil āṭit taṉṉuṭaiya niyamaṅkaḷai niṟaivēṟṟip
   piṭi nalla katiyai aṭaiyak kaṭavateṉṟu ataṟku nīrkkaṭaṉāṟṟi
   vayantakaṉai aṭaintu, "iṉip pakaṟ kālattil yām maṟaintirukka vēṇṭiya
   iṭattai aṟiya vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu" eṉṟu colli ālōcikkaiyil
   vayantakaṉ, " ivviṭam nāṭṭuc canti; aṉṟiyum kāṭṭakat tuṟaiyuṅ kaṭuviṉai
   vāḻkkai vēṭṭuvar payilum iṭam. ātalāl inta iṭam iruttaṟkut takutiyuḷḷa
   taṉṟu. iṉi iraṇṭu kāta vaḻittūram ceṉṟāl nammavar irukkum nakarattiṟkuc
   cellum vaḻiyai nām aṟintu cellalām" eṉru coṉṉāṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ taṉ
   kaiyilirunta vāḷaiyum kēṭakattaiyum avaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttuviṭṭu avaṉ
   kaiyilirunta villaiyum ampaiyum tāṉ vāṅkik koṇṭu kāñcaṉamālaiyai nōkki,
   "iṉi vācavatattaiyai eḻuppikkoṇṭu naṭantu ceṉṟu tuṉpamillāta iṭattai
   yām aṭaiya vēṇṭum" eṉṟaṉaṉ. kāñcaṉa mālai avaḷai eḻuppi naṭantu
   varumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉaḷ. vēṇṭavē, pūmalark kōtaiyum poṟai eṉpavaḷākiya
   vācavatattai utayaṇaṉpāluḷḷa kātaṉ mikutiyāl, taṉ meṉmaiyai aṟiyāmal
   mikunta ūkkattōṭu naṭappāḷāyiṉaḷ. appoḻutu avaḷuṭaiya muṉṉiṭattai
   utayaṇaṉum piṉṉiṭattai vayantakaṉum pakkattaik kāñcaṉaiyum kāttukkoṇṭu
   ceṉṟaṉar;
   "koṭippaṭai kōmaka ṉākak kūḻai
       vaṭuttīr vayantakaṉ vāḷvalam piṭittuk
       kaṭittakap pūmpaṭai kaivayi ṉaṭakkik
       kāval koṇṭa karuttiṉa ṉākap
       puricaic cuṟṟaṅ kāñcaṉai yāka" (1.53:138-42)

   vācavatattai viraivāka naṭantu ceṉṟaṉaḷ. cellukaiyil, aṉitta mitippiṉum
   paṉitta lāṉāta avaḷuṭaiya kālkaḷ koppuḷaṅkoṇṭu varuntiṉa. ataṉai aṟinta
   utayaṇaṉ, "ivaḷ varuntiṉaḷ, iṉic cellutal muṟaiyaṉṟu. maṟaintu taṅkum
   iṭattai viraintu aṟivāyāka" eṉṟu colla, vayantakaṉ ceṉṟu pārttu
   avviṭattē oru poykaiyum ataṉ karaiyil kiḷaikaḷ pakkattiṟ ṟāḻntiruttalāl
   uḷḷiṭam puṟattōrkku aṟivariyatākavum puḷḷiṉamum pukutaṟku ariyatākavum
   uḷla muḷḷilavamarap potumparaiyuṅ kaṇṭu terivikka, iruvarum avviṭam
   iruttaṟkuriyateṉṟu eṇṇi anta iṭattait tūttu ilaikaḷāṟ paṭukkai amaittu
   vācavatattaiyait tuyiṟṟik kāñcaṉaiyai aṅkē irukkac ceytuviṭṭut tām
   puṟattē niṉṟu pātukāppārāyiṉar.
   appoḻutu cūriyaṉ attamittāṉ; cantiraṉ utittaṉaṉ. vilaṅkukaḷum
   paṟavaikaḷum eṅkum oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu pakaittu muḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉa. vayantakaṉ,
   "aracē! urumaṇṇuvā uṟaiyum cayanti nakarattai iṉi yām aṭaiyalām eṉṟālō
   iruṭkālamātalāl vaḻiyiṟ palavakaiyāṉa tuṉpaṅkaḷ namakku uṇṭākum. mika
   melliyalāṉa vācavatattaiyum varuntuvāḷ; ātalāl yūkikku mikka
   anukūlaṅkaḷaic ceytukoṇṭu paranta paṭaiyōṭu iṭavakaṉ taṅkiyirukkum
   puṭpakanakarattai ivviruḷilēyē aṭaintu mikka cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ kālaiyil
   varuvēṉ. atu varaiyil pātukāttukkoṇṭu ivviṭattē iruntiṭuka" eṉṟu
   kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ iṭavakaṉum tāṉumē aṟinta ōraṭaiyāḷac ceytiyai
   avaṉukkuc colli "uṉṉai nampumpaṭi icceytiyai mutaliṟ colliviṭṭu appāl
   vācavatattaiyai uccaiṉiyiliruntu yāṉ piṭimēlēṟṟip pōntatumutal
   ivviṭattē vantu taṅkiyiruttal iṟutiyāka vuḷḷa ceytikaḷaik kūṟic
   cēṉaikaḷait tokuttukkoṇṭu kālaiyil varuvāyāka" eṉṟu colli avaṉai
   aṉuppiviṭṭu, vācavatattaiyiṉ kāṭciyaiyē at tuṉpak kaṭalaik kaṭattaṟkup
   puṇaiyāka eṇṇik kaṇaiyoṭu tiritaru kāmaṉ pōlat tāṉ ulāvikkoṇṭu, avaḷaik
   kāñcaṉamālaiyōṭu tuyilvittut tāṉ vāḷai valattē ēntip pātukāppavaṉāki
   muṉpuṟattē niṉṟāṉ. niṟkaiyil iravu pularntatu.
   appāl utayaṇaṉ kaṇkaḷaik kaḻuvik kālaikkaṭaṉai muṭittuviṭṭut taṉṉuṭaiya
   taṉimaiyai niṉaintu varuntupavaṉākic cēṉaiyuṭaṉ vayantakaṉ varutalaip
   pārttukkoṇṭē niṉṟaṉaṉ. appoḻutu, kuṭumi neṟṟiyaiyum kūruḷḷiyaṉṉa
   valvāyaiyu muṭaiya vayava ṉeṉṟum paṟavai yoṉṟu ilavamarattiṉ ucciyiṟ
   palamuṟai olittatu. ataṉaikkēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ, "iṉi viraiviṟ pakaippaṭai
   varumeṉpatai itu terivikkiṉṟatē" eṉṟu accaṅkoṇṭavaṉāki ampait tirutti
   villai iṭakkaiyiṟ ṟāṅkikkoṇṭu niṟpāṉāyiṉaṉ. appoḻutu pattirāpati vīḻnta
   malaiccāraṟkāṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷil uṟaipavarkaḷum avvaḻiyē
   cellupavarkaḷai varutti avarkaḷuṭaiya arum paṇṭaṅkaḷaik kavarntu ciṟu
   vilaikku viṟṟuṇpavarkaḷum iṉṉum paṟpala tīcceyalkaḷai
   uṭaiyavarkaḷumākiya cavararum puḷiñarum kūṭiniṉṟu aṭiccuvaṭukaḷai uṟṟu
   nōkkikkoṇṭē vantu yāṉai aṭiccuvaṭṭaik kaṇṭu "iṅkē naṭantu ceṉṟatu
   kāṭṭuppiṭiyaṉṟu; nāṭṭuppiṭiyē " eṉṟu ataṉ utiram vīḻnta vaḻiyē
   ōṭicceṉṟu pārttaṉar. pārkkaiyil, piṭi vīḻntu kiṭattalaiyum, ataṉ ayal
   tōṉṟu makaḷir āṭavarkaḷiṉ kāṟcuvaṭukaḷaiuṅ kavaṉittu "nēṟṟiruḷil
   ivvaḻiyē ceṉṟavarkaḷuṭaiya aṭiccuvaṭukaḷ ivai; iṉi avarkaḷai yām
   kāṇutal aritu." eṉṟu paraparappu aṭaṅki niṟkum poḻutu aṅkē uṇṭāṉa
   puṭkural oṉṟaikkēṭṭa nimittikaṉākiya mutiya vēṭaṉ oruvaṉ avarkaḷai
   ikaḻntu kūṟi "ippoḻutē nām celvōmāyiṉ, perumporuḷai uṭaiya oru
   talaivaṉaik kāṇpōm. mutalil avaṉ nammait tuṉpuṟuttip piṉpu nammuṭaiya
   kaiyil akappaṭuvāṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa avarkaḷ uṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭu,
   "vāṉmarap potumpuṅ kāṉamuṅ kaṭaṟum
       muḻaivaḷar kuṉṟuṅ kaḻaivaḷar kāṉamum
       payampum pāḻiyu miyaṅkuvaṉar vatiyum
       mutumarap pontum putumalarp poykaiyum
       iṉaiyavai piṟavu maṉaiyava ruḷvaḻic
       cerukkaya luṇkaṭ cītaiyait tērvuḻik
       kurakkiṉat taṉṉa parappiṉa rāki" (1:55: 104-10)

   ūṉṟi ūṉṟip pārttukkoṇṭē cellukaiyil utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭaṉar.
   palavakaippaṭṭa olikaḷaiyum eḻuppiṉar. nāṟpuṟamuñ cūḻntu tākkiṉar.
   ataṉaik kaṇṭu naṭuṅkum vācavatattaiyaip pātu kākkumpaṭi
   kāñcaṉamālaiyiṭam colliviṭṭu utayaṇaṉ puṟattē vantu oru kōṅka marattiṉ
   aṭiyai yaṭaintu niṉṟu villai vaḷaittu avarkaḷ mītu ampukaḷait toṭuttut
   toṭuttu mika varuttuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. ataṉaikkaṇṭum avarkaḷ ciṟitum añcāmal
   avaṉai neruṅki "piṭi vīḻntavuṭaṉ yām piḻaikkalāmeṉṟu nī oḷittu vantava
   ṉallaiyō? uṉ uyirai uṇpēm; eṅkē cellukiṉṟāy? nī yāvaṉ? uṇmaiyaic
   colvāyāka" eṉṟu koṭiya moḻikaḷaikkūṟi avaṉmītu ampukaḷait tūviṉar. avaṉ
   ciṟitum añcāṉāki avaṟṟai vilakki avarkaḷ meyyait tuḷaittukkoṇṭu
   pūmiyiṟputaiyumpaṭi ampukaḷai eytaṉaṉ; eykaiyil nāṟpattoṉpatu
   maṟavarkaḷ iṟantaṉar. iṟakkavē accamuṟṟu avarkaḷ āṟṟārāki, "aḻakiya
   vaṭivattōṭu nammaik kolvataṟku immalaiyiṭattē vanta kālaṉō ivaṉ?" eṉṟu
   colli avaṉuṭaiya villukku etirē niṟacṟaṟkum āṟṟal illātavarāki nimittaṅ
   kūṟiya mutiyavaṉaip palavāṟu paḻittuk koṇṭē mīṭṭum utayaṇaṉai vaḷaittu
   niṉṟārkaḷ.
   piṉpu avarkaḷ mēlē ceyvatu iṉṉateṉpatai aṟiyātavarkaḷāki "ivaṉ oruvaṉ;
   yām palarāyiruntum ivaṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭōm. mēlē yām ceyyavēṇṭiyavaṟṟai
   aṟintu kūṟumiṉ" eṉṟu paṟavai nimittaṅ kūṟuvōṉaik kēṭpa, "ilavam
   potumparaic cūḻnta iṭaṅkaḷai nīṅkaḷ tīkku iraiyākkiviṭṭu uḷḷē
   iruppavarkaḷaip puṟappaṭac ceytu tuṉpuṟuttuṅkōḷ" eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟiṉaṉ.
   uṭaṉē ellāp pakkaṅkaḷilum tīyai mūṭṭiviṭṭu oru ciṅkattaip pala narikaḷ
   cūḻntatupōla avarkaḷ utayaṇaṉaic cūḻntu niṉṟu tuṉpuṟuttiṉārkaḷ.
   ataṉaikkaṇṭa vācavatattai mikunta tuyaramaṭaintāḷ. appōtu utayaṇaṉ,
   "kāñcaṉamālāy, ivaḷai aḻaittukkoṇṭu vēṟoru pakkattiṉ vaḻiyē celvāyāka.
   yāṉ iv vaḻiyē ceṉṟu avarkaḷai veṉṟu mīḷvēṉ" eṉṟu colli aṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ
   kukaiyiṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭum pulipōla muḷḷilavamarap potum pariṉiṉṟum
   puṟappaṭṭaṉaṉ. kaṇṭa vēṭarkaḷ, attiyāḷiyaikkaṇṭa yāṉaikaḷpōlavē añci
   nīṅkiṉar. appoḻutu vācavatattaiyai mella naṭattikkoṇṭu utayaṇaṉ
   puṟappaṭuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. avvēṭarkaḷ tirumpavum nāṟpuṟamum vantu nerukki
   avaṉatu viṟpōr aṭaṅkumpaṭi porutu vil nāṇai aṟuttaṉar. aṟukkavē
   yotoṉṟaiyum ceyvataṟku iyalāmaiāl valaiyiṟ cikkuṇṭu akappaṭṭa
   ciṅkampōṉṟu ceytaṟkuriyatu iṉṉateṉpatai aṟiyātavaṉākip pāratappōriṟ
   palavīrarkku iṭaiyil karṇaṉāl nāṇaṟṟuppōṉa villaiyuṭaiyavaṉākit taṉiyē
   niṉṟu pōrceyta apimaṉṉuvaippōlavē utayaṇaṉ avarkaḷ eyyum ampukaḷai
   villālē vilakkikkoṇṭu niṉṟaṉaṉ. ataṉaikkaṇṭu āṟṟāta vaḷāki vācavatattai
   naṭuṅkit tāṉ aṇintirunta āparaṇaṅkaḷaik kaḷaintu, "ivaṟṟai vēṭṭuvar
   kaiyiṟ koṭu" eṉṟu kāñcaṉamālaiyiṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttuviṭṭu vēṭarai nōkki "
   nīṅkaḷ varuttātīrkaḷ" eṉṟu kūṟi aḻutaṉaḷ. kāñcaṉamālai, "ivaṟṟai
   vēṭarkaḷ kaiyiṟ koṭēmāyiṉ mikka koṭuntuṉpam viḷaiyum" eṉṟukūṟi
   āparaṇaṅkaḷai utayaṇaṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttaṉaḷ.
   atukaṇṭavēṭar "nīṅkaḷ yār?" eṉṟu kēṭpa, utayaṇaṉ "vēṭarkaḷē!
   utayaṇaṉuṭaiya vāṇikar yāṅkaḷ. vattavanāṭṭiṉiṉṟum piraccōtaṉaṉatu nāṭu
   ceṉṟu poruḷkaḷai īṭṭi avaṟṟaip piṭimītēṟṟik koṇṭu vantōm; vaḻiyil
   appiṭi vīḻntuviṭavē apporuḷkaḷai iruḷil oru cōlaiyiṭattiṟ putaittu
   aṭaiyāḷam iṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. avaṟṟaip peṟṟukkoḷḷutalil uṅkaḷukku maṉam
   iruppiṉ ikkolaittoḻilai oḻimiṉ, emmuṭaṉ variṉ ap poruḷaik kāṭṭuvōm"
   eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ.
   "utayaṇaṉuṭaiya vāṇikar yāṅkaḷ" eṉṟa collaik kēṭṭa vēṭarkaḷiṉ talaivaṉ
   ciṉantaṇintu taṉvillai maṭakkikkoṇṭu pōrceyyum ēṉaiyōrkaḷaiyum aṭakki
   āyutaṅkaḷait tūrattiṟ pōṭumpaṭi ceytu mēlāṭaiyāl utayaṇaṉatu
   muṉkaiyaikkaṭṭi "ap poruḷaik kāṭṭutaṟku eḻuvāyāka" eṉṟu kūṟa utayaṇaṉ
   anta iṭattaip pārppavaṉpōlavē viraintu ciṟitu tūram ceṉṟu pārttu "nām
   pālai nilattu vaḻiyē vantamaiyāl melivuṟṟut taṅkiya cōlai oṉṟil
   avaṟṟaip putaittōm; nīṅkaḷ mūṭṭiya neruppālum pukaiyālum putaitta
   iṭattaik kāṇa muṭiyavillai. ivvaḻal āṟumaḷavum nīṅkaḷ cummā nilluṅkaḷ"
   eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. avarkaḷ maṉanteḷintu, "aḻal avintapiṉpu nī kāṭṭāyāyiṉ
   uṉṉaik koṉṟuviṭuvōm" eṉṟaṉar.
   ataṉai aṟintu vācavatattai mikavum naṭuṅki aḻutaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ "yām
   putaitta āparaṇaṅkaḷaip peṟutaṟku viruppamiruppiṉ ematu kaikkaṭṭai
   aviḻttu viṭuṅkaḷ; ivaḷuṭaiya tuyarattai nīkkip pukai tīrntavuṭaṉ
   āparaṇaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭuvōm" eṉṟu kūṟa. avarkaḷ "colliyavaṇṇañ ceyyāṉāyiṉ
   ivaṉ piḻaittaṟku vaḻiyillai" eṉṟu collik koṇṭu maiyaṇi yāṉaiyait
   taṭuttut taḻumpiya avaṉ kaikaḷiṉ kaṭṭai aviḻttuviṭṭu avaṉaic cūḻntu
   niṉṟārkaḷ. utayaṇaṉ iṉiya moḻikaḷāl vācavatat taiyait tēṟṟit taṉ
   melliya karattāl taivara avaḷ kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum nīrttuḷikaḷ cintiṉa.
   oruvarai oruvar nōkki avalaṅkoṇṭu niṟpārāyiṉar,.
   ivarkaḷ ippaṭiyirukka, kaṉṟaippirinta kaṟavaipōla utayaṇaṉatu
   pirivāṟṟāmal avaṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyāl iruḷiṟ ceṉṟa vayantakaṉ puṭpakanakarattai
   aṭaintāṉ.
   aṅkē iṭavakaṉ "utayaṇaṉuṭaiya kuṇa mikutikaḷai ellām aṟinta
   piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya tamar, utayaṇaṉ ellāvaṟṟilum ciṟantu mikka
   ceṟukkuṭaiyavaṉāka iruttalāl avaṉai eppaṭiyēṉum aṭakkiviṭa
   vēṇṭumeṉṟeṇṇi uḷḷē āyutaṅkaḷaip parappiya poynnilamoṉṟaik kāṭṭi atil
   vīḻac ceytaṉar" eṉṉum poymoḻiyaic cilar kūṟak kēṭṭu mikunta kavalaiyai
   uṭaiyavaṉāki, " icceyti uṇmaiyāyiṉ yām uyir tuṟappēm" eṉṟu eṇṇi,
   uccaiṉinakar ceṉṟu nikaḻntatai aṟintuvarum āṟṟalai uṭaiya oṟṟaṉ
   oruvaṉai varuvittu avaṉiṭam ac ceytiyaik kūṟi annakarukku aṉupputaṟku
   vantu niṉṟaṉaṉ. antac camayattil, aṅku etirē vanta vayantakaṉai avaṉ
   kaṇṭu, tōḻaṉākiya utayaṇaṉ uyirōṭiruttalai mutalil aṟintu maṉamuvantu,
   mutti ulakattaip peṟṟavaṉ pōla iṉpuṟṟu utayaṇaṉuṭaiya
   caukkiyamutaliyavaṟṟai vicārittut terintukoṇṭu avaṉ vanta kāriyattai
   aṟintu ārāytaṟku avaṉōṭu taṉiyē ceṉṟaṉaṉ; uccaiṉinakarattu nikaḻnta
   nīrviḻaviṟ piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya cōrvai aṟintu vācavatattaiyaip
   piṭimītēṟṟikkoṇṭu iruḷiṟ pōntatu mutaliya ceytikaḷaiyum utayaṇaṉ kūṟiya
   aṭaiyāḷac ceytiyaiyum vayantakaṉ kūṟiṉaṉ. avaṟṟaik kēṭṭa iṭavakaṉ,
   "cēṉaikaḷai amaittu nām viraivil cella vēṇṭum" eṉṟu eḻuntu,
   utayaṇaṉukkum vācavatattaikkum vēṇṭiya ūrtikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ piṉṉē
   varumpaṭi vēlaikkārarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭu vayantakaṉuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭuc
   cēṉaikaḷōṭu ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉirunta muḷḷilavam potumparai aṭaintaṉaṉ.
   aṭaintapoḻutu aṅkē utayaṇaṉuṭaiya ampukaḷāl vīḻnta vēṭarkaḷiṉ
   irattattai uṇṭu maraṅkaḷiṉmītu aṅkaṅkē irunta kākkaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum
   kaḻukiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum pukaippaṭalattaiyuṅ kaṇṭu, "itu nam iṟaivaṉ
   irunta iṭam"; avaṉukku ētō oru tuṉpam nērntatupōlum; yām uyirai,
   viṭutalē nalam" eṉṟu vayantakaṉ pulampiṉaṉ: iṭavakaṉum
   varuntuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. appōtu uṭaṉvanta paṭai vīrar, aṅṅaṉam naṭantirukka
   māṭṭāteṉpatait takka kāraṇattuṭaṉ colli avarkaḷuṭaiya varuttattaip
   pōkki appāṟ ciṟitu tūram aḻaittuc cellukaiyil, varacavatattaiyuṭaṉ
   utayaṇaṉ niṟṟalaiyum vēṭarkaḷ cūḻntu nerukkutalaiyuṅ kaṇṭu ciṟitu
   āṟutaluṟṟaṉar.. kutirai vīrarum yāṉai vīrarum viraintu vantu vēṭarkaḷai
   varuttattoṭaṅkiṉar. appōtu "nīṅkaḷ viraivil ōṭippōmiṉ; pōkīrāyiṉ uṅkaḷ
   uyir ippōtē keṭum eṉṟu oru paṟavai terivikkiṉṟatu" eṉṟu muṉpu
   nimittaṅkūṟiya mutumakaṉ vēṭarkkuk kūṟiṉaṉ. kūṟiyum ataṉai matiyāmal
   tuttarik kompukaḷai ūti muḻakkiṉar. utayaṇaṉ aṅkē vantōr tammavar
   eṉpatai aṟintum ataṉai veḷippaṭuttāmal maṉattil aṭakkikkoṇṭu
   avvēṭarkaḷai nōkki "iṅkē vanta paṭai nummuṭaiyatō piṟaruṭaiyatō?" eṉṟu
   viṉaviṉaṉ. viṉāvavē ippaṭai utayaṇaṉ mantiriyākiya iṭavakaṉ paṭai; nī
   emmōṭu vā; vārāyāyiṉ ippaṭaiyāl tuṉpuṟuvai" eṉṟu tam uruvai
   maṟaittukkoṇṭu cilar pōṉa iṭam teriyātapaṭi ōṭiyē pōyiṉar. cilar
   pullilum putarilum maṟaintu tammaip pātukāttukkoṇṭaṉar. cilarmaṭṭum
   etirt tup pōrceytu niṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉam niṉṟavarkaḷai iṭavakaṉ vīrar
   āyutaṅkaḷāṟ palavāṟu varuttiṉar. varuttavē, avarkaḷum ōṭi viṭṭaṉar.
   tammavar vaḻaṅkum paṭaikaḷum vēṭarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum paṭaikaḷum taṅkaḷ mēlē
   paṭātapaṭi utayaṇaṉ pātukāttuk koṇṭē aṅku niṉṟaṉaṉ. iṅṅaṉam
   kuṟavarkaḷai veṉṟa paṭaivīrar, tiṅkaḷaic cūḻnta viṇmīṉpōla utayaṇaṉaic
   cūḻntu pātukāttu niṉṟaṉar, utayaṇaṉ mukamalarcciyuṭaṉ vācavatattaiyōṭu
   appaṭaiyiṭaiyē viḷaṅki niṉṟāṉ.
   appōtu, vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum arukē vantu utayaṇaṉaip pārāṭṭiṉārkaḷ.
   avarkaḷ paṇitta ēvalkaḷaik kēṭṭu iyaṟṟik koṇṭē paṭaivīrar cūḻntu
   niṉṟārkaḷ. avviruvaraiyum nōkki utayaṇaṉ "tuṉpamākiya peruṅkaṭalaik
   kaṭattaṟku nīvir perum puṇaiyāyiṉīr" eṉṟu aṉpuṭaṉ pārāṭṭiṉaṉ. appoḻutu
   cayanti nakarattaic cārnta malaiccāraliṟ palavakaiyiṉarum taṉit taṉiyē
   taṅkutaṟkēṟṟa koṭṭilkaḷ amainta mika vicālamāṉa pāṭi nakaramoṉṟu
   amaikkappeṟṟatu. utayaṇaṉukkum vācavatattaikkum uriya paṭamāṭaṅkaḷ atil
   amaikkappeṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟil palavakaip poruḷkaḷum āyattañceytu niṟaittu
   vaikkapṭṭaṉa. utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyuṭaṉ vantu appaṭaivīṭṭil taṅki
   iṉpuṟṟiruntaṉaṉ.
   vācavatattaikku amaintaṉavākiya pāvai, muṟṟil, vēyṅkuḻal, poṟ kavaṟu,
   paḷikku nāy, cantaṉap palakai, cantaṉap pēḻai, cāntarai ammi, akiṟ
   pukaittuḷaiyakal, cikkam, kōtaicceppu, kiḷi, mayil, pūvai, poṟkaraṇṭam,
   aṇikalap pēḻai, maṇik kaṇṇāṭi, maṇiviḷakku, mayirviṉaittavicu,
   paṭṭamaḷi, ālavaṭṭam, cāntāṟṟi, mālaippantu, mutaliyavaṟṟai ēntiya
   maṅkaiyar orupāl niṉṟaṉar. utayaṇaṉukku amaintaṉavākiya parimā, yāṉai,
   tēr, koṟṟakkuṭai, vēl, koṭi, kavari, muracu, caṅkam, paṭavam, vitāṉam,
   ciṅkātaṉam, poṅku pūntavicu, mutaliyavaṟṟaiyuṭaiya varkaḷāyc cilatarum,
   iyavarum, cintutēcattuḷḷa palavakaip pāṣaimākkaḷum orupāl niṉṟaṉar.
   kaik kōl iḷaiñarum, kaṇakkarum, meykāppāḷarum, vīrarum, piṟarum
   puṟṟiliruntu puṟappaṭum īyaṟ kaṇam pōl vantu kūṭic cūḻntu niṉṟaṉar.
   nāḻikaik kaṇakkar vanta uṇṇutaṟkuriya kālattait terivikka, iruḷiṟ
   piṭiyai ūrntu vanta nalivālum, tuyil koḷḷāta melivālum, vēṭarkaḷōṭu
   pōrceyta tuṉpattālum, pala poḻutu uṇṇāta paci mikutiyālum mikka
   varuttamuṟṟirunta utayaṇaṉ vallavaṉ vakutta vāca eṇṇey pūcik kāppuṭai
   naṟu nīrāṭi, maruttuvar vakutta arumpeṟalaṭicilait tōḻarōṭu uṇṭu
   makiḻntāṉ.
   vaṇṇa makaḷir mutaliyōr vantu mika upacarittu nīrāṭṭi alaṅkarittu
   vācavatattaiyai uṇpittārkaḷ. avaḷum mikka āṟutalaiyaṭaintu makiḻntāḷ.
   paṭamāṭaṅkaḷiṉ varicaikaḷālum yāṉaikaḷiṉ perumuḻakkattālum
   alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya pāṟkaṭalaiyum palavakaiyāṉa iṉpa nukarccikaḷāl uttara
   kuruvaiyum pōṉṟu apputunakar ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
   appoḻutu cayantinakarañ cellutaṟku yāṉai oṉṟai alaṅkarittuk koṇarntu
   niṟuttiṉar. utayaṇaṉ mēkattiṉmēlē tōṉṟum parutiyañ celvaṉpōla ataṉai
   ūrntu viḷaṅkiṉaṉ. pūrṇacantiraṉaippōlē veṇkoṟṟak kuṭai niḻaṟṟiyatu;
   tōḻar kaḷiṟukaḷilēṟi avaṉ irupuṟattuñ ceṉṟaṉar. kaṭuṅkaṭkāvalar
   caṭṭaiyiṭṭu vāḷaiyēntip pātukāttuk koṇṭu vantārkaḷ. yāṉaikaḷum
   kutiraikaḷum kālāṭpaṭaikaḷum cūḻntu ceṉṟaṉa. potiyiṟcantaṉam
   vintamalaiyiluḷḷa yāṉaittantam, vaṭamalaippoṉ, mēlkaṭaṟ koṭippavaḻam,
   koṟkaittuṟai muttam, viñcaiyamperumalai veḷḷi, ilaṅkaiyīḻattuc ceppu,
   imayattu vayiram, kaṭārattu irumpu ākiya ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu,
   'yavaṉat taccaru mavantik kollarum
       makatattup piṟanta maṇiviṉaik kārarum
       pāṭaliṟ piṟanta pacumpoṉ viṉaiñarum
       kōcalat tiyaṉṟa vōviyat toḻilarum
       vatta nāṭṭu vaṇṇak kammarum' (1.58:40-44)

   tattam kaittoḻil tiṟamai pulappaṭumpaṭi iyaṟṟiya āram mutaliya
   uṟuppukkaḷaiyum, nāṇmīṉ kōṇmīṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ eḻucci attamayaṅkaḷaip
   pulappaṭuttum poṟimaṇṭalam amainta uḷḷiṭattaiyum, cāmaraiyiraṭṭal
   mutaliyavaṟṟaic ceyyum entirap pāvaikaḷ pala niṟkum pakkaṅkaḷaiyum,
   piṉṉum paṟpala vicittira vēlaippāṭukaḷaiyum uṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkum oru
   tēriṉmītu kāñcaṉa mālaiyiṉ tōḷaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu vācavatattai ēṟi
   intiraṉuṭaiya celvakkumāripōl viḷaṅkip piṉṉē varuvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
   pututtuṇaimakaḷir avaḷuṭaiya aṭiyīṭukaḷai ēttiṉar. kōṟkaik kāvalāḷar
   pakkattē pātukāttu vantārkaḷ. palavakaik koṭikaḷ acaintu tōṉṟiṉa.
   malaiyil etiroli uṇṭakumpaṭi muracaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkiṉa.
   appōtu malaiyaic cārnta ciṟṟūrkaḷil vāḻuṅ kuṟumparkaḷ yāṉaikkompu,
   malaittēṉ, palākkaṉi mutaliya kaṉikaḷ, vīṇattaṇṭu, mūṅkil muttu, akil,
   paṉṟiyiṉ eṟi, pulittōl, ūkam, piṇai mutliya kāṇikkaip poruḷkaḷai
   ēntikkoṇṭu vantu aracaṉ muṉṉē vaittu vaṇaṅki, "aracē!
   aṟṟa kālattu muṟṟa nōkki
       aṭiyuṟai ceytoḻiṟ kuṭimutal piḻaittal
       irunilam peyariṉu memmāṭṭil" (1.58: 90-92)

   eṉṟu paṇivuṭaṉ moḻintu paṭaikaḷōṭu kūṭittāmum pātukāttu vantaṉar.
   milaiccamaṉṉar muṟpaṭaiyilum, vīrar piṟpaṭaiyilum kāval ceytukoṇṭu
   vantārkaḷ. palarum iṅṅaṉamē paṇiceytuvara iṭaiyilē uḷḷa
   ciṟṟaraṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya ūrkaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṭantu utayaṇaṉ
   cayantiyampatiyai aṭaintaṉaṉ.
   -----------------
                     ___________________________________

                         2. ilāvāṇa kāṇṭam

   utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyuṭaṉ varutalaik kēṭṭa cayanti nakarattār mikunta
   makiḻcciyaṭaintu vītikaḷ tōṟum naṭaikkāvaṇamiṭṭuk kīḻē pūkkaḷaip
   parappip paḻukkulaikkamuku, kulaivāḻai, karumpu mutaliyavaṟṟai iraṇṭu
   pakkaṅkaḷil muṟaiyē kaṭṭi mutta mālaikaḷaiyum pavaḻamālaikaḷaiyum
   aṅkaṅkē nāṟṟi vāyiltōṟum tōraṇa kampaṅkaḷai nāṭṭip peruṅkoṭikaḷaiyum
   ciṟu koṭikaḷaiyuṅ kaṭṭip piṉṉum palavakaiyāṉa alaṅkāraṅkaḷaiyuñ
   ceytaṉar. ceytu, ventuya 1raruviṉai vīṭṭiya 2 aṇṇalai intiravulakam
   etir koṇṭāṟpōla, avarkaḷ utayaṇaṉai etir koṇṭārkaḷ. muracaṅkaḷ
   muḻaṅkiṉa; palavakaiyāṉa vāttiyaṅkaḷ olittaṉa. makaḷirum maintarum
   vāyilkaḷil vantu niṉṟu avaṉaiyum vācavatattaiyaiyuṅ kaṇṭu kaṇkuḷirntu
   maṉaṅkaḷittu, "eṅkaḷuṭaiya tuṉpamākiya iruḷaip pōkkutaṟkuc
   cūriyaṉpōlavē tōṉṟiya poṅkumalart tārōy! pukuka" eṉpōrum,
   "teyvayāṉaiyaip peṟavēṇṭic cēṉaiyiṉṟit taṉiyē pōyp pakaivaṉāṟ
   paṟṟappaṭṭu uccaiṉi ceṉṟu veṉṟu appakaivaṉuṭaiya celvap putalviyaik
   kaippaṟṟi aḻaittukkoṇṭu vanta āṇ ciṅkamē! varuka" eṉpōrum,
   --------
   1.aruviṉai - kātikarmam. 2. aṇṇal - arukaṉ.
   "nī illāta camayam pārttuk kaucāmpi nakarattaik kaippaṟṟiya āruṇi
   aracaṉai veṉṟu putalvarkaḷākiya eṅkaḷaip pātukāttuc ceṅkōl
   celuttikkoṇṭu neṭuṅkālam vāḻ vāyāka" eṉpōrum, "tirumāl mārpaic cārntu
   iṉpuṟṟirukkun tirumakaḷpōla eṅkaḷ aracaṉaic cārntu iṉpuṟutaṟku
   muṟpiṟappil tavañceyta puṇṇiyap pāvaiyē! eṅkaḷaip pātukāttaruḷvāyāka"
   eṉpōrum, " centāmaraik kaṇṇaṉākiya nammaracaṉuṭaṉ piraccōtaṉac
   cakkaravarttiyiṉ arumaip putalviyākiya vācavatattai makiḻntu varutalaik
   kaṇṭōm; yāmē mikka puṇṇiyamuṭaiyōm; ippiṟappil iṉi yām aṭaiya vēṇṭiya
   payaṉ yātuḷatu?" eṉpōrum, "piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya peṇkaḷ palaruḷ
   vācavatattaiyeṉpavaḷ pēraḻakiṉaḷeṉṟum kāṇutaṟku ariyaḷeṉṟum collak
   kēṭṭiruntatuṇṭu; kaṇṭatillai; antak kaṭṭaḻakiyai nām eḷitiṟ kaṇṭu
   kaṇkaḷ peṟṟa payaṉai aṭaiyumpaṭi aḻaittuvantu namakkuk kāṭṭiya
   maṉṉarmaṉṉaṉ maṉṉuka" eṉpōrum " pirac cōtaṉaṉ kāṭṭil māyayāṉaiyaik
   kāṭṭi vañcittu nam perumāṉaip piṭippittut taṉ nakarkku varuvittuc
   ciṟaippaṭuttiya ceytiyaik kēṭṭut tāṉ iṟantu viṭṭatāka yāvaraiyum
   niṉaikkac ceytuviṭṭu māṟu vēṭam pūṇṭu uccaiṉiyiṟ pukuntu
   ceytaṟkariyavaṟṟaic ceytu taṉ talaivaṉaic ciṟaimīṭṭup pakaimaṉṉaṉuṭaiya
   naṅkaiyaik kaikkoṇṭu celka eṉṟu collip pōyvarukaveṉṟu aṉuppiya
   pērāṟṟalaiyuṭaiya yūki ivvulakil nīṭūḻi vāḻvāṉāka" eṉpōrum, "nam
   aracaṉukku vanta peruntuṉpam muṭivil vācavatattaiyai avaṉ aṭaiyumpaṭic
   ceytataṉṟi nammuṭaiya tuṉpattaiyun tīrttuviṭṭatu. ātalāl, itu mikavum
   viyappait tarukiṉṟatu" eṉṟu avaṉatu puṇṇiyattaip pukaḻvōrum, "yāḻil
   mikka tērcciyuṟṟut tarukki iṇaiyaṟṟu viḷaṅkum utayaṇaṉukku
   vācavatattaiyai maṇañ ceytukoṭukka eṇṇiya piraccōtaṉaṉ, ' nam makaḷai
   maṇañ ceytukoḷḷumpaṭi irappōmāyiṉ avaṉ ceytu koḷḷa māṭṭāṉ; ivaḷai
   avaṉpāl aṉuppi avaṉukku maṇañceyvippōmāyiṉ, kulattiṟ ciṟiyavaṉeṉṟu
   nammai ulakam ikaḻum’ eṉṟu nāṇi, yāṉai māyattāl nam cēṉaikkiḻavaṉaip
   piṭippittuc ciṟaippaṭutti vācavatattaikku yāḻ kaṟpikkum vaṇṇañ ceytu
   avaḷuṭaiya aḻakil īṭupaṭumpaṭi paṇṇi avaṉuṭaṉ avaḷai aṉuppiṉaṉ.
   makaḷiraik koṭup pōruṭaiya kuṟippu ituvākum" eṉpōrum, tattamakkut
   tōṟṟiyavāṟē iṉṉum ivaipōṉṟa palavaṟṟaic collupavarkaḷumāy aṅkaṅkē
   niṟka, utayaṇaṉ amarāpatiyiṟ pukum intiraṉ pōlavē cayanti nakarattiṟ
   pukuntu taṉṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyai aṭaintu vīṟṟiruntu viḷaṅkuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   irukkum nāṭkaḷilē oru nāḷ, kuṭai, ācaṉattiruttal, ceruppoṭu pukutal,
   cēṉaiyeḻucci, yāṉai, tāṉai mutaliya mariyātaikaḷait taṉṉuṭaiya kalvip
   perumaiyāṟ peṟṟavaṉākiya peruṅkaṇiyāṉavaṉ vantu maṇañ ceytaṟkuriya
   nalla nāḷait terivittaṉaṉ. terivikkavē tirunāḷ, paṭaināḷ, maṇanāḷeṉṉum
   nāṭkaḷilaṉṟi maṟṟai nāṭkaḷil muracaṟaiyāta vaḷḷuva mutumakaṉ, veṇcāntu
   pūci veṇmalar mālaikaḷai aṇintu ventukil uṭuttuc ceṉṟu, poṉvār vicittu
   muttamālai malarmālaikaḷai aṇintu ēṟṟuri pōrttappeṟṟatākiya
   aracaṉuṭaiya koṟṟa muracattaip perumpaṇaik koṭṭiliṟ pūcittuk
   koṟṟavaiyait tutittu ataṉai yāṉaiyiṉatu paṇai eruttattil ēṟṟip palar
   taṉṉaic cūḻntu varat tāṉ anta yāṉaiyiṉ mutukiluḷḷa aṇaiyil iruntukoṇṭu,
   tērōṭum vītikaṭōṟum ceṉṟu nakarattārellārum aṟiyumpaṭi, "aracaṉ vēl
   veṟṟipeṟṟu viḷaṅkuka! pūmakaḷ puṇarka: maṇmakaḷ malika; nakarattīr! nāṉ
   kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaik kēḷuṅkaḷ: pāvacceyalkaḷai nīkkit tiruvaiyuṭaiyīrāyp
   pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkuṅkaḷ; piraccōtaṉa cakkaravarttiyiṉ kulamakaḷākiya
   vācavatattaiyai nammuṭaiya aracarpirāṉ maṇañceyyum nāḷ iṉṉatākum" eṉṟu
   kuṟuntaṭiyāl ammuracai aṟaintu terivittaṉaṉ. kēṭṭa nakarattārkaḷ
   makiḻntu atika viruppattuṭaṉ tattam vāyilkaṭōṟum tōraṇaṅkaḷai nāṭṭi
   mikavum vicālamāṉa pantarkaḷaip pōṭuvittu avaṟṟiṉ akattum puṟattum
   vicittiramākiya alaṅkāraṅkaḷaic ceyvittaṉar.
   palavakaiyāṉa paḻaṅkaḷum veṟṟilaiyum palavakaip pākkukkaḷum nirampiya
   peṭṭikaḷaiyum cuṇṇap peruṅ kuṭaṅkaḷaiyum tam pakkattil vaittukkoṇṭu
   vantōrkku varaiviṉṟi avaṟṟaik koṭuppōr muḻakkamum, muraṭkōliḷaiyarkaḷiṉ
   kāval cūḻap peṟṟuk kīḻē veṇmaṇal parappiya taṇṇiḻaṟ pantaraic
   cuṟṟuppakkattiluṭaiyaṉavum maṭaittoḻil vaḻuvāta vāḻkkaiyar
   payiṉṟaṉavumākiya aṟaccōṟṟaṭṭilkaḷiṉ akattum puṟattum antaṇāḷarkkum
   allāta piṟarkkum amutiṉaṉṉa aṭicilkaḷaiyum neyyālākkiya
   ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷaiyum eppōtu virumpiṉālum appōtē aḷittalait tavaṟiṉṟic
   ceyvōr muḻakkamum, koṭuppavarkaḷ vīḻttiya kuṅkumak kaḷiyum malarkaḷum
   vētiyarkaḷiṉ vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷiṟ cintiya camitaikaḷum, vācaṉaipporuḷkaḷait
   tokuppōrkaḷiṉ vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷiṟ cintiya vācaccuṇṇamum, pulanta makaḷir
   eṟinta malarmālaikaḷum, ciṟār vīḻttiya cempoṟ kaṇṇikaḷum, vītikaḷiṉ
   teyvaṅkaḷukkuk koṭukkappaṭum palikaḷum tampalamumākiya kuppaikaḷait
   tūttuc cantaṉak kuḻampāl tukaḷai avittuppōtu pala parappiya vāyilkaḷiṟ
   ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷa alaṅkāraṅkaḷai aḻittuviṭṭa ciṟu piḷḷaikaḷaip
   piṭittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya kuṟṟacceyalkaḷait tāymārkaḷukku
   aṟivuṟuttum ēvaṉ makaḷiruṭaiya muḻakkamum, cakkara vaṭivattiṉavākiya
   āṟu entiraṅkaḷ oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu māṟāy niṉṟu nīraic coriyāniṟkac cantirātitta
   mutaliya mukkuṭaikaḷ entirattāṟ puṭaipeyarntu acaiyāniṟka viḷaṅkum
   ciṉālayaṅkaḷil tīrttaṅkararkaḷuṭaiya carittiraṅkaḷai viḷaṅka
   eṭutturaittup periyōrkaḷ arukaṉaip pūcikkum muḻakkamum, kaṇṇāṟ
   kaṇṭavaṟṟaik kaiyāṟ ceyyum nūlviṉaik kammiyar paṉaiyōlai, neṭṭi, tukil
   mutaliyavaṟṟāl nāṟṟam tōṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟitum vēṟṟumaiyillātapaṭi iyaṟṟiya
   ilai, koḻuntu, mukiḻ, pōtu, malar eṉ pavaṟṟaiyuṭaiya palavakai
   maraṅkaḷai aṅkaṅkē pantark kāltōṟuṅ kaṭṭi, appaṭiyē muṉcuvarkaḷil
   ōviyaṅkaḷait tīṭṭik 1kālattaip pulappaṭuttum entiraṅkaḷai uriya
   iṭaṅkaḷil amaittup pārttavar kaṇkaḷai mīḷaviṭātapaṭi piṉṉum
   ceyyavēṇṭiya alaṅkāraṅkaḷaiyellām aṅkaṅkē ceypavarkaḷiṭattuṇṭākiya
   muḻakkamum, aracaṉuṭaiya vēlaikkārarkaḷil mutiyavarkaḷ palar
   nāṟpuṟattumuḷḷa kaṭaivītikaṭōṟum vantu, "kirīṭamallāta peruṅkalaṅkaḷuḷ
   evaṟṟai yāvar vēṇṭiṉālum uṭaṉē aṉpuṭaṉ avaṟṟaik koṭuttuviṭalām.
   ----------
       1kālēntiram.

   koṭuttavaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭu maṭaṅku aracaṉ piṉpu makiḻntu aḷippatu tiṇṇam"
   eṉru vāṇikarkaḷukkuc collum perumuḻakkamum, alaṅkāramiṉṟic cummā ōṭi
   viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟa tammuṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷai aḻaittu, "entaimīr! iṅkē varuka;
   nam aracarpirāṉuṭaiya kaliyāṇa maṇṭapattiṟkup putiyavaṟṟai aṇintu num
   tantaimārkaḷōṭu celluṅkaḷ" eṉṟu colli nalla uṭaikaḷai uṭutti
   avarkaḷuṭaiya ucci mutal pātamvaraiyil aṇiya vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aṇintu
   taḻuvi muttamaḷittu avarkaḷai aṉuppum arivaimārkaḷiṉ muḻakkamum,
   uṇṭavar kaiviṭa muṭiyāta palavakaippaṭṭa pāṉavakaikaḷait tam
   illaṅkaḷtōṟum tokuttuvaittu, "vaḷḷattiṟ peyyuṅkaḷ; piṟarkkuk koṭuṅkaḷ;
   tāruṅkaḷ" eṉṟu mikka ūkkattōṭu kūṟuṅ kaḷḷuṇṇāḷaruṭaiya peru muḻakkamum,
   pakaivarai veṉṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya kuṭarai mālaiyākac cūṭuvaṉavum, mūttōr
   peṇṭir tuṟaviyar pālareṉpavarkaḷukku oru poḻutum tuṉpañ ceyyātaṉavum
   uttama ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uṭaiyaṉavumākiya āyiratteṭṭu yāṉaikaḷai alaṅkarittu
   aracaṉ āṭutaṟkuriya maṇṇunīr koṇartaṟkāka avaṟṟaic celuttukiṉṟa
   pākarkaḷuṭaiya perumuḻakkamum, mikka aḻakaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷum īṉṟōraiyum
   etirnōkkātavarkaḷumākiya kaṉṉi makaḷiruḷ, "maṇṇunīr cumattaṟkuriya
   puṇṇiyamuṭaiyavarkaḷē! vāruṅkaḷ" eṉṟu etirkoḷḷum kōyiṉmakaḷirkaḷiṉ
   pāṭakavōcaiyum, ivaipōṉṟa pallāyira ōcaikaḷum tammuḷ kalakkappaṭṭu
   nakar kaṭimaṇac celvattāl viḷaṅkiyatu. kalyāṇattiṟku vēṇṭiya ellām
   amaikkap peṟṟaṉa. appoḻutu cōtiṭar vantu mukūrttavēḷaiyait
   terivittaṉar. aṟutoḻil, muttī aruntuṟai pōkiya maṟainavil
   nāviṉaiyuṭaiya purōkitaṉ vantu maṇappantaril utayaṇaṉaiyum
   vācavatattaiyaiyum ācaṉattil irukkac ceytu etiril akkiṉiyaivaḷarttu
   uriya poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu ākutipaṇṇit ticaikaṭōṟum uriya teyvaṅkaḷaip
   piratiṭṭaiceytu pūcittu, nivētittu, ammiyēṟṟutal mutaliya vicēṭaṅkaḷai
   nikaḻtti, "naṉṉilaiyulaki ṉāval pōlavum, poṉṉaṇi neṭumalai pōlavum
   maṉṉuka ivar" eṉa vāḻttiṉaṉ. piṉṉar vācavatattaiyiṉ muṉkaiyaip paṟṟik
   koṇṭu utayaṇaṉ tīyai valam vantaṉaṉ.aruntati kāṭṭal mutalākiya maṟṟac
   caṭaṅkukaḷum vitippaṭi niṟaivēṟiyapiṉ iruvaraiyum kaṭṭilēṟṟiṉar.
   appāl āṟāntiṅkaḷiṟ peruṅkaṇic caṅkattārum, tiṇaikaḷum kaṇakkarum,
   piṟarum vantu uṭalmayir kaḷaitalākiya vicēṭattaiyum vitippaṭi
   nikaḻttiṉar.
   piṉpu, utayaṇaṉukku maṇṇunīr āṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭu aimperuṅkuḻuvum,
   eṇpērāyamum uṟutic cuṟṟattārum, piṟarum pakkaṅkaḷil neruṅki vārāniṟka,
   āyiratteṭṭu yāṉaikaḷiṉ mēl kuṭaikaḷ kavippa āyiratteṭṭup poṟkuṭaṅkaḷ
   ceṉṟaṉa. akkuṭaṅkaḷai iṟukap piṭittukkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ irupuṟattum kaṉṉi
   makaḷir iruntārkaḷ. muḻavum piṟa vāttiyaṅkaḷum muḻaṅkiṉa; muracaṅkaḷ
   cilaittaṉa; caṅkaṅkaḷ olittaṉa; ciṟu koṭikaḷum peruṅkoṭikaḷum uyarntu
   acaintu viḷaṅkiṉa; mēṟkaṭṭikaḷiṉ kīḻē patiṉāṟu maṅkalap poruḷkaḷai
   ēntikkoṇṭu maṅkala makaḷir muṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar; vēl vāḷ kōl ivaṟṟaik kaiyiṟ
   koṇṭa kāvaliḷaiyar pātukāttuc ceṉṟaṉar; intac ciṟappuṭaṉ ceṉṟu oru
   vāviyil nīrai mukantu koṇarntu, utayaṇaṉuṭaiya iṭappakkattil
   vācavatattaiyai vaittu iruvarukkum vitippaṭi nīrāṭṭi alaṅkarittaṉar.
   piṉ nikaḻtta vēṇṭiyavai muṟaippaṭi naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. appāl iruvarum iṉiya
   uṇavukaḷai uṇṭu aṉpu mikkavarkaḷāki iṉpuṟuvārāyiṉar.
   akkālattil aṟiñar cilar utayaṇaṉai aṭaintu "tēvālayattaiyum
   nakarattaiyum valañceytal vivākattiṟkuriya vitiyākum" eṉru kūṟa,
   utayaṇaṉ ataṉai aṅkīkarittu muṟaippaṭi vācavatattaiyōṭu kōyilai aṭaintu
   mukkuṭaikkīḻ viḷaṅkum aruntavak kiḻavaṉākiya arukatēvaṉait taricittut
   taṉṉuṭaiya pirārttaṉaiyai niṟaivēṟṟikkoṇṭu puṟattē pōntu mikka
   vaipavattuṭaṉ nakarvalañ ceytaṉaṉ. ceyta poḻutu, avaṉuṭaiya putumaṇak
   kōlattaiyum vācavatattaiyaiyum ulācciṟappaiyum pārttaṟku nakarattuḷḷa
   irupālārum vāyil māṭaṅkaḷiṉmītum kaṭṭumalaikaḷiṉmītum, nakarkāṇ
   ēṇiyiṉmītum, ēṟik kaṇṭukaḷittārkaḷ. ataṉpiṉ avviruvarum araṇmaṉaiyai
   aṭaintu oruvarai oruvar viṭṭuppiriyāmal mikka kātalai uṭaiyavarkaḷāki
   oppaṟṟa iṉpamaṭaintu vāḻvārayiṉar.
   avviruvarum iṅṅaṉam vāḻntu varukaiyil, pukaḻiṉ perumaiyaiyum vēṟu
   vēṟiṭattup piṟanta āṉpālum tēṉum kalantāṟpōlat tōḻar vēṟupaṭṭēkiṉum
   kūṟupaṭṭiyalāta aṉpumikka naṇpiṉ amaitiyaiyum irāca kāriyaṅkaḷil
   taḷumpupaṭṭa amaicciṉ āṟṟalaiyum ivaipōṉṟa piṟa kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum taṉpāl
   tāṅki uccaiṉiyil ellāvaṟṟilum veṟṟipeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya yūki, utayaṇaṉ
   ciṟaippaṭṭuc celvattai nīttaṉa ṉeṉṟu niṉaiyāmal, piraccōtaṉaṉ taṉ makaḷ
   vācavatattaiyai avaṉukku aṉpōṭu koṭuttamaiyālētāṉ avaṉ
   piṭimēlēṟṟikkoṇṭu taṉ nakar ceṉṟaṉaṉ eṉpataiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum,
   'kōyiṉ muṟṟattum vāyiṉ maruṅkiṉum
       vampalar moytta vampalat takattum
       yāṉait tāṉattu maruntavap paḷḷiyum
       tāṉaic cēriyun tāṉeṭut turaikkum
       pāṭai yaṟiyāt tēcikac cēriyum
       ōtunar cālai yakattu mōvāc
       cūtuporu kaḻakat tarukalun tōmil
       nallatun tīyatu maṟintakat taṭakkā
       maṭṭumakiḻ makaḷir tuṭṭac cēriyum
       kārikai pakaruṅ karuṅkaṭai maḻaikkaṇ
       vārkoṭi makaḷir vaḷanakar varaippiṉum
       kutiraip pantiyuṅ kōṭikar varaippiṉum
       matimayak kuṟūu maṟukaṇi kaṭaiyiṉum
       nīrttuṟaik karaiyilum kūttuṟai cēriyum
       maṉṟuñ cantiyum’ (2.8 : 56-70)

   ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ pēcuvittu nakarattārellārum aṟiyumpaṭi paṇṇi, aṅkē
   uḷḷavarkaḷ pēcuvaṉavaṟṟaiyellāṅ kēṭṭu, mākaḷavaṉattiṟ kāḷi kōyilil
   irunta taṉ tōḻarkaḷaiyum vīrarkaḷaiyum piṟaraiyum ōriṭattiṟ kūṭṭi
   mukamaṉ kūṟi, "innakar vaṟumai aṭaiyumpaṭi iṉi nīṅkaḷ peṟuvaḻiyē
   taṉittaṉiyākac ceṉṟu viraivil nam maṉṉaṉai aṭaiyumiṉ" eṉṟu colli
   avarkaḷaip puṟappaṭac ceytāṉ.
   appāl avaṉ ākavēṇṭiyavaṟṟai nāṭiṉaṉ. nāṭi, tuṟantōrkaḷiṉ vēṭam pūṇṭu
   nakarattāruṭaiya maṉaṅkaḷaik kavarntu kāriyaṅkaḷai muṭippavarkaḷāyk
   kirāmaṅkaḷilirukkum taṉ tōḻarkaḷaik kaṇṭu aṅkuḷḷa muṉivar
   terintukoḷḷātapaṭi vēṟṟōṉpōlat taṉṉaik kāṭṭic colla vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic
   colli avarkaḷai utayaṇaṉpāṟ cellumpaṭi aṉuppiṉāṉ. aṉuppiyapiṉ, āṇṭuḷḷa
   muṉivarkaḷiṭam camaya vikaṟpaṅkaḷaiyum nītikaḷaiyum pakaṟkāla muḻuvatum
   pēcikkoṇṭē iruntuviṭṭu iraviṟ puṟappaṭṭut taṉṉuṭaṉ muṉṉamē vantu āka
   vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaip piṟaraṟiyāmaṟ ceytukoṇṭirunta cātakaṉeṉṉuṅ kuya makaṉatu
   illattai aṭaintu aṅkē irunta cāṅkiyat tāyaik kaṇṭu nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyum
   nikaḻtta vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum avaḷukkuk kūṟip paciyaiyum veppattaiyum
   pōkkum maruntoṭu kalanta aval vaittuḷḷa kiḻiyaiyum taṇṇīrk
   karakattaiyum kaikkoṇṭu puṟappaṭutaṟku āyattamāka irukkumpaṭi avaḷukkuc
   coṉṉāṉ.
   piṉṉar avaṉ, utayaṇaṉuṭaiya naṇpaṉāṉayavaṉappāṭit talaivaṉai aṭaintu
   colla vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum taṉ puṟappāṭṭaiyum avaṉukkuc colli avaṉ
   koṭutta yantira vaṇṭiyākiya pūṇiyiṉṟiyum poṟiyiṉ iyaṅkum vaiyattaik
   kaikkoṇṭu, "īṇṭu eñciyuḷḷa tōḻarkaḷaiyellām puṟappaṭac ceytuviṭṭē nī
   puṟappaṭavēṇṭum" eṉpatai avaṉukkuc collit taṉakku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaiyellām
   av vaṇṭiyil vaittukkoṇṭu cāṅkiyat tāyaiyum atil ēṟṟi atai ūrntu
   puṟappaṭṭup puṟanakar kaṭantu pūmiyil vimāṉamēṟic cellum intirakumaraṉ
   pōlap puṭ paka nakarattainōkkic ceṉṟāṉ. appōtu piṟar aṟiyāmal
   taṅkavēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkic ceṉṟu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatellaiyil anta
   vaṇṭiyai niṟutta vēṇṭiya muṟaippaṭiyē niṟuttic cāṅkiyattāyai oru
   pātukāppāṉa iṭattil iṟakkip piṉṉē varumpaṭi colli viṭṭut tāṉ māttiram
   puṭpaka nakarattaik iravil aṭaintu oruvarum terintukoḷḷātapaṭi taṉ
   vaṇṭiyaip pirittu orupāl vaittuviṭṭu iṭavakaṉaik kaṇṭu maṉṉavaṉuṭaiya
   caukkiyattaik kēṭṭut taṉṉuṭaiya iḷaippukaḷaiyellām aṅkē iruḷiṟ
   pōkkiviṭṭu mēlē nikaḻtta vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai yōcittaṟku eṇṇi ventiṟaṉ
   milaiccar vantōrai vilakkikkoṇṭu pātu kāttirukkum mantiramāṭattil
   avaṉuṭaṉ maṟaintiruntu, yāṉai māyattāṟ piṭiyuṇṭu ciṟaiyil akappaṭṭa
   utayaṇaṉai viṭuttal vēṇṭit tāṉ iṟantu viṭṭatākap peyar paṇṇi uccaiṉiyai
   aṭaintatu mutaliya ceytikaḷai yellām avaṉukkuk kūṟiṉaṉ; kēṭṭa iṭavakaṉ,
   "keṭṭa kālai viṭṭaṉa reṉṉātu
       naṭṭō reṉpatu nāṭṭiṉai naṉṟu". (2.9:118-9)

   eṉap pārāṭṭiṉaṉ.
   piṉṉar yūki, utayaṇaṉ nīrviḻaviṉ kaṇṇē vācavatattaiyaip piṭimītēṟṟi
   vanta piṉpu nikaḻnta ceytikaḷaic col" eṉṟu kēṭpa iṭavakaṉ, "
   pattirāpati ainnūṟellaiyaḷavu ōṭip pālai nilattiṉ iṭaiyilē uyir
   tuṟantatu; utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōr naṭantu vantu pakaṟkālam nīṅkum varaiyil
   oru muḷḷilamarap potumparil maṟaintu taṅkiṉar. iraviṟ paṭaikaḷait
   tokuttuc celluvataṟku vayantakaṉ vantaṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyaip
   pātukāttukkoṇṭu taṉiyē aṅkiruntaṉaṉ. irukkaiyil cūriyaṉ utittapiṉpu ak
   kāṭṭiluḷḷa cavarar, puḷiñarkaḷ ivarkaḷuṭaiya aṭic cuvaṭukaḷaiyum
   piṭiyiṉ aṭiccuvaṭukaḷaiyum pāttuk koṇṭē vantu utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu cūḻntu
   pōr ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. avarkaḷaik kaṇṭu varuntiya vācavatattaiyai
   utayaṇaṉ 'añcaṟka" eṉṟu collik kāñcaṉaiyōṭu avaḷait taṉiyē
   niṟuttiviṭṭup pōntu villai vaḷaittu ampukaḷai eytu nāṟpattoṉpatu
   maṟavarkaḷai vīḻttiṉaṉ. vīḻttavē iṟantavaruṭaiya tamar accamuṟṟup
   pōrceytalai niṟutti antap potumpariṉ nāṟpuṟattum neruppai vaittu
   mūṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭiṉar" eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ, yūki mūrccittut tiṭīreṉṟu kīḻē
   viḻuntaṉaṉ. iṭavakaṉ, avaṉai eṭuttut taḻuvit tāṉ acceytiyaic colla
   vēṇṭiya muṟaippaṭi mellac collātataṟkuk kavaṉṟu caittiyōpacārañ ceytu
   uṇarcciyai uṇṭākki, "utayaṇaṉaṉ uyiruṭaṉ irukkiṉṟaṉaṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ.
   uṭaṉē yūki eḻuntiruntu, "ataṉmēl nikaḻntavaṟṟaik kūṟu" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ.
   appōtu iṭavakaṉ, "āṭukaḷiṉ iṭaiyē pāyum ciṅkam pōlavē avvēṭar
   kūṭṭattiṉiṭaiyē utayaṇaṉ pāyntu pōrceyyat toṭaṅkutalaik kaṇṭa
   vācavatattai añci naṭuṅkiṉaḷ. avaḷai mella naṭattikkoṇṭu ōriṭattil avaṉ
   niṟkaiyil, urōkiṇiyōṭu niṉṟa tiṅkaḷaic cūḻnta ūrkōḷaip pōla vēṭarkaḷ
   avarkaḷai vaḷaintukoṇṭu tuṉ puṟuttat toṭaṅkiṉar. utayaṇaṉ, 'yām
   putaittirukkum poruṭṭokutiyai umakkuk kāṭṭuvōm; tuṉ puṟuttāmal irum’
   eṉṟaṉaṉ. ataṉaikkēṭṭu avarkaḷ cummā niṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu nāṅkaḷ
   cēṉaikaḷōṭu ceṉṟōm. utayaṇaṉ eṅkaḷai aṟiyātavaṉ pōlavē avarkaḷōṭu
   cērntu mutalil niṉṟu appāl vācavatattaiyōṭu oru pakkattil niṉṟaṉaṉ.
   cēṉaiyiṉ perukkattaik kaṇṭu avvēṭarkaḷ ōṭiviṭṭārkaḷ. piṉpu,
   avviruvaraiyum muṟaippaṭi aḻaittuk koṇṭu cayantinakaram aṭaintu maṇam
   ceyvittōm. utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyōṭu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   cūriyacantirar utittalum attamittalum avaṉukkut teriyā; taṉṉuṭaiya
   irācatāṉiyākiya kaucāmpiyaik kaippaṟṟikkoṇṭu āruṇiyeṉṉum aracaṉ āṇṭu
   varutalaiyum aṟintilaṉ. uyirpōṉṟa taṉ tampiyarkaḷākiya piṅkala
   kaṭakaraiyum niṉaikkiṉṟāṉillai. nakuṭarācaṉ pōlak kāma iṉpam oṉṟaiyē
   niṉaintu ataṉaiyē pārāṭṭikkoṇṭu piṟarkkuc camayaṅ koṭāṉāyiṉaṉ. nām eṉ
   ceyvōm!" eṉṟu varutta muṟṟuk kūṟiṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭa yūki puṉmuṟuval ceytu,
   "aṉpē! itukāṟum tuṉpuṟṟirunta aracaṉ iṭaiviṭāmal iṉpattai nukarvatu
   iyalpē!" eṉṟu mutaliṟ kūṟiviṭṭu, appāl aṅkē vantirunta cāṅkiyattāyai
   varuvittut tarum iyalpaik kēṭpavaṉpōlavē cila nēram pēcikkoṇṭiruntu,
   nīṅkāmal ayalil niṟkum oṟṟar pōṉṟavarkaḷukku ovvoru kāriyattaik kūṟi
   aṉuppiviṭṭu avaḷai nōkki, "utayaṇaṉai iṉimēlum pātukāttal ummuṭaiya
   kaṭamaiyākum;
   'aṟṟaṅ kāttali ṉāṇmai pōlavum
       kuṟṟaṅ kāttaliṟ kuravar pōlavum
       oṉṟi yoḻukali ṉuyirē pōlavum
       naṉṟi yaṉṟik kaṉṟiyatu kaṭitaṟkut
       takavila ceytaliṟ pakaivar pōlavum' (2.9; 219-23)

   oḻukutal aṉparatu ceyaleṉpatu nīr aṟintatē. utayaṇaṉ iṉpaccēṟṟuḷ
   iṟaṅkiṉaṉ. ātalāl iṉi ilāvāṇa nakarattiṉ pakkattēyuḷḷa malaiyaic cārnta
   cōlai oṉṟil avaṉai uṇṭāṭumpaṭi ceyvittu avaṉ aṅṅaṉam ceyyum nāṭkaḷuḷ
   orunāḷ vācavatattaiyai avaṉ piriyumvaṇṇañ ceytu aṅṅaṉam pirintu
   ceṉṟapoḻutu avaḷ irunta vīṭṭait tīkkiraiyākki avaḷaic curuṅkaivaḻiyē
   aḻaittuvantu nīr eṉṉiṭam cērppikka vēṇṭum. neṭuntūra
   vaḻippirayāṇattiṟkāka vaittirunta avalai uṇṭu vikki "yūki
   iṟantuviṭṭāṉeṉpataiyum avaṉukkuc colli avaṉai aracāṭcikku uriyavaṉākac
   ceytal ummuṭaiya kaṭamai. itu nāṅkaḷ ellōrum ālōcitta ceytiyākum" eṉṟu
   virivākac colli, iṉṉa iṭattil taṉṉai vantu pārkka vēṇṭum eṉṟu avaḷukkum
   tōḻarkkum terivittuviṭṭu avalai uṇṭu vikki iṟantavaṉpōlat taṉṉaip
   pulappaṭuttiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē iṭavakaṉum ēṉaiyōrum, "emmuṭaiya uyirttōḻaṉākiya
   yūki iṟantapiṉpu yām iṉi uyirōṭu irēm" eṉṟu aḻutu tammuṭaiya tukkattaip
   piṟarkkup pulappaṭuttiṉar. piṉpu avarkaḷ, "ciṟanta aṟivuṭaiyōṉākiya
   ivaṉaic cuṭutal muṟaiyaṉṟu; ivaṉ uṭampaik kaṅkā tīrattil iṭuvatutāṉ
   muṟai" eṉṟu collip piṟaraṟiyāmal avaṉ uṭampai maṟaittuvaittaṉar.
   cāṅkiyattāyai nōkki, "nīr ceṉṟu aracaṉiṭattum araciyiṭattum muṉpōlavē
   aṉpōṭu paḻaka vēṇṭum. yūki kūṟiyavaṟṟai uriya kālattil niṟaivēṟṟa
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu avaḷai utayaṇaṉpāl aṉuppiṉar. aṅṅaṉamē avaḷ puṟappaṭṭut
   tayaṅkitaḻt tāmarait taṇpaṇai taḻīiya cayantiyam perumpatiyai aṭaintāḷ.
   uṇṭa aval vikkiyatāl yūki iṟantāṉeṉṟa ceyti eṅkum paraviyatu.
   cayantinakarai aṭainta cāṅkiyattāy utayaṇakumaraṉaik kaṇṭāḷ. tāyaik
   kaṇṭa kaṉṟaippōla avaṉ viruppaṅkoṇṭu upacarittup pīṭamoṉṟil avaḷai
   irukkacceytāṉ. irunta avaḷ avaṉukku upacāra moḻikaḷaik kūṟi,
   "vācavatattaiyai nī aḻaittuvanta piṉpu uccaiṉi nakarattil nikaḻntavai
   ivai" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. kūṟiyavuṭaṉ, "iṉiya naṇparkaḷuṭaiya kaṭamaiyait
   tīrtta yūki nummuṭaṉ vantilaṉō?" eṉṟu avaṉ viṉāviṉaṉ. ataṟku avaḷ
   viraivāka viṭai kūṟāmaṟ caṟṟu nēram ārāyntu piṉ, "nī vācavatattaiyaip
   piṭimēl ēṟṟikkoṇṭuvanta iravil aṅkē pōr ceytaṟku vanta vīrarkaḷaiyellām
   veṉṟu an nakariṉ akattum puṟattum uḷḷār tattamakkut tōṟṟiyavāṟē
   kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaiyellām kēṭṭup piṉṉarc ceyvikkavēṇṭiyavaṟṟai yellām aṅkē
   ceyvittuviṭṭu iruṭpoḻutil maṟaintu entira vaiyamoṉṟil eṉṉaiyum
   ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ippāl vantu pātukāp puḷḷa iṭamoṉṟil eṉṉai irukkacceytu,
   "vēṭar. kaḷuṭaiya uṟaiviṭamākiya ivviṭattiṟ ceytaṟkuriya kāriyaṅkaḷaic
   ceytuvaruvēṉ; nīr aracaṉai aṭaiyum' eṉṟu piṉṉē taṅki viṭṭāṉ" eṉṟaṉaḷ.
   eṉṟavaḷai nōkki utayaṇaṉ, "vācavatattaiyaip pārttuviṭṭu varuka"
   eṉṟaṉaṉ.
   cāṅkiyattāy kaṉṟaik kāṇum kaṟavaiyaippōla āvaluṭaṉ ceṉṟu
   vācavatattaiyaik kaṇṭaṉaḷ. avaḷ iḷamai toṭaṅkit taṉṉaip pātukāttuvanta
   ceviliyātaliṉ, kaṇṭavuṭaṉ eḻuntu uvakaik kaṇṇīr perukac cāṅkiyattāyait
   taḻuvi mikap pārāṭṭiṉaḷ. pārāṭṭavē avaḷ," tāymār patiṉāṟāyiravarum
   tōvimārkaḷum piṟarum uṉṉuṭaiya pirivāṟṟāmaṟ pulampukaiyil,
   piraccōtaṉaṉ. 'yāṉē vācavatatattaiyai utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ aṉuppiṉēṉ' eṉṟa
   collakkēṭṭu avarkaḷ varuttan taṇintu uyntaṉar" eṉṟaṉaḷ. eṉṉavum
   vācavatattai piṉṉum tammavarai niṉaintu varuntuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. cāṅkiyattāy
   avaḷuṭaiya kaṇṇīrait taṉ viralāl tuṭaittu, "pāvaiyē! piṟanta
   vīṭṭiṉaraiyē niṉaintu nī iṅṅaṉam avalaṅkoḷḷātē; koḷḷutal maṅkala
   makaḷirkku marapaṉṟu" eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷai uṭaṉaḻaittuc ceṉṟu aṅkē mika
   uyarntatākavuḷḷa kaṭṭumalai oṉṟiṉ mēl ēṟṟi ataṉ ucciyil avaḷai niṟutti,
   "naṅkaiyē! pārppāyāka; aracar perumāṉākiya uṉ tantaiyiṉ nakaram atō
   tōṉṟuvatākum; ēṉ kavalai aṭaikiṟāy?" eṉṟu avaḷ uvappak kūṟip piṉpu kāma
   iṉpattil aḻuntit taṉ araca urimai.yaiyum, pakaivaṉ nakaraik
   kaikkoṇṭiruttalaiyum niṉaiyāmalirukkum utayaṇaṉukku nallaṟivai
   uṇṭākkutaṟ poruṭṭu yūki kūṟiya aḻaṟkarumattai mantaṇamākac
   colliyavuṭaṉ, naṉṉeṟi nūlvaḻit tiṇṇaṟivāḷaṉ varunti nōṟṟa aruntavam
   pōlap piṟpayamuṭaimaiyait teḷintu vācavatattai uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷ.
   appāl cāṅkiyattāy maṟaintirunta yūkiyai aṭaintu nikaḻntavaṟṟaik kūṟavē,
   avaṉ utayaṇaṉatu vaṭivam pōṉṟa ōviya moṉṟaip paṭattil eḻuti
   avvuruvattil nāṉku kaṇkaḷaip poṟittu avaṟṟuḷ mēlēyuḷḷa kaṇṇai
   maḻukkiyatāka amaippittu appaṭattai avaḷiṭaṅ koṭuttu, "itaṉai
   utayaṇaṉukkuk kāṭṭum" eṉṟaṉaṉ. avaḷ ceṉṟu vācavatattaiyōṭu avaṉ irunta
   camayam pārttu appaṭattaik kāṭṭiṉaḷ. avaṉ ataṉaip pārttu atil amainta
   nuṭpa vēlaikaḷaik kaṇṭu viyantu maṉitaruṭaiya uṟuppukkaḷuḷ, "aṭikaḷē
   āṭkaḷ; tōḷ maṉaivi; pal makkaḷ; kaṇ tōḻar; talai irumutu kuravar" eṉṉum
   nūṟkaruttai aṟintavaṉātaliṉ, nāṉkaṉuḷ mēlāya kaṇ maḻuṅkiyiruttalai
   aṟintu,"nammuṭaiya tōḻarkaḷ nālvaruḷ mēm paṭṭavaṉāṉa yūkikku ētō tuṉpam
   uḷatu" eṉṟu kavalaiyuṟṟu nōkkiyapoḻutu cāṅkiyattāy, "uccaiṉiyiliruntu
   varukaiyil avalai uṇṭu vikki iṟantu pōyiṉaṉ avaṉ" eṉṟaṉaḷ. eṉavē
   utayaṇaṉ poṟikeṭṭa entirampōlak kaṇcōrntu kīḻē vīḻntu taṉṉai maṟantu
   kiṭantaṉaṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṭa vācavatattaiyum iṭiyēṟuṇṭa nākampōl tuyaruṟṟu
   avaṉ mārpil vīḻntāḷ. atu terintu araṇ maṉaiyil uḷḷār ellārum
   tuṉpuṟṟaṉar. appoḻutu arukiliruntōr caittiyōpa-cārattāl avaṉuṭaiya
   mūrccaiyaip pōkkavē avaṉ eḻuntu kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum avalak kaṇṇīr peruka,
   " viṇṭōy kāṉattu vēḻa vēṭṭattuc
       ciṟaikoḷap paṭṭiyāṉ celcār vaṟuttapiṉ
       maṟaikoṇ māyamoṭu tuṟainakar viḻaviṉuḷ
       ētiṉ maṉṉaṉ kātali payanta
       mātarait taḻīip pōtarap puṇarttup
       pōtuva leṉṟōy poytta ṉaiyō? (2.10: 125-30)

   iṉi vāḻtal āṟṟēṉ. iḷamaitoṭaṅkit tāyiṉumeṉpāl aṉpuṭaiyāy! eṉṉaiyum
   uṭaṉaḻaittuc cellavēṇṭum aṉṟō? nī maṭṭum muṉṉē taṉittuc ceṉṟatu
   muṟaiyō!" eṉṟu palavāṟu vilāvittaṉaṉ.
   aṅkirunta aṟiñar, "virōtikaḷukkumuṉ iṅṅaṉam nī varuntutal iṟaimai aṉṟu.
   taḷarcciyiṉṟi iruttal vēṇṭum. nalviṉai tuṇaiceyyumāyiṉ yūkiyaip
   peṟalām" eṉṟu tēṟṟa avaṉ mēl āka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai niṉaintu ciṟitu varuttan
   taṇintiruntāṉ. āṉālum yūkiyiṉ niṉaivāl, aparapakkattut tiṅkaḷ oḷi
   kuṉṟuvatupōl avaṉ ellā vakaiyilum kuṟaivuṟuvatai aṟinta tōḻar,
   aracaṉukkuriya ellāk kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum yūkiyaip pōlavē ceytaṟkuriyavaṉ
   urumaṇṇuvāvē eṉṟaṟintu akkāriyaṅkaḷai ellām avaṉiṭamākac cārttiṉar.
   avaṉ aṅṅaṉamē aracaṉukkuriya ellāvaṟṟaiyum aṟintu ceyvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   appaṭi nikaḻuṅkālattil orunāḷ urumaṇṇuvā tōḻarai nōkki, "aracaṉuṭaiya
   tuṉpattai nīkkutaṟkuriya parikāra vaḻikaḷ pala uḷa. avai
   pakaivaraiveṉṟu avar koṭukkum kappaṅkaḷaik koṇarntu muṉṉiṭutal,
   makaḷiruṭaṉ teppattilēṟṟi nīr viḷaiyāṭac ceytal, uṇṭāṭṭayarvittal
   mutaliyaṉavām.. avaṟṟuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟāl aracaṉ kavalaiyaip pōkkuvatuṭaṉ
   malaiccāraliluḷḷa muṉivarai aṭaintu viṉāviṉ nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyum
   nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟaiyum kūṟuvāreṉpataic colli aṅṅaṉamē kēṭpittum, avaṉatu
   tukkattaip pōkkavēṇṭum" eṉṟaṉaṉ. ataṉaic colla vēṇṭiya muṟaippaṭiyē
   maṟṟat tōḻar utayaṇaṉukkuk kūṟiṉar.
   kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ, " aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟum muṉivarum uḷarō?" eṉṟa poḻutu
   arukilirunta urumaṇṇuvā, "aracē! niṉṉaik karuvuṟṟup pūraṇa karppiṇiyāka
   irunta mirukāvati nilāmuṟṟattiṟ civanta āṭaiyaip pōrttukkoṇṭu
   tuyilumpoḻutu avaḷait tacait tiraḷeṉṟu kimpuṭpaṟavai oṉṟu eṭuttuc ceṉṟu
   vipulakiriyil vaikka, nī aṅkē utittu vaḷarntu kaṟṟut tērntu uṉ māmaṉ
   vikkiraṉāl ēyarkulattiṟkuriya iṟaimai peṟṟu aracāṭci ceytukoṇṭiruntāy.
   appōtu, innikaḻcciyaic ciṟitum aṟiyāta uṉ tantai kaucāmpi nakarattiṉ
   ayalatākiya vaṉattil uḷḷa oru muṉivarai aṭaintu vaṇaṅkit taṉ kuṟaiyai
   viṇṇappañ ceytaṉaṉ. ammuṉivar nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyum nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟaiyum
   tammuṭaiya ñāṉattāl aṟintu kūṟiṉār. ataṉaik kēṭṭa catāṉikaṉ uṉṉaiyum uṉ
   tāyaiyum kaucāmpikku aḻaittuc ceṉṟu taṉṉuṭaiya aracāṭciyaiyum uṉṉiṭam
   oppittut tuṟantaṉaṉ. appaṭiyē ippoḻutum oru muṉivarai aṭaintu kēṭpiṉ
   ellām terintu koḷḷalām. yūki muṉ pōlavē iṟantu muṭikkuṅ kāriyam yātēṉum
   iruttalum kūṭum. atu viḷaṅkavillai" eṉṟaṉaṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ
   uṇṭāṭṭayartalukku uṭaṉpaṭṭāṉ. tōḻarum uṇṭāṭṭayartal uṟutiyuṭaittu
   eṉṟaṉar. acceytiyai vaḷḷuva mutumakaṉ muracaṟaintu nakarattārkkut
   terivittaṉaṉ.
   uṭaṉē maṭamoḻi makaḷirum maintarum vāṉ pūt taṉṉa annakaram
   vaṟuvitākumpaṭi vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kaikkoṇṭu nalla nāḷiṟ puṟappaṭṭut tēr
   mutaliyavaṟṟai ūrntu oru kālattil ilāvāṇa nakariṉ ayalil uḷḷatum takkōr
   uṟaiyum tāpatappaḷḷikaḷaiyum kaṟṟōr uṟaiyum kaṭavuṭṭāṉaṅkaḷaiyum,
   āṭappukuntavar mīḷuvataṟkut tuṇiyāta cuṉaikaḷaiyum, kōyilkaḷaiyum,
   kurampaikaḷaiyum taṉpāl uṭaiyatum, palā mutaliya palavakai maraṅkaḷ
   koṭippantar mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu ceṟintatumākiya malaiccāralai aṭaintaṉar.
   utayaṇaṉ vācavatattai mutaliyavarkaḷōṭum maṟṟap paricaṉaṅkaḷōṭum vantu
   aṅkē taṅkiṉaṉ. tūcakkuṭiñai, tulāmaṇṭapam, pal kāḻttirai,
   paṭākaikkoṭṭil, kūṭam mutaliyavaṟṟai irupuṟattum peṟṟavītikaḷālum
   aṅkaṅkēyuḷḷa maṉṟukaḷālum vīṟeyti, intiraṉ taṉṉuṭaiya nantaṉavaṉattōṭum
   urimaiyōṭum vantu taṅkiṉāṟpōṉṟa aḻaku peṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu appāṭi.
   maintarum makaḷirum cuṉaippūk kuṟṟum, cuḷḷi cūṭiyum, cuṉaippū aṇintum,
   koṭippūkkoytum, makiḻiṉ vaṭṭamalarkaḷait tokuttum, acōkiṉ centaḷirkaḷāṟ
   kaṇṇi kaṭṭiyum, mālai toṭuttum, malaivaḷam pukaḻntum, kuravaiyāṭiyum,
   mayilkaḷiṉ āṭalkaḷaik kaṇṭu uvantum, kiḷikaḷiṉ maḻalaic coṟkaḷaik kēṭṭu
   iṉpuṟṟum, pala makaḷiriṉ iṭaiyē kaḷippuṟṟu viḷaṅkum aracarkaḷaippōlap
   pala piṭikaḷi ṉiṭaiyē viḷaiyāṭikkoṇṭu iṟumāntu niṟkuṅ kaḷiṟṟiṉaṅkaḷai
   nōkkiyum, nīr viḷaiyāṭiyum, tiṉaik kollaikaḷilum cuṉaiyaic cūḻnta
   pāṟaikaḷilum mika uyarnta maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṭukaḷilum pūñcōlaikaḷilum
   koṭivīṭukaḷilum māṟāttāṉai maṉṉaṉai vaḻutti āṟāk kātaloṭu tiḷaittum,
   amaiyātavarkaḷāki āṭiyum, pāṭiyum kūṭiyum, pirintum, ūṭiyum uṇarntum,
   ōṭiyum oḷittum, nāṭiyum nayantum, nalam pārāṭṭiyum viḷaiyāṭi
   iṉpuṟuvārāyiṉar.
   iṅṅaṉam iru pālārkaḷum aṉpuṟṟu ammalaic cāralil viḷaiyāṭikkoṇ
   ṭirukkaiyil, aimperuṅ kuḻuviṉarum eṇpērāyattārum utayaṇaṉukku
   aṅkaṅkēyuḷḷa iṉiya kāṭcikaḷaik kāṭṭikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu, avaṉait toṭarntuvarum
   urimaiccuṟṟattāraip piṉṉē varumpaṭi ceytu avarkaḷukkut takka pātukāppai
   amaittuviṭṭup paṇṭai nūṟporuḷkaḷai aṟintu piṟarkkum aṟivittut tavam
   ceytu viḷaṅkum oru muṉivaruṭaiya tavappaḷḷiyākiya cōlai oṉṟai
   aṭaintārkaḷ. mukkuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum nīkkiya periyōr taṅkiyiruttaliṉ tuṭṭa
   vilaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṇṭākum accattiṟku atiṟ ciṟitum iṭamillai.
   kāvalaiyē vēṇṭātatu atu. mēlum mikunta vaḷattai uṭaiyatāki aintiṇai
   maraṅkaḷum paintaḷirk koṭikaḷum neruṅkappeṟṟu veppamnīṅki, nīruṭ
   pukuntāṟpōṉṟa taṇmaiyuṟṟu, ūriṉ uḷḷiṭattaip pōṉṟa virinta iṭaṅkaḷai
   uṭaiyatāy malart tavicu aṭuttut taḷirkkuṭai ōṅkip pūṅkulaik kavari
   puṭaipuṭai vīci miñiṟum curumpum vaṇṭum tumpiyum kuyilum pāṭum
   maḻalaiyum pāṭalāṟ kēṭpōruṭaiya maṉattai vayappaṭuttuvatākip piṟarkkuk
   koṭukkak karutit tāṅki vaittiruntāṟpōlavē tōṟṟumpaṭi amutamayamāṉa
   kaṉikaḷai ēntip payaṉuḷḷa maraṅkaḷaiyē muṟṟum peṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatu
   accōlai.
   avarkaḷ, ataṉ puṟattai aṭaintu, "utayaṇaṉ oru kāriyattaik karutit
   taricikka vantirukkiṉṟaṉaṉ" eṉpatai am muṉivarukku muṟaippaṭiyē colli
   viṭuttaṉar. muṉivar avaṉuṭaiya peruṅkulap piṟappaiyum arumporuḷ
   vakaiyaiyum maṉṉuyirait taṉṉuyir pōlavē eṇṇip pātukākkum kaṭappāṭṭaiyum
   matittu avaṉaik kāṇutaṟku virumpi, intiraṉai etir koḷḷum teyva
   muṉivaraippōlavē muṟaippaṭi varavēṟṟu, acōka marattiṉ aṇiniḻalil
   maṇaliṉmītu kiṭantuḷḷa palavakaippaṭṭa malart tokutiyākiya iyaṟṟāt
   tavicil irukkumpaṭi kaiyāṟ kuṟippittaṉar. avaṉ anta muṉivarai vaṇaṅki
   avar kāṭṭiya iṭattil iruntaṉaṉ. iruntavaṉ piṉṉar ammarattiṉ aṭiyaic
   cārntu yūkiyaiyē niṉaintu kavalai koṇṭiruntāṉ. irukkaiyil, avaṉ
   piṭittirunta veḷḷaippū oṉṟu kaiyiṉiṉṟum naḻuvik kāliṉkīḻē vīḻntatu.
   avaṟṟai yellām aṟinta muṉivar, nimittap payaṉai aṟintu colluvatil mikka
   vallunarātaliṉ avaṉai nōkki, "araca! nī pacumarattaic
   cārntiruntamaiyāl, atu niṉ uyirttōḻaṉ uyirōṭiruttalaiyum avaṉ piṉpu
   vantu uṉṉai aṭaitalaiyum naṉku pulappaṭuttum. itu tavaṟātu; teḷivāyāka.
   piṭitta veṇpūviṉ vīḻcci nī ippōtu anupavikkum pōkattiṟku iṭaiyūṟu
   uṇṭeṉpatai aṟiviyā niṟkum. antappū vēṟiṭam cellāmal uṉ kāliṉ kīḻiṭattai
   aṭaintamaiyāl antap pōkattai mīṭṭum aṭaivai. nī pūmiyiṉmēl iruntamaiyāl
   niṉ talainakarattaiyum nāṭṭaiyum peṟṟu araca urimaiyaiyum aṭaivai.
   ciṟitum aiyamillai. piṟkālattil uṉakkuc cāraṇaṉuṭaiya upatēcamum uṇṭu.
   uṉṉuṭaiya āyuḷ nāḷiṉ aḷavu ittuṇaiyākum" eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. utayaṇaṉ,
   "ivaruṭaiya aruḷmoḻi niccayamākavē iruttal kūṭum" eṉṟu eṇṇi yūkiyai
   aṭaintavaṉ pōṉṟavaṉāki vaṇaṅki avariṭam viṭai peṟṟup puṟampōntu
   vācavatattaiyai aṭaintu avaḷuṭaṉ aṅkē taṉakku amaikkappaṭṭirukkum paṭa
   māṭattil uṟaipavaṉāyiṉaṉ.
   aṅṅaṉam uṟainta utayaṇaṉ, oruvāṟu kavalai tīrntu vācavatattaiyuṭaṉ
   ceṉṟu kāṉyāṟṟuk kaṭumpuṉalāṭiyum;palaniṟa malarkaḷ pūttu, mēl mūṭiyait
   tiṟanta āparaṇap peṭṭipōlat tōṉṟum kuṟuvāyc cuṉainīril muḻavoliyaip
   piṟappittu attāḷattiṟ kēṟpap pāṭiyum; kāṭṭiluḷḷa aramakaḷirpōṉṟavarum
   kuḻaiyar, kōtaiyar, iḻaiyar, iṇarttaḻaiyar, tāriṉarkaḷumākiya palavakai
   makaḷirkaḷiṉ iṭaiyē niṉṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya āṭalaik kaṇṭum; pāṭalkaḷaik
   kēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟum; pāṟaiyākiya uralil aivaṉanellaic corintu yāṉaik
   kompākiya ulakkaiyāl tam aiyaruṭaiya iyalpukaḷaip pukaḻntukoṇṭu pācaval
   iṭikkum vēṭa makaḷiruṭaiya ammaṉai, vaḷḷaippāṭṭaik kēṭṭum;
   aruviyāṭiyum; cuṉai viḷaiyāṭiyum; pūṅkuḻaimakaḷir poṉvaḷḷattēntiya
   tēṟalaiyum matuvaiyum uṇṭum; ōr uyirai uṭaiya iru talaip puḷḷaippōl
   oṭṭi iṉpuṟṟum; ammalaic cāralil uṇṭāṭṭayarntaṉaṉ.
   uṭaṉvanta makaḷir, veṇmuttaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟṟil iḻaittum, kōṅkam pūkkaḷaip
   paṟittu ivai poṟ paṇiyeṉṟu pāvaikaḷiṉ talaiyil vaittum,
   acōkañciṉaikaḷiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum taḻaikkayiṟṟu ūñcalkaḷil iruntu
   āṭiyum, mullaip pōtukaḷaip paṟittuk kattikai koṭuttum, pittikai
   piṇaittum, taḷirkaḷaik kaiyiṟkoṇṭu pala vicittira vaṭivaṅkaḷ tōṉṟumpaṭi
   kiḷḷiyum, caṇpakac cōlai aṭaintu taḻai uṭaikaḷai amaittum,
   vācavatattaiyiṉ celvattaiyum ciṟappaiyum pāṭikkoṇṭē kurāmarattiṉ
   niḻalil vaḷaikaḷ olippa maṟalik kuṟavaiyāṭiyum, pantāṭiyum, pāvai
   puṉaintum, taḷirppaṭukkaiyiṉmēlē kaṇpaṭai koṇṭum, pāṉavakaikaḷai uṇṭum
   viḷaiyāṭiṉar. maṟṟaiyōrkaḷum tattamakku iyaṉṟavaṇṇam ippaṭiyē
   viḷaiyāṭik kaḷittu iṉpuṟṟaṉar.
   iṅṅaṉam uṇṭāṭṭayarum nāṭkaḷil oru nāḷ, yāttirai ceypavarkaḷ vantu
   taṅkum koṭṭilkaḷaiyum koṭippantarkaḷaiyum piṉṉum ilaipōṉṟa iṉiya pala
   iṭaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaiyatāyk kāṇpāṉāyiṉ intiraṉum vēṟiṭam cellat tuṇiyāta
   mikka aḻakaip peṟṟu, iṉiya paḻamaraṅkaḷaiyum naṟuviya pūmaraṅkaḷaiyun
   taṉpāl koṇṭu mayil mutaliya paṟavaikaḷiṉ ceṟivai uṭaiyatāy eṉṟum
   nalivōr illāmaiyāl uyirttokutikaḷ kavalaiyiṉṟi vāḻum polivai uṭaiyatāy
   viḷaṅkum poḻil oṉṟil maravuri uṭukkaiyum civanta caṭaiyaiyum uṇmai
   ñāṉattaiyum viratattaiyum uṭaiya muṉivar oruvar tavañceyyum
   ācciramattai aṭaintu acōka marattiṉ niḻalil maṇaṟparappil, utayaṇaṉ
   vayantakaṉuṭaṉ iruntāṉ.
   iruntapoḻutu avaṉiṭam viṭaipeṟṟu vācavatattai kāñcaṉaiyōṭu cōlai vaḷaṅ
   kāṇutaṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ. ēvalāḷarkaḷum ovvoru kāriyattai muṉṉiṭṭu vēṟu
   vēṟiṭaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉar. aracāṭci yait tuṟantu maṉaiviyuṭaṉ vantu aṅkē
   tavam ceytu koṇṭirukkum ōr aracamuṉivaruṭaiya puttiriyum
   pēraḻakiṉaḷumākiya viricikaiyeṉṉum peyaruṭaiya ciṟu pirāyattāḷ orutti
   aṅkē iruntaṉaḷ. avaḷ muṉivarkaḷai aṉṟi vēṟu āṭavarkaḷ yāraiyum
   kaṇṭaṟiyātavaḷ. avaḷ utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu, "ivaṉ kāmaṉ" eṉṟu eṇṇik
   kātalkoṇṭu tāṉ vaittirunta pāvai pantukaḷai ōriṭattē vaittuviṭṭu mellac
   ceṉṟu nantiyāvarttam, nākam, cintuvāram, cēpālikai, kuruk katti, cuḷḷi,
   ciṟucēṭam, caṇpakam ākiya ivaṟṟiṉ pūkkaḷaip paṟittut taṉ kuṭaṅkaiyil
   aṭakkikkoṇṭu vantu utayaṇaṉai aṭaintu, "aiyā! immalarkaḷaip
   piṇaiyalkaḷākat toṭuttu īmiṉ; īntāṟ pāvaiyumyāṉum aṇintukoḷvōm" eṉṟu
   colli irantu ammalarkaḷai nīṭṭiṉaḷ. avaṉ avaḷaip pārttu, "it
   tavavaṉattil uṟaiyun tirumakaḷō ivaḷ?" eṉṟu mutalil eṇṉi, avaḷuṭaiya
   kaṇkaḷiṉ imaikaḷ āṭutalaiyum aṇintamālaikaḷ vāṭutalaiyum kaṇṭu, "ivaḷ
   muṉivar makaḷē" eṉṟu teḷintu teyvat tikiriyaiyuṭaiya aracar
   makaḷirkkuriya ciṟanta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai avaḷpāl kaṇṭu yāḻnarampai
   varuṭutalālum paṭaik kalaṅkaḷaip pakaivarmēṟ celuttutalālum
   taḻumpupaṭṭut tiruntiya aṅkaiyāl taṉ mēlāṭaiyai virittu atil
   ammalarkaḷai vāṅkip pala niṟamuḷḷa maṇikaḷ niṟam māṟupaṭa aḻuttappeṟṟa
   irattiṉamālaipōlat tōṟṟumpaṭi pala vakai niṟamuḷḷa ammalarkaḷai uriya
   iṭaṅkaḷil vaittup palavakai mālaikaḷ kaṭṭi avaḷukkuk koṭukka, avaḷ
   ammālaiyait takkavāṟu aṇintu koḷḷutaṟku aṟiyātavaḷāki varunti
   alamantaṉaḷ. ataṉai aṟinta avaṉ, "iṅkē vā" eṉṟu aḻaittu avaḷait taṉ
   maṭimēl vaittu avaṟṟai avaḷ ucciyilum talaippakkattilum mayir muṭiyilum
   alaṅkāramākap puṉaintu pārāṭṭi, "mellac cel" eṉṟu viṭukka avaḷ ceṉṟāḷ.
   utayaṇaṉai viṭṭup pirinta cila nērattai oru varuṭamāka niṉaintu avaṉ
   pirivāṟṟāmaiyāl varuntit taḻaikaḷaiyum kaṇṇikaḷaiyum ēntikkoṇṭu naṟpūṅ
   kompar naṭaipeṟṟāṅku naṭantu viraintu varum vācavatattai, taṉmuṉ varum
   viricikaiyaiyum avaḷ kūntalil aṇiyappaṭṭirukkum mālaikaḷaiyuṅ kaṇṭu
   ammālaikaḷ utayaṇaṉāl toṭuttuc cūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai aṟintu ciṉantu
   kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum veyya nīrttuḷikaḷaic cintik kaiyilirunta kaṇṇikaḷaiyum
   taḻaikaḷaiyum eṟintuviṭṭu, "kiṭaitta tāmaraip pūviluḷḷa tēṉai
   uṇpataṟkuc cellum vaṇṭaip pōlvār āṭavar eṉpatai iṉṟu kaṇkūṭākak
   kaṇṭēṉ." eṉṟu kaṭuñ ciṉamkoṇṭu, uṭaṉvarum cāṅkiyattāyaiyum
   kāñcaṉaiyaiyum nōkki, " eṉ tantaiyiṉ aṇi nakarattiṟku eṉṉai
   aṉuppiviṭuṅkaḷ" eṉṟu collit taṉṉuṭaiya kōlaṅkaḷaic citaittukkoṇṭē
   accōlaiyiṉ oru pakkattē celvāḷāyiṉaḷ. ataṉaik kāñcaṉaiyāl aṟinta
   utayaṇaṉ accamuṟṟuc ceṉṟu avaḷait taḻuvi avaḷuṭaiya muṭiyaṇikaḷait
   tiruttiyum; pāta kiṇkiṇiyait tuṭaittum tukaḷpōkkiyum, kūntal aṇikaḷaip
   puṉaintum, vaḷaikalaik kaiyil ēṟṟiyum, kaṇṇīrttuḷikaḷait tuṭaittum
   kātaṇikaḷaic cariyāka aṇintum, putut taḷirkaḷaik kaiyiṟ koṭuttum,
   puṟattait taivantum, '*nī iṅṅaṉam kōpippāyāyiṉ aṉ uyirukkup perun tīṅku
   viḷaittāyāvāy' eṉṟu kūṟiyum palavāṟu tēṟṟiṉāṉ. tēṟṟavum avaḷ ciṟitum
   ūṭal taṇiyāmaṟ kaṭuñciṉattōṭē iruntaṉaḷ. appōtu am malaiccāraliluḷḷa
   oru palāmarattilirunta āṇ kuraṅkoṉṟu tiṭīreṉṟu kutittu vantatu. ataṉaik
   kaṇṭa vācavatattai añci naṭunaṭuṅki viraintu avaṉait taḻuvikkoṇṭaṉaḷ.
   avaṉ maṉam uvantu vēṟu yātoru kavalaiyum illātavaṉāki akkaṇattil
   avaḷuṭaṉ kālattai iṉitu kaḻippāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ilāvāṇanakariṉatu puṟattē oṭuṅkiyirunta yūki annakarattiluḷḷa
   araṇmaṉaikkum tāṉ irukkum iṭattiṟkum piṟar aṟiyāmal oru curuṅkai
   vaḻiyai iyaṟṟuvittup piṉ ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai urumaṇṇuvāviṟkum
   vayantakaṉukkum uṇarttiṉaṉ. avarkaḷ avaṉ kūṟiyataic cāṅkiyattāykkut
   terivittaṉar. cāṅkiyattāy utayaṇaṉai aṭaintu, "aracē, koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ
   ulāvum malaiccāralil iruntāl oru camayam yātēṉum oṉṟāl vācavatattai
   accam aṭaiyakkūṭum. ātalāl iṉimēl ilāvāṇa nakarattuḷḷa araṇmaṉaic
   cārntiruttal nalamākum" eṉṟaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ ataṉai aṅkīkarittu
   vācavatattaiyōṭum puṟappaṭṭu an narattai aṭaintu araṇmaṉaiyil
   vāḻvāṉāyiṉaṉ. iṅṅaṉam cila nāṭkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.
   ippaṭiyirukkaiyil tōḻarkaḷ utayaṇaṉai nōkki, "oru kāriyamum illāmal
   cummā iruntāl tirumakaḷ nammai veṟuppāḷ. ātalāl eṅkēṉum ceṉṟu vara
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyiṉ piriviṟku añci
   ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭavillai. cila tiṉam ceṉṟapiṉ piritaṟkuriya ūḻviṉai
   vantamaiyāl, aracaṉai nōkki vācavatattai, "aṭikaḷē, vēṭṭañ ceṉṟu
   arumpu, malar, taḷir eṉpavaṟṟāṟ kaṇṇi kaṭṭi eṉakkut tantaruḷuka" eṉṟu
   vēṇṭiṉaḷ. atuceytaṟku virumpiya utayaṇaṉ maṟunāḷ kālaiyiṟ parimā ūrntu
   kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟaṉaṉ; mantirikaḷ tām eṇṇiyatai niṟaivēṟṟutaṟku atu nalla
   camayam eṉṟu niṉaittu, "aracaṉ kāma iṉpattaiyē virumpi ūkkam
   ilaṉāyiṉāṉeṉṟu vēṭarkaḷ ūraic cuṭutaṟku vantu cūḻntukoṇṭaṉar" eṉṟa
   pēccai eṅkum paravacceytu ēvalāḷarkaḷaikkoṇṭu araṇmaṉaiyiṉ
   nāṟpuṟattaiyum tīkku iraiyākki antappurattiṟ ceṉṟu, veḷiyē puṟappaṭṭuc
   cellutaṟku muṭiyāmal tavittu alamantu niṟkum vācavatattaiyaiyum
   cāṅkiyat tāyaiyuṅ kaṇṭu, " intac curuṅkai vaḻiyākac celluṅkaḷ" eṉṟu
   avarkaḷai aṉuppiviṭṭu avarkaḷ irunta iṭattiṟ kolaiciṟaiyāḷar iruvarai
   valintu pukutti vācavatattaiyiṉ aṭikkalamutal talaikkalam iṟutiyāka
   uḷḷa āparaṇaṅkaḷai ellām ōriṭattiṟ citaṟivait tārkaḷ. araṇmaṉaiyiṉ
   nāṟpuṟattaiyum tīkkiṟaiyākkiya karumak kaḷvarait tākkkikkoṇṭē
   urumaṇṇuvā orupāṟ celvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   appōtu cāṅkiyattāy veytuyirttu naṭuṅkum vācavatattaiyai curuṅkai vaḻiyē
   aḻaittukoṇṭu yūki irukkum iṭamceṉṟāḷ. cellukaiyil vācavatattaiyai nōkki
   avaḷ,"nī ciṟitum añcātē. uṉṉuṭaiya uyirttuṇaivaṉukku ākkamaḷikka
   virumpi avaṉuṭaiya uyir naṇpaṉākiya yūki ceyta māyac cūḻcciyākum itu"
   eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. antac camayattil yūki, tāṉ eṇṇiyatu muṭintateṉṟu
   puṉmuṟuval koṇṭu aṅkē vantu vaṇaṅki, "tāyē! maṟamāccēṉaṉ kātal makaḷē!
   kēṭṭaruḷka; uṉṉuṭaiya tantai cēṉaikaḷiṉ iṭaiyē utayaṇaṉaic
   ciṟaippaṭuttit taḷaiyiṭṭamaiyāl pāñcālarāyaṉ kaucāmpi nakarattaik
   kaippaṟṟikkoṇṭāṉ. piṅkala kaṭakar añciyōṭiṉar. avaṟṟai aṟiyāta uṉ
   talaivaṉ yātoṉṟaiyum niṉaittaliṉṟik kavalaiyaṟṟu uṉṉuṭaṉ iṭaiviṭāmal
   uṟaivāṉāyiṉaṉ. pakaivaṉaik koṉṟu uṉ talaivaṉai nalla pataviyil niṟutta
   vēṇṭumāyiṉ nī cila nāḷ pirintirukka vēṇṭuvatē, ātalāl, uṅkaḷ kuṭikku
   vanta ciṟucol nīṅkumpaṭi āvataic collum uyir pōṉṟa eṉṉai nōkkiyum, niṉ
   kulattai nōkkiyum talaivaṉ taṉ talaimaiyiṉiṉṟum vaḻuviya nilaimaiyai
   nōkkiyum, nilam puṭai peyariṉum vicumpu vantiḻiyiṉum ciṟitum kalaṅkāta
   niṉ kaṭavuṭ kaṟpai nōkkiyum, inta varattai aruḷic ceyya vēṇṭum" eṉṟu
   mikavum irantu vēṇṭiṉaṉ.
   vācavatattai, "nammuṭaiya aracarpirāṉukku iyainta nūl vallāḷarkaḷākiya
   mantirikaḷ nālvaruḷ yūki iṟantaṉaṉ; ivaṉ yārō?" eṉṟu eṇṇik kavalaiyuṟṟu
   viraintu viṭaikoṭāmal ārāyvāḷāyiṉaḷ. avaḷuṭaiya nilaimaiyai aṟinta
   cāṅkiyat tāy, "ivaṉ yūkiyē. aracaṉ kavalaiyaṟṟiruppatai māṟṟuvataṟkē
   tāṉ iṟantuviṭṭatākap peyar paṇṇiviṭṭu ivaṉ iṅkē vantirukkiṟāṉ. nī
   kavalaiyai viṭṭuviṭu" eṉṟu terivikkavē avaḷ tēṟiṉaḷ. yūki kūṟiya
   niyāyaṅkaḷai yellām uṭkoṇṭavaḷāyiṉum avaḷ utayaṇaṉ pirivai niṉaintu
