   varuntip piṉpu, "ivaṉ eṇṇiyatu uṟuti payappatākum" eṉṟu tuṇintu, "eṉ
   iṟaivaṉ, muṉ māya yāṉaiyāṟ ciṟaip paṭṭapoḻutu avaṉai mīṭpataṟku māya
   iṟuti vallaiyākiya nītiyāḷa! nī vēṇṭiyataic ceyvāyāka" eṉṟu avaṉukku
   upacāra moḻikaḷaik kūṟi oruvāṟu kavalai yoḻintiruntaṉaḷ.
   vācavatattaiyiṉ pirivāl aracaṉ tīṅkillāmal iruttalai aṟintu koḷḷum
   varaiyil avviṭattilēyē iruttaṟku niccayittuk koṇṭu yūki av viruvarōṭum
   mēlē āka vēṇṭiya kāriyaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi ārāypavaṉāki avarkaḷuṭaṉ aṅkē
   iruntāṉ. itu niṟka;
   utayaṇakumaraṉ vācavatattai virumpiyapaṭiyē taḻaiyum, tārum, kaṇṇiyum,
   piṇaiyalum kaikkoṇṭu kutiraiyai ūrntu avaḷaik kāṇa viraintu varukaiyil
   iṭakkaṇ āṭiyatu. iṭattōḷ acaintatu. piṉṉum cila turnimittaṅkaḷum tīya
   cakuṉaṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟiṉ payaṉai ārāyntu pārkkaiyil, "uyirkkiḻatti
   nīṅkiṉaḷ; iṅkē ilaḷ; avaḷaik kāṇāta varuttattāl tōḻarkaḷōṭu tāṉ
   cilakālam puṟattē iyaṅkutal kūṭum" eṉpatu teriyavantatu. ataṉāl mikka
   kavalaiyuṟṟu varum avaṉ, etirē perum pukaiyiṉ eḻucciyaik kaṇṭu mikavum
   viraintu varukaiyil, vācavatattai irunta iṭattilum neruppup paravit
   tōṉṟak kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭu ūkkaṅ kuṉṟiyavaṉākic cellum poḻutu taṉakku oru
   cōrvum nērātapaṭi uru maṇṇuvāvum vayantakaṉum māya maḷḷaraik kūṭṭat
   tōṭu turatti aṭippavarkaḷākit taṉ varavu pārttuk koṇṭu etirē niṟpataik
   kaṇṭaṉaṉ. appōtu, kāñcaṉamālai,
   "nāvalan taṇpoḻi ṉaṇṇā rōṭṭiya
       kāvalaṉ makaḷē ! kaṉaṅkuḻai maṭavōy !
       maṇviḷak kāki varattiṉ vantōy !
       peṇviḷak kākiya peṟalarum pētāy !
       poṉṉē ! tiruvē ! aṉṉē ! arivāy !
       naṅkāy ! nallāy ! koṅkār kōtāy !
       vīṇaik kiḻattī! vittaka vuruvī!
       tēṉēr kiḷavī! ciṟumutuk kuṟaivī!
       utaiyaṇa kumara ṉuyirttuṇait tēvī!
       putaiyaḻa lakavayiṟ pukka ṉaiyō!" (2.18:76-85)

   eṉṟu kāṭṭut tīyiṉ iṭaiyē akappaṭṭu varuntum mayilaippōla iṭaiviṭāmaṟ
   pulampi alamantu vīḻntu puraṇṭu puraṇṭu aḻutalai avaṉ pārttaṉaṉ. pārttu
   vācavatattaikku ētō oru tuṉpam vantuviṭṭa teṉṟu niccayittu aṟivu
   mayaṅkic cōrntu poṟiyaṟṟa pāvaipōlak kutiraiyiṉiṉṟum tiṭīreṉṟu nilattil
   vīḻntaṉaṉ. vīḻntu aṟivaṟṟuk kiṭakkum aracaṉait tōḻar aṇuki mūrccaiyai
   māṟṟutaṟkuriya poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu māṟṟa, eḻumaiyun toṭarnta aṉ puṭaiya
   avaḷai niṉaintu araṟṟikkoṇṭē avaṉ eḻuntāṉ. eḻuntavaṉai avarkaḷ cūḻntu
   pala vakaiyāṉa camātāṉaṅkaḷaic collit tēṟṟavum avaṉ tēṟāmal, "eṉ
   uyirttuṇaivi mūḻkiya tīyil yāṉum pukuvēṉ" eṉṟu attīyiṟ pukuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   appōtu tōḻar, "uṉṉuṭaiya pakaivar makiḻumpaṭi nī iṅṅaṉam eṇṇutal
   mikavum tavaṟākum; 'yūki illāviṭiṉ utayaṇaṉukku yātum ceyalillai' eṉṟu
   aṟivilār colluñ col eṅkaḷ maṉattaic cuṭā niṟkum. ataṉai nī aṟintu uṉ
   kulattait tāṅkutal naṉṟu" eṉṟu vilakka, kaṭal karaiyaik kaṭavātatupōla
   avaṉ tōḻarkaḷ vārttaiyaik kaṭavāṉāki, "eṉ uyirttuṇaiviyiṉ uṭampaip
   pārkka vēṇṭum; ātalāl viraintu neruppai avittu ataṉaik kāṭṭumiṉ"
   eṉṟaṉaṉ.
   avarkaḷ, "aracar karippiṇattaik kāṇpatu marapaṉṟu" eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttavum
   avaṉ taṭaippaṭāmaṟ kāṇpataiyē virumpit tuṉpuṟṟaṉaṉ. "nammuṭaiya
   peruntēvi uyir nīṅkumpaṭi ivar pārttirāreṉṟu aracaṉ eṇṇuvāṉāyiṉ, atu
   nām eṇṇiya kāriyattiṟku mikka iṭaiyūṟākum" eṉṟu tōḻar avaṉai
   aḻaittukkoṇṭu oruvakaiyākap paḷḷippēraṟai ceṉṟu aṅkē amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa
   curuṅkai avaṉukkut tōṉṟātapaṭi ataṉ vāyilai naṉṟāka mutalil
   maṟaittuviṭṭu aṅkē karintu kiṭanta iraṇṭu kuṟaip piṇaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭi,
   "cāṅkiyattāyum tattaiyum iṟantamai poyyaṉṟu; uṇmaiyē!" eṉṟu avaṉ
   maṉaṅkoḷḷac ceytu aṅkaṅkē citaṟuṇṭu kiṭantaṉavum tīyil mūḻkiṉamaiyāl
   oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkuvaṉavumākiya avaḷuṭaiya āparaṇaṅkaḷaip poṟukkivantu
   avaṉukku muṉṉē iṭṭaṉar. poṉṉarimālai, neṟṟippaṭṭam, tilakam, ceviyaṇi,
   poṟkoṭi, ilaipperumpūṇ, itayavācaṉai, kaḷikai, muttāram, paṉmaṇippūṇ,
   ciṉmaṇittāli, muttaṇivaṭam, cittiravutti, nāṇ, toṭar, tōḷaṇi, iṭaiyaṉi,
   viralaṇi mutaliya aṇikalaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟaiyum avaṉ taṉittaṉiyē
   eṭutteṭuttup pārttup pārttu viḷittu viḷittup palavāṟu pulampi
   araṟṟiṉaṉ.
   piṉpu avaṉuṭaiya melivaiyum kavalaiyaiyum aṟinta mantirimār, "ivaṉ
   cōrvai aṟintu pāñcālarāyaṉ pōr ceyvataṟku varuvāṉāyiṉ innakarattuḷḷē
   varaviṭāmal taṭuttuviṭuka" eṉṟu collic caturaṅkap paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ
   vīrarkaḷai annakarattaic cūḻa amaittu avarkaḷukkum cēṉaikaḷukkum
   vēṇṭiya pōrkkaruvikaḷaiyum aḷittu, uṇavup poruḷkaḷaiyum aṅkaṅkē nirampa
   vaittataṉṟi perumpaṭaikaḷait ticaitōṟum aṉuppip pātukākkumpaṭi ceyvittu
   utayaṇaṉukkum ataṉait terivittu avaṉatu tuṉpattai oruvāṟu tīrttu
   varuvārāyiruntaṉar. piṉṉum avarkaḷ kāmattāl uṇṭākum tīṅkukaḷaip
   palavāṟu eṭutteṭuttu uraittu avaṉuṭaiya varuttattaip pōkka, avaṉ
   āṟutaluṟṟiruntaṉaṉ.
   ataṉai aṟinta tōḻar, "aracaṉukku yātoru tīṅkum illai. iṉi vēṟiṭattiṟku
   cellutal nalam" eṉṟu yūkikkut terivikka, avaṉ vācavatattaikku ac
   ceytiyait terivittaṉaṉ. avaḷ mantirattāṟ piṇippuṇṭa nākakaṉṉikaipōla
   yūkiyiṉuṭaiya nītimoḻiyāṟ piṇippuṇṭavaḷākit taṉṉuṭaiya kuṟaiyai
   veḷippaṭuttāmal talaivaṉ ākkamaṭaitalaiyē perum payaṉāka niṉaintu
   āṟutaluṟṟaṉaḷ. piṉpu am mūvarum uṭampai vēṟupaṭuttuvatākiya maruntoṉṟāl
   vēṟu niṟampeṟṟu antaṇa vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu mellap puṟappaṭṭu oru malaiyaic
   cārntu aṅkē tavam ceytukoṇṭirunta urumaṇṇuvāviṉ tantaiyākiya muṉivaṉai
   aṭaintu tām iṉṉāreṉpatai avaṉukku pulappaṭuttāmal ākavēṇṭiyavaṟṟai
   avaṉiṭañ colli mika aṇukāmalum mika akalāmalum avaṉatu tavappaḷḷiyil
   taṅkiyiruntaṉar.
   vācavatattaiyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmal varuntukiṉṟa kāñcaṉaikkut tām eṇṇiya
   kāriyattai urumaṇṇuvā mantaṇamākat terivittut tēṟṟi avaḷai aḻaittu
   vantu vācavatattaiyiṭam cērppittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaṉ. avaḷ vācavatattaiyait
   tēṟṟuvittu avaḷuṭaṉ iruntaṉaḷ. irukkum nāṭkaḷil oru nāḷ an nālvarum,
   "nām iṅkē iruttalai ayalār aṟiyiṉ, nammuṭaiya kāriyam paḻutupaṭum" eṉṟu
   eṇṇi, "iṉi yām iruttaṟkuriya iṭam yātu?" eṉṟu ālōcittu oruvarukkun
   teriyāmal puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟu urumaṇṇuvāviṉ tantaiyiṉ tōḻaṉum ukkira
   kulattiṟ piṟantavaṉumākiya vicayavaraṉeṉṉum aracaṉuṭaiya irācatāṉiyum
   aṅka nāṭṭil uḷḷatumāṉa caṇpaiyeṉṉum perunakarattil vāḻum peruñ
   celvavāṉākiya mittirakāmaṉeṉṉum vaṇikarperumāṉuṭaiya vīṭṭil taṅkik
   kālam kaḻippārāyiṉar.
   -------------------

                            3. makata kāṇṭam

   ēyar kulattiṟkup paḻampakaivaṉum atupaṟṟiyē kaucāmpi nakarattai
   kaippaṟṟiyavaṉumākiya pāñcāla aracaṉ, yūki iṟanta ceytiyaiyum
   vācavatattaiyai iḻantamaiyāl utayaṇaṉ melivuṟṟiruttalaiyum aṟintu appāl
   taṉakku yātoru tīṅkum uṇṭāka māṭṭāteṉṟu eṇṇik kaucāmpinakarattaip
   pātukāvāmal celvakkaḷippāl mikunta makiḻcci aṭaintiruntaṉaṉ. ataṉai
   aṟinta urumaṇ ṇuvā mutaliyōr, "iṉi makata maṉṉaṉōṭu naṭpuppūṇṭu
   avaṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷaiyum cērttukkoṇṭu pakaivaṉai vella vēṇṭum." eṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉukkuk kūṟiṉar. avaṉ, "yūki mutaliyavarai iḻantapiṉpu eṉakku
   vēṇṭuvatu yātu? eṉṟu maṟuttaṉaṉ. nāḷukku nāḷ utayaṇaṉuṭaiya uṭampu
   melivuṟṟatu. appoḻutu tōḻar, "ciṟanta vittaiyil vallavaṉākiya
   ilāmayaṉeṉṉum oru muṉivaṉ irunta vaṉaṅkūṭat tīyāl ventuviṭṭatu" eṉṟu
   eṭuttuk kūṟi, "tīyāl tuṉpamuṟutal yāvarālum vilakkaṟ pālataṉṟu" eṉṟu
   tēṟṟikkoṇ ṭiruntaṉar.
   aṅṅaṉam irukkaiyil pārppaṉat tōḻaṉākiya icaiccaṉeṉpavaṉ, "yāṉ
   kūṟuvataik kēṭṭiṭuka; mantirattāl muṭiyāta kāriyam yātum illai.
   vācavatattai iṟantu vēṟu piṟappai aṭaintiruppiṉum paḻaiya uruvattōṭu
   avaḷait taruvataṟku urittāṉa mantiram oṉṟu uṇṭu. ataṉaik kaṟṟu
   vallōṉākiya antaṇaṉ oruvaṉ irācakiriyameṉṉum nakarattiṉpāl uḷḷāṉ.
   avaṉai aṭaintu vaḻipāṭāṟṟi vēṇṭiṉ, vācavatattaiyai yām peṟutal
   eḷiyatākum" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ, "aṅṅaṉam ceyyum mantiramum
   uṇṭō?" eṉṟu viyantu, talaiyeṭuppa anta vaḻiyē iṉi muyala vēṇṭum eṉṟu
   niccayittu, "iṅṅaṉam periyōr collak kēṭṭirukkiṟēṉ" eṉṟu icaiccaṉ piṉṉum
   kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ, "ippoḻutē avviṭattiṟku yām viraintu cella
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu collat tōḻarkaḷ avaṉaik kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum taṉiyē pirittu
   aḻaittukkoṇṭu vantu mikka āṟṟalaiyum kalviyaiyum uṭaiyavarkaḷākiya nūṟu
   vīrarkaḷai varuvittu vēṟu vaṭivam koḷḷumpaṭi colla, avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉamē
   koṇṭaṉar.
   aracaṉum tāmum yāttiraikku ēṟṟa pārppaṉa vaṭivaṅ koṇṭavarkaḷākip
   puṟappaṭṭup puṉṟāḷakameṉṉum nāṭṭaiyum kāḷa vaṉattaiyum kaṭantu
   karuppāca meṉṉum kāṭṭāṟu oṉṟait tāṇṭi appāluḷḷa kāṭṭiṉvaḻiyē ceṉṟaṉar.
   appōtu utayaṇaṉ, aṅkē tiriyum kalaimāṉ pātukāppap peṇmāṉ maruṇṭu nōkki
   niṟṟalaik kaṇṭu, vācavatattaiyiṉ pārvaiyai niṉaintu maṉam puḻuṅki,
   "piṇaiyē! eṉ uyirttuṇaivi ceṉṟa iṭattai nī kūṟuvāyāyiṉ yāṉ avviṭam
   ceṉṟu uyvēṉ; nī kūṟuka" eṉṟu pulampiyum appaṭiyē mayil, puṟavu, vaṇṭu,
   teṉṟal mutaliyavaṟṟait taṉittaṉiyē viḷittu araṟṟikkoṇṭum cellutalai
   aṟinta tōḻar iraṅki avaṉuṭaṉ,
   'tuṟakkam puriyun tollaiyi ṉiyaṉṟatu
       piṟappaṟa muyalum periyōr piṟantatu
       ciṟappiṭai yaṟāta tēcika muṭaiyatu
       maṟapperun takaiyatu māṟṟō rillatu
       viṟaṟpuka ḻuṭaiyatu vīriya mamaintatu
       ulakiṟ kellān tilakam pōlvatu
       alakai vēnta ṉāṇai kēṭpatu
       arampu mallaluṅ karampu millatu
       celvap peruṅkuṭi ciṟantaṇi peṟṟatu
       nalkura vāḷarai nāṭiṉu millatu
       naṉperum pulavar paṇpuḷip paṉṉiya
       pukaḻcci muṟṟā makiḻcciyiṉ malintatu
       iṉṉavai piṟavu meṇṇuvaram pikanta
       maṉperuñ ciṟappiṉ makatanaṉ ṉāṭu' (3.3:41.54)

   ceṉṟu cārntaṉar.
   cārntavarkaḷ avaṉukku mikka āṟutal colpavarkaḷāki an nāṭṭiṉ aintiṇai
   vaḷaṅkaḷaiyum nōkkik koṇṭē ataṉ talainakarākiya irācakiriyattai
   aṭaintu, ataṉ akaḻi, matil, palavakaic cātiyāruṭaiya cērikaḷ, nālvakai
   varuṇattāruṭaiya terukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai ellāṅ kaṇṭu mikavum
   uvakaiyuṟṟu uḷnakar ceṉṟaṉar. utayaṇaṉ ataṉ vaḷaṅkaḷaiyellāṅ kaṇṭu
   vācavatattaiyiṉ pirivu maṉattai varutta mikunta vāṭṭamuṟṟu avarkaḷuṭaṉ
   taṅkiṉāṉ; taṅkiyavaṉ,
   viḻupperuñ celvamoṭu veṉṟi tāṅkiya
       aimpati ṉiraṭṭi yavaṉac cēriyum
       eṇpati ṉiraṭṭi yeṟipaṭaip pāṭiyum
       aḷapparuñ ciṟappi ṉāyira mākiya
       talaipperuñ cēṉait tamiḻac cēriyum
       kolaipperuṅ kaṭuntiṟaṟ kollar cēriyum
       milaiccac cēriyun talaittalai ciṟantu
       vittaka viṉaiñar pattiyiṟ kuyiṟṟiya
       cittira cālaiyu mottiyain tōṅkiya
       oṭṭuviṉai māṭamuṅ koṭṭuviṉaik koṭṭilum
       taṇṇīrp pantarun takaiyamai cālaiyum
       aṟattiyal koṭṭilu mampalak kūṭamum
       maṟappōrk kōḻi marapiṟ poruttum
       viṟaṟpō rāṭavar virumpiya kaṇṇum
       maṟakkaḷi yāṉai vaṭikkum vaṭṭamum
       kaṭicel puravi muṭukum vītiyum
       aṭuttoli yaṟāa varaṅkamuṅ kaḻakamum
       aṟaccōṟ ṟaṭṭilu mampalac cālaiyum
       ..............................................................neruṅ
       ki' (3.4: 7 - 29)

   viḷaṅkuvatum ataṉ ayalil uḷḷatumāṉa puṟanakarattil kāmaṉkōyiliṉ
   pakkattēuḷḷa poykaik karaiyil viḷaṅkum iḷamarakkāvil irukkum tāpatap
   paḷḷi oṉṟil avarkaḷuṭaṉ taṅki vācavatattaiyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmal mikavum
   varuntuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   avaṉ uyiraik kāppāṟṟa niṉainta tōḻar aṅkēyuḷḷa kaṇkavar pēroḷik
   kākatuṇṭakareṉṉum oru periyavarai aṭaintu tammuṭaiya kuṟaikaḷaiyellām
   avariṭam viḷaṅkak kūṟi, utayaṇaṉukkut tairiyam uṇṭāka vēṇṭiya vaḻikaḷai
   iṉṉa iṉṉa vakaiyākac colla vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli uṭaṉpaṭuvittu avarai
   aḻaittu vantu avaṉukkuk kāṭṭi, " muṉṉam yām kūṟiya periyōr ivarē"
   eṉṟaṉar. utayaṇaṉ mikunta āvalōṭu avar kūṟuvataik kēṭpataṟku virumpi
   avarai vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ. avar avaṉai nōkki, "iraṇṭu mātam viratattōṭē nīr
   iṅkē iruttal vēṇṭum. appāl īmattil yām ceyya vēṇṭiya cātaṉai oṉṟu uṇṭu;
   atu muṭintapiṉ vācavatattai paḻaiya uruvattōṭu varutal orutalai.
   ummuṭaiya irācciyamum umakkuk kiṭaikkum. itaṉai nīr nampum eṉṟu collik
   kaikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya viratattaiyum ataṉ vitiyaiyum terivittut tammiṭam
   ceṉṟaṉar.
   kēṭṭa urumaṇṇuvā mutaliyōr, "nilattiṟ pukutalum vicumpiṟ cellutalum
   kaṭal nīrai vāyk" koṇṭu umiḻtalum malaiyai ēntutalum ākiya ivai pōṉṟa
   viccai payiṉṟavarkaḷaik kaṇkūṭākavē kaṇṭirukkiṟōm, cettōrai mīṭkum
   viccaiyai uṭaiyōrai ituvaraiyiṟ kēṭṭum aṟintilam. perum puṇṇiyam
   uṭaiyōmātaliṉ ivaraik kaṇṭōm.
   ivar vācavatattaiyai varuvittu aḷittal orutalai" eṉṟu viyappuṟṟuk
   kūṟiyavarāki aracaṉait tēṟṟip puṟanakarattē muṉpu kūṟiya iṭattil
   avaṉuṭaṉ taṅkiṉar. "nam aracaṉai makata maṉṉaṉukku naṇpaṉāka
   eppaṭiyēṉum ceytuviṭa vēṇṭum" eṉṟa ālōcaṉai avarkaḷukku mikutiyāka
   iruntatu.
   appōtu annakaril kāmatēvaṉukku viḻā naṭantatu. naṭakkaiyil aracaṉ
   kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi, "patumāpati kāmaṉai vaḻipaṭutaṟku iṉṟu celvāḷ;
   nakarattai alaṅkarittiṭumiṉ" eṉṟu vaḷḷuva mutumakaṉ muracaṟaintu
   terivittaṉaṉ. kēṭṭavarkaḷ aṅkaṅkē alaṅkārañ ceyvittārkaḷ.
   tirumakaḷāyiṉum uruviṉum uṇarviṉum oppumaiyāṟṟāp patumāpati, nīrāṭi
   alaṅkārañ ceytukoṇṭu yāppiyāyiṉi mutaliya tōḻiyarum kāñcukiyar
   mutaliyōrum cūḻa oru vaiyattai ūrntu cellukaiyil, atu puṟanakarattē
   uḷḷa kāmaṉ kōṭṭattu vāyilil vantu niṉṟatu.
   vaiyam niṉṟa poḻutu ataṉai mūṭiya tirai kāṟṟāl vilakiyatu. vilakavē
   ataṉuḷḷē irunta patumāpatiyai etiril puṉṉaimarattiṉ kīḻē
   vācavatattaiyiṉ niṉaivuṭaṉ irunta utayaṇaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. patumāpatiyum
   avaṉaik kaṇṭāḷ; oruvarai oruvar virumpi maṉam kuḻaintārkaḷ.
   vācavatattaikkum avaḷukkum vēṟṟumai tōṉṟāmaiyāl, "nām karuti vanta
   periyōr vācavatattaiyai mīṭṭu aḷittaṉarō?" eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ eṇṇiṉāṉ;
   "ivaṉ, taṉṉai vaḻipaṭavanta eṉatu niṟaiyai aḷattaṟku eṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiya
   kāmatēvaṉō?aṉṟi antaṇa vaṭivattōṭu tōṉṟiya vēṟu tēvakumāraṉō?" eṉṟu
   patumai eṇṇiṉaḷ. appoḻutu tōḻi vantu vaṇaṅkiṉaḷ. avaḷ tōḷaip
   paṟṟikkoṇṭu vaṇṭiyiliruntu iṟaṅkiya patumāpati, kōyiliṉuḷḷē ceṉṟu
   kāmatēvaṉuṭaiya māṭattai valamvantu, vitippaṭi tāṉaṅkaḷaic ceytu
   nōṉpiyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉaḷ. toṭaṅkiṉavaḷ, taṉ muṉṉē niṉṟa mutiya
   kāvalāḷarkaḷai nōkki, "iṅkē tāṉam peṟa varum antaṇarkaḷait
   taṭaiceyyāmal uḷḷē varac ceymmiṉ" eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaḷ.
   "ellā nalamum amainta utayaṇaṉaippōṉṟa talaivaṉai ivaḷ aṭaitaṟkuriyaḷ"
   eṉṟu aṅkē niṉṟa mutiyavaḷ orutti oru pāṭṭaip pāṭiṉaḷ. ataṉaik kēṭṭa
   patumai, 'ippōtu puṉṉaiyiṉ kīḻē niṉṟōṉ avaṉaip pōṉṟavaṉē' eṉṟu
   karutiṉaḷ. viḻāviṉatu mutal nāḷiṟ ceyya vēṇṭiya tāṉaṅkaḷ vitippaṭi
   ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. appoḻutu mutumakaḷir, "cūriyaṉ attamittaṟku muṉpē nām
   kaṉṉimāṭam celvatu nalam" eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. kūṟavē cellutaṟku avaḷuṭaiya
   maṉam taṭumāṟiyatu. tōḻimār vantu ēṟṟa, patumāpati vaiyamēṟik
   kaṉṉimāṭam ceṉṟaṉaḷ. ceṉṟapiṉ, "ivaḷuṭaiya nōṉpu inta viḻāvil
   muṭivupeṟum; antak kālattil ivaḷ tāṉam ceyvāḷ; vāṅka varupavarkaḷai
   nīvir vilakka vēṇṭām" eṉṟu cilatiyar coṟṟaṉar. ataik kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ,
   "mīṭṭum ivaḷai nām pārttal kūṭum" eṉṟu taṉṉuṭaiya maṉattiṟku āṟutal
   ceytukoṇṭiruntāṉ.
   iruntapoḻutu piṉṉē mellac cellum airāvatiyeṉṉum avaḷ tōḻiyaik kaṇṭu,
   "ivaḷ yār? ivaḷ peyareṉṉa? ic cōlaiyiṉkaṇ ivaḷ vanta kāriyam yātu?"
   eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ viṉaviṉaṉ. "inta antaṇāḷaṉ poruḷ nacaiyāl vantavaṉ" eṉṟu
   niṉaittu airāvati, "ivaḷ innakarattu aracaṉ paṭṭattut tēviyākiya,
   civamatiyeṉpavaḷuṭaiya cakōtariyum kācirācaṉ maṉaiviyumāṉa utayaiyōṭai
   eṉpavaḷuṭaiya makaḷ; patumāpatiyeṉṉum pēruṭaiyāḷ; kaliyāṇamākātavaḷ;
   maṉmataṉukku nakaram ceyyum viḻāviṉ muṭivil vētiyarkaḷukku
   vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik koṭukka eṇṇiyirukkiṉṟāḷ. poruḷil viruppam iruppiṉ nīr
   iṅkē irum" eṉṟu coṉṉataṉṟi, "ummuṭaiya nāṭu etu? ūr etu? kōttiram
   yātu?" eṉṟum kēṭṭaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ avaḷ kēṭṭataik kuṟittu uḷḷē makiḻntu,
   "eṉṉuṭaiya nāṭu kāntāram; ūr irattiṉapuram; eṉṉuṭaiya tantai
   cāṇṭiyaṉeṉṉum antaṇaṉ; eṉ peyarai māṇakaṉeṉṟu colluvar" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
   kēṭṭa airāvati makiḻntu viraintu kaṉṉimāṭañ ceṉṟāḷ.
   appāl utayaṇaṉ tōḻarai aṭaintāṉ. cūriyaṉ attamittatu; mullai
   malarntatu; tāmaraikaḷ kuvintaṉa; piṉṉum uḷḷa mālaikkāla nikaḻccikaḷaik
   kaṇṭu kāma vēkam atikarikkappeṟṟa utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyai maṟantu
   patumāpatiyiṉ aḻakaiyē niṉaintu,
   "vaṉappamai vaiyam taṉakkumaṟai yākiya
       kañcikai kaṭuvaḷi yeṭuppa mañciṭai
       vāṉara makaḷiriṟ ṟāṉaṇi cuṭara
       mukainalak kāntaṇ mukiḻvira ṉōvat
       takaimalarp poykait taṇceṅ kaḻunīr
       cilleṉap piṭittu melleṉa viḻintu
       naṇṇa varuvōḷ pōlu meṉkaṇ
       āṟṟēṉ; (3.7: 47-54)

   tavañceytēṉum yāṉ ivaḷai aṭaivēṉ" eṉṟu tuṇintu taṉ karuttait
   tēḻarkaḷukkuk kūṟi varuntiṉaṉ.
   ceṉṟa patumāpatiyum avaṉaiyē niṉaintu varuntip
   piṉpu amaḷiyil ēṟi,
   'naṟumalark kāviṉuṭ ṭuṟumiya pūntuṇark
       koṭikkuruk kattik koḻuntaḷir piṭittu
       nāṇmalarp puṉṉait tāṇmuta laṇaintu
       paruku vaṉṉa nōkkamoṭu paiyāntu
       uruku muḷḷamō ṭorumara ṉoṭuṅki
       niṉṟōṉ pōlavu meṉṟōḷ paṟṟi
       akalat toṭukki nukarvōṉ pōlavum
       arimalar neṭuṅka ṇakavayiṟ pōkāp
       purinūṉ mārpaṉ puṇṇiya naṟuntōḷ
       tīṇṭum vāyil yātuko leṉṟutaṉ
       māṇṭa cūḻcci maṉattē maṟuki
       āci laṇiyiḻai tīyayal vaitta
       meḻukucey pāvaiyi ṉuruku neñciṉaḷ
       paḷḷi koḷḷā ḷuḷḷupu vatiya (3.7: 77-90)

   ivvaṇṇam iruvarum iruvayiṉotta iyaṟkai nōkkamoṭu oruvayiṉotta uḷḷa
   nōyai uṭaiyavarkaḷāyt tuṉpuṟṟaṉar; anta irāttiriyāṉatu kolla vantatu
   pōlavē avarkaḷukkut tōṟṟi 1ēḻiruḷ pōlac ceṉṟatu.
   ---------------
       1 "nerunaṟṟuc ceṉṟāreṅ kātalar yāmum, eḻunāḷē mēṉi pacantu"
       tirukkuṟaḷ, 1288.

   maṟunāḷ kālaiyil mīṭṭum avaṉaik kāṇa vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppattai
   maṉattilēyē aṭakkikkoṇṭu patumāpati kaṇkaḷaik kaḻuvit teyvattai
   vaḻipaṭṭuk kavalaiyuṭaṉ iruntāḷ. iruntapoḻutu avaḷuṭaiya vēṟupāṭṭaik
   kaṇṭa cevilittāy mutaliyōrkaḷ palavāṟu kavalaiyuṟṟuc camayam pārttu,
   "vaiyamēṟic cōlaikkuc celvōmā?" eṉṟu avaḷai viṉāviṉar. avaḷ, "nōṉpu
   muṟṟumvaraiyil eṉṉaik kēṭka vēṇṭuvatillai; aracaṉuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷai
   illaiyāyiṉum cella vēṇṭuvatē muṟai" eṉṟaṉaḷ. uṭaṉē avarkaḷ avaḷai
   vaiyattil ēṟṟic cōlaikku aṉuppiṉar. aṉuppiyapoḻutu mutalnāḷiṟpōlap
   puṉṉaimarattiṉ kīḻē niṉṟa utayaṇaṉum avaḷum oruvarai oruvar nōkkit tam
   viḻaivaip pulappaṭuttikkoṇṭu mayaṅkiṉar.ippaṭiyē cila tiṉaṅkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.
   orunāḷ aṅkē vanta patumāpati taṉ kāmanōyai veḷippaṭuttum
   ūkkamuṭaiyavaḷāki airāvatiyiṉ kaḻuttil taṉ kaiyaic cērttut taḻuvikkoṇṭu
   oru maṇṭapattiṉmēl ēṟi niṉṟāḷ. appaṭiyē utayaṇaṉ taṉ viruppattai
   veḷippaṭuttum kuṟippōṭu vayantakaṉ tōḷai oru kaiyāl taḻuvi maṟṟoru
   kaiyāl mika aḻakitākiya pūmpantoṉṟai uruṭṭik koṇṭu puṉṉaiyiṉkīḻē
   niṉṟāṉ. aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa vaṉaik kaṇṭa patumāpati airāvatiyai nōkki,
   "antaṇa uruvattōṭu aṅkē niṟpavaṉ yāvaṉ? nī avaṉai aṟivāyō?" eṉṟu
   viṉāviṉaḷ, airāvati, "muṉṉorunāḷ nī kaṉṉimāṭam ceṉṟapiṉpu nāṉ aṉpiṉ
   mikutiyāl avaṉ varalāṟṟaik kēṭṭu aṟintu koṇṭēṉ. māṇakaṉeṉpatu avaṉ
   peyar. avaṉ antaṇaṉ; innāṭu pārttaṟku vantāṉ. avaṉum avaṉuṭaiya
   tōḻarkaḷum uṉṉuṭaiya tāṉattai virumpiyē iṅkē tāmatittaṉar pōlum.
   viraivil tirumpic celvārallar" eṉṟaṉaḷ.
   kēṭṭa patumai, "piṟar aṟiyāmal kuṟippittu avaṉ kaiyiluḷḷa pantai vāṅkit
   tarutal kūṭumō?" eṉṉa, airāvati kaiyait taṭṭi avaṉai nōkki, "uṉ
   kaippantai ivaḷukkut tara vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kaṇṇālum kaiyālum avaṉukkut
   terivittaṉaḷ.ataṉai aṟintu maṉattil aṭakkikkoṇṭa avaṉ, "tattaiyai
   niṉaintu uḻalum eṉ maṉattait taṉpāl ivaḷ iḻuttukkoṇṭu taṉ maṉattai
   eṉṉiṭattē niṟuttik kalanta nēyam uḷḷavaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉaḷ" eṉṟu tōḻarkku
   uṇarttiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa tōḻaruḷ icaicca ṉeṉpavaṉ, "inta nāṭṭu makaḷir
   niṟaiyai uṭaiyār; maṉattaip piṟarpāṟ celuttār. aracaṉuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷaiyum
   atuvē. patumāpatiyum attaṉmaiyaḷē. aḵtaṉṟāyiṉ taṉ maṉaivi tīppaṭṭā
   ḷeṉpatu terintu varunti utayaṇaṉ pārppaṉa vēṭaṅkoṇṭu vantuḷāṉeṉpatai
   aṟintōr collak kēṭṭu avaṉiṭam nammuṭaiya karuttait terivittal kuṇameṉṟu
   taṉṉuṭaiya karuttai veḷiyiṭṭiruttal kūṭum. aṉṟāyiṉ tōṟṟappolivāl mikka
   ivarkaḷ vantu tāṉam ēṟṟal takuti aṉṟu eṉṟu naṉmatippōṭum nammai
   nōkkiyiruttal kūṭum. ātalāl, avaḷiṭattil viruppattaic celuttutal
   muṟaiyaṉṟu" eṉṟu taṭuttaṉaṉ. maṟṟat tōḻarkaḷum ak karuttaiyē taḻuvik
   kūṟiṉar.
   avaḷukkum taṉakkum uḷḷa aṉpai aṟiyāta maṟṟat tōḻarkaḷait tēṟṟa vēṇṭi
   utayaṇaṉ, "malar mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ceyta kaṇṇiyai ippoykaiyiṉ pakkattuḷḷa
   marakkiḷai oṉṟil avaḷ kāṇak kaṭṭi viṭṭu nāṉ maṟaintiruppēṉ. avaḷ ak
   kaṇṇiyaik kaikkoḷvāḷāyiṉ yāṉ kūṟiyatai uṇmaiyeṉṟu koḷka" eṉṟu
   avarkaḷukkuk kūṟiṉaṉ. piṉpu avaṉ appaṭiyē kaṇṇiyoṉṟu kaṭṭit tāṉ
   karutiya cōlaiyiṟ puṉṉai, ñāḻal, makiḻ ākiya maraṅkaḷiṉ ceṟiviṟ
   kiḷaiyoṉṟil avaḷ kāṇumpaṭi ataṉaik kaṭṭiviṭṭup piṉṉum tāṉ avaḷai
   mutalil kaṇṭa kāmaṉ kōṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum tammuḷ nikaḻnta ceytikaḷai
   pulap paṭuttum cittiraṅkaḷaiyum vāḻaik kuruttil nakattāṟ poṟittu
   avaṟṟai āṇṭuḷḷa ñāḻal marattiṉ kiḷaiyil toṅkac ceytuviṭṭut tāṉ ayaliṭañ
   ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   patumāpati, yāppiyāyiṉiyōṭu ceṉṟu appoy kaiyil nīrāṭit taṉṉai
   alaṅkarittukkoṇṭu karaiyil taṉiyē ceṉṟu cōlaiyaip pārttapoḻutu aṅkē
   utayaṇaṉ kaṭṭi nāṟṟiya kaṇṇi mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭāḷ. kaṇṭu
   puṉmuṟuvalkoṇṭu, oru kaḻunīrp pūvaik kaiyil paṟṟi ataṉ taṇṭil taṉ peyar
   poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa mōtirattaic ceṟittu ataṉaiyum piṇaiyalaiyum
   cantaṉattaiyum avaṉ vaitta kaṇṇiyaic cāra orupāl vaittaṉaḷ. piṉpu avaṉ
   vaitta kaṇṇi mutaliyavaṟṟai eṭuttuvantu akkaṇṇiyait taṉ ceṉṉiyil aṇintu
   piṟavaṟṟai mārpil appikkoṇṭu mika aḻaku peṟṟu viḷaṅkiniṉṟāḷ. niṉṟavaḷai
   aṅku vanta yāppiyāyiṉi kaṇṭu, "nī yār? uṉṉaippōṉṟavaḷum eṉ uyirttōḻiyum
   tarucakaṉ taṅkaiyumākiya patumāpatiyai yāṉ pirintēṉ" eṉṟaṉaḷ. taṉ
   vēṟupāṭṭai aṟintu avaḷ kēṭkiṉṟāḷeṉṟu patumāpati nāṇi mukam kaviḻntaṉaḷ.
   piṉpu avviruvarum tammiṭañ ceṉṟārkaḷ.
   aṅkē nikaḻntavaṟṟai maṟaintirunta urumaṇṇuvā kaṇṭu maṟrait tōḻarkkuk
   kūṟiṉaṉ. ellōrum otta maṉamuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiṉar. vayantakaṉ ceṉṟu ārāyntu
   avaḷ utayaṇaṉukkāka aṅkē vait tirunta piṇaiyal, cāntam, kaḻunīrppū,
   maṇiyāḻi ivaṟṟaik kaṇṭu eṭuttuvantu utayaṇaṉukkuk koṭuttaṉaṉ. avaṉ yūki
   mutaliyavarkaḷaip peṟṟavaṉpōla makiḻvuṟṟup patumāpati āṭiya poykaiyil
   nīrāṭi avaḷ vaitta cantaṉattai mārpilum tōḷilum pūci ap piṇaiyalaic
   cūṭi mōtirattaip piṟar aṟintukoḷḷātapaṭi taṉ viraliṟ cērttik
   kaḻunīrmalaraik kaiyiṟ piṭittukkoṇṭu ciṟitum kavalaiyē illāmal
   mukamalarcciyōṭum pōntapoḻutu māṭattiṉmēlē niṉṟa patumāpati kaṇṭu, "
   yām kaṇṇāṟ kūṭiya kūṭṭamaṉṟik kaṇṇimutaliyavaṟṟālum
   kalappuṭaiyēmāyiṉēm. neñcē! kavalaippaṭāmal iru" eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ cila
   vārttaikaḷaic collikkoṇṭu taṉṉiṭam ceṉṟaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ taṉakku avaḷ
   urimaipūṇṭaṉaḷeṉṟu tōḻarkkut terivittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟu kavalai yiṉṟit
   tuyiṉṟāṉ.
   appoḻutu avaṉuṭaiya kaṉavil vācavatattai melintu vāṭiya uṭampōṭu
   tōṉṟiṉaḷ. tōṉṟavē
   "mācil kaṟpiṉ vācava tattāy!
       vaṉka ṇāḷaṉēṉ puṉkaṇ tīra
       vantaṉaiyō" (3.1;158-60)

   eṉa utayaṇaṉ, vāytiṟantu araṟṟiṉaṉ. araṟṟavē avaḷ, "ayalnāṭṭiṟku eṉṉait
   tuṟantu ceṉṟāy; aṉpuṭaiyārpōl kūṟuṅ kaṭṭuraiyai oḻivāyāka" eṉṟu colli
   akalvāḷāyiṉaḷ. akalavē avaṉ kuṟuki, "uṉṉaip piḻaippittut tarupavaṉ
   ivvūril uḷḷāṉ eṉpataik kēṭṭē pala malaikaḷaiyum kāṭukaḷaiyuṅ kaṭantu
   iṅkē vantēṉ. nī vekuḷātē" eṉṟu irakka, avaḷ, " patumāpatiyiṉiṭattē
   aṉpuṟṟu avaḷ vaitta mālaiyaiyum cantaṉattaiyum ippoḻutu aṇintāyaṉṟō"
   eṉṟu colliviṭṭup piṉṉum nīṅka avaṉ, " patumāpatiyeṉkiṉṟa peyarai
   iṉṟutāṉ niṉ vāyāl kēṭṭēṉ. vēṟu aṟiyēṉ" eṉṟu piṉṉum irakkumpoḻutu avaḷ
   maṟaintaṉaḷ. cūriyaṉ utittaṉaṉ.
   viḻitteḻunta utayaṇaṉ tāṉkaṇṭa kaṉavait tōḻarkkuk kūṟiṉaṉ. avarkaḷ,
   "vācavatattai ciṉaṅ koṇṭāḷātalāl, patumaiyiṉ mālai mutaliyavaṟṟai nī
   aṇintatu piḻaiyākum: ivaḷpāl vaitta kāmattai viṭṭoḻivāyāka" eṉṟu mika
   vaṟpuṟuttavum avaṉ taṉ koḷkaiyai viṭāṉākip patumaiyai aṭaivataṟku mikka
   muyaṟci ceyvāṉāyiṉaṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṇuṟṟa tōḻar, "ivaṉait tarucakaṉukku
   naṇpaṉākac ceytaṟku vanta namakkuiṅṅaṉam nērntataṟkuk kāraṇam
   ūḻviṉaiyiṉ vali" eṉṟu maṉamuvantu ālōcippārāyiṉar. appoḻutu utayaṇaṉ,
   "patumaiyōṭu māṭattiṟ pukuntu avaḷuṭaṉ irēṉāyiṉ eṉ uḷḷam kalaṅkāniṟkum"
   eṉṟu taṉkuṟaiyait terivittaṉaṉ. tōḻar, "avaḷuṭaiya māṭattil nī
   pukuvāyāyiṉ uṇṭākun tīṅkukaḷ iṉṉavai" eṉpataik kūṟit taṭuppa, utayaṇaṉ
   uṭaṉpaṭṭāṉāki oru nāḷ 1 tōḻarkaḷaip pirintu mutumai vēṭam pūṇṭu, tāṉam
   vāṅkac celpavarkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟu kāmaṉ kōṭṭattiṉ uḷḷē irukkum kaṭiyaṟaiyil
   oruvarum aṟiyātapaṭi oḷintiruntāṉ.
   ----------
       1 itaṉ mēluḷḷa mūlappakuti kaiyeḻuttuppiratiyiṟ citaintu
       pōyviṭṭamaiyāl, ataṟkup piṉṉum muṉṉum uḷḷa ātāraṅkaḷaikkoṇṭum vēṟu
       nūlkaḷaikkoṇṭum ārāyntu katai iṅkē eḻutalāyiṟṟu.

   irukkaiyil, patumāpati taṉiyē avvaṟaiyiṉ uḷḷiṭattai aṭaintāḷ.
   aṭaintapoḻutu utayaṇaṉ taṉ iḷamaik kōlattōṭu etirē niṉṟāṉ. niṉṟavaṉai
   maṉam tuṭkeṉakkaṇṭu avaḷ nāṇik koṭi pōla acaintu otuṅki niṉṟāḷ. avaṉ
   pārāṭṭi, "tīṇṭilēṉāyiṉ uyyēṉ; aruḷ" eṉṟu vēṇṭi ṉaṉ. appōtu
   avviruvarukkum kāntaruva maṇam naṭaipeṟṟatu. piṉpu avaḷ puṟampōntu
   tōḻimārōṭu vaiyam ūrntu taṉ māṭañ ceṉṟāḷ. avaḷ ceṉṟapiṉ, piṟar aṟiyāmaṟ
   puṟampōnta utayaṇaṉ, oru nāḷ vēṟṟu vaṭivattōṭu tarucakaṉ araṇmaṉai
   vāyilai aṭaintu aṅkē niṉṟa pēraṟivāḷaṉ oruvaṉaik kaṇṭu,
   "tarucakarācaṉukku enta vittaiyil viruppam uṇṭu? " eṉṟu viṉāva, avaṉ,
   "taṉṉuṭaiya takappaṉ īṭṭiya perumporuḷ vaikkappaṭṭa iṭam teriyāmaiyāl
   tarucakaṉ kavalaiyuṟṟu varuntukiṉṟāṉ. ataṉai aṟintu colvōrukku avaṉ
   taṉṉuṭaiya uyiraiyum aḷippāṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kūṟavē., "putaiyal eṭukkum
   vittaiyilum kīḻnīrkaḷai aṟiyum nūlkaḷilum nāṉ mikka payiṟci uṭaiyēṉ;
   aracaṉaip pārttalil eṉakku viruppam mikutiyāka uṇṭu" eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ
   colla, am mutiyōṉ ceṉṟu, " putaiyal eṭuttal mutaliya vittaikaḷil mikka
   payiṟciyuḷḷa oruvar nummaik kāṇutaṟku viruppaṅkoṇṭu vāyilil vantu
   kāttirukkiṉṟaṉar" eṉṟu tarucakaṉiṭam terivittāṉ. terivikkavē tarucakaṉ,
   "kaṟṟavarkaḷaik kāṇpatu aracāṭciyāl ākiya payaṉākum" eṉṟu colli avaṉai
   aḻaittuvarac ceytu taṉiyē ceṉṟu ōr ācaṉattil iruttik kaṟṟa
   vittaikaḷaiyellām viṉāvi aṟintu, "eṉṉuṭaiya tantai putaitta poruḷkaḷai
   yāṉ aṟiyumpaṭi ceyya vēṇṭum" eṉṟu terivikka, utayaṇaṉ tāṉ putaittu
   vaitta poruḷkaḷ uḷḷa iṭattaik kāṭṭupavaṉ pōlavē avaṉukku avviṭattai
   kāṭṭiṉāṉ.
   kāṭṭavē avaṟṟaik kaṇṭu aracaṉ mikavum viyappuṟṟu,"ivaṉ peṟutaṟkariyaṉ.
   etaṉai virumpiṉālum ataṉai ivaṉukkuk koṭuttaṟkuk kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ" eṉṟu
   collip piṉpu, "ivvaraṇmaṉaiyilum itaṉaic cārnta iṭaṅkaḷilum nalla nīr
   irukkumāyiṉ ataṉaiyum kāṭṭa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kūṟa, utayaṇaṉ appaṭiyē nīr
   nilaikaḷai aṟitaṟkuriya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai muṟaiyē terivittataṉṟi nalla
   nīruḷḷa iṭattai akaḻvittukkāṭṭi makiḻvippa, tarucakaṉ makiḻntu avaṉai
   araṇmaṉaiyiṉ akattē irukkumpaṭi collip poṉṉaṟaip pātu kāvalarākiya
   kaṇakku viṉaiyāḷarai avaṉuṭaṉ iruntu pātukākkumpaṭi ceytaṉaṉ.
   utayaṇaṉ aṅkiruppavaṉpōlat taṉṉaik kāṭṭip piṟar aṟiyāmal uriya kālattiṟ
   ceṉṟu kāmaṉ kōṭṭattuḷḷa kaṭiyaṟaiyil iruntu varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. ataṉai
   aṟinta urumaṇṇuvā vēṟṟu vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu ceṉṟu aracaṉpāl oru vēlaiyaip
   peṟṟu ataṉai naṉku ceypavaṉāki araṇmaṉaiyilēyē iruntu utayaṇaṉaip
   pātukāttu varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. aṅṅaṉamē icaiccaṉum vayantakaṉum avaṉaip
   pātukāttaṟ poruṭṭu vēṟṟu vaṭivaṅ koṇṭu tarumanūl mutaliyavaṟṟaip
   patumaiyiṉ tāykkuc colpavarkaḷākiyum kaṇṭōr viyakkumpaṭi malarkaḷaip
   palavakaip piṇaiyal kaḷākak kaṭṭiyum kaṭṭuvittum patumaikku
   aṉuppupavarkaḷākiyum aṅkē iruntaṉar. uṭaṉvanta ēṉaivīrarkaḷum vēṟu vēṟu
   vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu tattamakku ēṟṟa toḻilkaḷaic ceypavarkaḷākic camīpamāṉa
   vēṟu vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkiṉar.
   ippaṭi irukkaiyil patumai utayaṇaṉait taṉṉuṭaṉ kaṉṉimāṭattiṟkup piṟar
   aṟiyāmal aḻaittuk koṇṭu cella niṉaintu ataṟkuriya upāyam oṉṟai ārāyntu,
   "palavakaiyāṉa tāṉaṅkaḷaic ceytaṟku niccayittirukkiṟēṉ" eṉṟu colli
   ataṉaip palarum aṟiyac ceytaṉaḷ. ceytapiṉ civikaiyai varuvittu atil
   ēṟic ceṉṟu kāmaṉkōṭṭattiṉ vāyilil iṟaṅkit tāṉ irukkum aṟaiyiṉ vāyiliṟ
   civikaiyai vaikkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭuviṭṭut taṉiyē aṟaiyuṭ ceṉṟu aṅkē
   otuṅkiyirunta utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu uvantu avaṉuṭaṉ aṅkē kālattaip
   pōkkiṉaḷ. piṉpu taṉatu nōṉpiṟkukuriya nāṭkaḷiṉ iṟutit tiṉam aṉṟātalāl
   vanta yāvarkkum maṇiyum, muttum, pavaḻamum, mācaiyum, aṇiyum, āṭaiyum,
   ācil uṇṭiyum, pūvum, nāṉamum, pūcuñ cāntamum yāvai yāvai avaiyavai
   avaravar vēṇṭārāyiṉum īṇṭa vīci avarkaḷuṭaiya ācikaḷaip peṟṟu avarkaḷai
   avaḷ aṉuppiṉaḷ. piṉpu kāmaṉ kōyilil maṇiviḷakkēṟṟi makaḷir pāṭiṉar.
   appoḻutu uyirttōḻiyākiya yāppiyāyiṉi avaḷatu kuṟippaṟintu vantu āṅku
   niṉṟārai nōkki, "em iṟaivi iṉṟu paṭṭiṉiyuṟṟāḷ. jaṉa nerukkattāl
   veppamaṭaintu varuntukiṟāḷ. nīvir ellōrum puṟattē cellumiṉ" eṉṟu colla
   ellōrum uṭaṉē puṟattē pōyiṉar.
   maṟaintu vantu kañcikaic civikaiyil utayaṇaṉ taṅkiṉaṉ; patumaiyum ceṉṟu
   atil taṅkiṉaḷ. appoḻutu curukkuttiraiyai virittu ac civikaiyai
   maṟaittaṉar. kāvalāḷarkaḷ, "ic civikaiyaik kaṉṉi māṭattuḷḷa
   paḷḷiyaṟaiyiṉuḷḷē ceṉṟu vaittiṭuka" eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu piṉceṉṟaṉar. yārum
   aṇukātapaṭi kaṭuṅkāvalarākiya cilata mākkaḷ ucumpik koṇṭu ayaliṭañ
   ceṉṟārkaḷ. puṟa nakarattuḷḷa kāvum vāviyum kāmaṉkōṭṭamum pūvīḻkoṭiyiṟ
   polivilavāka akanakarañ ceṉṟu pala vītikaḷaiyuṅ kaṭantu araṇmaṉaiyuṭ
   ceṉṟu kaṉṉimāṭam pukuntu mallar uḷḷē ceṉṟu civikaiyai vaittaṉar.
   appoḻutu patumai, "eṉakku mikavum veppam uṇṭākiṉṟatu; ellōrum pōmiṉ"
   eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭavē, āṅkuc ceṟintu niṉṟa yāvarum puṟattē ceṉṟaṉar.uṭaṉē
   tiraiyai akaṟṟip patumai taṉṉuṭaiya paḷḷiyaṟaiyiṟ ceṉṟu pukuntaṉaḷ.
   yāppiyāyiṉi paḷḷiyaṟaiyiṉ nāṟpuṟattuñ ceṉṟu ārāyantu yārum illai
   eṉpataic coṉṉapiṉpu, utayaṇaṉ piṟar aṟiyāmaṟ ceṉṟu aṅkē taṅki mikka
   makiḻcciyuṭaiyavaṉāyiṉaṉ. "yāvarāy iruppiṉum camayam aṟiyāmal iṅkē
   vāraṟka" eṉpataip patumai tōḻimārkkut terivittataṉṟi, "yāḻum pāṭṭum iṉi
   naṉṟākap payilum eṇṇam uṭaiyēṉātaliṉ eṉakkuriya uṇavu vakaikaḷ iṅkēyē
   varuvaṉavāka" eṉṟum kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaḷ. aṅṅaṉamē avai varuvaṉavāyiṉa. piṉ
   avaḷ eḻunilai māṭattil uyarnilai aṟaiyiṟ ceṉṟu āṇṭuḷḷa aṇaiyiṉmicai
   utayaṇaṉōṭu iruntu aḷavaḷāvi makiḻcciyaṭaintiruntaṉaḷ.
   iruntapoḻutu utayaṇaṉ oru nāḷ nāṟpuṟattum uḷḷa nuṭpamāṉa
   ciṟpavēlaikaḷaip pārttup pārttu viyappuṟṟāṉ. patumāpati, avaṉ taṉṉaip
   pārāmal vēṟiṭattiṟ karuttaic celuttikkoṇṭē iruttalai aṟintu pulantu
   kaṭiya coṟkaḷaic colli uṭal viyarttuk kaṇṇīr perukkit taṉṉuṭaiya
   aṇikalaṅkaḷaiyum mālaikaḷaiyum eṭuttu eṟintuviṭṭuk kalaṅkuvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
   ataṉai aṟinta utayaṇaṉ, "naṅkāy! yāṉ yātoru kuṟṟamum ceytilēṉ;
   niṉaittum ilēṉ; varuntal vēṇṭā; poṟuttiṭuka" eṉṟu pala muṟai
   kuṟaiyirantu avaḷuṭaiya puṟattait taivantu tōṭṭaik kātil aṇintu maṟṟa
   āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum tirutti nalam pārāṭṭic cantaṉattaip pūcit
   taḻuvuvataṟku neruṅkukaiyil avaḷ, "eṉṉait tīṇṭātīr" eṉṟu ciṉan turaittu
   vēṟiṭañ cellap puṭaipeyarntaṉaḷ. appoḻutu, pakkattuḷḷa pūñcōlaiyiṉ
   kiḷaiyilirunta kōṭṭāṉ oṉṟu vantu ammāṭattiṉmēl uḷḷa cūlattil ēṟiyiruntu
   kēṭpōr añcumpaṭi olittatu. olikkavē patumai naṭukkamuṟṟu ōṭivantu
   utayaṇaṉait taḻuvikkoṇṭāḷ. utayaṇaṉ avaḷuṭaiya accamuyakkattai mika
   virumpi, "añcaṟka" eṉṟu kūṟik kūkaiyiṉ utaviyai neñcattuḷḷē pārāṭṭi
   avalaip pulliyum tiḷaittum, "amararākkiya amiḻtē!" eṉap pukaḻntum
   oḻukuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   iṅṅaṉam oḻukum nāṭkaḷil oru nāḷ patumai tōḻiyai aḻaittu, "ivaṉ kaṟṟa
   vittaikaḷai nām ārāyntu aṟiya vēṇṭum" eṉṟu colla, tōḻi avaṉai
   viṉāviṉaḷ. viṉāvavē avaṉ, "vēḷvikkuriya vittaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vallēṉ.
   eṉṉuṭaiya maṉaivi virumpiyapoḻutu kuṭamuḻaveṉṉum vāttiyattai avaṭkup
   payiṟṟiṉēṉ" eṉṟaṉaṉ. "atil valla ivvantaṇaṉ icaiyilum vallavaṉāka
   iruttal kūṭum. ivaṉ cuvaittoḻil makaṉ" eṉṟu collip patumai nakaittut
   taṉ yāḻai varuvittut tāṉ vācittaṟku āṟṟātavaḷāki, "itaṉai ivaṉiṭam
   koṭu" eṉṟu tōḻikkuk kuṟippāl uṇarttiṉaḷ. avaḷ appaṭiyē ceṉṟu ataṉai
   avaṉiṭaṅ koṭuttu, "itaṉaic ceppañceytu taruka" eṉṟaṉaḷ. avaṉ,
   "emmuṭaiya kulattiṟku ovvāta itaṉai vācikkumpaṭi eṉṉai varuttātu oḻika.
   yāḻ vittaiyai iṉik kaṟṟaṟku virumpiṉēṉ" eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ, "narampukaḷai
   valittu iṟukkit tāṉattil iruttalaiyē yām virumpiṉēm" eṉṟāḷ. avaṉ,
   "appaṭiyāṉāl oruvāṟu pārppēṉ; ataṉaik koṭu" eṉṟu colli vāṅkit tivavai
   iṟukkip pārttu, atu paṭṭa marattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉpataiyum piṟa
   ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyuṅ kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa patumai,"ivaṉ yāḻaṟi vittakaṉ pōlum;
   ataṉai naṉku aṟintukoṇṭu vācikkac ceyvāyāka" eṉṟu tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉaḷ.
   avaḷ vantu kuṟaiyirappatai aṟinta utayaṇaṉ, "ivaḷukku aṟivu peritu"
   eṉṟu eṇṇi, "yāṉ itil vallunaṉallēṉ" eṉṟāṉ. patumai, "orumaṉam
   uṭaiyavarkkut teriyāta kāriyam oṉṟum illai; iṉpa mayakkam aṭaintu emmai
   vañcittal aṟaṉ aṉṟu; maṟātaruḷka" eṉṟaṉaḷ. appōtu cūriyaṉ attamittaṉaṉ;
   malarnta mullaippū maṇattuṭaṉ teṉṟal cāḷarattuḷḷē vantu tavaḻntatu.
   utayaṇaṉ taṉatu yāḻākiya kōṭapatiyiṉai niṉaintu varuntip piṉpu anta
   yāḻait tīṇṭi vācittaṉaṉ. avaṉuṭaiya kuralicaikkum yāḻolikkum vēṟṟumai
   tōṟṟavillai;
   "ceviccuvai yamirta micaittaliṉ mayaṅki
       māṭak koṭumuṭi maḻalaiyum puṟāvum
       āṭamai payiru maṉṉamuṅ kiḷiyum
       piṟavu miṉṉaṉa paṟavaiyum paṟavā
       āṭukiṟa koṭukki māṭañ cērak
       koymmalark kāviṟ kuṟiñci mutalāp
       paṉmara mellām paṇintaṉa kuraṅka
       maimmalark kaṇṇiyu makiḻntumeym maṟappa
       ēṉōrk kicaippi ṉētan tarum" (1.34:277-85)

   eṉṟu eṇṇip patumai, "yāḻvittaiyiṟ ciṟantavaṉ kāmaṉ; avaṉukkuppiṉ
   tumpuru; avaṉukkuppiṉ utayaṇaṉ ciṟantavaṉeṉṟu colluvar; avaṉilum
   ivvantaṇaṉ ciṟantōṉāvāṉ" eṉṟu niṉaintu uḷḷaṅkoḷḷā uvakaiyaḷāki avaṉaip
   peritum pēṇuvāḷāyk kaḻiperuṅ kāma nukarcciyaik kaḷaviṉiṟ kaḻittu
   oḻukuvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
   anta nāṭkaḷil oru nāḷ, patumaiyiṉ tōḻi utayaṇaṉai nōkki, "maṟaiyōmpāḷa,
   kēṭpāyāka; piramatēvaṉ koṉṟaikkāyaip perukkiyum payaṟṟu neṟṟaic
   ciṟukkiyum karumpaic curukkiyum mūṅkilai mika nīṭṭiyum icaivilavākap
   paṭaittāṉ. atupōla yāṉaiyai vaṇakkum yāḻil valla utayaṇaṉilum mikka
   uṉṉuṭaiya iyalpai muṉṉam uṇarēmāyiṉēm. iṉi uṉ māṇākkiyarāvēm" eṉṟu
   colli, "itai cevvitākkit tara vēṇṭum" eṉṟu ōr yāḻai avaṉ kaiyiṟ
   koṭuttaṉaḷ. avaṉ, "itu keṭṭuppōṉa marattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu vācittaṟku
   utavātatu" eṉṟu ataik kīḻē vaittuviṭṭaṉaṉ. piṉpu tarucakaṉ patumaikku
   aṉpuṭaṉ aḷitta ōr yāḻai avaḷ aḷippa avaṉ, "inta yāḻ ciṟitum kuṟṟam
   illātatē; āṉālum itiluḷḷa narampukaḷ maṭṭum mikavum kuṟṟamuṭaiyaṉa;
   vēṟu narampukaḷ iruntāl avaṟṟait taruka" eṉṟaṉaṉ; avaḷ appaṭiyē vēṟu
   narampukaḷai varuvittaḷippa avaṉ, "iṉṉa iṉṉa kāraṇattāl ivaikaḷum
   kuṟṟamuṭaiyaṉa" eṉṟu maṟuttaṉaṉ. piṉṉum avaḷ putiya vēṟu narampukaḷai
   varuvittaḷippa avaṉ, "ivaikaḷum kuṟṟamuṭaiyaṉavē" eṉṟaṉaṉ. avaḷ,
   "kuṟṟaṅkaḷai viḷaṅkac colla vēṇṭum" eṉṟu vēṇṭa, avaṉ avaṟṟai
   ātārattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiṉaṉ. avaḷ, "yāḻum pāṭṭum yāvarum aṟivar.
   ivaṉaippōla avaṟṟiṉ kuṇakuṟṟaṅkaḷai aṟintu viritturaippavarkaḷaik
   kaṇṭatillai" eṉṟu colliviṭṭu uḷḷē ceṉṟu poṉṉait tirittāṟpōṉṟa
   narampukaḷaik koṇarntu koṭuppa, avaṉ avaṟṟai vāṅki yāḻil amaittuc
   cevvaiceytu koṭuttaṉaḷ. ataṉaip patumai peṟṟu makiḻntāḷ. piṉpu,
   'kuṟivayiṟ puṇarntu neṟivayiṟ ṟiriyār
       vāyiṉuñ ceviyiṉuṅ kaṇṇiṉu mūkkiṉum
       mētaku meyyiṉum ōta liṉṟi
       uṇṭuṅ kēṭṭuṅ kaṇṭum nāṟiyum
       uṟṟu maṟṟivai yaṟṟa miṉṟi
       aimpula vāyilun tampulam peruka
       vaika ṟōṟu meyvakai terivār
       ceyvaḷait tōḷiyaic cērntunala ṉukarvatōr
       teyvaṅ kolleṉat teḷita lāṟṟār
       uruviṉu muṇarviṉu moppō rilleṉa
       varivaḷait tōḷiyoṭu vattavar perumakaṉ
       oḻukiṉaṉ" (3.15:63-74):

   ivvāṟu oru tiṅkaḷ ceṉṟatu.
   ippaṭiyirukkaiyil, patumāpatiyiṉ maṇattai virumpi ataṟkuriya
   paricaṅkaḷai muṉṉē cellaviṭṭuk koṇṭu kēkayatēcattaracaṉākiya accuvap
   perumakaṉ eṉpavaṉ oru nāḷ vantu tarucakaṉukkut taṉ varavait
   terivittaṉaṉ. tarucakaṉ nakarai alaṅkarippittut taṉ parivāraṅkaḷōṭu
   etirkoṇṭu ceṉṟu aḷavaḷāvi avaṉai aḻaittu vantu taṉ araṇmaṉai ceṉṟu
   viruntaḷittu makiḻntiruntaṉaṉ.
   appōtu tarucakaṉuṭaiya 1mēmpāṭṭaip poṟātavarkaḷākip pakaimai koṇṭu
   oḻukupavarḷākiya viricikaṉ, arimāṉ, eliccevi, aṭavi, ayōttiyaracaṉ,
   milaiccaṉ, caṅkaraṉ, mallaṉ, vēcāli eṉṉum aracarkaḷ oruṅku kūṭic
   caturaṅka cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭup palapurikaḷālākiya oru peruṅkayiṟṟāl
   yāṉaiyaip piṇippatupōla irācakiriya nakarattaic cūḻntukoṇṭu
   perumuḻakkattaic ceytaṉar. ataṉai aṟinta tarucakaṉ mikunta kavalaiyuṟṟu
   terumaral eytiṉaṉ.
   -----
   1patumaiyaik kēkayattaracaṉukkuk koṭukkak karutiyatu *terintu
   tarucakaṉpāl vēṟṟaracar pakaimai koṇṭārkaḷeṉṟu vēṟu nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
   ataṉaik kēṭṭa patumai, "ic ceytiyai māṇakaṉukkuccol" eṉṟu airāpatiyai
   viṭuttaṉaḷ. avaḷ kāmaṉ kōṭṭattiṟku vantu nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyum patumaiyiṉ
   karuttaiyum utayaṇaṉukkuc colla avaṉ, "iṅṅaṉam pakaivar vantu
   pōrceytaṟkuc cūḻntamai namakku aṉukūlamāṉa kāriyam" eṉṟu eṇṇi
   airāpatiyai nōkki, "iraṇṭu mātattiṟ patumaiyai vantu pārppēṉ; itaṉai
   avaḷukku nī ceṉṟu kūṟi avaḷaip pātukāttukkoḷ" eṉṟu aṉuppiviṭṭup piṉ,
   "vāṇika uruvattōṭu pakaivaruṭaiya paṭaiyuṭ ceṉṟu avarai aḻippōm" eṉṟu
   niccayittukkoṇṭu ciṉṉaccōlaiyeṉṉum malaimēl ēṟic caṅkētayamākiya oru
   paṇṇaip pāṭiṉaṉ.
   kēṭṭa avaṉuṭaiya vīrar ellōrum aṅkē vantu kūṭiṉar. avarkaḷai nōkki
   utayaṇaṉ, "iṉi emakku yātoru tīṅkum ilatu" eṉṟu eṇṇi mēlē ceyya
   vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai avarkaḷukkuk kūṟiviṭṭu, tāṉum tōḻarkaḷum vāṇika
   vēṭaṅkoṇṭu vāṇikattiṟkuriya pala poruḷkaḷaiyuñ cēkarittuk kaikkoṇṭu
   pakaivaruṭaiya pāṭikaḷaic cārntapoḻutu, muṉpu iṭavakaṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭa
   icaiccaṉ pala kutiraikaḷōṭu aṅkē tōṉṟiṉaṉ. tōṉṟiyapoḻutu vayantakaṉaik
   kutirai viyāpārikaḷukkut talaivaṉāka niyamittu avaṉuṭaṉ pakaivaruṭaiya
   pāṭikaḷiṟ pukuntu avarkaḷai nōkki utayaṇaṉ. "yām kutirai viyāpārikaḷ;
   pala varuṭaṅkaḷāka makatarācaṉōṭu paḻakka muṭaiyōm; ippoḻutu avaṉ
   emmiṭam maṉa vēṟupāṭu uṭaiyaṉātaliṉ, veṉṟu avaṉ emmuṭaiya nakaramākac
   ceytukoḷḷa eṇṇiṉēm; nīvirum emakku naṭpuṭaiyavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum"
   eṉṟu colla, avarkaḷ aṅkīkarittu avaṉai uṭaṉirukkac ceytaṉar. ceyyavē
   tōḻar tamakkuriya iṭaṅkaḷai amaittu avaṟṟil iruppavarkaḷākap
   pakaivarpāluḷḷa ellā irakaciyac ceytikaḷaiyum kāla aṭaivil aṟintu ap
   pakaivarkaḷai iṉṉavakaiyāka vella vēṇṭum eṉpatait tammuḷ
   niccayittukkoṇṭu tāṅkaḷ yutta caṉṉattarkaḷāki vēṟuvēṟāka viraiviṟ
   ceṉṟu, "viricikaṉ vāḻka" eṉṟu kūṟi mallaṉpāṭi kātta vīrarkaḷaik
   koṉṟu,pakaivarkaḷ tammuḷ virōtaṅ koṇṭu oruvarōṭoruvar pōrceyvatu pōlavē
   tōṟṟumpaṭi ippaṭiyē pōrceytu muṭivil, "vattavaṉ vāḻka" eṉṟu kūṟip pōr
   ceytārkaḷ; ataṉaik kaṇṭa pakaivarkaḷ, "namakkuḷ yārō virōtattai
   uṇṭākkutaṟku iṅṅaṉam pōr ceykiṉṟārkaḷ; iṉi iṅkiruttal ākātu. poḻutu
   viṭivataṟkuḷ eppaṭiyēṉum nām puṟattē ceṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu niccayittut
   tattam poruḷkaḷ pāṭikaḷil aṅkaṅkē kiṭappa ōṭippōyt tamakku araṇāka uḷḷa
   oru malaiyai aṭaintu aṅkē taṅkiṉar.
   urumaṇṇuvā mutaliyōr tammuṭ kūṭi makiḻvuṟṟu, "pakaivarai veṉṟō mātalāl,
   patumaiyai virumpiya utayaṇaṉukku iṉi ākkamuṇṭu" eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭu, "taṉ
   tēvi vācavatattaiyai iḻantu taṉ varuttattait tīrttukkoḷḷutaṟku in
   nāṭṭiṟku vanta utayaṇaṉ tarucakaṉuṭaiya pakaivar pōr ceytaṟku
   nakarattaic cūḻntatu terintu taṉ tantaikkum makatarācaṉukkum mikunta
   naṭpu uṇṭu eṉpatai aṟintōṉātaliṉ, avaṉukku vanta tuṉpattaip pōkka
   vēṇṭuvatu taṉ kaṭamaiyeṉṟu niṉaintu valintu ceṉṟu pōr ceytu pakaivarai
   veṉṟu ōṭac ceytuviṭṭu ataṉaiyē kaiyuṟaiyaiyākakkoṇṭu kāṇavantāṉ eṉpatai
   nī ceṉṟu colluka" eṉṟu vayantakaṉai aṉuppiṉar. avaṉ ic ceytiyai
   vītitōṟum collikkoṇṭē aracaṉ māḷikaikkuc celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. iḵtu ivvāṟāka.
   "in nakarattaic cūḻnta pakaivarai vellutaṟku vaḻi yātu?" eṉṟu
   kavalaiyuṟṟu attāṇiyilirunta tarucakaṉukku, "utayaṇaṉ paḻamaiyaip
   pārāṭṭi vantu tuṇaivaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu pōrceytu veṉṟu pakaivarai ōṭṭiviṭṭāṉ"
   eṉṟa ceyti eṭṭiyatu. uṭaṉē avaṉ eḻuntu veḷiyē vantu, "ic ceytiyai
   aṟintu vantōrkaḷai eṉakku nēril kāṭṭa vēṇṭum" eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉavē ēvalāḷar
   pōntu vayantakaṉai aḻaittu vantu kāṭṭiṉar. nikaḻntavaṟṟai avaṉ
   kūṟakkēṭṭa tarucakaṉ, "viṟalvēl ucayaṇaṉ ivaṇ varap peṟṟēṉ; tavamika
   uṭaiyēṉ" eṉṟum, "kēṭṭa kālaiyuṅ kēṭṭōr uvappa naṭṭōrk kāṟṟum
   naṉṉarāḷaṉuṭaiya varavai etirkoḷḷakkaṭavēṉ" eṉṟum colli nakarai
   alaṅkarippittu etirkoṇṭu cellumpoḻutu "utayaṇaṉait teḷintu aḻaittal
   tītu" eṉṟu cilar taṭuttaṉar. taṭukkavum ataṉai matiyātu tarucakaṉ
   avaṉait taḻuvi aḻaittuvantu mikappārāṭṭit taṉiyē vacati amaittu atil
   irukkac ceytu avaṉukkup pōrceytalāl uṇṭāṉa varuttattaip pōkkuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ivarkaḷ iṅkē ippaṭiyirukkaiyil, tōṟṟōṭiya ap pakaiyaracar tammuḷḷē kūṭi
   ārāyntu taṅkaḷuḷ yāriṭattum piḻaippillaiyeṉṟu teḷintu, 'iṉi yāraiyum
   nampit teḷitalākātu' eṉṟu niccayit tukkoṇṭu cūḷuṟavu ceytu ellōrum
   oṉṟucērntu perumpaṭaikaḷōṭu vantu maraṅkaḷai veṭṭi vīḻttiyum viḷainta
   neṟkatirkaḷaik koḷuttiyum piṟa tīṅkukaḷaic ceytum nāṭṭiṟkup perun
   tuṉpattai viḷaivittaṉar. ataṉai aṟinta paṭaiyoṟṟāḷar viraintu vantu
   tarucakaṉukkut terivittaṉar. avaṉ mikka kōpaṅkoṇṭu tāṉē pōrceytaṟkup
   puṟappaṭa niccayittaṉaṉ.
   atu terinta utayaṇaṉ, "yāṉē ceṉṟu pōrpurintu veṉṟu varuvēṉ. taṉṉuṭaiya
   nālvakaip paṭaikaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṟkut talaivaṉāka oru vīraṉaiyum eṉakkut
   tuṇaiyāka aṉuppumāṟu tarucakaṉiṭam collivā" eṉṟu vayantakaṉai
   aṉuppiṉāṉ. avaṉ vantu collak kēṭṭa tarucakaṉ mantirikaḷuṭaṉ yōcittut
   taṉṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷaiyum vīrarkaḷaiyum avaṉpāl aṉuppiṉāṉ. atai aṟinta
   kēkayattaracaṉ, "eṉṉai aṉuppiṉ nāṉ viraivil pakaivarai veṉṟu varuvēṉ"
   eṉṟu terivikka, tarucakaṉ, "eṉṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷukkut talaivaṉākac ceṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉuṭaṉiruntu pakaivarōṭu pōrceytu varuka" eṉṟu avaṉai
   aṉuppiviṭṭu, "taṅkaiyai virumpi vanta kēkaya aracaṉum varukiṉṟāṉ.
   avaṉukku yātoru tīṅkum nērātapaṭi pāṭukāttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉukku col" eṉṟu vayantakaṉai aṉuppiṉāṉ. avaṉum appaṭiyē vantu
   ataṉai utayaṇaṉukkuk kūṟiṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ yāṉaimītēṟip pōrukkup
   puṟappaṭṭaṉaṉ. urumaṇṇuvā mutaliya munnūṟṟāṟu vīrarkaḷ tamakkuriya
   ūrtikaḷil ēṟi utayaṇaṉaip puṟaṅkāttuc ceṉṟaṉar. ak kālattil tarucakaṉ
   vantu, "kēkayattaracaṉaip pātukāttukkoḷka" eṉṟu maṭṭum utayaṇaṉukku
   nēriṟ colliviṭṭu mīṇṭu taṉṉiṭam ceṉṟaṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ, "cēṉaiyiṉ naṭuvē,
   kēkayattaracaṉum urumaṇṇuvāvum eṉakku muṉṉē celluka. iṉṉār eṉakkup
   piṉṉē varuka" eṉṟu kūṟupaṭutti amaittukkoṇṭu pakaivaratu pācaṟaiyai
   aṭaintaṉaṉ. uṭaṉē pakaivarellōrum taṅkaḷukkuriya perumpaṭaiyōṭu vantu
   etirttārkaḷ.
   appōtu iruvakaip paṭaikaḷukkum perumpōr nikaḻntatu. utayaṇaṉ eliccevi
   aracaṉuṭaiya tampi ēṟiyirunta yāṉaiyiṉmēl pāyntu avaṉaip piṭittuk
   kaccāl avaṉ tēril kaṭṭiṉaṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṭa uru maṇṇuvā, "nammuṭaiya
   karuttai muṭittaṟkuriya kālam itutāṉ" eṉṟu eṇṇik kēkayattaracaṉōṭu
   cērntu elicceviyaracaṉ paṭaiyōṭu pōrceytaṟku eḻuntāṉ. uṭaṉē
   elicceviyaracaṉ viraintu vantu kēkayattaracaṉuṭaiya talaiyai veṭṭi
   vīḻttiviṭṭut taṉ tampi utayaṇaṉāṟ ciṟaippaṭṭiruttalai aṟintu
   urumaṇṇuvāviṉ yāṉaiyiṉmēl kutittu, "eṉ tampiyai viṭṭuviṭumpaṭi
   ceyvāyāyiṉ nī piḻaippāy" eṉṟu colla, ataṉaik kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ, "
   tarucakaṉukkum ivaṉukkum vēṟupāṭillai. ivaṉaip pōṟṟu vāyāyiṉ uṉ
   tampiyaip nī peṟuvāy" eṉṟu collip pōrceytaṉaṉ, appoḻutu, veḷḷattāl
   uppaṇai karaivatupōlap pakaivarpaṭai uṭaintupōyiṟṟu. pōkiyum pakaivar
   urumaṇṇuvāvaip piṭittuk koṇṭu tammiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉaic ciṟaippaṭutti
   viṭṭaṉar.
   vicayamuracattōṭu tōḻarkaḷ cūḻacceṉṟu utayaṇaṉ veṟṟic ceytiyait
   tarucakaṉukkut tūtar mūlamāka aṟivittāṉ. tarucakaṉ puṟampōntu
   utayaṇaṉaip palamuṟai pulli, "nikaḻntataic colla vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kēṭka,
   "kēkayattaracaṉ pakaip paṭaiyiṉ eḻucciyaiyum ataṉāl uṇṭākum tīṅkaiyum
   niṉaiyātavaṉāki yām vilakkavum aṭaṅki nillāmal viraintōṭit tamaraiyum
   emaraiyum nīṅkip pōrceytu elicceviyaracaṉuṭaiya vāḷāl veṭṭuṇṭu vīra
   cuvarkkam aṭaintāṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ; atukēṭṭa tarucakaṉ,
   "eṉkaṭaṉ ṟīrē ṉāyi ṉēṉavaṉ
       taṉkaṭaṉ ṟīrntu takka tāṟṟiṉaṉ" (3.20:141-2)

   eṉpataik kūṟi uḷḷam nekiḻnturukip pōrkkaḷattil ārāynteṭuttu avaṉ
   uṭampait takaṉañ ceyvittu maṟṟak kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum muṭittuviṭṭu
   utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ akanakar pukuntāṉ. pukuntapoḻutu kaṇṭōr makiḻntu
   utayaṇaṉaip palavāṟu vāḻttiṉārkaḷ. aṅṅaṉam vāḻttikkoṇ ṭirukkat
   tarucakaṉōṭu cērntu utayaṇaṉ araṇmaṉaikkuṭ pukuntāṉ.
   appoḻutu tarucakaṉ, "turiyōtaṉātiyar nūṟṟuvarait taṉiyē veṉṟa peruviṟal
   vāynta vīmaṉaippōlat taṉiyē ceṉṟu pakaivarai veṉṟa utayaṇaṉuṭaiya
   cuṟṟattāreṉṟu collappaṭum aracar paḻam piṟappil tavamuṭaiyōr" eṉṟeṇṇit
   taṉ taṅkaiyākiya patumāpatiyai avaṉukku maṇam ceyvikka niṉaintu ac
   ceytiyai avaḷiṭattum utayaṇaṉiṭattum collivarumpaṭi mutumakaṉākiya
   amaiccaṉ oruvaṉai aṉuppa, avaṉ ceṉṟu taṉiyē irunta patumaiyaik
   kaṇṭu,"nammuṭaiya pakaivarai veṉṟavaṉum yāṉaiyai vaṇakkum vīṇai
   vittakaṉumākiya utayaṇaṉukku uṉṉai maṇañceyvikkat tāṉ eṇṇiyiruppatāka
   aracaṉ uṉakkut terivikkac coṉṉāṉ" eṉṟu colla avaḷ puṉmuṟuvalai
   uṭaiyavaḷāki, "mantira nāviṉ antaṇaṉ kēṇmai irunilam pēriṉum tīrtal
   iṉṟu. ātalāl, iṅṅaṉam nēriṉ yāṉ uyiruṭaṉ vāḻēṉ" eṉṟa uṟutiyuṭaṉ pēcāmal
   iruntāḷ.
   appāl, avvamaiccaṉ utayaṇaṉai aṭaintu aracaṉ karutiyataic colla,
   utayaṇaṉ neṭunēram yōcittuk koṇṭiruntu piṉpu, "yāṉ kuṟaikoḷḷuṅ
   kāriyattai avaṉē kuṟaikōṭal eṉ tavattiṉ viḷaivu" eṉa uvanta uḷḷamoṭu
   taṉ makiḻvai veḷippaṭuttātavaṉāki, "vācavatattai tīppaṭṭamaiyāl mikka
   kavalaiyuṭaṉ eṉṉuṭaiya aracāṭciyait tuṟantuviṭṭu avaḷaiyum yūkiyaiyum
   iḻantapiṉ vāḻvataṉāl uḷatākum payaṉ yāteṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. eṉakku nī colliya
   kāriyattaic ceytalil viruppamillai" eṉṟu maṟuttāṉ. kēṭṭa avaṉ, "nī
   kūṟum col kuṭippiṟantōrkku ottataṉṟu" eṉṟu vaṇaṅki niṉṟu, "matta
   yāṉaiyai vaṇakkum nalyāḻ vittaka vīra! iv vēṇṭukōḷai niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭum"
   eṉṟu mīṭṭum irappa, utayaṇaṉ, "tarucakaṉum nīyum mika vaṟpuṟuttutalāl
   nāṉ ceyvataṟku yātu taṭaiyuḷatu?" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. av vamaiccaṉ makiḻntu
   mīṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ.
   ceṉṟa amaiccaṉ nikaḻntavaṟṟait tarucakaṉukkuc colla avaṉ kēṭṭu
   makiḻntaṉaṉ. iḵtu ivvāṟāka;
   "māṭattil oṭuṅkiyirukkum māṇakaṉē nām eṉpataip patumāpati aṟiyāḷātaliṉ,
   nammai maṇattaṟku uṭaṉpaṭāḷ. ātalāl, avaṉē nām eṉpatai mutalil avaḷukku
   ētēṉum oruvāṟṟāl terivikka vēṇṭum. ataṟku upāyam yātu?" eṉpatai
   niṉainta utayaṇaṉ vayantakaṉai aḻaittu, "eṉṉuṭaiya uyirttōḻaṉākiya
   icaiccaṉeṉpavaṉ irumutukuravaraiyum iḻantōṉ. avaṉukku ōr antaṇak
   kaṉṉiyai maṇam purivitta piṉpē eṉṉuṭaiya maṇattaik kuṟittu niṉaittal
   vēṇṭum eṉpatai eṉ vārttaiyākat tarucakaṉiṭañ colli ataṉai muṭittukkoṇṭu
   varutal uṉatu kaṭamaiyākum" eṉṟu colli aṉuppa vayantakaṉ ceṉṟu
   tarucakaṉukku utayaṇaṉuṭaiya karuttai muṟaiyākat terivittaṉaṉ.
   tarucakaṉ, "patumaiyiṉ pārppaṉattōḻiyākiya yāppiyāyiṉiyeṉṉum kaṉṉi
   oḻukkattilum kulattilum viḻuppam mikkavaḷātalāl, avaḷai icaiccaṉukku
   maṇañ ceyvittal nalam eṉpatai utayaṇaṉukkut terivittiṭuka" eṉṟu
   colliyaṉuppiṉaṉ. vayantakaṉ vantu ataṉai utayaṇaṉukkut terivittāṉ.
   tōḻarkaḷ kēṭṭu makiḻntārkaḷ. piṉpu anta maṇattiṟkuriya nāḷ
   kuṟippiṭappaṭṭatu. ataṉai aṟinta tarucakaṉ, "patumaiyai utayaṇaṉukkum
   yāppiyāyiṉiyai avaṉ tōḻaṉ icaiccaṉukkum maṇañ ceyvikka
   niccayittiruttalaiyum yāppiyāyiṉiyiṉ maṇattai muṉṉatākavē nikaḻtta
   vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum taṉṉuṭaiya tāykkut terivittut tāyiṉ aruḷvakai aṟintu
   vammiṉ" eṉṟu tammiyal vaḻāp perumūtāḷarai viṭuttaṉaṉ. avarkaḷ ceṉṟu
   collak kēṭṭa tāy uṇmali uvakaiyaḷāy yāppiyāyiṉi aṇintu koḷḷutaṟkēṟṟa
   āparaṇaṅkaḷaik koṭuttaṉuppiṉaḷ.
   ic ceytiyaik kēṭṭa patumai, "māṇakaṉaip pirintu eṉṉuṭaiya perun
   tuyarattai nīkkikkoḷḷutaṟkup peruntuṇaiyāka irunta yāppiyāyiṉikku
   nīṅkutiṟaṉ uṇṭeṉiṉ eṉ uyirait tāṅkum vaḻiyai aṟiyēṉ. vilakkutalum
   iyalpaṉṟu. eṉ ceykēṉ" eṉṟu niṉaintu āṭai, aṇikalam, mālai
   mutaliyavaṟṟai yāppiyāyiṉikku aṉpuṭaṉ aḷittu, "vatuvai vicēṭattāl eṉṉai
   maṟantoḻiyāmal viraintu vā" eṉṟu colli avaḷait taḻuvit taṉṉuṭaiya
   kavalaiyaip pulappaṭuttāmal naṟṟāy ceytaṟkuriya paricukaḷaiyellām
   avaḷukkuc ceytu taṉṉuṭaiya uḻaikkalaṅkaḷaiyum ciṟanta civikai
   oṉṟaṉaiyum aḷittaṉaḷ.
   tarucakaṉ, patumaiyiṉ peruntakaimaiyai niṉaintu makiḻntataṉṟi
   yāppiyāyiṉiyiṉ vatuvaic ceytiyai nakarattār ellārum aṟiyumpaṭi ceyvittu
   maṇattiṟku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai mikac cevvaiyāka amaippittaṉaṉ. uriya nāḷil
   vatuvai maṇṭapattil icaiccaṉum yāppiyāyiṉiyum ācaṉattil iruntaṉar.
   vatuvaic caṭaṅku naṭaipeṟṟatu. ac camayattil utayaṇaṉ arukē iruntu
   icaiccaṉai nōkki, "tām makatattiṟku vantatu mutaṉmaiyāṉa
   kōcampinakarattaiyum yamuṉai yāṟṟaiyum piṟavaḷaṅkaḷaiyum yāppiyāyiṉi
   kaṇṭu makiḻntu uṟaivataṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu" eṉṟu avaḷ kātil paṭumpaṭi
   kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa avaḷ, "ikkural māṇakaṉ kuralāka irukkiṉṟatē" eṉṟu
   kaṭaikkaṇṇāl avaṉaip pārttu aiyam oḻintu, "utayaṇaṉē pārppaṉa uruvōṭu
   patumāpatiyiṉ yāppuṭai neñcam aḻittaṉaṉ; otta iṭattaṉṟi ovvāta iṭattil
   maṉam cellāteṉpatu uṇmaiyē" eṉṟuniṉaintu, "ic ceytiyai viraivil
   avaḷukkut terivikka vēṇṭum" eṉṉum eṇṇattōṭu camayam nōkkikkoṇṭē
   iruntaṉaḷ. vatuvaikkuriya ēḻunāṭkaḷum ceṉṟaṉa. cellavē maṇakkōlattōṭu
   taṉṉaik kāṇa varumpaṭi patumai colli, viṭutta civikaiyil yāppiyāyiṉi
   ēṟic ceṉṟu avaḷaik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāviya piṉṉar. "māṇakaṉāka vantu
   uṉṉuṭaiya uḷḷattaik kavarntavaṉ utayaṇaṉē; iṉi varuntutalai oḻika" eṉṟu
   tuṇintukūṟiṉaḷ.
   "uṉṉai maṇanta antaṇaṉ utayaṇaṉuṭaiya tōḻaṉ eṉpatai muṉṉamē nāṉ
   kēṭṭaṟin tiruppatu uṇṭu. māṇakaṉukku urimaipūṇṭa eṉ niṟai
   vaṭuppaṭātiruttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? ārāyntu pār" eṉṟu patumai coṟṟaṉaḷ.
   patumaiyiṉ karuttai yāp piyāyiṉi utayaṇaṉukkut terivikka avaṉ taṉ vaṭi
   vattai oru paṭattil eḻuti, "ōr iraviṟ kaṉṉi māṭattil eṉṉuṭaṉ irukkaiyil
   kōṭṭāṉatu kuralaik kēṭṭu mikavum añciṉaḷ. anta aṭaiyāḷattaiyum col"
   eṉṟu collit taṉ paṭattaik koṭuttaṉuppiṉaṉ. paṭattil utayaṇaṉ vaṭivaik
   kaṇṭa patumai, "māṇakaṉ vaṭivattiṟkum itaṟkum ciṟitum vēṟṟumai illai;
   āyiṉum itaṉai naṉku ārāyntē teḷiya vēṇṭum. itaṉait toṭutalum tavaṟu"
   eṉṟu colli ataṉaik kaiyil vāṅkāḷāyiṉaḷ. uṭaṉē naḷḷiraviṟ kōṭṭāṉuṭaiya
   olikku añciya aṭaiyāḷattai avaḷ coṟṟaṉaḷ. collavē patumai taṉ kaṇavaṉ
   uyiraik kūṟṟuvaṉ kaiyiṉiṉṟum mīṭṭa cāvittiriyaippōla mikka iṉpa
   maṭaintu tōḻiyaik kaiyāl taṭṭi ap paṭattait tāṅka muṭiyāta viruppatōṭu
   mārpil oṭukki ataṉ tōḷait tīṇṭip puṉmuṟuval ceytukoṇṭu, "neñcaṅ koṇṭa
   neṭumoḻiyāḷa! vañca uruvoṭu valaip paṭuttaṉai" eṉṟu pārāṭṭuvāḷāyk
   kavalai oḻintiruntaṉaḷ.
   cila nāḷ ceṉṟapiṉ, aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi cēṉaik kaṇiyāl vatuvaikkuriya
   nalla mukūrttam vaikkap peṟṟatu; nakaram alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu,
   patumaikkum utayaṇaṉukkum maṇam vitippaṭi naṭaipeṟṟatu. utayaṇaṉ, "mika
   viraintu urumaṇṇuvāvaic ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum mīṭṭuvarac ceyya vēṇṭum" eṉṟu
   tarucakaṉukkut terivittaṉaṉ. avaṉ ataṉai aṅkīkarittataṉṟi. "utayaṇaṉ
   valintu vantu nammuṭaiya pakaivarai veṉṟa naṭpāḷāṉāka iruppatallāmal
   cuṟṟamākavum iruttaliṉ avaṉ pakaivaṉākiya āruṇiyai veṉṟu vatta
   nāṭṭaiyum kaucāmpi nakaraiyum avaṉukku uriyaṉavākac ceyya vēṇṭiyatu
   nammuṭaiya kaṭappāṭu" eṉṟu eṇṇiṉāṉ. eṇṇi, tāmē ceṉṟu tam viṉai muṭikkum
   mantirikaḷuḷ ciṟanta varuṭakāraṉ, tārakāri, tarumatattaṉ cattiyakāyaṉ
   mutaliya mantirikaḷaiyum,
   'irunū ṟāṉaiyu mirāyiraṅ kutiraiyum
       aṟunūṟ ṟiraṭṭi yaṭaṉmaṇit tērum
       aṟupati ṉāyira meṟipaṭai maḷḷarum
       tirumaṇic civikaiyum poruviṉaip paṭākaiyum
       ceṅkāṟ pāṇṭiya naṉṟu pūṇṭa
       paimpo ṉūrtiyum pavaḻak kaṭṭilum
       paṭāak koṭṭilum paṇṭipaṇ ṭāramum
       kaṭāak kaḷiyāṉaik kāvalaṟ kiyainta
       paṇaittōṭ cilacoṟ patumā naṅkaikku
       amaikkap paṭṭa vakaṉpari yāḷamum
       aṉṉavai yellā mannilai nalki' (3.23:30-40)

   piṉṉum vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai appoḻutu appoḻutu varaviṭuvēṉ" eṉṟu
   mantirikaḷiṭañ coṉṉataṉṟi, "utayaṇaṉukku āvaṉavaṟṟai aṟintu avvappoḻutē
   ceytuvara vēṇṭuvatu ummuṭaiya kaṭamai" eṉṟu ovvoru mantirikkum
   taṉittaṉiyē kūṟiṉaṉ;
   ’vaṭuttoḻi lakaṉṟa varuṭa kāra!
       uṭaṟṟunark kaṭanta utayaṇa kumaraṉ
       aṭaikkala niṉakku’ (3.23:47-9)

   eṉṟu ōmpaṭuttu mantirimārai nōkki, "oṟṟiṉāṉum upāyattāṉum āruṇiyait
   tolaittuviṭṭē nīr peyarntu varal vēṇṭum" eṉṟu colla, ellōrum
   kōcāmpi nakarattai nōkkip paṭaiyuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭārkaḷ.
   puṟappaṭavē tarucakaṉ piṉṉum toṭarntuvantu utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu aṉpu mikka
   coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟaic coṉṉataṉṟi, "vantu utavi ceyya vēṇṭumeṉpataip
   piraccōtaṉaṉukkut terivittāl avaṉum namakku anukūlañ ceypavaṉāvāṉ.
   vēṇṭumāyiṉ yāṉum varuvēṉ; terivittāl aṉpuṭaiya piṟa aracarkaḷum
   varuvārkaḷ. ellāvaṟṟaiyum ārāyntu ceyka; uṉakku veṟṟiyuṇṭākuka" eṉṟu
   kūṟiṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ tarucakaṉai nōkki, "aṭaiyakkūṭiya iṉpaṅkaḷai iṉpamāka
   yāṉ eṇṇuvatu urumaṇṇuvāvai aṭainta piṉputāṉ. ātalāl, avaṉaip patiṉaintu
   tiṉattuṭ ciṟaiviṭuvittu varuvittu aṉuppuvatu uṉakkuk kaṭaṉākum"
   eṉpatait terivittaṉaṉ. tarucakaṉ, urumaṇṇuvāviṉ perumaiyaiyum
   avaṉiṭattē utayaṇaṉukkuḷḷa ārvattaiyum aṟintu maṉamuvantu "appaṭiyē
   ceyvēṉ" eṉṟu colli viṭai peṟṟut taṉ nakarkkuc ceṉṟāṉ. ceṉṟa piṉpu,
   'aṭaṟpē riyāṉaiyu malaṅkumayirp puraviyum
       paṭaikkūḻp paṇṭiyum paḷḷi vaiyamum
       naṭaittē roḻukkum naṟkōṭ ṭūrtiyum
       koṭippaṭai pōkkip paṭippaṭai niṟīip
       puṭaippaṭai puṇarttu' (3.24: 35-40)

   utayaṇaṉ cellutalai aṟinta maṉṉarkaḷ, " utayaṇaṉ pōrai mēṟkoṇṭu
   cellukiṉṟāṉ. ic celavu yārmēlatō? iḵtu añcaṟpālatu" eṉṟu tammuṭ
   kavalaiyuṟṟārkaḷ.
   avaṉ aṅṅaṉañ ceṉṟu ōriṭattil taṅkiyirunta poḻutu, "pāñcālaṉōṭu pōr
   ceyvataṟku viraivil eḻa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu yūkitaṉakku irakaciyamāka eḻutiya
   ōlaiyai vayantakaṉ piṟittu vaittuviṭṭu, aṅkē vanta piṅkala kaṭakaraik
   kaṇṭu avarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟai aṟintu, utayaṇaṉai aṭaintu, " uṉṉuṭaiya
   tampikaḷākiya piṅkala kaṭakar muṉṉam kōcampinakarattaip
   pātukāttukkoṇṭiruntaṉar. piṉpu an nakarattaic cūḻntuvantu muṟṟiya
   pāñcālarācaṉuṭaiya pōrukku āṟṟārākit tōlviyuṟṟu yamuṉaiyāṟṟil vīḻtaṟkuk
   kutirai ūrntu viraintu cellukaiyil, iṭaiyē araṇuḷḷatum peṇkaḷ maṭṭum
   uṟaivatumākiya nakaram oṉṟaik kaṇṇuṟṟu atil paṉṉiraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ
   taṅkiyiruntu pāñcālarācaṉuṭaiya āṭciyaip poṟātavarkaḷākit tamkuṟaiyai
   uṉakkutterivittaṟku iṅkē vantirukkiṉṟaṉar" eṉṟu colla utayaṇaṉ mikunta
   āvalōṭu avarkaḷaip pārkka virumpiya kālattil, piṅkala kaṭakar
   paṉṉīrāyiram vīrarkaḷōṭum aṟṟa kālaikku amaikkappaṭṭa ēṉaik koṟṟat
   tāṉaiyōṭum vantu paṇintaṉar. avarkaḷ, "uṉakku vaḻipāṭu ceytalait
   tavirntataṉṟip peṟṟa tāykkup paṇi ceytukoṇṭiruttalaiyum oḻintēm;
   nalviṉai illēm" eṉṟu kalaṅkik kaṇṇīr āṟākap perukat talai kālil
   paṭumpaṭi vaṇaṅkiṉar.
   utayaṇaṉ avarkaḷai eṭuttut taḻuvi, "mantirikaḷuṭaiya eṇṇattiṉ vaḻiyē
   naṭavāta pullaṟiviṉaiyuṭaiyēṉ ceyta kuṟṟattiṉ payaṉ itu. nīṅkaḷ iṉi
   varunta vēṇṭām eṉṟu iṉiya moḻikaḷaik kūṟi, " uṅkaḷai, aṭaintamaiyāl
   iṉip pakaivar erivāyppaṭṭa pañci pōlvār. yāṉ yātoru kuṟaiyum illēṉ"
   eṉṟum, " iṉi yūkiyaiyum vācavatattaiyaiyum yāṉ eḷitil aṭaivēṉ;
   eṉṉuṭaiya tavam ummait tantu ellāt tuyaraiyum nīkkiyatu" eṉṟum colli
   iṉpuṟṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya kaṇṇīrait tuṭaittuviṭṭu mantirikaḷai nōkki, "
   pāñcāla rācaṉai vellutaṟkuriya ālōcaṉaiyaic ceyya vēṇṭum" eṉṟaṉaṉ.
   uṭaṉē varuṭakāraṉ, "nām mēl naṭatta vēṇṭuvatu iṉṉatu" eṉ patai viḷaṅkak
   kūṟip pāñcālarācaṉiṭattuḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷai aṟintu varumpaṭi oṟṟarkaḷaik
   kōcampinakarukku aṉuppiṉaṉ. ceṉṟa oṟṟar aṅkē nikaḻntavaṟṟai aṟintuvantu
   viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉar.
   appāl varuṭakāraṉum piṟarum iṭaiyiṭaiyē ceyta muyaṟcikaḷ pala.
   irutiṟattārum pōrceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. perumpōr nikaḻntatu. iṟantōr palar.
   piṉṉar pāñcāla rācaṉ cēṉaikaḷuṭaṉ pōr ceyyap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. tīya
   nimittaṅkaḷ pala nikaḻntaṉa. pūraṇa kuṇṭalaṉeṉṉum mantiri, " ippoḻutu
   yām pōrukkuc cellutal muṟaiyaṉṟu," tirumpi viṭutalē nalam" eṉṟu
   taṭuppavum āruṇi kēḷāṉākic ceṉṟu pōr ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉaṉ. appoḻutu,
   ' cilaittaṉa tūci; malaittaṉa yāṉai;
       ārttaṉar maṟavar; tūrttaṉar palkaṇai!
       vilaṅkiṉa voḷvāḷ; ilaṅkiṉa kuntam;
       viṭṭaṉa tōmaram; paṭṭaṉa pāymā;
       tuṇintaṉa taṭakkai; kuṉintaṉa kuñcaram;
       aṟṟaṉa paintalai; iṟṟaṉa palkoṭi;
       cōrntaṉa palkuṭar; vārntaṉa kuruti;
       kuḻintatu pōrkkaḷam; eḻuntatu centukaḷ;
       aḻintaṉa pūmi; viḻuntaṉar mēlōr;
       ippaṭi nikaḻnta kālai' (3.27:170-16)

   āruṇiyaic cārnta vīrarkaḷ palar utayaṇaṉuṭaiya vīrarkaḷāl
   kollappaṭṭaṉar. kārttavīriyārccuṉaṉaik koṉṟa paracurāmaṉaip pōlap
   pāñcālarācaṉai utayaṇaṉ veṭṭi vīḻttiṉāṉ. appāl, "araca urimaiyai
   utayaṇaṉ peṟṟāṉ" eṉṟu nāṭṭiṉarum nakarattiṉarum aṟintukoḷḷumpaṭi
   yāṉaiyiṉmēl veṟṟi muracattai ēṟṟi aṟaiyac ceytaṉar. utayaṇaṉ piṉpu
   uriyavarkaḷaik koṇṭu pāñcālarācaṉukku īmakkaṭaṉaik kaḻippittaṉaṉ.
   varuṭakāraṉ kōṭṭai vāyilai aṭaintu katavai aṭaittukkoṇṭu uḷḷē irukkum
   nakarattārkaḷai nōkki, "iṉi nīṅkaḷ ciṟitum añca vēṇṭām. uṅkaḷ
   talaivaṉākiya utayaṇaṉ pāñcālarācaṉaik koṉṟu viṭṭāṉ. ātalāl katavait
   tiṟantiṭumiṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa nakarattār, "emmuṭaiya paḻaiya
   aracaṉākiya utayaṇaṉuṭaiya muttirai amainta ōlaiyaik kaṇṭālaṉṟi yām
   katavait tiṟavēm" eṉṟu maṟuttaṉar. uṭaṉē avarkaḷuṭaiya cantēkam
   nīṅkumpaṭi, "utayaṇaṉuṭaiya mantiriyākiya iṭavakaṉ vantirukkiṉṟāṉ"
   eṉpataik kūṟiṉaṉ. kūṟavē avarkaḷ mikunta makiḻcciyuṟṟuk katavait
   tiṟantu koṭi mutaliyavaṟṟai uyarttik koṇṭu etirkoṇṭārkaḷ. aṅkē niṉṟa
   pāñcālarācaṉuṭaiya naṇpaṉākiya kumpaṉ eṉpavaṉaik kaṇṭu avaṉaik koṉṟu
   piṉṉum, "pakaivaṉuṭaiya tamar iṅku uḷarāyiṉ avarkaḷaiyum kolvēm" eṉṟu
   collikkoṇṭu āyutapāṇikaḷākap pala vīrarkaḷ pātukākkac ceṉṟu vītitōṟum,
   "utayaṇaṉ vāḻka" eṉṟu colluvārāki yāvarum aṟiyumpaṭi yāṉaiyiṉ mēl
   paṟaiyai ēṟṟi viṭuttaṉar.
   --------------
                     ___________________________________

                           4. vattava kāṇṭam

   appāl, utayaṇaṉ varuṭakāraṉait taḻuvi mikap pārāṭṭit taṉṉuṭaiya
   yāṉaiyaiyum āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum avaṉukkuk koṭuttaṉaṉ. piṉpu tarumatattaṉai
   nōkki, "pāñcālarācaṉai veṉṟatu uṉṉāltāṉ" eṉṟu mukamaṉ kūṟi, "avaṉai
   veṉṟataṟku ivai aṟikuṟikaḷ" eṉṟu neyttōrp paṭṭikaiyaiyum pattu
   ūrkaḷaiyum avaṉukku aḷittaṉaṉ. nalla vēḷaiyil puṟappaṭṭu yāṉaiyiṉ mēl
   ēṟikkoṇṭu, tōḻarkaḷ cūḻa, veṇkoṟṟak kuṭai kavippa, kavarikaḷ iraṭṭa,
   caṅkamum muracum iyampa, mā caṉaṅkaḷiṉ vāḻttoli mika, "neṭuntakāy!
   neṭuṅkālam ulakattaip pātukāttu vāḻvāyāka" eṉṟu palar kōpurantōṟum
   niṉṟu pūmaḻai poḻiyāniṟpa utayaṇaṉ araṇmaṉaiyai aṭaintāṉ. aṭaintavaṉ,
   āruṇiyōṭu aḻaṟ paḷḷiyai aṭaiyāta avaṉ maṉaiviyarkaḷukku vēṇṭum poṉṉai
   aḷittu vēṟiṭam ceṉṟu irukkumpaṭi ceyvittāṉ. "iṟantōrkaḷukkuriya
   kaṭaṉkaḷaik kaḻippīrkaḷāka" eṉṟu maṟṟavarkaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittāṉ.
   ippaṭiyē ceyya vēṇṭiya palavaṟṟaiyum ceytu muṭitta piṉpu mutaṉmaiyāṉa
   vēṟōr araṇmaṉaiyait taṉakku iyaṟṟuvittut taṉakku uriyavarkaḷāṉa
   amaiccarkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷai muṟaiyē uṭaṉ amaittukkoṇṭu ellā
   aṅkaṅkaḷaiyum nirappi, araṇmaṉaiyiluḷḷa pērattāṇiyiṉiṭaiyē pakaivaṉai
   veṉṟa makiḻcciyuṭaṉ vīṟṟiruntāṉ.
   āruṇi aracaṉāl iṭaiyē amaikkappaṭṭa putiya koṭuṅkōl muṟaikaḷaiyellām
   piṉpu utayaṇaṉ aṭiyōṭē akaṟṟiviṭṭuc ceṅkōṟcelvam ciṟappacceytu
   paramparaiyākak kāriyam pārttu vanta kuṭimakkaḷai nakarilum nāṭṭilum
   uyarttiṉāṉ. tēvālayaṅkaḷ, aruntavar paḷḷi, antaṇar irukkai, tōṭṭam
   vāvi, āvaṇakkaṭai, nāṟcanti, muccanti teru mutaliyavaṟṟaip putuppittu,
   "tamakku uriyavaṟṟai iṭaiyē iḻanta māntar avaṟṟai aṭaivārāka" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. "vaṟumaiyai uṭaiyīrum, ūkkam mutaliyavaṟṟai iḻantu
   mūppāl muṭaṅkiṉīrum, kaṇavaṉ kaiviṭutalāl vāḻvai iḻantu varuntum
   peṇṭirum, uṟuppuk kuṟaintīrum, piṟarum vantu vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai vēṇṭiyavāṟē
   peṟṟu vāḻakkaṭavīr" eṉṟu maṉṟaṅkaḷ tōṟum muracaṟaivittuc cāṟṟi
   vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai avarkaḷukku upakarikkac ceytāṉ.
   'pōriṉ vāḻnarum pulattiṉ vāḻnarum
       tāriṉ vāḻnarun tavāap paṇṭattup
       payattiṉ vāḻnarum paṭiyiṟ ṟiriyā
       ōtatiṉ vāḻnarum oḻukkiṉ vāḻnarum
       yātta ciṟpak kayiṟṟiṉ vāḻnarum
       uyarntōr talaiyā iḻintō rīṟā' (4.2:47-52)

   yāvarkkum ciṟitēṉum tuṉpam uṇṭākātapaṭi ceyvittāṉ. aṟam ōṅkiṟṟu; pāvam
   nīṅkiṟṟu; kūṟṟu akālattil vantu uyirkaḷaik kavarntu cellutalait
   tavirntatu; nāṉilaṅkaḷum ceḻittaṉa. tampiyar, pakaivaruṭaiya ūrtōṟum
   ceṉṟu avarkaḷai aṭakki varu vārāyiṉar; tarucakaṉuṭaiya cēṉaiyōṭu vantu
   utavi purinta vīrarkaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittu aṉuppiṉaṉ. aruvilai
   naṉkalam mutaliyavaṟṟait tamarmukamāka avaṉukku viṭuttaṉaṉ.
   ivvāṟu utayaṇaṉ, paṭṭameyti, patumāpatiyuṭaṉ iṉpuṟuvāṉākic ceṅkatirc
   celvaṉuṭaiya eḻucciyum, pāṭum, tiṅkaḷum, nāḷum teḷitaliṉṟi makiḻveytic
   ceṅkōlōccit taṉṉuṭaiya nāṭṭai vaḷamuṟac ceytaṉaṉ.
   avaṉ taṉ nāṭṭil uḷḷāruṭaiya tuṉpattai iṅṅaṉan tīrttapiṉ, "ceyya
   vēṇṭuvatu yātu?" eṉṟu ārāyntu, uñcaiyiliruntu taṉṉai ēṟṟivantapiṭi
   vīḻnta iṭattai viḷaṅkac colli, "ataṉ eṉpu, tōl mutaliyavaikaḷai
   viraivil koṇṭuvāruṅkaḷ" eṉṟu cilarai aṉuppiviṭṭu, antap pakkattuḷḷa
   vēṭṭuvat talaivarkaḷaiyum kuṟumparkaḷaiyum aḻaittu, "emmaic cumantu
   vantu kāṭṭil viḻunta piṭi ceyta pērutavikku yām ceyyum kaimmāṟu yātum
   illai. āṉālum ataṉai maṟavāmal iruttal māṭcimai uṭaiyatātalāl, atu
   vīḻnta iṭattil oru māṭam eṭuppittu atil ataṉ uruvattai amaippittuk
   kālai mālaikaḷil ataṉai vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum. av vaḻiyē cellupavarkaḷukkup
   paci tīrumpaṭi aṭiciṟcālai amaittu uṇavu aḷittal vēṇṭum. tākattait
   tīrppataṟkut taṇṇīrp pantarkaḷ vaittu uṇṇīr aḷittal vēṇṭum.
   yārāyiruppiṉum tuṉpam uṟṟavarkaḷukku uriya nukarporuḷkaḷaik koṭuttal
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu colli avaṟṟukku vēṇṭiya tiraviyaṅkaḷai avarkaḷukku aḷittu,
   avarkaḷuṭaṉ am māṭam eṭuppittaṟkuriya taccar mutaliyōrkaḷaiyum ataṉ
   vaṭivam amaippōrkaḷaiyum aṉuppiṉaṉ.
   ataṉpiṉ, muṟkūṟiyavāṟu ap piṭiyiṉ eṉpu mutaliyavaṟṟaik
   koṇarntavarkaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittu aṉuppiviṭṭu, avaṟṟaik
   kaucāmpiyiṉiṭaiyē ōr iṭattil cēmittuvaittu aṅkē oru koṟṟavāyilaiyum
   ataṉ muṉṉē mika uyarnta māṭam oṉṟaiyum iyaṟṟuvittu av vāyilukku ap
   piṭiyiṉ uruvattai amaippittu av vaṭivattai vaḻipaṭutaṟku
   uriyavarkaḷaiyum piṟaraiyum niyamittu aṭiciṟ cālaikaḷaiyum taṇṇīrp
   pantarkaḷaiyum am māṭattiṉ pakkattē amaittu ataṉaiccūḻak kūttiyar
   irukkaiyaiyum tarumacaritaṅkaḷaip paṭippōrkaḷuṭaiya iruppiṭaṅkaḷaiyum
   amaittu mātantōṟum viḻāk koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭu vēṇṭiya
   poruḷkaḷaiyum koṭuttaṉaṉ.
   iṅṅaṉam utayaṇaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya kaṭappāṭukaḷai niṟaivēṟṟi
   makiḻvuṟṟirukkumpoḻutu, uñcainakarattiluḷḷa piramacāriyum vētam
   mutaliyavaṟṟai naṉku ōti uṇarntavaṉum pēriyāḻ mutaliya yāḻvakaiyaiyum
   piṟa nūlkaḷaiyum mika naṉṟāka aṟintavaṉumākiṟa 1aruñcukaṉ eṉṉum antaṇaṉ
   oruvaṉ kaucāmpi nakarattiluḷḷa taṉ uṟaviṉaraik kāṇutaṟporuṭṭup
   puṟappaṭṭu vantāṉ. iṭaivaḻiyil nīr uṇṇutaṟku eḻunta yāṉaik
   kūṭṭattiṉiṭaiyē avaṉ akappaṭṭu accamuṟṟu, aṅkē niṉṟa mika uyarnta
   vēṅkaimaram oṉṟiṉ kiḷaimītu ēṟi niṉṟu nāṟpuṟattaiyum nōkkiṉaṉ.
   nōkkukaiyil, kōṭapatiyeṉṉum yāḻ, talaivaṉaip pirinta kaṟpuṭaiyāḷpōla
   aḻaku keṭṭu narampukaḷai mūṅkil varuṭumpoḻutu 'tāmatīm' eṉṉum muḻakkam
   uṭaiyatāki aṅkē māṭṭikkoṇṭu kiṭappataik kaṇṭāṉ.
   ----------
       1 ariñcayaṉeṉṟu ip peyar vēṟu nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

   ataṉ iṉiya ōcaiyaikkēṭṭu yāṉaikaḷ kōpam nīṅkic cāntamuṟṟuc ceṉṟatai
   aṟintu avaṉ ataṉ camīpattil ceṉṟu, 'itu kiṉṉararāl iṭappaṭṭatāyiṉum
   iyakkarkaḷ meymmaṟantu pōkaṭṭatāyiṉum vēṟu yāvatāyiṉum ākuka' eṉṟu
   niṉaittup pala malarkaḷāl aruccittu ataṉaik kaiyil eṭuttukkoṇṭu
   kaucāmpiyai aṭaintu utayaṇaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyiṉ ayaliṭattē uḷḷa taṉ
   cuṟṟattāraic cārntu avarkaḷōṭu makiḻvuṟṟiruntāṉ. mālaikkālam vantatu.
   appōtu ataṉai vācittāṉ.
   vācikkaiyil, mēl māṭattil cāḷarattait tiṟantu vaittukkoṇṭu
   patumāpatiyōṭu aḷavaḷāvi makiḻvuṟṟirunta utayaṇaṉ anta yāḻoliyaik kēṭ
   ṭaṉaṉ. kēṭṭavuṭaṉ avaṉ vimmitamuṟṟu ārāyntu pārttu, "ivvoli nammuṭaiya
   yāḻiṉ oliyākum" eṉṟu teḷintu uṭaṉē ataṉai varuvikka niṉaintu puṟattē
   vantu taṉ meykāppāḷarait tēṭiyapoḻutu aṅkē viḻippuṭaṉ paṭuttirunta
   vayantakaṉ eḻuntu viraintu ceṉṟu, "yātu nikaḻntatu?" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ.
   utayaṇaṉ,"kōṭapatiyiṉ kural iṅkē kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu; nīyum kēṭpāyāka.
   viraintu ceṉṟu atu vācippavaṉai iṅkē aḻaittuvā" eṉṟaṉaṉ. uṭaṉē
   vayantakaṉ māṭattiṉiṉṟum kīḻē iṟaṅki avvoli tōṉṟiya etir vīṭṭai aṭaintu
   uḷḷē pukuntu yāḻ vācippavaṉai nōkki, "inta yāḻ eppaṭi uṉakkuk
   kiṭaittatu?" eṉṟu kēṭka, avaṉ, "uñcaiyiliruntu nāṉ narumataiyāṟṟaik
   kaṭantu varumpoḻutu oru malaiccāralil itaṉaip peṟṟēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ.
   kūṟavē, anta yāḻuṭaṉ avaṉai aḻaittukkoṇṭu vantu kāṭṭi,"nām
   uñcaiyampatiyiṉiṉṟu varum nāḷil naḷ iruḷil pattirāpati mikunta vēkamāka
   varukaiyil vintamalaiyiṉ teṉticaic cāralil viḻunta kōṭapatiyākum itu"
   eṉṟu vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉiṭam kūṟiṉaṉ.
   kūṟavē, utayaṇaṉ tāṅkā uvakaiyaṉāki, "varukaveṉ ṉalliyāḻ vattava
   ṉamutam" eṉṟu ataṉai vāṅki, "eṉ uyirt tuṇaiyākiya inta yāḻait tanta nī
   vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaik kūṟuvāyāka" eṉṟu collip vēṇṭiya aruṅkalaṅkaḷaiyum
   perum poruḷaiyum ōr ūraiyum aruñcukaṉukkuk koṭuttu, "nī in nakarattē
   irukka vēṇṭum" eṉṟu collip piṉṉum virumpuvaṉavaṟṟai aḷittu avaṉai
   aṉuppiviṭṭu, ulavā viruppoṭu pulartalaikāṟum uḷḷiyum, urukiyum, ataṉaip
   pulliyum pārāṭṭi vācavatattaiyai niṉaintē irāppoḻutaik kaḻittaṉaṉ.
   viṭintavuṭaṉ anta yāḻaip putuppittu vācavatattaiyai niṉaintu,
   "vācavatattāy! nī kaṟṟa yāḻ itō vantuviṭṭatu. nī vantu itaṉai vācittu
   makiḻvāyāka. eṉṉai maṟantāy" eṉṟu avaḷai niṉaintu niṉaintu
   pulampuvāṉāki, iḻanta tampikaḷaip peṟṟum kōṭapatiyai aṭaintum ciṟitum
   makiḻviṉṟik kālam kaḻippāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   avaṉ iṅṅaṉam irukkaiyil irācakiriya nakarattil, tarucakaṉ, tāṉ
   pātukāttu vaittirunta caṅkamaṉṉaruṭaiya urimaip poruḷkaḷai avarkaḷiṭam
   aṉuppiviṭṭu, "aṅkē uḷḷa aracarkaḷaic ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum nīkki iṅkē viraivil
   aṉuppuka" eṉpataic collumpaṭi taṉṉuṭaiya karumamākkaḷai ap pakaivarpāl
   viṭuttaṉaṉ. kēṭṭa avarkaḷ mika makiḻntu,
   "iḻinta mākkaḷō ṭiṉpa mārtaliṉ
       uyarnta mākkaḷō ṭuṟupakai yiṉitu" (4.4:21-22)

   eṉṟu kūṟi makiḻntu tarucakaṉaip pukaḻntu urumaṇṇuvāvaic ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum
   nīkki avaṉpāl aṉuppiṉar. urumaṇṇuvā vantu tarucakaṉaik kaṇṭu
   elicceviyaracaṉ tampiyākiya cittirāṅkataṉaiyum maṟṟa aracarkaḷaiyum
   ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟu nīkki avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik koṭuttaṉuppumpaṭi
   ceytapiṉpu, utayaṇaṉpāl nikaḻnta ceytikaḷai ellām kēṭṭu ellaiyaṟṟa
   makiḻcciyōṭu aṅkē iruntāṉ.
   iruntapoḻutu, yūki koṭuttatāka ōr ōlaiyaic cātakaṉ koṇarntu
   urumaṇṇuvāviṭam koṭuttāṉ. av vōlaiyil, "yām eṇṇiya kāriyam iṉitu
   niṟaivēṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iṉi vācavatattaiyai utayaṇaṉpāl oppittal nalam" eṉṟu
   eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu. ataṉai aṟinta urumaṇṇuvā cātakaṉai nōkki, "emmai
   viṭṭup pirintapiṉpu nikaḻntavaṟṟaic collavēṇṭum" eṉṉa, avaṉ
   avaṟṟaiyellām kūṟiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē urumaṇṇuvā avaṉuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu iṭaiyē
   taṅka vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil taṅki., puṇṭarameṉṉum nakarattai aṭaintu aṅkē
   irunta yūkiyaiyum vācavatattai mutaliyavarkaḷaiyum pārttu, mikunta
   uvakaiyuṟṟu avarkaḷuṭaṉ kaucāmpikaiyaic cārntu ataṉ puṟattē uḷḷa
   matukāmpīrameṉṉum cōlaiyil putiyarāy varum aracarkaḷukkāka
   amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa māṭam oṉṟil, vācavatattai mutaliyavarkaḷai irukkumpaṭi
   ceytuviṭṭut tāṉmaṭṭum utayaṇaṉpāl celvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   urumaṇṇuvā celluvataṟku muṉpu oru nāḷ utayaṇaṉ kōṭapatiyai
   vācittukkoṇṭu makiḻntirukkumpoḻutu patumai, "araca, niṉṉai vaḻipaṭṭu
   vācavatattai kaṟṟu vallaḷātaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa inta yāḻai aṭicciyum
   vaḻipaṭṭuk kaṟṟaṟku viruppamuṭaiyēṉ; terivittaruḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu mellac
   colli añcali ceyyavē, avaṉ vācavatattaiyiṉ niṉaiviṉāl maṉam nekiḻntu
   uruki vimmutaluṟṟup patumaikku yātoru viṭaiyum koṭāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ataṉai aṟinta patumai, "ippoḻutu yām pulattal poruntuvataṉṟu" eṉṟum,
   "vācavatattaiyai ivar niṉaintu varuntutal muṟaiyē; iḵtu uyarntōr
   oḻukkam" eṉṟum eṇṇi yātoṉṟaiyum aṟiyātavaḷpōla iruntu vēṟoru kāriyattai
   muṉṉiṭṭu eḻupavaḷpōlavē eḻuntu tōḻiyarkaḷ cūḻat taṉ māṭattai aṭaintāḷ.
   appoḻutu vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉai nōkki, "itukāṟum iraṇṭu maṉaiviyaraiyum
   camamāka niṉaintu oḻukiya nī vācavatattaiyai niṉaintu iṉṟu ivaḷpāl aṉpu
   kuṟaintataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉai?" eṉṟu viṉāva, avaṉ ataṉaiyum kēḷāṉāyiṉaṉ;
   patumāpatiyaiyum niṉaintilaṉ. piṉpu, naḷakiriyai aṭakki ataṉ piṭaril
   ēṟiyapoḻutu vācavatattaiyaik kaṇṭatumutal avaḷ aḻalvāyppaṭṭu iṟantaṉaḷ
   eṉpatu iṟutiyākavuḷḷa ceytikaḷai iṭaiviṭātu colli araṟṟikkoṇṭu
   paṭukkaiyil cāyntaviṭattuc ciṟitu tuyil vantatu. appoḻutu, pāṟkaṭaliṉ
   iṭaiyē uḷḷa veḷḷēṟu oṉṟu vantu taṅkappeṟṟa veṇṭāmaraip pū oṉṟai oru
   teyva maṅkai taṉakkuk koṭuttatāka oru kaṉāk kaṇṭaṉaṉ. kaṇṭapiṉ eḻuntu
   arukaṉait toḻutu oru muṉivaraik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkit tāṉ mutal nāḷ iravil
   kaṇṭa kaṉāvait terivittu ataṉ payaṉaic colla vēṇṭumeṉṟu vēṇṭiṉaṉ.
   vēṇṭavē avar, "vācavatattai tīyil mūḻki iṟantatāḷeṉṟu
   eṇṇiyirukkiṉṟaṉai. atu poy. avaḷai viraivil aṭaivāy. aṭaintapiṉpu
   avaḷiṭamāka vittiyātarar ulakai āḷum cakkaravarttiyātaṟkuriya oru
   makaṉaip peṟuvai" eṉṟu colli ataṟkuriya kāraṇattaiyum kūṟiṉar, kēṭṭa
   utayaṇaṉ, "muṉivaruṭaiya vākku eṉṟum vīṇpōvatillai. vācavatattaiyai nām
   etirppaṭuvatum uḷatākumō?" eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇ ṭiruntaṉaṉ. irukkaiyil,
   urumaṇṇuvāviṉ varavai vāyiṟkāvalar uṇarttiṉar. uṇarttavē utayaṇaṉ,
   "varuka" eṉṟu colliviṭṭut tāṉ eḻuntu viraintu ceṉṟu taṉ aṭiyil vīḻnta
   urumaṇṇuvāviṉ kaiyaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu yūkiyai aṭaintāṟ pōṉṟa makiḻcciyaip
   peṟṟut taṉṉuṭaiya urimaip paḷḷiyai aṭaintāṉ.
   aṭainta utayaṇaṉ avaṉuṭaṉ taṉiyē ōriṭattil iruntu "nikaḻntavaṟṟaik
   kūṟuka" eṉṟu kēṭka, uru maṇṇuvā tāṉ ciṟaippaṭṭatu mutaliya ceytikaḷai
   viḷaṅkac colliviṭṭu, "iṅkē nikaḻnta ceytikaḷaik kūṟu" eṉṟu
   vayantakaṉaik kēṭpa, avaṉ utayaṇaṉ kōṭapatiyaip peṟṟataic coṟṟaṉaṉ.
   collavē utayaṇaṉ atu kaṟṟa vācavatattaiyai niṉaintu mīṭṭum
   araṟṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. appoḻutu avaṉuṭaiya maṉavaruttattai oruvakaiyāka māṟṟa
   virumpiya vayantakaṉ, "maṉaiviyar iruvaruḷ aṟivilum oḻukkattilum
   ciṟantavaḷ yār? yāriṭattil uṉakku viruppam atikam?" eṉṟu kēṭpa, "eṉakku
   vācavatattaiyē ciṟantavaḷ; yāṉ tavam illēṉātaliṉ avaḷ tīyil oḷittaṉaḷ"
   eṉṟu colli utayaṇaṉ piṉṉum maṉam maṟukuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   kaṇṭa vayantakaṉ. "vācavatattaiyai mīṭṭut tarum periyōrait taricittu
   vaḻipāṭāṟṟutaṟku yām makata nāṭu ceṉṟapoḻutu patumaiyaik kaṇṇuṟṟu
   avaḷai virumpi avaḷ *maya*ka iruntaṉai; aṅṅaṉam iruntatu uṉakku nalamō?
   uṉṉuṭaiya maṉam oruvaḻippaṭṭataṉṟu" eṉṟu colla utayaṇaṉ,
   "vācavatattaikkum patumaikkum uruvattilum kuṇaṅkaḷilum ciṟitēṉum
   vēṟupāṭiṉmaiyiṉ yāṉ virumpalāyiṉēṉ; aṉṟiyum patumaiyai yāṉ
   virumpiyataṟkuk kāraṇam ūḻviṉaiyum ākum; ataṉai maṟuttaṟku yārālē
   muṭiyum?" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa vayantakaṉ, "aṟiyātavaṉeṉṟu eṉṉai
   ikaḻaṟka; yāṉ collutalai ūṉṟik kēṭṭiṭuka; muṉpu irācakiriyattiṉ
   puṟattēyuḷḷa cōlaiyil yām kaṇṭa peṟutaṟkariya peri yōr iṉṟu
   vantirukkiṉṟaṉar; vācavatattaiyai avar varuvittut taruvatākavum valintu
   kūṟiṉar. ātalāl, nī cila tiṉam patumaiyai nīṅkit taṉiyē
   tūyaiyākiyiruttal naṉṟu" eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ. coṉṉavuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉ viraintu
   ceṉṟu patumāpatiyai nōkki, "eñciyuḷḷa pakaivaraik keṭuttaṟkuriya
   ciṟanta ālōcaṉai oṉṟu uṇṭu; ataṉai yām taṉittuc ceyya vēṇṭum; ātalāl,
   tōḻimārōṭu nī vēṟiṭattiṟkuc celluka" eṉṟu colli aṉuppi viṭṭu,
   avaḷuṭaiya antappurattait tūymaiceytu viḷakkēṟṟuvittut tāṉum tūyaṉāki
   veḷḷuṭaiyuṭuttu, "teyvamē! vācavatattaiyai eṉakkut tara vēṇṭum" eṉṟu
   collikkoṇṭum vīṇaiyaik kaiyāl aṇaittukkoṇṭum tuyilvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   avaṉ aṅṅaṉam tuyilukaiyil, vēṟu makaḷiriṭattu avaṉukku viruppam
   illāmaiyum pakaivaṉai vellutaṟkut tarucakaṉuṭaiya naṭpaip peṟa vēṇṭiyē
   avaṉ taṅkaiyākiya patumāpatiyai utayaṇaṉ maṇantaṉaṉ eṉpataiyum iravum
   pakalum vācavatattaiyaiyē niṉaintu tuyiluṅkālattum araṟṟip palavāṟu
   pulampikkoṇṭē iruttalaiyum kāṭṭi vācavatattaiyait tēṟacceyya virumpi
   mantirikaḷ avaḷai oru civikaiyil ēṟṟik koṇarntu, "uṉṉuṭaiya talaivaṉ
   iyalpai nī pārppāyāka" eṉṟu avvantappurattiṉuḷḷē aṉuppiṉar.
   ac camayattil utayaṇaṉ, "vācavatattāy! uṉakku vīṇaiyaip payiṟṟuvikkum
   poḻutu uṉṉuṭaiya pārvaiyāl varuntiya yāṉ uṉṉaip pirintu āṟṟēṉāyiṉēṉ;
   eṉṉai maṟantāyē" eṉṟu araṟṟikkoṇṭum aṅkēyuḷḷa pūmālaikaḷai vāṭacceyyum
   vevviya *uyi**piṉaṉākiyum kaṉavil avaṉ varuntutalai vācavatattai
   kaṇṭāḷ. "naṉavilum eṉ uyirt tuṇaivaṉ iruttal ivvaṇṇantāṉō? tammaiyaṉṟi
   vēṟu teyvam illāta makaḷiruṭaiya piriviṉkaṇ avarkaḷai niṉaintu
   varuntutal uyarnta kaṇavarkaḷatu iyalpeṉṟu nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatai iṉṟu nērē
   kaṉṭēṉ" eṉṉum taṇiyāta uvakaiyaḷāki, "iṅṅaṉam nalla oḻukkattaiyuṭaiya
   ivarukku maṟumaiyiṉpamum iyaivatāka" eṉṟu colli avaṉukku añcaliceytu
   mella arukil ceṉṟu kōṭapatiyaik kaṇṭu pirinta kuḻantaiyaik kāṇum tāyaip
   pōlavē āṟutaluṟṟu ataṉai mella eṭuttu vācikkat toṭaṅkiṉaḷ. avvoli
   utayaṇaṉ kātilpaṭṭatu. paṭavē avaṉ, "vācavatattāy! vantaṉaiyō?" eṉṟu
   kūntaṉmutalāp pūmpuṟam nīvi, "eṉṉai neṭunāḷ pirintu nī eṅṅaṉam āṟṟiṉai?
   ataṉaic colla vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kēṭpa, avaḷ viyantu oṉṟaṟkum viṭai kūṟāḷāki
   vaṇaṅkic cummā iruntaṉaḷ. irukkavē avaṉ mīṭṭum, "poḻil viḷaiyāṭṭaik
   karuti yām ilāvāṇaka malaiccāralil taṅkiyapoḻutu oru nāḷ,
   "taḻaimutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇarntu tara vēṇṭum" eṉṟu nī collakkēṭṭu yāṉ
   koṇṭuvarutaṟkuk kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟaviṭattu vīṭṭil neruppuppaṟṟa iṉṉuyir
   nītta ilaṅkiḻai maṭavōy! uriya aṇikaḷaiyellām nīkki melintu piṟaḷpōlē
   tōṟṟukiṉṟāy; neruppil ventōr iyalpu ippaṭiyō? itaṉaic colla vēṇṭum"
   eṉṟu avaḷ meyyait tīṇṭit tuyilat toṭaṅkiṉaṉ.
   appoḻutu vayantakaṉ vantu avaḷai nōkki, "nīr iṅkē vara vēṇṭuvatu
   nāḷaittiṉam, yūkiyōṭētāṉ; talaivaṉ eppoḻutum ummiṭattu aṉpōṭu
   iruppataik kāṭṭutaṟkē ippoḻutu aḻaittuvantēm" eṉṟu colla, avaḷ
   piriviṟku añci, naṭuṅkum maṉattōṭu taṉmēlē kiṭanta utayaṇaṉ kaiyai
   mella eṭuttu aṇaiyil vaittuviṭṭu avaṉai vaṇaṅki uṭampiṉiṉṟum nīṅkum
   uyirpōla veḷippōntu cāṅkiyattāyaiyum yūkiyaiyum aṭaintaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ
   puṭaipeyarntu eḻuntu, "eṉakku ēṉ viṭaitaravillai?" eṉṟu avaḷait
   taḻuvuvataṟkuc ceṉṟu avaḷai aṅkē kāṇāṉākic ceyalaṟṟut tuyilmayakkam
   oḻintu, uyarnta māṇikkattaip peṟṟa vaṟiyavaṉ oruvaṉ ataṉai āḻamāṉa
   nīrnilaiyil pōkaṭṭuviṭṭut taḷarvatupōlat tuyiliṟkaṇṭa taṉ uyirt
   tuṇaiviyai viḻittapiṉ kāṇāmaiyāl araṟṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ataṉai aṟinta vayantakaṉ mika viraintu ceṉṟu "iṅṅaṉam iravum pakalum
   tuṉpattōṭu araṟṟutal aracarkku iyalpaṉṟu" eṉṟu takka kāraṇaṅkaḷaik
   kāṭṭik kūṟiṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ "vācavatattai nēṟṟiravil eṉ paḷḷiyaṟaiyil
   mella vantu kōṭapatiyai eṭutatu vācittuk koṇṭiruntaṉaḷ. irukkaiyil,
   eṇṇeyiṉṟi aḻukkaṭaintu piṇaṅkiya kūntal piṉṉalaiyuṭaiya avaḷuṭaiya
   puṟattaiyum neṟṟiyaiyum; taivantu melivoḻintu kaṇpaṭai koṇṭēṉ.
   koṇṭapoḻutu avaḷ eṉṉaiviṭṭu nīṅkiṉaḷ. eṉṉuṭaiya neñcam eṉakku
   vayamākiṉṟatillai. yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ?" eṉṟu colla vayantakaṉ "kaṉavil kaṇṭa
   poruḷai naṉavil aṭaital tēvarkkum icaivataṉṟu. ik kūṟṟaik kēṭpiṉ piṟar
   nakaippar. vācavatattaiyiṉ vaṭivaṅkoṇṭa iyakkiyeṉṉum teyvam iṅkē uṇṭu.
   ataṉait taṭuttaṟku ēṟṟa mantirattai yāṉ aṟivēṉ" eṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa
   poruḷillāta vārttaikaḷ palavaṟṟaic colli mikka tuṇivōṭu iruntaṉaṉ.
   ippoḻutu poḻutu viṭintatu.
   uṭaṉē utayaṇaṉ kaṇkaḻuvi niyamaṅkaḷai muṭittuk koṇṭu kuḷampum, kōṭum,
   poṉ aḻuttiyaṉavum, putiya āṭaikaḷ aṇiyap peṟṟaṉavumākiya pacukkaḷ
   palavaṟṟai vācavatattaiyai niṉaintu arumaṟaiyāḷarkku eḻumuṟai vīcit taṉ
   kaṉavai vēṟu piṟarkkuc collāmal, "virakamuṭaiyavarkaḷukku uḷatākum
   kāriyam ituvō?" eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭiruntaṉaṉ. irunta poḻutu vayantakaṉ vantu
   "yām muṉpu makatattil kaṇṭa periyōr in nakarattaic cārnta
   matukāmpīravaṉa meṉṉum cōlaiyil vantu taṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē
   celvōmāka" eṉṟu collavē utayaṇaṉ makiḻantu tēr ēṟi avaṉuṭaṉ ac cōlaiyai
   aṭaintaṉaṉ.
   aṅkē uḷḷa māṭattiṉ ōr aṟaiyil tavak kōlattōṭu yūki iruntaṉaṉ. maṟṟōr
   aṟaiyil cāṅkiyattāyōṭu vācavatattai iruntaṉaḷ. vayantakaṉ mutalil
   utayaṇaṉ varavai yūkikku uṇarttip piṉ avaṉōṭu ceṉṟu yūkiyaik kāṭṭa
   utayaṇaṉ, "ivaṉ yām makatattil kaṇṭa muṉivaṉ allaṉ; yūkiyē. ivaṉait
   taḻuvutaṟku maṉam viraikiṉṟatu; itaṉai naṉṟāka aṟital vēṇṭum" eṉṟu
   ārāykaiyil, avaṉatu mārpiṉkaṇ yāṉaikkompāl uḷḷa taḻumpu kāṇappaṭṭatu.
   paṭavē aiyam tīrntu veytuyirttu eḻuntu niṉṟu;
   "ūṟil cūḻcci yukanta rāya!
       nāṟiruṅ kūntalai māṟip piṟantuḻik
       kāṇat tarukuṟu muṉivaṉai nīyiṉi
       yāṇarc ceykai yuṭaittatu teḷintēṉ;
       vantaṉai yeṉṟutaṉ cantaṉa mārpiṟ
       pūntār kuḻaiyap pulliṉaṉ porukkeṉat
       tīntēṉ kalanta tēmpāl pōla
       nakaiyurut teḻutaru mukatta ṉākit
       tuṟantōrk kotta taṉṟuniṉ ciṟanta
       aruḷvakai" (4.7:186-95)

   eṉṟu kūṟi, "iṉi etaik karutiyāvatu nīṅkuvāyāyiṉ, eṉ uyir nīṅkiviṭum"
   eṉṟu coṉṉapoḻutu yūki, "vācavatattaiyai viraintu ivaṉukkuk kāṭṭa
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē tōḻar vācavatattai cevilittāyō ṭirunta
   aṟaikku *avaḷai aḻaittuc ceṉṟaṉar. vācavatattai, "numakkup periya
   anukūlam piṟakkum eṉpatai uṇartti num aṉpar colliyatai mēṟkoṇṭu numatu
   kaṭṭaḷai peṟāmaiyākiya apacārattai mēṟ koṇṭa kallāk kaṟpiṟ kayattiyēṉ
   yāṉ" eṉṟu mikka nāṇaṅkoṇṭu naṭuṅki eḻuntu kaikuvittut taṉ kūntal avaṉ
   kālil puraḷavum kaṇṇīr avaṉ puṟavaṭiyai naṉaippavum vaṇaṅkikkiṭantaṉaḷ.
   utayaṇaṉ avaḷai eṭuttup palamuṟai taḻuvi, akala niṉṟa cāṅkiyattāyai
   nōkki,
   tuṉpak kālattut tuṇaiyemak kāki
       iṉpa mītaṟ kiyaintukai viṭātu
       perumutu talaimaiyi ṉorumīk kūriya
       uyartavak kiḻamainum muṭampi ṉākiya
       ciṟṟupa kāram vaṟṟal cellātu
       āla vittiṟ peruki ñālattu
       naṉṟi yīṉṟatu" (4.7:219-25)

   eṉṟu aṉpumayamākiya moḻikaḷai avaḷukkuc cālak kūṟiṉaṉ.
   piṉpu vācavatattaiyiṉ tōṟṟattai aṟintu utayaṇaṉ maṉam varuntiṉāṉ.
   avaḷait tāṉ aṭainta naṟceytiyai nakarattārkku muracaṟaivittut
   terivittāṉ. aṟinta nakarattār tattam māṭaṅkaḷiṉmītu koṭiyeṭuttu
   viḻavayarntaṉar. yūkiyiṉ āṟṟalaiyum avaṉatu naṇpiṉ mēmpāṭṭaiyum
   vācavatattaiyiṉ niṟaiyaiyum viyantu matittup palapaṭap pārāṭṭiṉārkaḷ.
   appāl utayaṇaṉum vācavatattaiyum yūkiyum pañcavaṇṇattup paṭākaikaḷ pala
   acaiyumpaṭi yāṉaiyiṉ piṭariyiṟ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa puṇṇiyatīrtta nīrāṭi
   vilai varampaṟṟa ciṟanta āparaṇaṅkaḷai aṇintu koṇṭu mikka iṉpattōṭu
   vīṟupeṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
   appāl utayaṇaṉ yūkikkuc colla vēṇṭiya mukamaṉkaḷaic collutal
   1vēṟupāṭṭaip pulappaṭuttum eṉṟu eṇṇi oṉṟum collātavaṉākic cāṅkiyattāyiṉ
   aruḷ mikutiyai mīṭṭum pārāṭṭiṉaṉ. "uṉ tavvaiyai vantu pārttuc cella
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu patumāpatikku avaṉ colliyaṉuppa, uṭaṉē avaḷ, talaivaṉukku
   vācavatattai uyirttuṇaivi eṉpatai naṉku aṟintavaḷātalāl, am moḻiyai
   viruppattōṭu ēṟṟukkoṇṭu ciṟanta aṇikalaṅkaḷaik kāṇikkaiyāka ēntit
   tōḻimār palar cūḻntuvara aracaṉai nīṅkit taṉiyē irukkum camayam aṟintu
   taṉ varavai avaḷukku muṉṉatākavē terivittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vācavatattaiyai
   vaṇaṅkiṉaḷ.
   -------
       1"iṇaiya rivaremak *kiṉṉamayā meṉṟu puṉaiyiṉum pulleṉṉu naṭpu"
       tirukkuṟaḷ 790.

   vaṇaṅkavē avaḷ patumaiyait taḻuviyeṭuttu, "kaṟpil iṉṉum mēmpaṭuvāyāka"
   eṉṟu ācikūṟi mikunta iṉpamaṭaintāḷ. appāl iruvarum yāṉaittantattāl
   ceyyappaṭṭa kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭṭiliṉmēluḷḷa mellaṇai oṉṟil ēṟiyiruntu
   oru tāmarai malaril cērntu taṅkiya iraṇṭu tirumakaḷirpōla viḷaṅkiṉar.
   utayaṇaṉ pērattāṇiyil pala periyōr cūḻa iruntu yūkiyai nōkki, "uñcai
   nakarattil nāṉ aṭainta ciṟaittuṉpattai nīkkiyapiṉpu mīṭṭum uṉakku
   nērnta māyaccākkāṭṭiṟkum vācavatattai tīyil pāyntaṉaḷeṉṟa māyac
   ceytikkum uriya kāraṇaṅkaḷai viḷaṅkac collavēṇṭum" eṉṟu kēṭka yūki,
   "pāñcāla rācaṉ, kaucāmpiyait taṉṉatāka ākkikkoṇṭiruntataiyum
   piraccōtaṉaṉōṭu naṭpuk koḷḷutaṟku muyaṉṟu ataṟkuriya kālattai avaṉ
   pārttukkoṇ ṭiruntatu mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum akat toṟṟāḷarāl naṉku aṟintu
   avaṉuṭaiya māṟupāṭṭaiyum perumuyaṟciyaiyum kuṟaikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu
   tuṇintum, avaṉai veṉṟu paḻaiya aracāṭciyaip peṟutaliṟ ciṟitum
   kavalaiyiṉṟi vācavatattaiyai oru poḻutum piriyāmal iṉpuṟṟirunta uṉatu
   iyalpai vēṟupaṭutta eṇṇiyum, tarucakaṉai uṉakkup paṭait tuṇaiyākka
   niccayittum, piṅkalakaṭakarai uṉṉuṭaṉ cērkka niṉaittum iṅṅaṉam ceytēṉ.
   eṇṇiyavellām teyvattāl cevvaṉē niṟaivēṟiṉa. uṉ tiṟattum vācavatattai
   tiṟattum yāṅkaḷ eṇṇiyavellām tiṇṇiyavāy niṟaivēṟiyataṟkuk kāraṇam antac
   cāṅkiyattāyiṉ pērutaviyē. uṉṉuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷaiyiṉṟi 1urimaiyāl yāṉ
   ceytaṉavaṟṟaip poṟut taruḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ
   "eṉṉuṭaiya talaimaiyai yāṉ aṭaiyumpaṭi ceytāy eṉpatai ayalār
   irukkumpoḻutu colluvēṉāyiṉ atu vēṟṟumai payakkum. yām iruvarum ōr
   uyirē" eṉṟu mukamaṉ kūṟi avaṉai aḻaittukkoṇṭu araṇmaṉai ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   -------
       1 "viḻaitakaiyāṉ vēṇṭiyiruppar keḻutakaiyāṟ kēḷātu naṭṭār ceyiṉ."
       tirukkuṟaḷ, 804.

   ceṉṟapiṉ avaṉ patumaiyai nōkki, "vācavatattaiyōṭu nī oru kalattilēyē
   uṇṇa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu colliyapoḻutu avaḷ vaṇaṅki,
   "peruntaku kaḻaṟpiṉem perumaka ṭaṉṉoṭu
       pirinta tiṅka ḷellām piriyātu
       oruntava ṇuṟaital vēṇṭuva laṭikaḷ!
       avvara maruḷit taruta leṉkuṟai" (4.8:92-5)

   eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ avaḷai aṉpuṭaṉ mikap pārāṭṭi aṉuppiṉaṉ. atu
   terinta vācavatattai patumāpatiyiṭam avaṉ vaittuḷḷa aṉpai niṉaintu mika
   ūṭiṉaḷ. atukaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ avaḷai nōkki "aṟivilum tōṟṟappolivilum uṉṉai
   ottirun tamaipaṟṟiyē patumaiyai maṇañceytēṉ" eṉṟu teḷivittāṉ. āyiṉum
   taṉṉaippōṉṟavaḷ avaḷ eṉṟa col maṉattai varutta vācavatattai kaṭaikkaṇ
   civantu appāl ceṉṟaṉaḷ,. appōtu avaṉ avaḷaip palamuṟai nayantu pārāṭṭi,
   "uruviṉallatu peṇmaiyil niṉṉai avaḷ ovvāḷ" eṉṟu avaḷ makiḻtaṟkuriya
   iṉiya moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟaic collit teruṭṭiyum, teḷittum, maruṭṭiyum
   makiḻntum avaḷatu tuṉiyait tīrttaṉaṉ. appāl kaṇṭōr viyakkumpaṭi
   oruvarai oruvar piriyāmal uṟaivārāyiṉar.
   aṅṅaṉam uṟaikaiyil utayaṇaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya paricaṉattār yāvaraiyum
   vaittukkoṇṭu urumaṇṇuvāviṟkuc cēṉāpatip paṭṭattai aḷittu ēṉāti
   mōtirattait tāṉē avaṉ viralil ceṟittataṉṟi, pala ilakkam poṉ
   varuvāyuḷḷa pala ūrkaḷaiyum nālvakaip paṭaikaḷaiyum koṭuttup patumaiyiṉ
   tōḻiyākiya irācaṉai eṉpavaḷai avaṉukkup peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ maṇam
   ceyvittuc cayanti nakarattaiyum ilāvāṇaka nakarattaiyum aḷittu
   1"kuravaraik kaṇṭu vaḻipāṭu ceytukoṇṭu avviṭattilēyē iruntu yām
   virumpiya kālattil iṅkē vantu celvāyāka" eṉṟu colli avaṉai aṉuppiṉaṉ.
   ----------
       1 kuravar-iṅkē tantaiyār.

   piṉpu cēti nāṭṭaiyum vaicāli nakarattaiyum yūkikku aḷittu ac ceytiyai
   an nāṭṭārum nakarattārum aṟintu koḷḷumpaṭi tirumukam aṉuppit
