   terivittaṉaṉ. iṭapakaṉukku, muṉaiyūr eṉṟu peyaruḷḷa oru talainakarum
   kāṭṭunāṭu aimpatum koṭuttup piṉṉum cila varu vāykaḷai aḷittu "puṭpaka
   nakarattai aṭaintu naṭpāḷarōṭu makiḻntiruntu aḻaittapiṉ varuka" eṉṟu
   avaṉaip pōkkiṉaṉ. vayantakaṉukkup patiṉoru nakaraṅkaḷai aḷittatuṭaṉ
   ovvoru nāḷaikkum āyiram poṉ vētaṉam aḷittu "eppoḻutum eṉṉuṭaṉ uṟaika"
   eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ; icaiccaṉ mutaliya tōḻarkkup pala ūrkaḷai aḷittu
   virumpiya iṭaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu irukkumpaṭi ceyvittāṉ. tāṉ uñcaiyampatiyil
   ciṟaiyil iruntapoḻutu pala iṭaṅkaḷil maṟaintiruntu pātukāttu vanta
   vīrarkaḷai muracaṟaivittu varuvittu vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittu aṉuppiviṭṭup
   pōril iṟantavarkaḷuṭaiya cantatiyāka uḷḷavarkaḷait terintaḻaittu
   avarkaḷukku virumpiyavaṟṟai īntaṉaṉ.
   cātakaṉ eṉṉum kulālaṉai aḻaippittu avaṉukkup peruṅkuyap paṭṭam aḷittu
   "nī kavalaiyiṉṟi ilāvāṇa nakaril iru" eṉṟu colli avaṉukku iraṇṭu
   ūrkaḷaik koṭuttaṉaṉ. taṉakkāka vantu makata nāṭṭil uḻantu utaviya
   māntarkaḷukku virumpiya poruḷkaḷai aḷittut tattam ūrkaḷil ceṉṟu
   irukkumpaṭi aṉuppiṉaṉ. ātuttiyatarumaṉ eṉṉum vīraṉukku ōr ūr nalkiṉaṉ.
   pōril uyirviṭṭa cattiyakāyaṉ eṉṉum vīraṉuṭaiya makkaḷai varuvittu
   avarkaḷuṭaiya kūṭṭattāruḷ talaimaiyai mēṟkoṇṭirukkum pataviyai
   avarkaḷukku aḷittaṉaṉ. yūkiyai maṭṭum taṉṉuṭaṉ irukkumpaṭi ceytuviṭṭu
   avaṉuṭaṉ irunta paṭaiyāḷikaḷ palaraiyum uriya iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkumpaṭi
   vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittu aṉuppiṉaṉ. taṉṉuṭaiya tāyai aṭaintu paṇintu
   "vēṇṭiya tāṉaṅkaḷaic ceyvāyāka" eṉṟu mikka varuvāyai uṭaiyatoru nāṭṭaic
   cērppittāṉ. vācavatattaikkum patumaikkum tēvikaḷukkuk koṭuttaṟkuriya
   viruttiyaik koṭuttāṉ. āṭal, pāṭal, aḻaku,eṉ pavaṟṟil mikka cēṭimārkaḷai
   iru kūṟākki aḷittup piṉṉum avarkaḷukku vēṇṭuvaṉa aḷittu avarkaḷuṭaṉ
   mika makiḻn;tu uṟaivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ippaṭi irukkaiyil oru nāḷ vāyilkāvalaṉ vantu vaṇaṅki "piraccōtaṉaṉ
   tūtuvar kaṭaittalaiyil vantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar." eṉṟu kūṟa utayaṇaṉ
   "avarkaḷai
   varaviṭuka" eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉavē,
   "maṇṇiyaṉ maṉṉarkkuk kaṇṇeṉa vakutta
       nīti naṉṉū lōtiya nāviṉaḷ;
       kaṟṟunaṉ kaṭaṅkic ceṟṟamu mārvamum
       muṟṟa nīṅkit tattuva vakaiyiṉum
       kaṇṇiṉu muḷḷē...
       kuṟippi ṉecca neṟippaṭa nāṭit
       tēṉṟōyt taṉṉa kiḷaviyiṟ ṟeḷipaṭat
       tāṉṟerin tuṇarun taṉmai yaṟiliṉaḷ
       uṟuppuppala vuṟuppiṉu muyirmuta ṟarukkiṉum
       niṟuttuppala vūci neruṅka vūṉṟiṉum
       kaṟuttuppala kaṭiyak kāṭṭiṉuṅ kāṭṭātu
       ciṟappupala ceyiṉun tirintupiṟi turaiyāḷ;
       piṟaippū ṇakalattup perumaka ṉavaṉmāṭṭuk
       kuṟittatu kūṟutal cellāk koḷkaiyaḷ;
       iṉṉatu ceykeṉa vēva liṉṟiyum
       maṉṉiya kōmāṉ maṉattatai yuṇarntu
       muṉṉiyatu muṭikku muyaṟciya ḷoṉṉār
       ciṟantaṉa piṉṉuñ ceyiṉu maṟiyiṉum
       puṟañco ṟūṟṟātu pukaḻun taṉmaiyaḷ;
       pullōr vāymoḻi yorīi nallōr
       tuṇinta nūṟporuḷ ceviyuḷaṅ keḻīip
       paṇinta tīñcoṟ patumai yeṉṉuṅ
       kaṭṭurai makaḷoṭu" (4.10:13-35)

   mēmpāṭuṟṟa kāriyakkārarum kaṇakkarum tiṇaikaḷum kāvalāḷarumākiya
   ivarkaḷ aimpatu yavaṉa vaiyam mutaliya poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟaik koṇarntu
   vaittu āyiram tōḻimārkaḷaiyum *mēṟkūṟiya narumatai mutaliya nāṭaka
   makaḷirkaḷaiyum piṟaraiyum uḷḷē aḻaittu vantu niṟutti utayaṇaṉukkuk
   kāṭṭi, "ivaṟṟaiyum ivarkaḷaiyum ummiṭam cērppikka vēṇṭumeṉpatu eṅkaḷ
   aracaṉatu kaṭṭaḷai" eṉṟu terivitta poḻutu patumaiyeṉṉuṅ kaṭṭuraimakaḷ
   vaṇaṅki, arukil ceṉṟa piraccōtaṉaṉ koṭutta tirumukattai vaṇakkattuṭaṉ
   utayaṇaṉmuṉpu nīṭṭiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉ ācaṉattiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅki mikunta
   vaṇakkattuṭaṉ kaikaḷāl ēṟṟu ataṉaip paṭittup pārttaṉaṉ.
   atil "piraccōtaṉaṉeṉṉum aracaṉuṭaiya ōlaiyai utayaṇaṉ kāṇka;
   taṉṉuṭaiya, uṟavaik koḷḷa virumpiya yāṉ pōrceytu taṉṉai vellutal arumai
   eṉṟu niṉaittu māya yāṉaiyaik kāṭṭit taṉṉaip piṭittu varumpaṭi muṉṉam
   eṉṉuṭaiya mantiri mutaliyōrai aṉuppiṉēṉ. ippoḻutu anta niṉaivai
   viṭṭuviṭṭēṉ; taṉṉuṭaiya tantaiyākavē eṉṉaik koḷḷa virumpiṉēṉ; yāṉum
   paṭṭattēviyum uvakkumpaṭi vācavatattaiyōṭu tāṉ iṅkē vantu makiḻcciyai
   viḷaivittiṭuka. pāñcālaṉai veṉṟu tāṉ koṟṟaṅkoṇṭataik kēḷviyuṟṟēṉ. yāṉ
   ceyya vēṇṭuvatait tāṉ ceytaṉaṉ. taṉṉuṭaiya kuṭai niḻalil taṅki ulaku
   iṉpuṟumpaṭi kuḻavi koḷpavariṉ ikaḻātu ōmpip pukaḻpaṭa vāḻka. yūkiyai
   viraivil ivviṭattiṟku aṉuppuka. avaṉaip pākka vēṇṭum eṉṉum viruppam
   eṉakku mikutiyāka uṇṭu. itil eḻutiyavaṟṟai yellām yāṉ viruppattōṭu
   eḻutiṉēṉeṉṟu eṇṇikkoḷka" eṉpatu eḻutap paṭṭiruntatu.
   utayaṇaṉ ellāvaṟṟaiyum pārttu avaṟṟai maṉattil aṭakkikkoṇṭu patumaiyai
   nōkki "vācavatattai tīyiṟpaṭṭatu poycceyti āyiṉum ac ceytiyai iv
   vōlaiyil ēṉ eḻutavillai?" eṉṟu kēṭkavē avaḷ kaikuvittu "eṅkaḷ
   cakkaravartti ic ceytiyaik kēḷviyuṟṟu mika vāṭṭamaṭaintu aṟivil ciṟanta
   oru muṉivarpāl ceṉṟu oṉṟum collāmal mukavāṭṭattōṭum avar muṉṉē niṉṟār.
   uṭaṉē avar ummuṭaiya mantirikaḷōṭu ārāyntu teḷintavarpōla,
   "vācavatattai maṟaintirukkum nāṭkaḷ ittuṇai; avaḷ ummai aṭaiyumkālam
   iṉṉatu" eṉṟu toṭutta mālaiyai eṭuttatupōlac colliyataṉṟi iṉṟuvaraiyil
   nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷaiyellām viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉar. am muṉivar
   aṉpuṭaiyavarātaliṉ ataṉai aracaṉ teḷintu aṭaṅkiṉaṉ. aracar talaiva, yāṉ
   colliyavai ottuḷḷaṉavā" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. ataṟkuḷ utayaṇaṉ, "ellām
   cariyākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa" eṉṟu colli am muṉivaraip pukaḻntu iṉiya
   tōḻarkaḷōṭu ceṉṟu aṅkē vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavākiya pala poruḷkaḷaiyum
   pārttu makiḻntu vantavarkaḷukku iṉiya vārttaikaḷaik kūṟi ac ceytikaḷai
   ellām vācavatattaikku avarkaḷaik koṇṭu collumpaṭi ceyvittu avarkaḷukkut
   taṉi iṭaṅkaḷai vēṟē amaippittu upacarittu vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittaṉaṉ.
   yūkiyai nōkki "nī piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam ceṉṟu iṅkē nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyellām
   colli uṉṉuṭaiya nīti nūl payiṟciyaiyum naṉku viḷakkit tāmatiyāmal
   viraivil varuka" eṉṟu coṟṟaṉaṉ.
   aṅṅaṉam coṉṉapiṉpu utayaṇaṉ "tammuṭaiya nāṭṭiṟkum eṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṟkum
   iṭaiyē uḷḷa kāṭukeḻu kuṟumpum kaṉamalaivaṭṭamum nāṉ iṅkillāta poḻutu
   ellaiyaṟṟup piṟarvacamāki aḻintupōyiṉa. avai eṅkaḷ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭālaṉṟi
   emmavar pukutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭā" eṉṟu mikka vaṇakkattuṭaṉ eḻutiya ōlai
   oṉṟaip piraccōtaṉaṉukkut tūtarvacam aṉuppiyataṉṟi "entak kālattum
   irukulattavarum iṉi maṉam ottē naṭakkumpaṭi ceyvittiṭuka" eṉpataiyum
   terivikkumāṟu colliyaṉuppiṉaṉ. piraccōtaṉaṉukkuk koṭukkumpaṭi pala
   poṉṉaṇikaḷaiyum ilakkaṇattil ciṟanta nālāyiraṅ kutiraikaḷaiyum ainnūṟu
   tērkaḷaiyum nūṟu yāṉaikaḷaiyum patiṉāṟāyiram pacukkaḷaiyum
   vācavatattaiyiṉ naṟṟāykkup paṇipuriyumpaṭi pāñ cālaṉuṭaiya urimaip
   paḷḷiyiṉiṉṟuṅ koṇarnta pala makaḷiraiyum aṉuppiṉaṉ. appaṭiyē kōpālakaṉ
   naṟṟāy, kōpālakaṉ, pālakumaraṉ, paratakaṉ, civētaṉ eṉpavarkaḷukkuk
   koṭukkumpaṭi takka poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟai vakai vakaiyāka aṉuppi mikavum
   aḻakitākiya vaiyākkiram eṉṉum tērai yūkikku aḷittu viṇṇuttarāyaṉ
   eṉpavaṉai meykāvalaṉāka avaṉuṭaṉ cellumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭu nalla
   tiṉattil ellōraiyum piraccōtaṉaṉpāl viṭuttaṉaṉ. viṭuttapiṉ iraṇṭu
   tēviyarkaḷum taṉakku vaḻipāṭu ceyya avarkaḷōṭu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu
   uṇṭāṭi mikavum makiḻcciyuṟṟiruntaṉaṉ.
   piṉpu utayaṇaṉ pāñcāla rācaṉuṭaiya urimaip paḷḷiyiṉiṉṟum paṟṟikkoṇṭu
   vanta araṅkiyaṉ makaḷir āyiratteṇmarkaḷaip paṅkiṭṭu iraṇṭu
   tēviyarkaḷukkum aḷittaṉaṉ. aḷittapiṉ tēviyar iruvarum aracaṉ puṟattē
   ceṉṟu ulāvivarum camayam pārttu muṟṟa veḷiyiṉkaṇ pantaṭittaṟku eṇṇi
   niṉṟārkaḷ. ac ceytiyai aṟinta vayantakaṉ "tēviyar iruvarum tōḻiyaruṭaṉ
   pantāṭutaṟku niccayittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. atu kāṇattakkatu. ātalāl nī
   puṟattē cellāmal peṇ vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu piṭiyiṉmītu ēṟi avarkaḷ
   aṟintukoḷḷātapaṭi pārttaruḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu terivittaṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ ataṟku
   uṭaṉpaṭṭut tāṉ puṟattē celvatākap piṟar niṉaikkumpaṭi ceytu
   cēṉaikaḷaimaṭṭum veḷiyē aṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ oru peṇ vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu vantu
   pala makaḷirkaḷiṉiṭaiyē peṇ yāṉai oṉṟaṉmēl iruntu kāṇpāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   aṅkē tēviyar iruvarum tattam tōḻimārkaḷuṭaṉ anta muṟṟa veḷiyil
   oruvarkkoruvar etiretir niṉṟārkaḷ. appoḻutu patumaiyiṉ tōḻiyākiya
   irācaṉai eṉpavaḷ ēḻu pantai eṭuttu āyiraṅkai aṭittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaḷ.
   appāl kāñcaṉamālai pantukaḷaik kaikkoṇṭu āyirattain nūṟukai
   aṭittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟāḷ. piṉṉar patumaiyiṉ maṟṟoru tōḻiyākiya airāpati
   eṉṉum kūṉi īrāyiram kai aṭittuc ceṉṟāḷ. vācavatattaiyiṉ tōḻiyākiya
   viccuvalēkai eṉṉum kuṟaḷi aṭittaṟkuriya kōlaik kaikkoṇṭu pantukaḷai
   eṭuttu īrāyirattainnūṟukai aṭittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaḷ. patumaiyiṉ
   uyirttōḻiyākiya āriyai eṉpavaḷ ativicittiramāka mūvāyiraṅkai aṭittup
   pantaip pōkaṭṭuviṭṭuc ceṉṟāḷ. appāl pantukaḷ aṭittaṟku yārālum
   eṭukkappaṭāmal kiṭantaṉa.
   appoḻutu vācavatattai talai cāyttu, " aṭittaṟkuriyavar yāvar?" eṉṟu
   kaṭaikkaṇṇāl pārttuk koṇṭu ciṟitu maṉa varuttattuṭaṉ niṉṟāḷ. akkuṟip
   paṟintu, avaḷiṭam paṇi purintukoṇ ṭiruppavaḷum pēraḻakiyum māṉaṉīkai
   eṉṉum peyar uṭaiyavaḷumāṉa maṅkai orutti vantu aṅkē niṉṟu, taṉakku
   muṉpu āṭiyavarkaḷuṭaiya āṭṭattai ikaḻntukūṟic cirittup pantāṭṭattiṉ
   ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟai aṅkē niṉṟa yāvarum aṟiyumpaṭi colliviṭṭu āṭat
   toṭaṅki irupattoru pantukaḷai eṭuttup piṟar viyakkumāṟu āṭiṉaḷ.
   āṭukaiyil,
   "cuḻaṉṟaṉa tāmam; kuḻaṉṟatu kūntal;
       aḻaṉṟatu mēṉi; aviḻntatu mēkalai;
       eḻuntatu kuṟuviyar; iḻintatu cānatam;
       ōṭiṉa taṭaṅkaṇ; kūṭiṉa puruvam;
       aṅkaiyi ṉēṟṟum puṟaṅkaiyi ṉōṭṭiyum
       taṅkuṟa vaḷaittut tāṉpurin taṭittum
       iṭaiyiṭai yirukā neṟitara maṭittum
       ............................................
       nittilak kuṟuviyar pattiyiṟ ṟuṭaittum
       paṟṟiya *kantukañ cuṟṟumuṟai yuraittum
       toṭaiyuṅ kaṇṇiyu muṟaimuṟai yiyaṟṟiyum
       aṭimuraṉ muṭivarai yiḻaipala tiruttiyum
       paṭintavaṇ ṭeḻuppiyuṅ kiṭantapan teṇṇiyum
       tēmalart toṭaiya ṟiṟattiṟam piṇaittum
       pantuvara ṉōkkiyum pāṇivara noṭittuñ
       cimpuḷit taṭittuṅ kampitam pāṭiyum
       āḻiyeṉa vuruṭṭiyum tōḻiyoṭu pēciyum
       cāripala vōṭṭiyum vāḻiyeṉa vāḻttiyum
       antaḷirk kaṇṇi yavantikai velkeṉap
       paintoṭi mātar paṟpala vakaiyāl
       eṇṇā yiraṅkai yēṟṟiṉa ḷēṟṟalum" (4.12"225-45)

   vācavatattai mikaviyantu, "aracaṉ ivaḷaik kāṇiṉ avaṉatu maṉam
   vēṟupaṭum" eṉṟu eṇṇi niṉṟaṉaḷ.
   niṟkaiyil māṉaṉīkaiyiṉ ciṟanta tōṟṟattaik kaṇṭu mayaṅki avaḷai aṭainta
   taṉ maṉattai oruvāṟu mīṭpataṟkum ciṟitum āṟṟal illātavaṉāki utayaṇaṉ
   uriya vaṭivattōṭu aṅkē tōṉṟiṉaṉ. avaṉaik kaṇṭa tēviyar iruvarum
   toḻimārkaḷōṭu tammiṭañ ceṉṟaṉar.
   avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉam cella, kāma mikutiyāl nontu taḷarnta utayaṇaṉ
   paḷḷiyampalattuḷḷē tāṉ iruntu tēviyar iruvaraiyum aḻaittuvarumpaṭi
   colliviṭuttaṉaṉ. tōḻiyarkaḷ ceṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ avviruvarum aracaṉ
   aḻaittataṟkuk kāraṇam iṉṉateṉpatai aṟiyātavarkaḷāki aiyamuṟṟu
   māṉaṉīkaiyaimaṭṭum ōriṭattil maṟaittu vaittuviṭṭu maṟṟat tōḻimārkaḷuṭaṉ
   ceṉṟu avarkaḷaip puṟattē niṟuttit tāmmaṭṭum aracaṉiṭam pōy vaṇaṅkiṉar.
   vaṇaṅkavē utayaṇaṉ "ivarkaḷuṭaiya tōḻiyarkaḷaiyum iṅkē varaviṭuka"
   eṉṟaṉaṉ. avarkaḷum vantu vaṇaṅki niṉṟapoḻutu avarkaḷaiyellām muṟaiyē
   nōkki "uṅkaḷuṭaiya cuṟṟattāraiyum uṅkaḷ peyaraiyum colluka" eṉṟaṉaṉ.
   avarkaḷ avaṟṟaic coṉṉapōtu utayaṇaṉ pārātavaṉpōlavē avarkaḷai muṟaiyē
   pārttu vācavatattaiyai nōkki "uṉṉuṭaiya tōḻimārkaḷuḷ oruttimaṭṭum iṅkē
   vārāmaikkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa" eṉṟu viṉāva, vācavatattai, "avaḷukkuc colla
   vēṇṭiyatu ētēṉum iruppiṉ ataṉait taṉiyē pārttukkoḷka; ik kūṭṭattil āka
   vēṇṭuvatu yātu?" eṉṟu colli mikka kōpaṅ koṇṭaṉaḷ. avaḷuṭaiya kaṇkaḷ
   mikac civantaṉa. avaḷatu cīṟṟattaik kaṇ civappāl aṟinta aracaṉ
   "pāñcālarācaṉ peṟṟa celvattaiyum avaṉukkuḷḷa piṟavaṟṟaiyum avaḷ naṉku
   aṟivāḷeṉṟu mantirikaḷāl aṟintuḷḷēṉ. avaṟṟait terintukoḷḷutaṟporuṭṭē
   avaḷaikkuṟittu ippoḻutu yāṉ viṉāviṉēṉ. kāraṇam vēṟē oṉṟum illai"
   eṉṟaṉaṉ.
   appāl vācavatattai māṉaṉīkaiyai aṅkē varuvittaṉaḷ. avaḷ vantu
   aṭivaṇaṅkiṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ avaḷai nōkki "pāñcāla rācaṉukkuriya pala
   paṇikaḷilum avaṉuṭaiya antappurattārkku alaṅkārañ ceytalilum nī
   vallāyeṉṟu collak kēṭṭuḷḷēṉ. uṉ peyaraiyum uṉ cuṟṟattār peyarkaḷaiyum
   col" eṉṟu kēṭka, avaḷ mīṭṭum vaṇaṅki añcali ceytu koṇṭu aracaṉai nōkki,
   "aṭiyēṉ kōcala tēcattu aracaṉuṭaiya peruntēviyākiya vacuntari
   eṉpavaḷuṭaiya cēṭiyāvēṉ. eṉ peyar māṉaṉīkai. pāñcālarācaṉ paṭaiyeṭuttu
   vantu em aracaṉuṭaiya paṭaikaḷai veṉṟu avaṉuṭaiya antappurattilirunta
   cilatiyarōṭu eṉṉaiyum paṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu taṉ tēvikku aḷittaṉaṉ. appōtu
   aṭicci at tēvikku vaṇṇamakaḷāka iruntēṉ. itu oṉṟaṉṟit tēvarīr
   aruḷicceyta vēṟoru ceyalaiyum aṟiyēṉ" eṉṟu colli aḻutukoṇṭu niṉṟāḷ.
   niṟkavē utayaṇaṉ avaḷai nōkki "nī vācavatattaikku iṉṟumutal
   vaṇṇamakaḷāka iruntu avaḷai nāḷ tōṟum alaṅkarippāyāka" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭa avaḷ appaṭiyē ceṉṟu kaṇṭōr viyakkumpaṭi aṭimutal
   muṭiyaḷavāka avaḷaip puṉaintaṉaḷ. piṉpu vācavatattai vantu utayaṇaṉukku
   avvaṇiyaik kāṭṭiṉaḷ. appōtu utayaṇaṉ "māṉaṉīkai alaṅkāram ceyvatil
   vallunaḷ allaḷ; yāṉ alaṅkarikkiṟēṉ; pār" eṉṟu avaḷ ceyta alaṅkārattaic
   citaittuviṭṭu yavaṉa pāṣaikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷai māṉaṉīkai naṉku kaṟṟavaḷ
   eṉpatai muṉpu kēṭṭiruntavaṉātaliṉ pūntātiṉaiyum cantaṉak kuḻampaiyum
   kūṭṭi vācavatattaiyiṉ neṟṟiyil "utayaṇaṉ ōlaiyai māṉaṉīkai kāṇka: nī
   āṭiya pantāṭṭattil uṉṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷ eṉ neñcattaip piḷakka ataṉāl
   puṇṇuṟṟēṉ; appuṇṇait tīrkkum maruntiṉai nī aṟivāy. eṉṉuṭaiya tuyaram
   āṟṟutaṟku ariyatu. irappōrkku avar virumpiyatai aḷittal naṉṟu. iṅkē
   eḻutuvataṟku iṭam iṉmaiyiṉ nāṉ curukkamāka eḻutiṉēṉ. nī eṉ karuttai
   naṉṟāka aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu eḻutiviṭṭu vācavatattaiyai nōkki
   "uṉakku yāṉ ceyta alaṅkāram ippoḻutu ottirukkiṉṟatu" eṉṟu colli avaḷai
   aṉuppiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē vācava tattai taṉṉuṭaiya antappurattai aṭaintu aṅkē
   vanta māṉaṉīkaikku "inta alaṅkārattai nī pār" eṉṟu kāṭṭiṉaḷ. avaḷ
   ataṉaip paṭittaṟintu kūci "aracar ceytuḷḷa alaṅkāram mikavum
   aḻakuṭaiyatu" eṉṟu vācavatattaiyai maruṭṭiṉaḷ. annāḷ nīṅkiyatu.
   maṟunāḷ kālaiyil vaḻakkampōlavē vācavatattaiyai māṉaṉīkai
   alaṅkarittuviṭṭu avaṉ eḻutiya pāṣaiyālēyē avaṉukkut taṉ karuttait
   terivikka niṉaintu "aṭiyēṉaip poruḷāka eṇṇit tēvarīr eḻutiya tirumukam
   muḻuvataiyum tavaṟiṉṟi aṟintēṉ. aṭiyēṉiṭattu aruḷ iruntālum ataṉai
   ippoḻutu tavirttal vēṇṭum. tēviyarkku mikka varuttattai uṇṭupaṇṇum
   ceyalaic ceytaṟku eṉ maṉam tuṇintilatu. kāvalil irukkum aṭiyēṉ piṟar
   kāṇār eṉṟu takātataic ceytal iyalpaṉṟu; ceytāl paḻippu uṇṭākum.
   tarumamum nīṅkum. ciṟiya eṉṉiṭattē vaitta viruppattai maṟappatu naṉṟu"
   eṉṟu avaḷuṭaiya neṟṟiyil eḻuti "eṉṉāl ceyyakkūṭiya alaṅkāram ituvē"
   eṉṟu colli aṉuppiṉaḷ.
   vācavatattai ceṉṟu aracaṉai vaṇaṅkiṉaḷ. vaṇaṅkavē avaṉ māṉaṉīkai
   eḻutiyataik kaṇṭu avaḷuṭaiya karuttai aṟintu aḻalāl uṇṭākiya puṇṇiṉ mēl
   kōliṭṭāṟpōlak kalaṅki avvaṇiyai aḻittu, "uṉṉai nāṉ viraivil aṭaiyēṉāyiṉ
   eṉ uyiraic ceṉṟatākavē eṇṇikkoḷka" eṉṟu avaḷ neṟṟiyil eḻuti "intap
   putiyatōr alaṅkāārattai avaḷukkuk kāṭṭuvāyāka" eṉṟu kūṟi
   mīḷaviṭuttaṉaṉ. ataṉaikkaṇṭu taṉ maṉattait tiṇṇitākki maṟunāḷ kālaiyil
   māṉaṉīkai "iṟṟai iraviṉ kaṇṇē kūttappaḷḷiyiṉiṭattē kuccarakkuṭikai
   vētikaiyiṉpāl vantaruḷka" eṉṟu anta iṭamum pulappaṭumpaṭi caṅkētamāka
   oruvakaik kōlattai eḻuti aṉuppiṉaḷ. vācavatattai vantu vaṇaṅki niṉṟāḷ.
   ataṉaikkaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ avaḷ eḻutiya kuṟippai aṟintu makiḻntu "māṉaṉīkai
   iṉṟaikkup payantu ceyta alaṅkāram mikavum aḻakuṭaiyatu" eṉṟu colli
   vācavatattaiyōṭu aḷavaḷāvikkoṇṭiruntaṉaṉ. pakaṟkālam nīṅkiyatu.
   iravil vācavatattaikkut teriyāmal māṉaṉīkai tāṉ kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattil
   ceṉṟiruntaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ "vācavatattaipāl tuyilaccelvēṉ" eṉṟu
   patumaiyiṭattum "patumaipāl tuyilac celvēṉ" eṉṟu vācavatattaiyiṭattum
   viruppak kuṟip piṉṟik kūṟiṉaṉ. kūṟavē vācavatattai aiyamuṟṟu ōriṭattil
   ceṉṟu iruntaṉaḷ. appoḻutu utayaṇaṉ eḻuntu otuṅki vēṟiṭam celvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ataṉaik kaṇṭa vācavatattai kāñcaṉamālaiyaik kai koṭṭi aḻaittu "aracaṉ
   cellum iṭattai nī aṟintu varuka" eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. collavē avaḷ avaṉ
   aṟintukoḷḷātapaṭi piṉṉarc ceṉṟu, aracaṉ āṭal pēraṟaiyaic cārntapiṉ
   avaḷum piṉ ceṉṟu aṅkē oru pakkattil maṟaintiruntaṉaḷ.
   ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉum muṉṉamē aṅku vantirunta māṉaṉīkaiyum oruvarai oruvar
   kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvi neṭunēram viḷaiyāṭikkoṇ ṭiruntapiṉpu vācavatattaiyiṉ
   neṟṟiyil kātalōṭu eḻutiviṭutta vācakaṅkaḷaiyellām iruvarum collikkoṇ
   ṭiruntaṉar. iruntapiṉ aracaṉ "nāḷtōṟum tavaṟiṉṟi iṅkē vara vēṇṭum."
   eṉṟu collit taṉṉuṭaiya ciṟuviral mōtirattai avaḷukkuk koṭuttuviṭṭu
   nīṅkiṉaṉ.
   ellāvaṟṟaiyum pārttukkoṇṭē irunta kāñcaṉamālai mikka maṉapporumalōṭu
   ceṉṟu nikaḻntavaṟṟaiyellām vācavatattaikkuc coṉṉāḷ. kēṭṭa vācavatattai
   kōpattai maṉattil aṭakkik koṇṭiruntuviṭṭuk kālaiyil aracaṉai aṭaintu
   vaṇaṅki avaṉai nōkki "nēṟṟiravil yāṉ oru kaṉāk kaṇṭēṉ; atu
   kuṟṟamaṟṟatu" eṉṟu colla avaṉ, "aḵtu yātu?" eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaḷ
   "nīr eṉakkut teriyāmal taṉittuc ceṉṟu oru maṭantaiyōṭu āṭaraṅku oṉṟil
   ēṟi neṭunēram viḷaiyāṭikkoṇṭu taṅki eṉ neṟṟiyil māṟimāṟi eḻutiya
   vācakaṅkaḷai ellām collikkāṭṭi umatu aṉpaip pulappaṭuttikkoṇṭē iruntu
   puṟappaṭumpoḻutu avaḷukku mōtirattai aḷittu vantuviṭṭīr. appāl eṉ
   kaṇkaḷ viḻittaṉa" eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. utayaṇaṉ "eṉ maṉattilum illātatai nī
   kaṇṭāyātalāl kalakkam aṭaintāypōlum" eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟaic colla avaḷ
   "kaṉavil kaṇṭataip piṟarōṭu coṉṉāl ataṉāl uṇṭākum tīmai nīṅkum eṉṟu
   nūlkaḷ kūṟutaliṉ nāṉ ippōtu terivittēṉ. aṉṟiyum evaṟṟaik kaṇṭālum
   avaṟṟai aracaṉiṭattataṉṟi yāriṭattup pēcuvatu?" eṉṟu colli viṭṭu
   maṉattuḷḷē nakaittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ, "tēvikku uṇṭāṉa
   kavalaiyai oru vaḻiyākap pōkkiviṭṭōm" eṉṟu uvantiruntāṉ. ippaṭi
   irukkaiyil pakaṟkālam nīṅkiṟṟu; iravu vantatu.
   vācavatattaiyō māṉaṉīkaiyaip piṭittuk koṇṭuvarac ceytu kāvalil
   vaittuviṭṭup piṟar aṟintukoḷḷātapaṭi kāñcaṉamālaiyōṭu ceṉṟu āṭaraṅkil
   ēṟi māṉaṉīkai irunta iṭattilē iruntāḷ. irukkavē utayaṇaṉ nikaḻntatai
   aṟiyāmal mellacceṉṟu avaḷ camīpattai aṭaintāṉ. aṭaiyavē vācavatattai
   "aracaṉ iṅkē eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāṉ? avaṉ iṉic ceyvatai aṟivōm" eṉṟu eṇṇik
   kaiyāl nīkka utayaṇaṉ "māṉaṉīkai ūṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉaḷ" eṉṟu niṉaintu
   "muracumuḻaṅku tāṉai yaracoṭu vēṇṭiṉum
       tarukuva liṉṉē paruvara loḻiyiṉi
       māṉē! tēṉē! māṉa ṉīkāy!" (4.3:227-9)

   eṉṟu avaḷ kālaik kaiyāl paṟṟiṉaṉ. paṟṟavē kālaik koṭātavaḷāki
   vācavatattai appāl ceṉṟaṉaḷ. ataṉaip poṟāta utayaṇaṉ "ētēṉum putiya
   ceyti oṉṟaic colli ivaḷuṭaiya ūṭalait tīrttu muṉṉilaip paṭuttikkoḷvōm"
   eṉṟu niṉaintu avaḷai nōkki "nēṟṟiravil nam iruvarkkum iṅkē
   nikaḻntavaṟṟai yellām kaṉavil kaṇṭatāka vācavatattai ciṟitum tavaṟiṉṟic
   collic ciṉappa nāṉ "eṉ neñcil ciṟitum anta eṇṇam illai" eṉṟu colli av
   varuttattai nīkkiṉēṉ. ataṉaiyum nī aṟintāyillaiyē. māṉaṉīkāy! aruḷceyya
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu aṭuttaṭuttu uraippavum, āṟṟāṉākavum, intac col collavum,
   vaṇakkam ceyyavum, oṉṟaiyum matiyātavaḷāki vācavatattai cirittukkoṇṭu,
   "nī kūṟiya māṉum tēṉum māṉaṉīkaiyum yāṉ allēṉ. eṉ peyar vācavatattai"
   eṉṟaṉaḷ. uṭaṉē utayaṇaṉ tiṭukkiṭṭu viraintu ōṭicceṉṟu vēṟoru
   maṇṭapattil taṉittu oḷittukkoṇ ṭiruntāṉ. vācavatattaiyum ciṉaṅkoṇṭu
   avaṉaip pārāmal ceṉṟāḷ. cellavē kaṅkul pularntatu.
   pularntavuṭaṉ vācavatattai māṉaṉīkaiyai varuvittu mikavum kōpittukkoṇṭu
   oru tūṇōṭu cērttuk kaṭṭi avaḷai nōkki " kūttappaḷḷiyil nī aracaṉōṭu
   pēciya vārttaikaḷaiyellām col" eṉṟu kūṟi arukē niṉṟa oruttiyaip pārttu
   "ivaḷ kūntalai aritaṟku oru kattarikai koṇṭu varuvāyāka" eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ.
   ataṉai ayaliṭattē irunta vayantakaṉ aṟintu viraintu ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉukkuk
   kūṟa avaṉ "māṉīkaiyiṉ oru mayiraik kattarikai tīṇṭumāyiṉ eṉṉuṭaiya uyir
   nīṅkum. intak koṭuñceyalai vilakkuvāyāka" eṉṟaṉaṉ. vayantakaṉ
   "nikaḻantatu yātu?" eṉṟāṉ.
   utayaṇaṉ "ellām uṉṉālē muṭintatutāṉ" eṉṉa, vayantakaṉ "pantu
   viḷaiyāṭṭai yāṉ pārkkac coṉṉatu uṇṭēyaṉṟi vēṟu etaiyēṉum ceyyac coṉṉatu
   uṇṭō? irācamātēviyiṉuṭaiya taṇiyāk kōpattait tīrkkum vaṉmai yāvarkku
   uṇṭu? āṉālum āṟēḻu nāḻikai aḷavil ak kōpattait taṇippēṉ. ataṟkuḷ vēṟu
   yāraiyēṉum aṅkē viṭuttaruḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟa colliviṭṭu viraintu ceṉṟu
   tēviyai aṭaintu "aracaṉ eṅkē yuḷḷaṉaṉ?" eṉṟu tēṭa, aṅkē uḷḷa kāvaṉmakaḷ
   orutti "itu talaivikkuk kōpamuḷḷa camayam" eṉṟu arukila vantu mellac
   colli, "cummā irum" eṉṟu kaiyai amaittaṉaḷ. uṭaṉē vayantakaṉ naṭuṅki,
   "talaivikkuḷḷa kuṟai yātu?" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaṉ. ataṉai vācavatattai aṟintu
   avaṉai nōkki, "araca vaṭivam koṇṭirukkum tūrttak kaḷḷaṉaik kēṭpīrāyiṉ
   eṉṉuṭaiya kōpattiṉ kāraṇattai nīr eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷutal kūṭum" eṉṟu
   colli āṇṭu uḷḷāḷ oruttiyai nōkki, " ik kattirikaiyāl ivaḷ kūntalaik
   kuṟai" eṉa mikavum ciṉantu moḻintaṉaḷ. vayantakaṉ, "appaṭiyāṉāl kēcam
   kuṟaittalil eṉakku mikka payiṟci uṇṭu" eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉavē avaḷ
   kuṟaikkumpaṭi kūṟa avaṉ vācavatattaiyai nōkki, "kattirikaiyiṉ
   ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ palavuḷḷaṉa. niṉ kaim malar mayirait tīṇṭutal takātu;
   karuviyait taruka" eṉṟu colli vāṅki ataṉ ilakkaṇattai viḷaṅkak
   kūṟupavaṉpōlak kattirikaiyiṉ vēṟupāṭṭilakkaṇam palavaṟṟaik kūṟi, " ik
   kattirikaiyāl ivaḷ mayirait tīṇṭiṉ ivaḷukku mikavum naṉmai uṇṭākum;
   ākalāl itu vēṇṭām" eṉṟu ataṉait tūrattil eṟintaṉaṉ. piṉpu avaḷāl
   koṭukkappaṭṭa kattirikai ovvoṉṟaṟkum ovvoru kuṟṟam kūṟik kūṟi
   eṟivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   avaṟṟaiyellām uṭaṉuṭaṉ oṟṟarāl aṟintu koṇṭē irunta utayaṇaṉ 1yūkiyai
   varuvittu nikaḻntavaṟṟaic curukkik kūṟiṉaṉ. kēṭṭa yūki, "yāṉum āṟēḻu
   nāḻikai aḷavu avaḷ kūntalaip pātukāppēṉ. ataṟkumēlē yātoṉṟum ceyya
   aṟintilēṉ" eṉṟu iḻinta palavakaippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaiyum taṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉmēl
   tāṅki nīṟṟai uṭampeṅkum pūcik koṇṭu pittar vaṭivam pūṇṭu, kaṇṭōr
   tuṭkeṉṟu añcumpaṭi aṅkē ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   ---------
       1 utayaṇaṉāl aṉuppappeṟṟup piracōtaṉaṉpāl ceṉṟa yūki avaṉaik kaṇṭu
       uṭaṉē kaucāmpikku vantuviṭṭāṉ eṉpataik kūṟum pakuti itaṉ mutaṉūlil
       citaintu pōyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu terikiṉṟatu; utitōtayakāvyam
       mutaliyaṉa ik karuttai valiyuṟuttum.

   vācavatattaiyaic cūḻntu niṉṟa makaḷirkaḷ avaṉaik kaṇṭu accamuṟṟup
   palaviṭattum ōṭuvarāy, viḻunarum, eḻunarum, mēlvara naṭuṅki aḻunarum,
   tēviyiṉpiṉṉē aṇainarum āyiṉar. vācavatattai ataṉaik kaṇṭu cirittukkoṇṭu
   ak kāṭciyaiyē virumpi niṉṟaṉaḷ. ac ceytiyaic collumpaṭi vayantakaṉ oru
   tūtiyai mantaṇamāka aracaṉpāl aṉuppiṉaṉ. aṉuppavē aracaṉ patumaiyai
   varuvittu ētō collat toṭaṅkit tikaittaṉaṉ. attikaippai aṟinta patumai,
   "eṉakku uraiyātatāṉa ceyal yātuḷatu? colliyaruḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu vaṇaṅkik
   kēṭpa avaṉ, "yāṉ collutaṟkuk kūcukiṉṟēṉ. uṉ tavvaiyai aṭaivāyiṉ avaḷ
   colluvāḷ. nī eṉ ceyir kāṇāta teyvamātaliṉ avaḷ kōpattait taṇittu eṉ
   uyirait tara vēṇṭum" eṉṟu avaḷai aṉuppiṉaṉ.
   patumai vācavattaiyai aṭaintu vaṇaṅkiṉaḷ. vaṇaṅkavē avaḷ
   nikaḻntavaṟṟaik kūṟap patumai, "nīr poṟuttiṭuka. iv varattai eṉakkuk
   koṭuttaruḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu collit tāṉ vanta kāriyattait terivittup piṉṉum
   vaṇaṅki avaḷ mukattaip pārttukkoṇṭē niṟpaḷāyiṉaḷ.
   appoḻutu vāyalkāvalaṉ vantu utayaṇaṉai vaṇaṅki, "kōcalattaracaṉ tūtuvar
   vāyiliṉkaṇ vantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar" eṉṟu collakkēṭṭu utayaṇaṉ avarai
   varuvittu, vanta ceytiyaik kūṟumpaṭi coṉṉapoḻutu, avar, "kōcalattaracar
   ummuṭaiya māperun tēviyārkku oru tirumukaṅ koṭuttaṉuppiṉar. ataṉai aṅkē
   aṉuppa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu koṭuttaṉar. appōtu utayaṇaṉ, "ic camayattil itu
   vantatum nalamē" eṉṟu eṇṇi ataṉai vācavatattaipāl aṉuppiṉaṉ. avaḷ
   ataṉai vāṅki, 'itaṉaip paṭittuc col" eṉṟu patumaiyiṉ kaiyil koṭuttaṉaḷ.
   patumai eḻuntu ataṉai vāṅkip pirittu, "kōcalat taracaṉ ōlaiyai
   vācavatattai kāṇpāḷāka; cōrviṭam pārttu eṉ ūrai eṟinta pāñcāla rācaṉāl
   1taṉ taṅkai vācavatattai āyattuṭaṉ kaikkoḷḷap paṭṭup pōyiṉaḷ.
   pāñcālarācaṉai veṉṟa utayaṇaṉāl pala maṅkaiyaruṭaṉ avaḷ kavarappaṭṭup
   piṉpu taṉpāl paṇippeṇṇāka iruppataik kēṭṭēṉ. avaḷ taṉṉai iṉṉāḷeṉṟum taṉ
   peyar iṉṉateṉṟum uṇarttāmal māṉaṉīkaiyeṉṟu peyar vaittuk
   koṇṭiruppataiyum aṟintēṉ. viraivil aṅku varuvēṉ. yāṉ varumaḷavum
   tuyarattait tīrttu avaḷaip pātukākka vēṇṭuvatu taṉ kaṭamai; iḵtu
   eṉṉuṭaiya kuṟaiyākum" eṉṟu atil eḻutiya vācakaṅkaḷaip paṭittu aṟintu
   vācavatattaiyaip pārttu, "itaṉiluḷḷa vācakam eṉakku viḷaṅkavillai; nīrē
   pārttaruḷka" eṉṟu avaḷiṭam koṭuttaṉaḷ.
   ---------
       1. vācavatataikkut tāyvarukkattaic cērntavaḷuṭaiya makaḷakip
       pirāyattāl ciṟiyavaḷakavum iruntatupaṟṟi, avaḷ 'taṅkai' eṉappaṭṭāḷ.

   vācavatattai ataṉai vāṅkip paṭittu iṭaiyiṭaiyē ēṅki ēṅkik kaṇṇīr
   ukuttukkoṇṭu, "peṇṇīrmaikku iyal piḻaiyē pōlum" eṉṟu colli māṉaṉīkai
   paṭṭa tuṉpattiṟku āṟṟātavaḷāki eḻuntu patumaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu māṉaṉīkaiyiṉ
   kaṭṭai aviḻttuviṭṭu avaḷai aṇaittukkoṇṭu, "kuḻūuk kaḷiyāṉai kōcalaṉ
   makaḷē! aḻēṟka eṉ pāvāy! arumpeṟaṟ ravvai ceytatu poṟu" eṉṟu colli avaḷ
   kaṇṇīrait tuṭaittuc cūḻntiruppavarkaḷaip puṟattē pōkumpaṭi ceytuviṭṭu
   avaḷai uṭaṉ irukkac ceytu avaḷ kūntalait taṉ kaiyāl vakirntu āṟṟi eṇṇey
   tēyttu nīrāṭṭit tukiluṭuttup patumaiyum tāṉum niṉṟu avaḷikku iṉiya
   vārttaikaḷ palavaṟṟaic collic ciṟanta iṭattiliruntu mūvarum oru
   kalattil uṇpārāyiṉar. ataṉaik kaṇṭa vayantakaṉ viraintu ceṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉukkuk kūṟa avaṉ kēṭṭu mikavum makiḻntu avaṉait taḻuvikkoṇṭaṉaṉ.
   appoḻutu vācavatattai, "kōcalatēcattu aracaṉ makaḷai maṇakkōlañ cey"
   eṉṟu patumaikkuk kūṟiviṭṭu aracaṉukku maṇac ceytiyaic colli viṭuttaṉaḷ.
   uṭaṉē maṇakkōlattuṭaṉ avaṉ vantu maṇappantaril ācaṉattiṉmītu iruntaṉaṉ.
   iruntapoḻutu vācavatattaiyum patumaiyum virumpi aḷikka avaṉ
   kōcalavaracaṉ makaḷai maṇam ceytukoṇṭaṉaṉ. ataṉpiṉ,
   'muṟṟiḻai makaḷir mūvarum vaḻipaṭak
       koṟṟa vēntar naṟṟiṟai yaḷappa
       nalvaḷan tarūum palkuṭi taḻaippac
       celva vēntaṉ ceṅkō lōcci' (4.14:182-5)
       makiḻntiruntāḷ.

   utayaṇaṉ ippaṭi irukkum kālattil, ilāvāṇa nakarattiṉ ayaliluḷḷa
   cōlaiyil muṉpu uṇṭāṭṭu nikaḻntapoḻutu iḷamaip paruvattil utayaṇaṉāl
   kaṇṇimutaliyavai cūṭṭappeṟṟa viricikaiyeṉpavaḷ avaṉ cūṭṭiya
   mālaiyaiyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟaiyum aṟiyātavaḷāki viratattuṭaṉiruntu
   paruvamutircci aṭaintu viḷaṅkiṉaḷ. ataṉai aṟintu avaḷ tantaiyākiya
   muṉivar viraintu vantu kaucāmpi nakaram aṭaintu tamatu varavait
   terivittu utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu, "yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟai aṅkīkarittuk
   kēṭṭiṭuka: nī viyācar mutalākiya periyōrkaḷ viḷaṅkappeṟṟa kurukulattil
   piṟantōṉ. yāṉ veḷḷiyaṅkiriyiluḷḷa mantaravaracaṉ. amaiccarōṭu aracāṭci
   ceytukoṇṭirunta yāṉ muttiyai virumpiya muyaṟciyai uṭaiyēṉāki eṉṉuṭaiya
   pārattaip putalvaṉiṭattē oppittuviṭṭuk kāciyaracaṉ puttiriyum nīlakēci
   eṉṉum peyariṉaḷumākiya eṉ maṉaiviyōṭum viricikaiyeṉṉum puttiriyōṭum
   kāṭoṉṟai aṭaintu tavam ceytukoṇṭiruntēṉ. iruntapoḻutu oru nāḷ yāṉ
   puṇṇiya tīrttam āṭuvataṟku ayaliṭam ceṉṟēṉ. accamayattil nī vantu eṉ
   makaḷ viricikaiyai maṭiyil vaittukkoṇṭu avaḷukku mālaicūṭṭic ceṉṟaṉai.
   ataṉaiyē taṉakkup paṟṟukkōṭākakkoṇṭu uṉṉaiyē virumpi avaḷ kālam
   kaḻippāḷāyiṉaḷ. uṉṉaiyē virumpiyirukkum avaḷuṭaiya uḷḷat tāmaraippōtu
   vēṟoruvar poruṭṭu malarvatu aritu. tammiṭattu aṉpuḷḷa kaṉṉi makaḷir
   yāvarāyiṉum avarait tāmum virumpi avaratu tuyarattait tīrttup
   pātukāttal āṭavar tilakattiṉ kaṭappāṭeṉpatu ulakam aṟintatē. "eṉ
   makaḷai yāṉ aḷippēṉ. nī peṟṟukkoḷka" eṉṟu terivittuk koḷḷutal marapu
   aṉṟāyiṉum collāniṟpēṉ. iyalpākavē uṉṉāl mālai cūṭṭappaṭṭavaḷātaliṉ avaḷ
   uṉ maṉaiviyē. ātalāl eṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi centīkkaṭavuḷiṉ muṉpu avaḷai
   nalla nāḷil maṇañ ceytukoḷḷutal naṉṟu" eṉṟu terivittaṉar.
   kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ ataṉai vācavatattaikku aṟivittāṉ. innikaḻcciyai
   iyalpākavē muṉṉam aṟintavaḷātaliṉ vācavatattai ciṟitum ūṭātavaḷāki
   aṅkīkarittaṉaḷ. avaḷatu uḷḷattai aṟinta utayaṇaṉ piṉputaṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭai
   ammuṉivarukkut terivittāṉ. am makiḻcciyāl aracaṉ yācakarkku arum
   poruḷkaḷai mika vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. inta maṇac ceytiyai nāṭṭiṉarum
   nakarattiṉarum aṟintu tattam iṭaṅkaḷai alaṅkarittal mutaliyavaṟṟaic
   ceyyum vaṇṇam muracaṟaivikkac ceytāṉ. alaṅkārattiṉ mikutiyāl an nakaram
   pōkapūmiyaippōla viḷaṅkiṟṟu. enta maruṅkilum nakarattār aṭaiyāk
   kaṭaiyarum varaiyā vaṇmaiyarum illōrkku uṟuporuḷ vīcupavarum āki,
   "tāpataṉ maṭamakaḷ iṅkē varuvatai yām pārkka vēṇṭum" eṉṟu virumpic
   ceṉṟu,
   "pūttōy māṭamum pulimuka māṭamum
       kūttā ṭiṭamuṅ koḻuñcutaik kuṉṟamum
       nāyiṉ māṭamu nakaranaṉ puricaiyum
       vāyiṉ māṭamu maṇimaṇ ṭapamum
       ēṉaiya piṟavum" (4.15:108-112)

   ēṟi niṟpārāyiṉar.
   atu terinta utayaṇaṉ "piṭikai, civikai mutaliyavaṟṟil ūrtaliṉṟi
   nakariṉuḷḷē viricikai naṭantē varuvāḷāka" eṉṟu colliviṭuttaṉaṉ.
   viṭuttavaṉ "vīṭṭuvāyilkaḷ tōṟum naṟumalar parappuka; yāṉai mutaliyavai
   vītiyil cañcarittalait tavirttiṭuka" eṉṟu ēvalarmukamāka nakarattārkkut
   terivittaṉaṉ. "araṇ maṉaiyiliruntu maṅkalamakaḷirum periyōrkaḷum naṇpil
   tiriyāmal paṇpoṭu puṇarnta kāñcukikaḷum etir ceṉṟu aḻaittu varuka" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ. appaṭiyē avarkaḷ ceṉṟārkaḷ. viricikaiyaik kāṇpataṟkuc
   caṉaṅkaḷ vaḻiyiṉ irupuṟattum neruṅki niṉṟārkaḷ. tavappaḷḷiyiliruntu
   puṟanakarattiṟku avaḷai aḻaittuvarumporuṭṭuc civikaiyāṉatu
   parivāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ aṉuppappeṟṟatu.
   aḻaikkac ceṉṟavarkaḷ viricikaiyaic civikaiyil ēṟṟi aḻaittuvantu
   puṟanakarattiṉkaṇ cōlaiyiluḷḷa māṭam oṉṟil iṟakkuvittu
   vācaṉaipporuḷkaḷai avaḷ uṭampil pūci naṟu nīrāl āṭṭuvittuc cilleṉ
   kūntalai melleṉa vāri muṭittu anta mayirmuṭiyil utayaṇaṉāl muṉpu cūṭṭap
   peṟṟatum vāṭiyatumāṉa piṇaiyaluṭaṉ taḷiriṉum pōtiṉum toṭutta putiya
   tāmattaic cūṭṭip paṉaṅkuruttōlaiyaik kātil ceruki neṟciṟu tāliyaik
   kaḻuttaṭiyilcērtti muttaik kōtta oru kāḻvaṭattai mārpil pūṭṭik kāvi
   uṭaiyai nīkkik kōṭit tukilai uṭutti ōr uttarīkattai mēlē cērttik kaiyil
   piṭittukkoḷḷumpaṭi oru kuvaḷaippōtaik koṭuttu avaḷai alaṅkārañ
   ceytaṉar.
   ceytapiṉ uṭaṉvanta muṉivarmaṅkaiyariṟ cilar, cāṅkiyattāy mutaliyarkaḷai
   nōkki,"ivaḷaip pātu kākka vēṇṭuvatu umatu kaṭamai" eṉṟu ompaṭai kūṟi,
   "iṉi ivaḷukkut tāyum, tantaiyum, tavvaiyarum utayaṇaṉē" eṉṟu colliviṭṭu
   avarkaḷiṭam viṭai peṟṟukkoṇṭu tammiṭam ceṉṟārkaḷ.
   ceṉṟavarkaḷ viricikaiyiṉ tantaikku nikaḻntavaṟṟaic colla, ammuṉivar tam
   maṉaiviyai aḻaittu, "viricikaiyai maṇam ceytukkoḷḷumpaṭi utayaṇaṉiṭam
   collic cērppittuviṭṭēṉ. iṉi uṉṉaiyum tuṟantu tavam ceyyac celvēṉ" eṉṟu
   kūṟiṉar. kēṭṭa avaḷ varuntip pulampiṉaḷ. ataṉai am muṉivar ēṟṟuk
   koḷḷātavarākik kaṭiya tavavoḻukkattai mēṟkoṇṭu vēṟiṭam ceṉṟār.
   ceṉṟapiṉ av vaṉattiluḷḷa maṅkaiyaril orucārār viricikaiyāl
   vaḷarkkappaṭṭu avaḷoṭu paḻakiya kuyil mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum
   tavaccālaiyiluḷḷa ariya poruḷkaḷaiyum aruṅkalaṉkaḷaiyum kaikkoṇṭu
   uṭaṉvara, vītiyil viricikai naṭantu celvāḷāyiṉaḷ. kaṇṭōr, " ivaḷ
   muṉivaruṭaiya vēḷviyil tōṉṟiya teyva maṅkai. ivaḷai māṉiṭa maṅkai eṉṟal
   meymmaiyaṉṟu" eṉṟum, "cūṭiya taḷirkkōtai vāṭutal mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷ
   ivaḷ teyvamaṅkai allaḷ eṉpatait terivikkiṉṟaṉa" eṉṟum "ivaḷ
   kayattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkik kuvaḷaimalar piṭittup pōnta nīraramakaḷāka iruppiṉ
   ivaḷuṭaiya kaṇkaḷ imaikkumō? utayaṇaṉai maṇamakaṉākap peṟutaṟkup
   paḻampiṟappil tavam ceyta orutti ivaḷ" eṉṟum "utayaṇaṉaic cārntu pōkam
   nukartaṟku vanta iyakki" eṉṟum "utayaṇaṉ ivaḷai viṭṭu neṭuṅ kālam
   pirintiruntatu tavaṟu" eṉṟum, "ivaḷukku uṟuppukkaḷē aṇikalaṉkaḷ" eṉṟum,
   "aḻakil tammai oppōr illaiyeṉṟu eṇṇiyirukkum makaḷir ivaḷaik
   kāṇpārkaḷāka" eṉṟum "utayaṇaṉukku ivaḷē ēṟṟavaḷ" eṉṟum collāniṟpa
   viricikai taṉ aṭikaḷ ciṉmalar mitittuc civantu calippuṟumpaṭi mella
   mella vītiyil naṭantu ceṉṟu kōyilvācalai aṭaintāḷ. aṭainta poḻutu,
   araṇmaṉaiyiliruntu maṅkalapporuḷkaḷai ēntikkoṇṭu cilar etirkoṇṭaṉar.
   viricikai kōyuliṉuḷḷē ceṉṟaṉaḷ. tēviyar mūvarum ceyya vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic
   ceytu avaḷuṭaiya kuravarpōlak koṭuppa utayaṇaṉ avaḷai maṇam ceytu
   koṇṭaṉaṉ.
   appāl tēviyar nālvarum vaḻipaṭa, mikkacelvattōṭu kūṭip palluyirkaḷaiyum
   utayaṇaṉ pātukāttu varupavaṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ------------------------------
                     ___________________________________

                         5. naravāṇa kāṇṭam

   piṉpu orunāḷ mantirimārkaḷōṭu utayaṇaṉ pērattāṇiyil ceṉṟu
   vīṟṟiruntaṉaṉ. appoḻutu oḻukkamuṭaiya vaṇikar cilar neruṅki vantu
   muṟaippaṭiyē ceṉṟu tamakkuḷḷa vaḻakkoṉṟai utayaṇaṉukkut teivittaṉar.
   ataṉait tīrkkumpaṭi urumaṇṇuvāvukku avaṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭa avaṉ atai naṉku
   vicārittut tīrppuccolliviṭṭu mīṇṭuvantu anta vivarattaik kūṟiṉaṉ. 1anta
   vaḻakku, cantatiyiṉmaiyāl uṇṭākum kuṟaiyaiyum aḵtu iruttaliṉ uṇṭākum
   perumaiyaiyum pulappaṭuttiyatātaliṉ utayaṇaṉ "namakkuc cantati illaiyē.
   makkaḷ ilaiyeṉiṉ mikkuyar ciṟappiṉ nam kulam iṭaiyaṟumē" eṉṟa
   varuttattai aṭaintu vācavatattaipāl ceṉṟāṉ.
   ---------
       1oru vaḻakkait tīrttatiliruntu taṉakkuc cantatiyillaiyē eṉṟa
       kavalai tuṣyantaṉukku uṇṭāṉatākac cākuntalattiṉ 6-ām aṅkattāl
       terikiṉṟatu.

   ceṉṟapoḻutu avaḷ nōṉpikaḷukku uṇavaḷittu avarkaḷuṭaiya āciyaip peṟṟu
   maṅkalak kōlattōṭu māṭattil ōriṭattil iruntaṉaḷ. iruntavaḷ oru puṟavu
   tāṉ vāykkoṇṭirunta ariya iraiyait taṉṉuṭaiya pārppiṉ vāyil corintataik
   kaṇṭu pārppiṉ pacivaruttattaiyum tāyppuṟaviṉatu aṉpiṉ perumaiyaiyum
   nōkki, ataṉ aṉpaip pārāṭṭikkoṇ ṭiruntatai aracaṉ viṉāvi aṟintu koṇṭu,
   tākittavarkaḷukkut taṇṇīrpōṉṟa avaḷuṭaṉ tuyiṉṟāṉ. tuyiṉṟapoḻutu,
   iṭaiyāmattil oru teyvamaṅkai vantu "em iṟaivaṉākiya kupēraṉ ummai
   aḻaikkiṟāṉ. varuka" eṉṟaṉaḷ. utayaṇaṉ, "vācavatattaiyaiyum uṭaṉ
   aḻaittuvara eṉ maṉam virumpukiṉṟatu. ataṉai nī aṅkīkarikka vēṇṭum"
   eṉṟaṉaṉ. uṭaṉē avaḷ, iruvaraiyum kaiyil tāṅkikkoṇṭu paṟantuceṉṟu
   kupēraṉai aṭaiya, avaṉ ciṅkātaṉam oṉṟaik kāṭṭi atil irukkumpaṭi colla,
   utayaṇaṉ tēviyōṭu atil irunta poḻutu kupēraṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi oru
   maṇimuṭiyai at teyvappeṇ koṇarntu utayaṇaṉukkuk koṭuppa avaṉ peṟṟu
   vācavatattaiyuṭaṉ nakarvantu atai avaḷukku aḷikka avaḷ atait taṉ
   mārpiṉiṟ cērttiṉaḷ. cērttavē atu cūriyaṉpōl tōṉṟi avaḷ vayiṟṟil
   pukuntuviṭṭatu. pukavē avaḷ añcit tiṭīreṉṟu eḻuntāḷ. tāṉum uṭaṉē
   eḻuntuviṭṭatāka avaṉ kaṉāvoṉṟaik kaṇṭu oru muṉivarai aṭaintu tāṉ
   kaṇṭakaṉāvaik kūṟi ataṉ payaṉaic colla vēṇṭum eṉṟu kēṭpa avar
   "veḷḷimalaiyai āḷum cakkaravarttiyākiya oru putalvaṉai nī peṟuvāy. avaṉ
   veṟṟi peṟṟu viḷaṅkuvaṉ. ciṟitum aiyamillai" eṉṟaṉar. utayaṇaṉ kēṭṭu
   atai vācavatattaikkuc colli makiḻvuṟṟiruntaṉaṉ.
   ippaṭi irukkaiyil vācavatattai karuvuṟṟāḷ. ataṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ avaḷ
   tēkattil kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. mayaṟkaiyaṭaintu varuntiṉāḷ. oru nāḷ varuntit
   tuyilukaiyil viṭiyaṟkālattil ākāyattil cellum veḷḷāṉai oṉṟu vantu
   tutikkaiyaic curuṭṭikkoṇṭu taṉ vayiṟṟil pukuntatākavum ap poḻutu
   ākāyattil tuntupikaḷ muḻaṅkap palar tutittatākavum piṉpu ataṉai
   umiḻntuviṭavē aḵtu iṟakukaḷaip peṟṟup paṟantu ceṉṟu oru veḷḷi
   malaiyiṉmēl ēṟi atil viḷaṅkiya oru cūriyaṉai viḻuṅkiyatākavum avaḷ
   kaṉavoṉṟu kaṇṭu tiṭīreṉṟu añci eḻuntāḷ. eḻuntavaḷ atai utayaṇaṉukkuk
   kūṟa,"vittiyātarar ulakil ceṉṟu perumpakaivaṉ oruvaṉai veṉṟu aṅkē
   cakkaram celuttum oru putalvaṉaip peṟuvāy" eṉṟu moḻintaṉaṉ.
   vācavatattaiyiṉ karuppattilirunta kuḻantaiyiṉiṭam ciṟanta vittai oṉṟu
   iruntamaiyiṉ, mēlē paṟantu ceṉṟu malaikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum pārkka
   vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam ataṟku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. anta viruppattai vācavatattaiyum
   aṭaintaṉaḷ. appoḻutu karuppattiṉ vaḷarcciyāl avaḷuṭaiya tēkam
   melivuṟṟatu. ataṉaik kaṇṇuṟṟa utayaṇaṉ "uṉakku vēṇṭuvatu yātu?" eṉṟu
   viṉāviṉaṉ. tāṉ virumpiyatu kiṭaittaṟku ariyatākaliṉ taṉṉuṭaiya eṇṇattai
   veḷippaṭuttāmal iruntaṉaḷ. avaṉ "nī etai virumpiṉālum ataṉai aḷittaṟku
   uriyēṉ; aiyam uṟātē" eṉṟu cūḷ aṟaintu vaṟpuṟuttik kēṭka avaḷ "eṉṉuṭaiya
   uḷḷam vittiyātararkaḷaippōla ākāyattil paṟantu ceṉṟa pala iṭaṅkaḷaip
   pārp pataṟku virumpukiṉṟatu" eṉpatai añcit terivittaṉaḷ. avaḷ
   viruppattai muṭittaṟkuriya ālōcaṉaikaḷai mantirikaṭaḷuṭaṉ avaṉ
   ceyāvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   aṅṅaṉam ārāyntukoṇ ṭirukkaiyil ilāvāṇa nakarattiliruntu vanta
   urumaṇṇuvāvukku vācavatattaiyiṉ karuttu iṉṉatu eṉpataiyum atai
   aṭaitaṟkuc ceyyum upāyattait tāṉ nāṭiyiruttalaiyum utayaṇaṉ terivikka
   avaṉ "muṉṉoru kālattil vantu nammuṭaiya 1tākattaip pōkkiya iyakkaṉai
   niṉaippōmāyiṉ avaṉ vantu nammuṭaiya karuttai muṭippāṉ" eṉṟu avaṉait
   tiyāṉittaṟku uriya mantirattai eḻutik koṭuttāṉ. utayaṇaṉ ataṉai aṟintu
   tūymaiyōṭum viratattōṭum iruntu maṉattai oṉṟu paṭutti am mantirattai
   ōtikkoṇṭirunta kālattil anta iyakkaṉ viṇṇiliruntu iṟaṅki aṅkē vantu
   avaṉ muṉṉē niṉṟāṉ.
   --------
       1 oru tēvaṉ vantu tākattait tīrtta ceytiyai muṉpu kāṇka.

   niṉṟavaṉ utayaṇaṉai nōkki "māya yāṉaiyaip piṭikkac ceṉṟu ciṟaippaṭṭa
   poḻutum, vēṭarkaḷiṉ iṭaiyē akappaṭṭapoḻutum, makata nāṭṭil tuyaram
   uṟṟapoḻutum, āruṇiyōṭu pōr ceytapoḻutum eṉṉai niṉaiyāmal mikka
   celvattāl iṉpamuṟṟirukkum nī ikkālattil eṉṉai niṉaittaṟkuk kāraṇam
   yātu?" eṉṟu kēṭpa utayaṇaṉ, " at tuṉpaṅkaḷellām yāmē tīrttaṟku
   uriyaṉavāka iruntamaiyiṉ niṉaikka villai. eṅkaḷāl iyalātavaṟṟai
   virumpiya vācavatattaiyiṉ viruppattaip pūrtti ceyavikka
   vēṇṭiyiruntamaiyiṉ ippōtu niṉaittēṉ" eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉavē anta iyakkaṉ, " uṉ
   tēviyiṉ vayiṟṟil uṟaipavaṉ vittiyātara cakkaravarttiyātaṟku uriyavaṉ.
   ataṟkuriya kāraṇattaik kēṭpāyāka. vintamalaiyaic cārnta narumataiyāṟṟiṉ
   oru karaiyil uḷḷa paruppatameṉṉum malaiyiṉmēl kupēraṉ taṉ
   urimaimakaḷiruṭaṉ iruntapoḻutu, pattirai mutalākiya teyvamakaḷir
   oṉpatiṉmaruḷ oruttiyākiya pattirāpati eṉpavaḷait taḷirmutaliyavaṟṟaik
   koytu tarumpaṭi avaṉ veḷiyē aṉuppiṉaṉ. ceṉṟa pattirāpati aṅṅaṉam koyta
   taḷirmutaliyavaṟṟaik kaiyil ēntikkoṇṭu niṉṟu, ōr āṇ yāṉaiyāl
   taḻavappaṭṭu niṉṟa peṇ yāṉaiyai nōkki, 'yāṉaip piṟappum virumpaṟpālatu'
   eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭu kupēraṉaiaṭaintāḷ. aṭaintavaḷuṭaiya eṇṇattai aṟinta
   kupēraṉ avaḷai nōkki, ' teyva iṉpattai virumpāmav yāṉaiyiṉ iṉpattaip
   peritāka niṉaittamaiyiṉ, maṇṇulakattil ceṉṟu peṇ yāṉaiyakap piṟantu nī
   virumpiyapaṭi āṇ yāṉaiyuṭaṉ viḷaiyaṭip pōkam nukarka' eṉṟu
   cāpamiṭṭaṉaṉ. iṭavē avaḷ naṭunaṭuṅki, 'ap piṟappiṉiṉṟum nāṉ nīṅkutal
   eppoḻutu?' eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaḷ.
   kēṭṭavaḷukku, 'nī piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya nakaril oru peṇ yāṉaiyākap piṟantu
   intap peyaraiyē peṟṟu vaḷarntu vāḻum kālattil avvaracaṉāl utaṇaṉukkuk
   koṭukkappaṭuvāy. avaṉ vācavatattaiyai uṉmītu ēṟṟi iruḷil celuttumpoḻutu
   nī neṭuntūram ceṉṟu kālakūṭameṉṉum nōyālum perumpaciyālum vīḻvāy.
   viḻntavuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉ pañcamantirattai uṉ kātil upatēcippāṉ. avvupa
   tēcattaip peṟṟu nī piṟappoḻintu teyva vaṭivaṅ koṇṭu iṅkē varuvāy' eṉṟu
   kūṟiṉaṉ. kūṟiyapaṭiyē nikaḻntamaiyiṉ avaḷ, uṉakku māṟṟupakāram oṉṟaic
   ceyya niṉaintu ataṟkuriya upāyattai nāṭik kupēraṉai aṭaintu vaṇaṅki,
   'eṉakku oru ciṟu varam tantaruḷal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaḷ. kēṭkavē
   kupēraṉ, nī virumpiyatai maṟaiyāmal col' eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ. appōtu
   avaḷ, 'yāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉatu nāṭṭiṉ ellaivaraiyil cumantu ceṉṟu
   viḻuntavuṭaṉ taṉkāriyam muṭintuviṭṭa teṉṟu niṉaiyāmal utayaṇaṉ eṉpāl
   vantu nāṉ ceyta utaviyai mikavum pārāṭṭik kaṇṇīr viṭuttu eṉ uṭampait
   taivantu nāṉ teyvappiṟappai aṭaintu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻumvaṇṇam
   pañcamantirattai eṉ kātil upatēcittut taṉ kaṭaṉaik kaḻittaṉaṉ. avaṉukku
   oru makaṉ uṇṭākumpaṭi aruḷ ceyyumāṟu vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ' eṉṟu kēṭkavē kupēraṉ,
   anta varattai avaḷukkuk koṭuttuviṭṭu viṇṇulakattuḷḷa cau tarmēntiraṉai
   aṭaintu uṉ perumai mutaliyavaṟṟai avaṉukkuc colli, 'utayaṇaṉukku
   makiḻvaḷikkat takka oru putalvaṉ piṟakka vēṇṭum' eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ.
   cautarmēntiraṉ "veḷḷiyam perumalaiyil cakkara varttiyāka irukka
   vēṇṭumeṉṟa viruppattōṭu cōtavaṉeṉṉum muṉivaṉ oruvaṉ tavam ceytu
   koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉaṉ. avaṉ utayaṇaṉukkup puttiraṉākappiṟantu vittiyātara
   cakkaravarttiyāki aracāṭci ceytuviṭṭu eṉpāl varuvāṉ" eṉṟu viṭaiyaḷittu
   avaṉai varuvittu "nī vācavatattaiyiṉ vayiṟṟil taṅkip piṟappāyāka" eṉṟu
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ. ātalāl uṉakkuc ciṟanta kumāraṉ uṇṭātal tappātu. antap
   pattirāpati iṉṉum uṉakku ētēṉum ōr utavi ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa
   viruppattuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟaṉaḷ. avaḷai niṉaittāl vantu uṉ kuṟaiyait
   tīrppāḷ. aṅṅaṉan tīrāḷāyiṉ yāṉ tirumpavum vantu tīrppēṉ" eṉṟu collip
   pattirāpatiyai aḻaittaṟkuriya mantiram oṉṟai upatēcittuviṭṭup pala upa
   cāra moḻikaḷāl utayaṇaṉaip pārāṭṭit taḻuvi viṭai peṟṟut taṉṉiṭam
   ceṉṟaṉaṉ. cellavē utayaṇaṉ am mantirattai ōti uruvēṟṟikkoṇṭiruntāṉ.
   iruntavaṉ piṉpu "vācavatattaiyiṉ viruppattait tīrttaṟku upāyam yātu?"
   eṉṟu nāṭip pala iṭattiliruntum taccarkaḷai varuvittu "vācavatattai
   ūrntu ākāyavaḻiyē ceṉṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷaiyum pārttuvarutaṟkuriya entira
   vimāṉam oṉṟaic ceyvittiṭuka" eṉṟu coṉṉapoḻutu avarkaḷ "aḵtu emmāl
   ceytaṟkariyatu" eṉṟaṉar,.
   itaṉai aṟinta pattirāpati "nāmē taccavaṭivaṅ koṇṭu ceṉṟu ik kāriyattai
   muṭippōm" eṉṟu niṉaintu oru taccuviṉai iḷaiñaṉpōlavē aṅkē vantu
   urumaṇṇuvāvāl aracaṉai aṭaintu "it taccarkaḷ ataic ceyya aṟiyār. yāṉ
   atai eḷitil cey vēṉ eṉṟu terivittaṉaḷ. aracaṉ uṭaṉpaṭṭu
   ataṟkuvēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik koṭukka, avaṟṟaikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu cila tiṉattil entira
   vimāṉam oṉṟaic ceytu muṭittuviṭṭu vantu aracaṉiṭam terivittaṉaḷ.
   utayaṇaṉ ceṉṟu ataṉaik kaṇṭu umaiyoṭu puṇarnta imaiyā nāṭṭattuk
   kaṇṇaṇaṅ kaviroḷik kaṭavuḷaippōla vācavatattaiyōṭu atil ēṟiyiruntu
   viḷaṅkiṉaṉ. 1urumaṇṇuvā mutaliya tōḻar nāl varum "eṅkaḷ maṉaiviyarkaḷum
   karuppamuṟṟavarkaḷātaliṉ itil ēṟic cellum viruppamuṭaiyār" eṉṟu colla
   utayaṇaṉ pattirāpatiyiṉuṭaiya anumatiyaip peṟṟu yūki mutaliya toḻar
   nālvaraiyum avarkaḷuṭaiya maṉaiviyarkaḷaiyum atil ēṟṟiṉaṉ. pattirā pati
   ataṉai ākāyavaḻiyē celuttikkoṇṭu poṉmalaimutal potiyilmalai iṟutiyāka
   pala malaikaḷaiyum, pala kāṭukaḷaiyum, pala tēcaṅkaḷaiyum, pala
   nakaraṅkaḷaiyum piṉṉum kāṭṭavēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum kāṭṭiviṭṭu
   mīṭṭum kaucāmpinakaram vantu cērntaṉaḷ.
   ----------
       1 urumaṇṇuvā mutaliya mūvarukkum vivākamāṉa poḻutē yūkikkum
       mutalmuṟai vivākam ākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu itaṉāl
       aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.

   cērntapiṉ utayaṇaṉ vimāṉam ceyta taccuviṉai iḷaiñaṉpōla utaviya
   pattirāpatiyai aḻaittu mikka upacāra moḻikaḷaik kūṟi "eṅkaḷuṭaiya perum
   tuyarattait tīrtta uṉakku emmāl ceytaṟkuriya kaimmāṟu yātu?" eṉṟu
   collit taṉṉuṭaiya aṇikalaṅkaḷaik kaḻaṟṟik koṭukkat toṭaṅkiyapoḻutu
   pattirāpati avaṟṟaik koḷḷāmal taṉ paḻaiya varalāṟu muḻuvataiyum colli
   "nāṉ pattirāpatiyeṉṉum peṇ yāṉaiyāka iruntapoḻutu eṉṉuṭaiya
   maraṇakālattil nīr ceyta utavikku māṟāka eṉṉāl ceytaṟku uriyatu oṉṟum
   illai. nīrum nāṉum vēṟalam" eṉṟu colli viṭaipeṟṟut taṉṉuṭaiya paṇṭait
   teyvap peṇ vaṭivattaik koṇṭaṉaḷ.
   piṉṉar avaḷ ākāyavaḻiyē cellutaṟkuriya mantiram oṉṟai avaṉukku
   upatēcittuviṭṭut taṉṉiṭam cella, utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōr avaḷai nōkkip
   pukaḻntu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
   utayaṇaṉ vācavatattaiyai mikavum pātukāttu avaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic
   ceytuvantapoḻutu avaḷ nalla nāḷil āṇkuḻantai oṉṟaip peṟṟāḷ.
   piṉṉar yūki mutaliya nālvarkaḷuṭaiya maṉaiviyarum āṇkuḻantaikaḷaip
   peṟṟārkaḷ. appōtu utayaṇaṉum tōḻarkaḷum maṟṟaiyōrum; mikka tāṉa
   tarumaṅkaḷaic ceytaṉar. muracaṟaivittu nāṭṭiṉarum nakarattiṉarum
   ciṟappu ayarumāṟum terivittaṉar. utayaṇaṉ ic ceytiyait tarucakaṉukkum;
   piraccōtaṉaṉukkum maṟṟaiyōrkkum colliviṭuttāṉ. paṉṉiraṇṭu tiṉaṅkaḷ
   ceṉṟaṉa. piṉpu nāmakaraṇam ceytaṉar. utayaṇaṉ putalvaṉ kupēraṉāl
   koṭukkappaṭṭavaṉātaliṉ, avaṉukku naravāṇatattaṉ eṉṉum peyar
   iṭappaṭṭatu. yūkiyiṉ putalvaṉukku marupūtiyeṉṟum, urumaṇṇuvāviṉ
   putalvaṉukku aricikaṉeṉṟum, vayantakaṉ putalvaṉukkut tavantakaṉ eṉṟum,
   iṭavakaṉ putalvaṉukkuk kōmukaṉeṉṟum peyarkaḷ muṟaiyē iṭap paṭṭaṉa.
   nakarattār palavāṟu pārāṭṭiṉar. piḷḷaikaḷ nāḷukku nāḷ piṟaipōla
   vaḷarcciyaṭaintārkaḷ.
   appāl 1patumāpati karuvuṟṟu oru putalvaṉaip peṟṟāḷ. avaṉukkuk kōmukaṉ
   eṉṉum peyar iṭap paṭṭatu. utayaṇaṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭa tūtarkaḷ yūkiyuṭaṉ
   uccaiṉiyai aṭaintu piraccōtaṉaṉukku ic ceytiyait terivittaṉar.
   terivittavarkaḷukkup piraccōtaṉaṉ mikka cammāṉam aḷittāṉ. taṉṉuṭaiya
   āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa patiṉāyiram nakaraṅkaḷil uḷḷavarkaḷai yellām varuvittu
   avarkaḷukku naṉkoṭai aḷittaṉaṉ; viḻā eṭuppittaṉaṉ.
   -------
       1 icceyti vēṟu nūlkaḷiliruntu aṟintu eḻutappaṭṭatu.

   appāl avaṉ orunāḷ pōttāṇiyiliruntu yūkiyaik kaucāmpikku aṉuppa eṇṇit
   taṉṉuṭaiya mantirikaḷuḷ mēm paṭṭa cālaṅkāyaṉaiyum yūkiyaiyum varuvittu
   etirē irukkacceytu avarkaḷuṭaiya kalviyai aḷattaṟku eṇṇi nītinūl vātam
   ceyvittaṉaṉ. avviruvarkkum vātappōr naṭantatu. appōril yūki veṉṟaṉaṉ.
   appoḻutu,
   "ēṟṟa mukatti ṉiṟaivaṉum virumpi
       naṇpiṉ māṭciyuṅ kalviya takalamum
       paṇpiṉ ṟoḻilum paṭaittoḻiṉ māṇpum
       kāyu mānta rāyiṉum yātum
       tīyavai kūṟap paṭāta tiṇmaiyum
       ivaṟkala tillai yivaṉāṟ peṟṟa
       avaṟkala tillai yaraciṉ māṭci" (5.7:106-112)

   eṉṟu yūkiyaip palapaṭap pārāṭṭi paratakaṉeṉṉum mantiriyiṉ taṅkaiyākiya
   tilakacēṉaiyaiyum cālaṅkāyaṉ taṅkai yāppiyaiyum avaṉukku maṇam
   ceyvittu,
   "naviltoṟu miṉiya ñāṉam pōlap
       payiltoṟu miṉiyaniṉ paṇpuṭaik kiḻamai
       uḷḷuto ṟuḷḷuto ṟuḷḷa miṉpuṟap
       pirivuṟu tuṉpa memāṭ ṭeyta (5.7:148-151)

   kaucāmpi nakarañ celvāyāka. utayaṇaṉ uṉṉai aṉuppumpaṭi
   terivittamaiyālēyē ippoḻutu aṉuppat tuṇintēṉ. uṉṉaippōṉṟa
   nalloḻukkamuṭaiyavar ulakattil uḷarō? eṉṟukūṟi upacarittu aṉuppiṉaṉ.
   avaṉ koṭutta aruṅkala mutaliyavaṟṟai muṉṉar aṉuppiviṭṭu avaṉ aḷitta
   perum celvattōṭu yūki piṉpu puṟappaṭṭuk kaucāmpikkuc ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   ceṉṟa yūki utayaṇaṉaik kaṇṭu piraccōtaṉaṉ ceyta mukamaṉkaḷaiyum
   piṟavaṟṟaiyum kūṟa, utayaṇaṉ makiḻvuṟṟut tōḻarkaḷōṭu aḷavaḷāvi aracāṭci
   ceytukoṇ ṭiruṉantaṉaṉ.
   naravāṇatattaṉ nāḷtōṟum perukutaluṟṟu marupūtimutaliya nāṉku
   tōḻarkaḷoṭum kūṭit taruma nūl mutaliyavaṟṟilum taṉur vētattilum mikka
   payiṟciyai uṭaiyavaṉākik kāḷaipparuvam uṟṟuk koṭaikkaṭam pūṇṭu mikka
   mēmpāṭṭuṭaṉ viḷaṅkuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   appoḻutu an nakarattil kalvi kēḷvikaḷil ciṟantavarkaḷākiya
   iraṇṭāyittainnūṟu kaṇikaiyarkaḷuḷ ciṟantavaḷāṉa kaliṅkacēṉai
   aṉpavaḷuṭaiya makaḷ 1mataṉamañcikai eṉpavaḷ taṉṉuṭaiya māṭattiṉmēl uḷḷa
   nilāmuṟṟattil tōḻimārōṭu āṭiya pantukaḷil oṉṟu cāḷarattiṉvaḻiyē vantu,
   vītiyil tōḻarkaḷ cūḻa yāṉaiyiṉmēl cellum naravāṇatattaṉuṭaiya melliya
   tukiliṉmēl viḻuntatu. ataṉai avaṉ eṭuttup pārttu, "itaṉai iṭṭavaḷ yār?"
   eṉṟu yāṉaiyil iruntapaṭiyē eṭṭip pārttaṉaṉ.
   --------
       1. mataṉamañcikaiyeṉpavaḷ, kaucāmpiyilirunta taṉamittiraṉeṉṉum
       vaṇikaṉukkuk kaliṅkacēṉaiyeṉpavaḷpāl tōṉṟiyavaḷeṉṟu
       utitōtayakāvyattāl terikiṉṟatu.

   pārttapoḻutu aṅkē kāṇappaṭṭa mataṉamañcikaiyiṉ aḻakiya vaṭivam avaṉ
   maṉattaik kavarntatu. kavaravē mayaṅkic ceyalaṟṟiruntu piṉpu ap
   pantaiyē taṉakkut tuṇaiyākakkoṇṭu ceṉṟu oru cōlaiyil iruntu uṭaṉvanta
   taṉ tōḻaṉākiya kōmukaṉai aḻaittu, "ippantai eṟinta maṅkaiyai nī
   aṟivāyāyil colla vēṇṭum" eṉṟu viṉāviṉaṉ. cittiravittaiyil ciṟanta
   kōmukaṉ appantaik kaikkoṇṭu cantaṉam pūciya kaiyāl am maṅkai aḻuntap
   piṭittiruntamaiyiṉ viral niraikaḷiṉ cuvaṭukaḷ atil aḻuntiyiruntai
   aṟintu avaḷuṭaiya vaṭivam muḻuvataiyum maṉattāl uṇarntu,
   'viralum viraliṟ kēṟṟa vaṅkaiyum
       aṅkaik kēṟṟa paintoṭi muṉkaiyum
       muṉkaik kēṟṟa naṉkamai tōḷum
       tōḷiṟ kēṟṟa vāḷoḷi mukamum
       māmpaṭu vaṭuvuṟaḻ malarneṭuṅ kaṇṇum
       tuppaṉa vāyu muttoḷi muṟuvalum
       oḻukukoṭi mūkku meḻutunaṉ puruvamum
       cēṭamai ceviyum cilliruṅ kūntalum' (5.8:100-107)

   ākiyavaṟṟaiyuṭaiya oru peṇvaṭivaip paṭattiṉil eḻutuk koṇarntu ap
   paṭattai naravāṇatattaṉukkuk kāṭṭi, "iv vaṭivattaip pōṉṟa
   vaṭivamuṭaiyāḷ in nakarattil mataṉamañcikai eṉpavaḷtāṉ" eṉṟu
   colliviṭṭup poṟparicaṅkaḷōṭu avaḷ vīṭṭiṟkucceṉṟu avaḷuṭaiya naṟṟāyaik
   kaṇṭu nikaḻn tataik kūṟiṉāṉ. kūṟavē avaḷ "vaḻipaṭuteyvam
   varantarukiṉṟatu" eṉṟu colli virumpi uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉaḷ. avaḷuṭaiya
   cuṟṟattār, "utayaṇaṉuṭaiya anumatiyaip peṟāmal aracakumāraṉukku ivaḷaik
   koṭuttal mikka tavaṟākum" eṉṟu maṟuttaṉar. kōmukaṉ ataṉai utayaṇaṉukkut
   terivikka, avaṉ peritum uvantu vācavatattaiyiṉ anumati peṟṟu
   naravāṇatattaṉukku mataṉamañcikaiyai nallanāḷil maṇampurivittaṉaṉ.
   appāl kaucāmpinakarattil mikka ciṟappuṭaṉ viḻāvoṉṟu naṭaipeṟṟatu.
   akkāṭciyai virumpi vittiyātara nakarattuḷḷa nūṟṟuppattu aracarkaḷuḷ
   ciṟantavaṉākiya māṉacavēkaṉ eṉpavaṉ vantu an nakarattuc
   ciṟappukkaḷaiyellām pārttuk koṇṭu cellukaiyil araṇmaṉaiyiṉuḷḷē
   ceyaṟkaiyākiya nāl vakai nilaṉum cūḻnta oru cōlaiyiṉiṭaiyē uḷḷa
   kaṭṭumalai oṉṟaṉ cikarattil niṉṟu nakaril nikaḻum viḻāvaṇiyaip
   pārppavaḷākiya mataṉamañcikaiyaik kaṇṭu viyantu avaḷai eṭuttukkoṇṭu taṉ
   nakarañ cellutaṟkuc camayam pārttukkoṇṭē iruntāṉ. avaṉ appaṭi
   irukkaiyil naravāṇatattaṉum mataṉa mañcekaiyum oru pūñcōlaiyai aṭaintu
   aṅkēyuḷḷa koṭivīṭoṉṟaic cārntu melliya malarkaḷ parappiya aṇaiyil
   ayarntu nittiraiceytaṉar. ataṉaik kaṇṭa māṉacavēkaṉ taṉ māyaiyāl
   naravāṇaṉai mika naṉṟākat tuyilumpaṭi ceytuviṭṭu mataṉamañcikaiyai
   maṭṭum mellat tūkki eṭuttukkoṇṭu ākāyattil eḻuntu "iṉṟu mikac ciṟanta
   arumaiyāṉa poruḷ oṉṟaip peṟṟōm" eṉṟu 1makiḻvuṟṟuc ceṉṟu taṉ nakarai
   aṭaintu avaḷaic ciṟantatōr iṭattil mella vaittaṉaṉ. avaḷ tuyiluṇarntu
   eḻuntu nāṟpuṟattaiyum pārttu "inta iṭam mikap putitāka irukkiṉṟatē.
   iṅkē nām vantataṟkuk kāraṇam yātu?" eṉṟu kavalaiyuṟṟu mayaṅki
   varuntuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. appoḻutu māṉacavēkaṉ avaḷai aṭaintu taṉ viruppattait
   terivittaṉaṉ. avaḷ uṭaṉpaṭāmal maṟuttaṉaḷ. maṟukkavē avaṉ ēṅki
   "mataṉamañcikaiyiṉ maṉattai eppaṭiyāvatu vēṟupaṭuttiviṭṭuvā" eṉṟu taṉ
   taṅkaiyākiya vēkavatiyeṉpavaḷai avaḷpāl aṉuppiṉaṉ. avaḷ vantu
   māṉacavēkaṉatu uyarvai eṭuttukkūṟi "nī eṉ tamaiyaṉukku uriyavaḷāka
   iruppāyāyiṉ mikka iṉpattai aṭaivāy" eṉṟu kāraṇaṅkāṭṭip palavāṟu
   vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟiyapoḻutu mataṉamañcikai "aḻaku kalvi vīram mutaliya
   ellāvaṟṟilum mēmpaṭṭuc ciṟantu viḷaṅkum eṉṉuṭaiya talaivaṉ
   naravāṇatattaṉai iṉi aṭaiyēṉāyiṉ yāṉ uyirainīttal orutalai" eṉṟu
   maṟuttuk kūṟiṉaḷ.
   ---------
       1 itaṉ piṉṉuḷḷa kataikkuriya mūlappakuti citaintu pōṉamaiyiṉ
       utitōtayakāvyam mutaliya vēṟu nūlkaḷiliruntu paṭittaṟintu
       eḻutalāyiṟṟu.

   appāl vēkavati taṉ tamaiyaṉatu kaṭṭaḷaiyai maṟantu naravāṇatattaṉpāl
   vēṭkai mikuntu "eppaṭiyum avaṉaik kaṇavaṉāka viraivil aṭaintuviṭa
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟa vēkamuṭaiyavaḷāy uṭaṉē vimāṉam ūrntu puṟappaṭṭu vantu
   kaucāmpiyai aṭaintaṉaḷ. appōtu naravāṇaṉ mataṉamañcikaiyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmal
   varunti mayaṅkic cōlaiyeṅkum avaḷait tēṭi alamantu uḻalutalaik kaṇṭāḷ.
   kaṇṭu avaṉ vaṭivaip pārttu mika viyantu viruppam mikappeṟṟu oru viccai
   valiyāl mataṉamañcikaiyiṉ vaṭivattait tāṉ eṭuttukkoṇṭu oru koṭivīṭṭil
   iruppa, aṅkē mataṉamañci kaiyait tēṭit tirintukoṇṭirukkum naravāṇaṉ
   vantu kaṇṭu kavalaiyoḻintu mikka paraparappuṭaṉ avaḷait taḻuvi
   iṉpuṟṟāṉ. piṉṉar avaṉ aiyuṟṟu "nī yār?" eṉṉa, avaḷtāṉ iṉṉāḷ eṉpataiyum
   taṉ tamaiyaṉākiya māṉacavēkaṉ mataṉamañcikaiyai virumpik kaippaṟṟic
   ceṉṟataiyum avaḷ avaṉukku iṇaṅkā mal ūṇuṟakkamiṉṟi
   varuttamuṟṟiruttalaiyum tāṉ vantataṟkuk kāraṇattaiyum kūṟip piṉpu
   taṉatu uṇmai uruvattaik kāṭṭiṉaḷ. kaṇṭa naravāṇaṉ makiḻntu
   mataṉamañcikaiyai maṟantu vēkavatipāl aṉpuṟṟuk kāmaparavacaṉāki
   avaḷuṭaṉ eppoḻutum iruppāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ivarkaḷ ippaṭiyirukka māṉacavēkaṉ ceṉṟa taṅkaiyaik kāṇāṉākip pala
   iṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭikkoṇṭu vantu kaucāmpiyaic cārnta cōlaiyil irukkum
   avviruvaraiyuṅkaṇṭu ciṉamuṟṟu avarkaḷaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu ākāya mārkkamākac
   ceṉṟapoḻutu iṭaivaḻiyil nāvāṇaṉait tiṭīreṉṟu kīḻē taḷḷi viṭṭāṉ.
   vēkavati taṉ viccaivaliyāl avaṉukku yātoru tuṉpamum tōṉṟātapaṭi
   ceyvittaṉaḷ. naravāṇaṉ vīḻnta iṭam 1catāṉika muṉivaruṭaiya
   tavappaḷḷiyātaliṉ, am muṉivaruṭaiya makimaiyaik kēṭṭaṟinta avaṉ, taṉ
   tuṉpattai avar eppaṭiyum tīrttuviṭuvār eṉṟu niccayittuc ceṉṟu avarai
   aṉpuṭaṉ vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ. avar ñāṉattāl avaṉai iṉṉāṉeṉṟu aṟintu
   "utayaṇaṉuṭaiya putalvaṉē! uṉakku maṅkaḷamum aṟivum uṇṭākuka" eṉṟaṉar.
   -------
       1ivar utayaṇaṉ tantai.

   "nam tantaiyiṉ peyarai ivar eppaṭi aṟintaṉar?" eṉṟu viyappuṟṟa avaṉ
   "tēvarīr yāvar?" eṉṟu kēṭṭa poḻutu avar "yāṉ uṉṉuṭaiya tantaiyiṉ
   tantai" eṉṟataṉṟit tam varalāṟu muḻuvataiyum kūṟiṉar. avaṉ
   "vittiyātarar ulaka āṭciyai nāṉ peṟutal kūṭumeṉṟu muṉṉam kēṭṭuḷēṉ.
   ataṉaip peṟutaṟku upāyam yātu?" eṉṟu kēṭpa avar "uṉṉuṭaiya nakaram
   aṭaintu utayaṇaṉ pāl ākāya mārkkamākac cellutaṟkuriya viccaiyaip peṟṟu
   ataṉ makimaiyāl anta āṭciyaip peṟuvai" eṉṟaṉar. piṉpu naravāṇaṉ avarai
   vaṇaṅki avar colliyapaṭi kaucāmpiyaic cārntu taṉṉaik kāṇāmal tēṭit tēṭi
   varuttamuṟṟirukkum tāytantaiyarai aṭaintu vaṇaṅki nikaḻntavaṟṟaik
   kūṟiṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ avaṉukku mēṟkūṟiya mantirattai upatēcittaṉaṉ . piṉpu
   naravāṇaṉ vimāṉam ūrntu ceṉṟu 1vicayārttam eṉṉum malaiyiṉ
   teṉpākattiluḷḷa srītarameṉṉum nakarattai aṭaintu ataṉ kōṭṭaivāyilil
   taṅki iruntaṉaṉ.
   ------
       1itu vittiyātararkaḷukku uṟaiviṭamākiya oru veḷḷi malai eṉpar.

   appoḻutu oruvaṉ vantu vaṇaṅki "kantaruva purattiṉ aracaṉākiya
   nīlavēkaṉukku nākatattai eṉpavaḷpāl tōṉṟiya anaṅkavilāciṉi eṉpavaḷ
   muṉṉorunāḷ tuyilumpoḻutu oru ciṅkakkuṭṭi pūmiyiliruntu vantu taṉakku
   mālai cūṭṭiyatākak kaṉāvoṉṟaik kaṇṭu ataṉait tantaikku terivittaṉaḷ.
   avaṉ cumittiraṉeṉṉum muṉivarai aṭaintu vaṇaṅki ak kaṉāvait terivittu
   ataṉ payaṉai viṉāviṉaṉ. avar, 'pūmiyiliruntu tīraṉāṉa ōr iḷaiñaṉ vantu
   uṉ makaḷukku mālai cūṭṭuvāṉ. cūṭṭiyapiṉ avaṉ iṅkē cakkaravarttiyumāki
   ellā aracarkaḷaiyum aṭakki āṭcipurintu viḷaṅkuvāṉ' eṉṟu
   viṭaiyaḷittaṉar. aḷikkavē ummuṭaiya nalvaravai nāṭōṟum nōkkik koṇṭē
   irukkum avaṉ nīr iṅkē vantatai aṟintu taṉpāl ummai aḻaittuvarumpaṭi
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaṉaṉ' eṉṟu terivikka, naravāṇaṉ makiḻntu avaṉuṭaṉ
   avvaracaṉai aṭaintu avaṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi anaṅkavilāciṉiyai maṇantāṉ.
   maṇantu aṅkē taṅkiyiruntapoḻutu paḻampiṟappil ceyta puṇṇiyattiṉ
   payaṉkaḷ muṟaiyē avaṉai aṭaivaṉavāyiṉa. intiraṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyāl tēvarkaḷ
   vantu avaṉukku navanitikaḷaiyum aḷittuc ceṉṟaṉar. avaṉatu viṟalai
   aṟinta avvulakattu aracarkaḷ ellōrum vantu vaṇaṅkip paṇintu
   tiṟaikoṭuttu avaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi oḻukuvārāyiṉar. ac ceytikaḷai aṟinta
   māṉacavēkaṉ mikavum añcit taṉṉiṭam irunta mataṉamañcikaiyai
   aḻaittuvantu naravāṇaṉpāl cērppittuviṭṭu, "eṉ kuṟṟattaip poṟuttaruḷa
   vēṇṭum" eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉaṉ. piṉpu avaṉ taṉ taṅkai vēkavatiyaiyum aṉpuṭaṉ
   naravāṇaṉukku maṇam ceyvittaṉaṉ.
   naravāṇaṉ taṉ pirivāl vāṭṭamuṟṟirunta mataṉa mañcikaiyait taḻuvi iṉiya
   moḻikaḷāl makiḻvittu avaḷōṭum vēkavati, anaṅkavilācaṉi eṉpavrkaḷōṭum
   piṉpu aṅkē maṇañceyta eṇṇāyiran tēvimārōṭum iṉpuṟupavaṉāki oṟṟaic
   ceṅkōl celuttic cakkaravarttiyāki avvulakai āṇṭu vantāṉ.
   ak kālattil taṉṉuṭaiya irumutukuravarkaḷait taricikka vēṇṭumeṉṟa
   viruppam avaṉukku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. uṇṭākavē avaṉ paricaṉaṅkaḷōṭum
   maṉaivimārōṭum puṟappaṭṭuk kaucāmpi nakaram aṭaintu tāy tantaiyarai
   vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ. avaṉuṭaiya perumaiyaik kēṭṭu avarkaḷ makiḻntārkaḷ.
   appāl naravāṇatattaṉ patumaiyiṉ putalvaṉāṉa kōmukaṉai iḷavaracāka
   niyamikkumpaṭi tantaiyai vēṇṭi avvaṇṇam ceyvittuviṭṭu viṭaipeṟṟukkoṇṭu
   maṉaivimār mutaliyavarkaḷōṭu vittiyātara ulakattai aṭaintu aracāṭci
   ceytukoṇṭu vāḻvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   maṉitaṉākap piṟantu vittiyātara cakkaravarttiyāka viḷaṅkiya avaṉuṭaiya
   puṇṇiya mikutiyai yāvarum aṟintu pārāṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
   --

                          6. tuṟavuk kāṇṭam

   utayaṇaṉ, taṉakku oppaṟṟa yūki mantiriyāṉatum, vācavatattai
   maṉaiviyāṉatum, vittiyātara cakkaravarttiyāka viḷaṅkum naravāṇatattaṉ
   putalvaṉāṉatum, piṟa nalaṅkaḷum tavattiṉ payaṉē ātalāl, piṉ tāṉmēṟ
   koḷḷaṟpālatu tavamē eṉṟu tuṇintu celvattai veṟuttaṉaṉa. ataṉai aṟinta
   tēvimār nīr viḷaiyāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl avaṉ maṉattai vēṟupaṭuttit
   tammaiyē karuti oḻukacceyya avaṉ maṉam anta vaḻiyil cilanāṭkaḷ ceṉṟatu.
   piṉpu orunanāḷ avaṉuṭaiya paṭṭattu yāṉai mataṅkoṇṭu puṟappaṭṭu nakarai
   aḻikkat toṭaṅki yārukkum aṭaṅkāmal pākarkaḷaiyum kuttukkōlkāraraiyum
   piḷanteṟintatu. ataṉāl nakaram naṭuṅkiyatu.
   ak kālattil an nakarattai aṭutta cōlai oṉṟil tavattāl cittipeṟṟa
   cāraṇar palar vantu taṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkut talaivarāṉa tarumavīrar
   eṉpavar tammai aṭaintavarkaḷukku vaḻakkappaṭi aṅkē tarumōpatēcañ
   ceytuvaruvārāyiṉar. avvupatēca moḻikaḷai vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷum
   kēṭpaṉavāyt tattañ ceyalkaḷai maṟantu uṇavoḻintu tām ceyta pāvaṅkaḷai
   niṉaintu tuṉpuṟṟavaṇṇamāy aṭaṅki niṉṟaṉa. mēṟkūṟiya paṭṭattu yāṉaiyum
   iyalpākavē aṅkē vantu anta aṟavuraiyaik kēṭṭuk kōpam nīṅkit taṉ
   pāvacceyalkaḷukku añcik kaṇṇīr ukuttukkoṇṭu yātoru koṭuñceyalum iṉṟi
   araṇmaṉai vāyilil vantu niṉṟatu.
   vāyil kāvalar yāṉai vantu aṭaṅkiniṟpatai aracaṉukkut terivikka, avaṉ
   vantu pārttu viyappuṟṟu niṟkaiyil yūki, "ētō aṟavuraiyaik kēṭṭu itu
   pakkuvamuṟṟu aṭaṅki niṉkiṉṟatupōlum; iṉi ūrntu cellutaṟku itu
   takutiyuḷḷatu" eṉṟaṉaṉ. utayaṇaṉ ataṉ piṭariyil ēṟiyapoḻutu yāṉai
   avaṉai mikka viruppattuṭaṉ tāṅkikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu mēṟ kūṟiya vaṉattai
   aṭaintu niṉṟatu. aracaṉ av vaṉattil periyōr palar vantiruttalai aṟintu
   iṟaṅki vaḻi pāṭṭuṭaṉ ceṉṟu tarumavīramuṉivarai vaṇaṅka, avar avaṉukku
   ōr irukkai aḷittut tarumōpatēcañ ceytaṉar. ataṉaik kēṭṭa aracaṉ
   iṉpuṟṟu, "inta yāṉaiyiṉ paṇṭai varalāṟu eṉṉa?" eṉṟu kēṭpa avar, "cāli
   eṉpatoru nāṭṭil kaṭakam eṉpatōr ūriluḷḷa iṭapakaṉ eṉpavaṉum avaṉ
   maṉaivi cāli eṉpavaḷum illaṟam naṭattikkoṇṭu varukaiyil, avaṉ paṇṭaip
   pāva vacattāl amarikai eṉṉum oru kaṇikai vayattaṉāyk kulavoḻukkattai
   viṭṭuviṭṭu mikka pāvac ceykaikaḷaic ceyvaṉāyiṉaṉ. iṟanta piṉpu avaṉē
   inta yāṉaip piṟappai aṭaintaṉaṉ. ataṉāl inta yāṉai tarumaṅkaḷaik
   kēṭṭavuṭaṉ koṭuñceykaikaḷait tuṟantu aṭaṅkiniṉṟatu" eṉṟaṉar.
   kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ atai naṉku pātukākkumpaṭi pākarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭu
   ataṉ pakkattai aṭaintu, "yāṉai aracē! uṉakku yāṉ ceyvitta tuṉpaṅkaḷaip
   poṟuttukkoḷ" eṉṟu colli ataṉai aṉpuṭaṉ taivantu taṉṉiṭam aṭaintaṉaṉ.
   ataṉ varalāṟṟaik kēṭṭa yāvarum viyappuṟṟut tarumōpatēcattiṉ perumaiyaip
   pārāṭṭiṉar.
   appāl aracaṉukku vairākkiyam uṇṭāka avaṉ naravāṇatattaṉai varuvittu,
   "nī inta aracāṭciyai oppukkoḷ. tuṟantu tavam ceyvataṟku eṉ maṉam
   viraikiṉṟatu" eṉṟu colla, avaṉ vaṇaṅki, "aṭiyēṉukkum aracāṭciyil
   ciṟitum viruppam illai. tuṟattaṟkē eṉ maṉam viraikiṉṟatu" eṉṟu taṉ
   karuttait terivittāṉ. piṉ utayaṇaṉ patumaiyiṉ putalvaṉākiya kōmukaṇai
   aḻaittu avaṉukku muṭicūṭṭi viṭṭuc ceṉṟu tēviyarukkut taṉ karuttait
   terivittaṉaṉ. avarkaḷum uṭaṉvantu tavam ceyvatākak kūṟiṉar.yūki
   mutaliya mantirikaḷ nālvarum vācavatattai mutaliya tēvimārum piṟarum
   puṭaicūḻntuvara utayaṇaṉ tavavaṉam ceṉṟu aṅkē tavamceytukoṇṭirunta
   tarumaccurutiyeṉṉum muṉivaraic caraṇam aṭaintu tutittu, "aṭikēḷ!
   tavavaḻikaḷai upa tēcittu aṭiyēṅkaḷai uyvittaruḷa vēṉṭum" eṉṟu
   pirārttittaṉaṉ. avarcila tarumaṅkaḷai virivāka upa tēcittaṉar.
   upatēcittapiṉpu avaṉ appoḻutu ceyyavēṇṭiya niyamaṅkaḷai muṭittuk koṇṭu
   tiyāṉātikaḷaic ceyvāṉāyiṉaṉ. tavavoḻukkamum oḷiyum avaṉpāl ciṟantu
   viḷaṅkiṉa.
   piṉpu utayaṇamuṉivaṉ yōkacamātiyaic ceytu muṭittuc cittapatattai
   aṭaintāṉ. tēviyarum mantirikaḷum tavam ceytu kaṟpalōkattai aṭaintu
   iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntārkaḷ.
   --------------------
                     ___________________________________

                  apitāṉa viḷakkam (apitāṉam-peyar)

   accuvap perumakaṉ: ivaṉ kēkayanāṭṭiṉ aracaṉ; kuṟṟamaṟṟa pukaḻai
   uṭaiyavaṉ; muṭiyuṭai maṉṉaṉ; mikka vīraṉ; patumāpatiyai maṇañceyya
   virumpik koṭuttaṟkuriya parica mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ vantu avaḷ
   tamaiyaṉākiya tarucakaṉaik kaṇṭu taṉ karuttaik kūṟi avaṉatu uṭaṉpāṭṭaip
   peṟṟu uṭaṉirunta kālattil avaṉōṭu pōrceytaṟku vanta pakaivaruṭaṉ yuttam
   ceytaṟku valintupōyp porutu eliccevi aracaṉāl kollappaṭṭavaṉ.
   aṭavi: iṭavakaṉukkuc cīvitamāka utayaṇaṉāl koṭukkappaṭṭa nāṭu
   amarikai: cāli nāṭṭilirunta oru kaṇikai.
   aricikaṉ: urumaṇṇuvāviṉ putalvaṉ.
   arimāṉ: tarucakaṉōṭu pakaimaikoṇṭoḻukiya aracarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ.
   aruñcukaṉ: uccaiṉiyil irunta ōr antaṇap piramacāri; vēta mutaliyavaṟṟai
   ōtiyuṇarntavaṉ; yāḻvakaiyaiyum, piṟa nūlkaḷaiyum naṉku aṟintavaṉ;
   kōṭapatiyeṉṉum yāḻaik kaṇṭeṭuttu utayaṇaṉiṭam koṭuttavaṉ.
   avanti: piraccōtaṉa aracaṉuṭaiya nāṭu; itaṉ talainakar uccaiṉi.
   aṉaṅkavilāciṉi: kantaruvapurattu aracaṉāṉa nīlavēṉatu
   puttiri;naravāṇatattaṉāl maṇam ceytu koḷḷappaṭṭāḷ.
   ātittiyatarumaṉ: oru vīraṉ.
   āppiyāyiṉi: patumaiyiṉ pārppaṉattōḻi; avaḷōṭu oruṅku vaḷarntavaḷ; avaḷ
   taṉiyē ālōcittaṟkuriya uyirttuṇaivi; tōḻamaikkuriya iyalpukaḷiṟ
   ciṟantavaḷ; ivaḷāl patumai aṭainta payaṉkaḷ pala; utayaṇaṉuṭaiya
   pārppaṉat tōḻaṉākiya icaiccaṉeṉpavaṉāl ivaḷ maṇañ ceytu koḷḷappaṭṭāḷ;
   ip peyar yāppiyāyiṉi eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
   āppiyai: cālaṅkāyaṉuṭaiya taṅkai; piraccōtaṉa aracaṉ virumpiyapaṭi
   yūkikku maṇam ceyvikkap paṭṭāḷ. yāppiyai yeṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvāḷ.
   āriyai: patumaiyiṉ tōḻi; avaḷuṭaiya maṉak kuṟippaṟintē oḻukupavaḷ;
   mikka mēṉmaiyai uṭaiyavaḷ; pantaṭittalil mikka āṟṟal vāyntavaḷ.
   āruṇi aracaṉ: ivaṉ pāñcālatēcattaracaṉ; mikka parākkiramattaiyum mikka
   vīriyamuḷḷa cēṉaiyaiyum uṭaiyavaṉ; kōcala tēyattaracaṉai veṉṟu avaṉatu
   ūrai aḻittu avaṉ makaḷ vācavatattai mutaliyavarkaḷaip piṭittuvantu taṉ
   urimaimakaḷirkkut tātiyarkaḷāka amaittaṉaṉ; ivaṉukkuk kārttavīriyaṉai
   uvamai kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar; ivaṉ paṭṭattu yāṉaikku mantarameṉṟu peyar;
   ēyarkulattārōṭu kulappakaiñaṉātaliṉ, ak kula aracāṭcikku uriyaṉāṉa
   utayaṇaṉ ciṟaippaṭṭa poḻutu, perumpaṭaiyōṭu ceṉṟu kōcampi nakarattaik
   kaippaṟṟikkoṇṭu muracaṟaivittut taṉ koṟṟattai yāvarkkum terivittu an
   nakaraiyē taṉatu irācatāṉiyākakkoṇṭu āṭci purintaṉaṉ; kāḷamayiṭaṉeṉṉum
   oṟṟaṉāl pakaippulattē nikaḻum ceytikaḷai aṟintu koṇṭē vantavaṉ;
   pūraṇakuṇṭalaṉ eṉpavaṉ ivaṉukku mukkiyamāṉa mantiri; piṉpu utayaṇaṉ
   vantu ceyta perum pōril avaṉāl kollap paṭṭāṉ; avaṉāl veṭṭuṇṭapōtu
   ivaṉpāl accakuṟikaḷ ciṟitum nikaḻavē illai; ivaṉ pāñcāla rācaṉ; pāñcāla
   rāyaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkap paṭuvāṉ.
   ālāṅkāṉam: ippeyaruṭaiyavai iraṇṭu; oṉṟu vipulakiriyiṉ pakkattil
   mirukāvatiyiṉ tantai tavam ceytukoṇṭirunta iṭam; maṟṟoṉṟu, pūkkoytaṟku
   vanta pattirāpati eṉṉun teyva makaḷ yāṉaiyiṉ cērkkaiyaik kaṇṭu maṉam
   vēṟu paṭṭup peṇ yāṉaiyākap piṟakkumpaṭi kupēraṉāl cāpam peṟṟa iṭam; itu
   vintamalaiyiṉ pakkattē narumataiyāṟṟaṅkaraiyil uḷḷatu.
   icaiccaṉ: ivaṉ utayaṇaṉuṭaiya pārppaṉat tōḻaṉ; avaṉuṭaiya
   amaiccaṉākavuṅ kūṟappaṭuvaṉ; iḷamaiyil iru mutukuravaraiyum iḻantōṉ;
   vācavatattaiyaiyum yūkiyaiyum iḻantataṉāl mikunta kavalaiyuṟṟu melinta
   utayaṇaṉait tēṟṟi avaṉai vēṟṟu vaṭivaṅkoḷvittu maṟṟait tōḻarkaḷōṭu
   irācakiriyanakarkku aḻaittuc ceṉṟavaṉ; ceṉṟaviṭattuk kāmaṉ kōṭṭattiṉ
   vāyilil kāṇappaṭṭa patumāpati taṉṉai virumpiṉāḷeṉṟu vēṭkaiyuṟṟa
   utayaṇaṉukku makatatēyattu makaḷiratu niṟaiyuṭamaiyaik kūṟi avaṉatu
   kāmavēkattait taṇivittavaṉ; avaṉ patumāpatiyiṉ kaṉṉimāṭattil vēṟṟu
   vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu maṟaintirunta kālattil maṟṟat tōḻarkaḷōṭu tāṉum vēṟṟuvaṭi
   vaṅkoṇṭu ayaliṭattil iruntu avaṉaip pātu kāttu vantōṉ; tarucakaṉōṭu
   perumpōr puritaṟku vanta pakaivaruṭaṉ pōr ceytaṟkuc ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉukkut
   tuṇaiyākac ceṉṟavaṉ; oḻukkiṉum kulattiṉum viḻuppamikka āppiyāyiṉi eṉṉum
   antaṇak kaṉṉikaiyai maṇam ceytu koṇṭavaṉ. mikka varuvāyaiyuṭaiya pala
   ūrkaḷaic cīvitamāka utayaṇaṉpāl peṟṟavaṉ.
   iṭavakaṉ: utayaṇaṉuṭaiya mantirikaḷil oruvaṉ; avaṉuṭaiya uyirt tōḻaṉ;
   cūḻcci, vāymai, naṉṟiyaṟivu, vīram ivai ivaṉuṭaiya ciṟappuk kuṇaṅkaḷ.
   maṉam kuḷirntu utayaṇaṉāl pārāṭṭap peṟṟa perumai vāyntavaṉ. uñcai
   nakarattiliruntu piṟar aṟiyātapaṭi nērē taṉapāl taṉiyē vantu viṉāviya
   yūkikku utayaṇaṉ ceyti muḻuvataiyum kūṟi mēlē āka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai avaṉ
   ārāyntu ceyyumpaṭi tūṇṭiyavaṉ. tarucakaṉ poruṭṭup pakaivarōṭu pōr
   ceytaṟku utayaṇaṉ ceṉṟapoḻutu avaṉukkut tuṇaiyākap pala vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ
   ceṉṟu pōr ceytu veṟṟi aṭaiyumpaṭi ceytavaṉ. utayaṇaṉāl ivaṉ cīvitamākap
   peṟṟavai, puṭpakameṉṉum nakaramum ciṟu nāṭukaḷ aimpatum piṟa
   varuvāykaḷum; ivaṉ puṭpakattaiyē talainakarākki aracāṭci ceytu
   koṇṭiruntu aḻaittapoḻutu ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉukku vēṇṭiyavaikaḷaic ceytu
   varupavaṉāka iruntāṉ. pala tēyaṅkaḷaiyum eḷitil pārttu varuvataṟku
   vācavatattaiyōṭu utayaṇaṉ ēṟic ceṉṟa vimāṉattil ceṉṟavarkaḷil ivaṉum
   oruvaṉ. ivaṉ peyar iṭapakaṉ eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
   irattiṉapuram: kāntāra nāṭṭil uḷḷatoru nakaram.
   irācakiriyam: itu pukaḻpeṟṟatum mikka paḻamaiyāṉatumāṉa oru periya
   nakaram. palavakai nukarccikaḷai uṭaimaiyālum accattai viḷaivittalālum
   pavaṇa lōkattaiyum celvamikutiyāl amarāvatiyaiyum oppatu; makatanāṭṭiṉ
   talainakar. itaṉ puṟattē yavaṉac cērikaḷ nūṟum, eṟipaṭaippāṭikaḷ
   nūṟṟaṟupatum, tamiḻvīrarkaḷiṉ cērikaḷ āyiramum, kollar cērikaḷ palavum,
   milaiccaccērikaḷ palavum, cittiracālaikaḷ mutaliya palavakai iṭaṅkaḷum,
   taṇṇīrp pantar mutaliya pala tarumattāṉaṅkaḷum, palavakait
   tēvālayaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. aḻakiya akaḻikaḷālum pala uṟuppukkaḷ vāynta
   matilkaḷālum cūḻappeṟṟa itaṉ akanakaril perumpaṭaic cēri, pōkaccēri,
   uḻavarcēri, antaṇarcēri, amaiccarcēri mutaliyaṉa amainta vītikaḷ
   palavaṟṟāl cūḻappeṟṟu iṭaiyē viḷaṅkum irāca māḷikai oru tāmarai malaril
   palavakai itaḻkaḷ cūḻappeṟṟa pūmpokuṭṭaippōla viḷaṅkiyatu. in
   nakarattōr yāvarum tattam kulavoḻukkaṅkaḷil ciṟitum vaḻuvāmal
   iruntaṉar. itaṉ aracaṉ tarucakaṉ eṉpavaṉ. utayaṇaṉ pārppaṉap piramacāri
   vaṭivam koṇṭu tōḻar mutaliyavarkaḷuṭaṉ cila mātaṅkaḷ maṟaintiruntu
   tarucakaṉukku utavi ceytu piṉpu avaṉuṭaiya naṭpaip peṟṟu avaṉ
   taṅkaiyākiya patumaiyai maṇantu cila nāḷ itil taṅkiyiruntaṉaṉ. iḵtu
   irācakiriyeṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
   irācaṉai: patumaiyiṉ tōḻi. mikka aḻakuṭaiyavaḷ. utayaṇaṉatu
   viruppiṉpaṭi ivaḷai urumaṇṇuvā maṇam ceytukoṇṭāṉ. pantāṭṭattil
   tērcciyuḷḷavaḷ.
   ilāmayaṉ: ōr antaṇaṉ. iṉiya moḻiyai uṭaiyavaṉ. mantira vittaiyil
   ciṟantavaṉ. utayaṇaṉukku ivaṉpāl naṉku matippuṇṭu. ivaṉ irunta iṭam
   kāḷavaṉam eṉpatu. "vācavatattai tīkku iraiyāyiṉaḷē! iṉi yātu ceyvōm"
   eṉṟu mikka varuttam aṭaintu ceyalaṟṟirunta utayaṇaṉ ivaṉ irunta iṭamum
   tīkku iraiyāyiṟṟeṉṟu tōḻar kūṟiṉamaiyāl entap poruḷukkum aḻivuṇṭu
   eṉpatai aṟintu ciṟitu āṟutalai aṭaintāṉ.
   ilāvāṇakam: iḵtu utayaṇaṉukku uriya periya nakarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu; pakaivar
   varutaṟku mika añcum pēraraṇkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum iṭaiyē mika
   aḻakiyatāṉa ōr araṇmaṉaiyaiyum uṭaiyatu; itaṉ pakkattē palavakai
   vaḷamuḷḷa oru malai uṇṭu; ataṉ cāral mikka iṉpam payappatāka iruntatu;
   uṇṭāṭṭukku uriya palavakai maraṅkaḷaiyum koṭikaḷaiyum uṭaiya
   pūñcōlaikaḷum, cuṉai mutaliya nīrnilaikaḷum, paṭamāṭam mutaliyaṉa
   amaittaṟkuriya iṭa vicēṭaṅkaḷum ic cāralil iruntaṉa; muṉivar
   ācciramaṅkaḷum tēvālayaṅkaḷum mēṟkūṟiya cōlaikaḷil uṇṭu; tavam
   ceytukoṇṭirunta ōr aracamuṉivaruṭaiya puttiriyākiya viricikai eṉṉum
   kaṉṉiyiṉ vēṇṭukōṭku iraṅki avaḷ koṭutta palavakai malarkaḷaik kaṇṇi
   mutaliyaṉavākak kaṭṭi utayaṇaṉ cūṭṭiyatu ac cōlaikaḷuḷ oṉṟilētāṉ; ak
   kaṉṉi vēṟoṉṟaiyum aṇiyāmal am mālaiyuṭaṉē iruntu muyaṉṟu utayaṇaṉukkut
   tēviyāyiṉaḷ; urumaṇṇuvāviṟku avaṉāl koṭukkappaṭṭa cīvitaṅkaḷuḷ in
   nakaramum oṉṟu; iḵtu ilāvāṇam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
   uñcēṉai: uccaiṉi nakaram; uñcaiyeṉavum vaḻaṅkum; iḵtu avanti nāṭṭil
   uḷḷatu; piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya irācatāṉi; matil mutaliya araṇkaḷālum.
   cēṉaikaḷālum, palavakaic celvaṅkaḷālum, ciṟpa amaitikaḷālum
   ciṟappuṟṟatu; kuḷircciyum palavakai nukarcciyum itil atikam. akanakar
   puṟa nakarkaḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷiṉāl mikkatu. yātoru kuṟṟamum illātatu.
   catturu payam itaṟku illai. itil vāḻvōr perumpālum murukakkaṭavuḷai
   vaḻipaṭuvār eṉṟu terikiṟatu. itu perunakar, mānakar eṉavum
   vaḻaṅkappaṭum. itaṉ ayalil makākāḷavaṉam allatu kāḷavaṉam eṉṟa oru tava
   vaṉamum, atil oru kāḷikōyil mutaliyaṉavum, oru natiyum, an nati
   pāyappeṟṟu oru yōcaṉai aḷavuḷḷatāṉa oru poykaiyum uṇṭu. an natiyiṉ iru
   karaiyilum ap poykaik karaiyilum in nakarattār varuṭattiṟku orumuṟai
   nīrviḻā ayarāniṟpar. utayaṇaṉ vañcaṉaiyāl piṭikkappaṭṭuc ciṟaiyil
   iruntatumutal vācavatattaiyai avaṉ piṭimēl ēṟṟicceṉṟatu iṟutiyākavuḷḷa
   kataippakuti in nakaril nikaḻntatu. in nakaram avantiyeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
   itu mutti nakaram ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. paḻaiya nūlkaḷālum cilācācaṉaṅkaḷālum
   itil caiṉa muṉivar palar iruntatākat terikiṟatu. vaṭanūlkaḷ
   palavaṟṟilum cilappatikāram yacōtarakāviyam mutaliya tamiḻnūlkaḷilum
   mikap pārāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. uccaiṉimakākāḷameṉṉum oru civakṣēttiram itil
   uṇṭu.
   utayaṇaṉ: ivaṉ in nūl katānāyakaṉ. kurukulattil piṟantavaṉ.
   catāṉikaṉukku mirukāvatipāl tōṉṟiyavaṉ. ivaṉ nāṭu vattavanāṭu.
   irācatāṉi kōcampi nakar. cūriyōtayakālattil piṟantatu paṟṟi ivaṉukku ip
   peyar amaintatu. tēviyar vācavatattai mutaliya nālvar. taṉṉikar
   illātavaṉē toṭarnilaic ceyyuṭkut talaivaṉāka iruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu āṉṟōr
   kūṟiyataṟku ēṟpak kalvi mutaliya ciṟappiyalpukaḷ palavaṟṟālum oppaṟṟu
   viḷaṅkiyavaṉ ivaṉ; aracarkku aracaṉeṉṟum, ēkacceṅkōliṉaṉ eṉṟum ivaṉ
   vaḻaṅkappaṭuvāṉ; ivaṉuṭaya tāy marapu, tantai marapu iraṇṭum mikap
   pukaḻ peṟṟavai; tantaiyiṉ kulam kurukulam; tāyiṉ kulam ēyar kulam; ivaṉ
   inta iraṇṭu kulattiṟkum urimai pūṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ; ēyar kuṭikkum
   cētināṭṭiṟkum uriyavar ivaṉ tāy marapiṉar; vatta nāṭṭiṟkum
   kurukulattiṟkum uriyavar ivaṉ tantai marapiṉar; iyaṟkai aḻakilum
   ceyaṟkai aḻakilum ciṟantavaṉ; mikka iḷamaip paruvattilēyē palavakaik
   kalaikaḷaiyum paḻampiṟappil ceyta tavattāl muṟṟak kaṟṟut tērntōṉ;
   viṟpaṭai mutaliyavaṟṟilum, yāṉaiyēṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟilum yāḻ viccaiyilum
   vallunaṉ; viṟpaṭai mutaliya vittaikaḷaik kaṟpittalilum ciṟantavaṉ; itu
   paṟṟiyē ācāṉ,tēcikaṉ eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarkaḷukku uriyaṉāka ivaṉ
   viḷaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟum, viccai vīraṉ eṉṟu ciṟappittup pārāṭṭutaṟku uriyaṉāka
   ivaṉ viḷaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟum terikiṉṟaṉa; celvattuḷ celvamākiya kēḷvic celvam
   ivaṉpāl amaintiruntatu; iṉṉum ivaṉ yāṉaiyai aṭakkum āṟṟal uṭaiyāṉ
   eṉṟum, yāṉai ilakkaṇattai naṉku aṟintavaṉ eṉṟum, yāṉaip pēccil vallavaṉ
   eṉṟum karutappaṭukiṟāṉ; piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya paṭṭattuyāṉaiyākiya naḷakiri
   eṉpatu mata veṟi koṇṭu tirintu oruvarukkum aṭaṅkāmal uñcēṉai
   nakarattaip pāḻpaṭuttiyapoḻutu avaṉ vēṇṭukōṭku iraṅkic ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum
   ceṉṟu ataṉai mika eḷitil aṭakkiyatupaṟṟiyē avaṉālum maṟṟaiyōrālum mika
   matikkappaṭṭu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ; itaṉālētāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ uñcēṉai nakaril
   kuñcaraccēriyiluḷḷa māḷikaiyai ivaṉukku iruppiṭamāy amaittaṉaṉ;
   ivaṉuṭaiya yāḻiṉ peyar kōṭapati eṉpatu; aḵtu ekkālattum taṉ taṉmai
   tiriyātatu; aḵtu intiraṉāl piramacuntara muṉivarukkum, avarāl ivaṉukkum
   koṭukkappaṭṭatu; ataṉpāl ivaṉukku mikka aṉpu uṇṭu; yāḻiṟkuriya
   maraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpaiyum narampukaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum ivaṉ naṉku
   aṟintavaṉ; ativicittiramāṉa kaṇṇi kaṭṭutalilum, vāḻaik kuruttil
   nakattiṉāl poykai mutaliya palavakaiyāṉa cittiraṅkaḷ poṟittal
   mutaliyavaṟṟilum vallavaṉāka iruntāṉ; taṉṉaic cumantuvantapiṭi iṟakkum
   taruṇatil naṟkati aṭaiyumāṟu pañca mantirattai ataṉ kātil ōtiṉaṉ eṉṟu
   teritalālum, piṟavaṟṟālum ivaṉ mantiravittaiyilum vallavaṉeṉṟu
   terikiṟatu; ovvoṉṟaiyum ārāyntē teḷipavaṉ ivaṉ; iṭavakaṉ, urumaṇṇuvā,
   yūki, vayantakaṉ eṉṉum nāṉku mukkiya mantirikaḷum, vēṟu pala
   mantirikaḷum ivaṉukku uṇṭu; avarkaḷ ivaṉukkākac ceyta utavikaḷ
   mikappala; in nālvarum ivaṉuṭaiya tōḻarkaḷākavum viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ;
   avarkaḷiṭattu ivaṉukku vicēṣa aṉpu uṇṭu; mēṟkūṟiya mantirikaḷākiya
   tōḻarkaḷaiyaṉṟi icaiccaṉ mutaliya aṉpuṭaiya tōḻarkaḷ palar ivaṉukku
   iruntārkaḷ; aṟappōrilum maṟappōrilum pērāṟṟal uṭaiyavaṉ; kuṟitta
   ilakkai ūṭuruvikkoṇṭu appāl uḷḷavaṟṟaiyum tuḷaittu uruvumpaṭi eyyavalla
   irāmaṉ, ōri eṉpavarkaḷaippōl ampukaḷai eyyum vīraruḷ vīraṉāka ivaṉ
   viḷaṅkiyatupaṟṟi valvalliyeṉṟum pārāṭṭappaṭuvāṉ; naṉṟiyaṟivil
   ciṟantavaṉ.
   utayaiyōṭai: ivaḷ patumāpatiyiṉ naṟṟāy; civamatiyiṉ cakōtari;
   kāciyaracaṉ maṉaivi. urumaṇṇuvā: ivaṉ palavakaip perumaiyum vāynta
   uyarkuṭiyil piṟantavaṉ; utayaṇaṉuṭaiya mantirikaḷ nālvaruḷḷum avaṉ
   uyirttōḻarkaḷuḷḷum oruvaṉ. vayantakaṉuṭaṉ cērntē perumpālum
   vaḻaṅkappaṭuvāṉ. yūkikku aṭuttapaṭi eṉṉum perumai vāyntavaṉ.
   cēṉaikaḷaip pōril naṭattuvatil mikka āṟṟal uḷḷavaṉ. entac camayattilum
   maṉanaṭukkam koḷḷātavaṉ. mikka pukaḻiṉaṉ. naṭpu, ūkkam, vīram,
   pakaivarkku añcāmai, kālam, iṭam mutaliyavaṟṟai naṉku ārāyntē
   ovvoṉṟaiyum ceyyat tuṇital naṉṟiyaṟivu mutaliyavai ivaṉpāluḷḷa vicēṭa
   kuṇaṅkaḷ. vācavatattaiyai valintu koṇṭuvanta utayaṇaṉai mikka
   upacārattuṭaṉ varavēṟṟu aḻaittuc ceṉṟu cayanti nakaril avaḷai avaṉukku
   maṇañ ceyvittu vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kuṟippaṟintu aḷittu avaṉukku yātoru
   kavalaiyum illātapaṭi pātukāttu makiḻvittu vantōṉ. avaḷaip pirintu
   varuntiya avaṉukku aṭicil, uṭai mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṭukkumpaṭi tōḻarkaḷāl
   niyamikkap peṟṟa uṇmai aṉpiṉaṉ. irācakiriya nakarattil utayaṇaṉ
   maṟaintiruntapoḻutu tāmum maṟaintu avaṉaip pātukāttu vantavarkaḷil
   ivaṉum oruvaṉ. utayaṇaṉuṭaiya vāḻvaik karutit taṉṉuṭaiya uyirkkuc
   ciṟitum iraṅkāmal pōril avaṉukku muṉ ceṉṟu pakaivarāl ciṟaip
   paṭuttappaṭṭōṉ. kōcampi nakarattaip peṟṟa piṉpu taṉ pirivāṟṟāmal
   varunti "irumaṇameytiya iṉpamellā, murumaṇṇulāviṉai yuṟṟataṟ piṉṉai"
   eṉa utayaṇaṉāl kūṟappeṟṟa uttama cīlaṉ. maṟaintirunta yūkiyiṉ karuttait
   tāṉ aṟintukoḷḷutaṟkum taṉ karuttai avaṉukkut terivittaṟkumākac
   cātakaṉeṉṉum ōr aṉpaṉai iṭaiyē vaittukkoṇṭu cila mukkiyamāṉa
   kāriyaṅkaḷai muṭittavaṉ. mikavum cikkalāṉa vaḻakkait tīrttalil
   vallavaṉ. utayaṇaṉ viruppattiṉpaṭi patumāpatiyiṉ tōḻiyākiya irācaṉai
   eṉpavaḷai maṇam ceytukoṇṭōṉ. pala parivāraṅkaḷiṉiṭaiyē utayaṇaṉ taṉ
   kaiyiṉāl alaṅkarittu aḷitta ēṉāti mōtira mum cēṉāpatip paṭṭamum
   peṟṟataṉṟi, cayanti, ilāvāṇakam eṉṉum periya nakaraṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟaic
   cūḻnta iṭaṅkaḷaiyum mikka varuvāyai uṭaiya pala ūrkaḷaiyum caturaṅkap
   paṭaikaḷaiyum peṟṟavaṉ. tuṟavaṟam pūṇṭu tavam ceytukoṇṭirunta taṉ
   tantaikku yātoru tuṉpamum nērātapaṭi vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittu vaḻipāṭu
   ceytu oḻukiyavaṉ. karuvuṟṟa vācavatattaiyuṭaṉ vimāṉa mēṟi ākāyavaḻiyē
   ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉōṭu kūṭac ceṉṟavarkaḷil ivaṉum oruvaṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya
   putalvaṉ pūtiyeṉpāṉ,.
   eliccevi: tarucakaṉōṭu pakaimaikoṇṭa aracarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ.
   ēyar kulam (ēkayar kulam) : itu mirukāvatiyiṉ tantai kulam.
   airāpati: ivaḷ patumāpatiyiṉ cevilittāyiṉ makaḷum avaḷ uyirttōḻiyumāka
   uḷḷavaḷ. kūṉiya uruvam uṭaiyavaḷātalpaṟṟik kūṉiyeṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvāḷ.
   irācakiriyattil kāmaṉ kōṭṭat tayalil patumāpatiyaik kaṇṭu mayaṅki
   viṉāviya utayaṇaṉukku avaḷ varalāṟukaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum colli avaṉukkum
   avaḷukkum tāṉ iṭaiyē niṉṟu iruvar karuttaiyum muṟṟuvittavaḷ. kūrtta
   matiyiṉaḷ. etaiyum mantaṇamāka muṭikkum āṟṟal vāyntavaḷ. pantaṭittalil
   mikka vallamai uṭaiyavaḷ; ip peyar ayirāvati eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
   kaṭakaṉ: utayaṇaṉ tampi. piṅkalakaṭakar eṉpataip pārkka.
   karuppācam: oru kāṭṭāṟu.
   kaliṅkacēṉai: kōcampi nakariluḷḷa talaikkōl paṭṭam peṟṟa
   iraṇṭāyirattainnūṟu kaṇikaimārkaḷuḷ orutti. kuṟṟamaṟṟa kaṟpuṭaiyavaḷ.
   mataṉamañcikaiyiṉ tāy.
   kākatuṇṭakaṉ: irācakiriya nakariṉ puṟattatākiya cōlai oṉṟiluḷḷa oru
   periyavaṉ. tōṟṟappolivuḷḷavaṉ. vācavatattai iṟantuviṭṭāḷē eṉṟu
   kavalaikkaṭalil aḻuntic ceyalaṟṟirunta utayaṇaṉ kavalaiyai māṟṟiyavaṉ.
   kāciyaracaṉ: ip peyariṉar iruvar; (1) patumāpatiyiṉ tantai. utayaiyōṭai
   eṉpavaḷuṭaiya kaṇavaṉ. mikka cēṉaiyai uṭaiyavaṉ (2) nīlakēci
   eṉpavaḷuṭaiya tantai.
   kāñcaṉamālai: vācavatattaiyiṉ uyirppāṅki; avaḷuṭaiya kaṇmaṇi pōṉṟavaḷ.
   pēraḻakiṉaḷ. cuvaipayakkum iṉiya moḻiyiṉaḷ. talaiviyiṉ kuṟip paṟintu
   naṭappavaḷ. vācavatattaiyiṉ karuttai ivaḷpōl aṟintu naṭappavarkaḷ
   illai. avaḷukku maṉakkalakkam nērumpōtellām takka parikāra moḻikaḷai
   mellak kūṟit teruṭṭupavaḷ. avaḷpāl uṇmai aṉpuṭaiyavaḷ. avaḷuṭaiya
   iṉpakkālattum tuṉpakkālattum piriyātavaḷ ivaḷ. pantu viḷaiyāṭṭil mikka
   payiṟciyuḷḷavaḷ. ivaḷ peyar kāñcaṉai, kañcaṉamālai eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
   kāmpōcam: ciṟanta kutiraikaḷ piṟakkum tēyattuḷ iḵtu oṉṟu.
   kāḷavaṉam: ip peyaruṭaiya vaṉam iraṇṭu; uñcai nakariṉ puṟattē uḷḷatu
   oṉṟu. itu makākāḷa vaṉam eṉṟu vēṟu nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭum. maṟṟoṉṟu
   utayaṇaṉpāl mikka aṉpuṭaiyavaṉākiya ilāmayaṉ eṉpavaṉ irunta vaṉam.
   kumpaṉ: ivaṉ āruṇiyaracaṉukkuk kaṇmaṇipōṉṟavaṉum tiṇṇiya aṟivai
   uṭaiyavaṉumākiya oru
   vīraṉ; pañcāla rācaṉ iṟanta piṉpu kōcampi nakarattē kollappaṭṭāṉ.
   kurukulam: caiṉanūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭum aivakaik kulattuḷ oṉṟu.
   kōcampi (kaucāmpi nakaram): iḵtu ātiyil kucāmpaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉāl
   uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu eṉpar; itu vattavanāṭṭiṉ talainakar;. yamuṉaiyāṟṟait
   taṉpāl uṭaiyatu. utayaṇaṉatu irācatāṉi. itiliruntu aracāṭci ceytuvanta
   utayaṇaṉ ciṟaippaṭṭu uñcai nakarkkuc ceṉṟapoḻutu pakaivaṉākiya pāñcāla
   tēcattaracaṉāl itu kaikkoḷḷap paṭṭatu. appāl avaṉaik koṉṟa utayaṇaṉāl
   āḷappaṭṭatu. vācavatattaiyai utayaṇaṉ maṇam ceytukoṇṭa piṉpu uñcai
   nakarattārum in nakarattārum maṉakkalappuṭaiyavarāyt tāṉiyaṅkaḷaiyum
   nāṇayaṅkaḷaiyum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi vaḻaṅkuvārāyiṉar. in nakaril ciṟanta
   viḻavoṉṟu naṭantatākat terikiṟatu.
   kōcalattaracaṉ: ivaṉatu iyaṟpeyar teriyavillai. ivaṉ piraccōtaṉaṉuṭaiya
   mutal maṉaiviyiṉ cakōtari kaṇavaṉ. orupoḻutu pāñcāla vēntaṉ
   paṭaiyeṭuttu vantu ivaṉai veṉṟu ivaṉ makaḷ vācavatattaiyaiyum
   avaḷuṭaiya āyattāraiyum kavarntuceṉṟu taṉṉuṭaiya tēvimārkaḷukkup
   paṇippeṇkaḷāka niyamittaṉaṉ; avaṉai veṉṟa utayaṇaṉ am makaḷirait taṉ
   tēviyarkkup paṅkiṭṭuk koṭuttuviṭṭu vācavatattai (māṉaṉīkai) eṉpavaḷai
   maṇam ceytu koṇṭaṉaṉ.
   kōcikaṉ: kōcāmpi nakarattirunta oru nimittikaṉ; yamuṉaiyil
   vīḻttuvataṟkuk koṇṭupōkap paṭṭa cāṅkiyattāyaik kāppāṟṟa niṉainta
   utayaṇaṉ viṉāviyapoḻutu avaḷuṭaiya varuṅkālac ceytiyai naṉku kūṟiyavaṉ.
   kōṭāpati. iḵtu utayaṇaṉuṭaiya yāḻ; yāḻvakaiyil pēriyāḻiṉpāl paṭum;
   piramacuntarar eṉṉum muṉivarāl utayaṇaṉukku yāḻvittaiyuṭaṉ
   koṭukkappaṭṭatu; teyvattaṉmai vāyntatu; avaṉ itaṉaik kaṟpikkumpoḻututāṉ
   vācavatattaiyiṉ aṉpaip peṟṟāṉ; avviruvarukkum itaṉpāl aṉpu atikam.
   kōpālakaṉ: ivaṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ paṭṭattēvimāruḷ oruttiyiṉ putalvaṉ; ivaṉ
   vācavatattaiyiṉ cakōtaraṉ allaṉ; utayaṇaṉuṭaiya virōtikaḷākiya
   irupatiṉmarai veṉṟaṉaṉ.
   kōmukaṉ: ip peyaruṭaiyār iruvar; oruvaṉ iṭavakaṉuṭaiya makaṉ;
   utayaṇaṉukkup patumāpatipāl tōṉṟiyavaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉ.
   kaucāmpi: kōcāmpiyaip pārkka.
   caṅkamaṉṉar: tarucakaṉōṭu pōrceytaṟku vanta elic ceviyaracaṉ mutaliyōr.
   caṅkaraṉ: tarucakaṉuṭaṉ pōrceytaṟku vanta aracarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ.
   *caṇmainakar: aṅkanāṭṭil uḷḷatoru periya nakaram; ellā vaḷattilum mikac
   ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvatu; ukkirakulattaraṉākiya vicaiyavaraṉ eṉpavaṉāl
   pātukākkappaṭṭuvantatu; inta nakarattil mittirakāmaṉ eṉṉum oru
   vaṇikarperumāṉ vīṭṭil yūkiyum, vācavatattai, kāñcaṉamālai, cāṅkiyattāy
   eṉṉum mūvarum tammaip piṟar aṟiyātapaṭi vēṟṟuvaṭivam koṇṭu avaṉāl
   pātukākkappaṭṭut taṅkiyiruntaṉar.
   cattiyakāyaṉ: ivaṉ tarucakaṉ mantirikaḷuḷ oruvaṉ; āruṇi aracaṉōṭu pōr
   ceytaṟku utayaṇaṉ puṟappaṭṭa kālattil avaṉukkut tuṇaiyākat tarucakaṉāl
   aṉuppappaṭṭa vīrarkaḷil oruvaṉ; ap pōril ivaṉ iṟantatupaṟṟi ivaṉ
   makkaḷukku utayaṇaṉ cīvitaṅkaḷ aḷittu mika mēmpaṭuttiṉaṉ.
   catāṉikaṉ: kurukulattil piṟanta aracaṉ; utayaṇaṉ tantai.
   cayanti: utayaṇaṉuṭaiya āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭatoru periyanakaram; itaṉai
   urumaṇṇuvā pātukāttu vantaṉaṉ; utayaṇaṉukakum vātavatattaikkum
   innakarattilētāṉ vivākam naṭantatu; itu maruta nilavaḷattāl ciṟantatu;
   itaṉ pakkattē mika aḻakitāṉa malai oṉṟu uṇṭu; innakaril vācava
   tattaiyōṭu utayaṇaṉ pala nāḷ kavalaiyiṉṟi vāḻntiruntaṉaṉ; innakaramum
   vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷum urumaṇṇuvāvukku utayaṇaṉāl cīvitamāka
   aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
   cāṅkiyattāy: ivaḷatu iyaṟpeyar teriyavillai! kōcampi nakarattil
   pārppaṉa kulattil piṟanta ivaḷai maṇanta kaṇavaṉ iḷamaiyil
   nīṅkiyatupaṟṟi ivaḷukku oḻukkat tavaṟu orupoḻutu uṇṭāyatu; atu terinta
   atikārikaḷuṭaiya kaṭṭaḷaiyāl yamuṉaiyāṟṟil ivaḷai vīḻttiviṭuvataṟkuc
   cilar koṇṭu celvataik kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ iraṅki viṭuvittut tīrttayāttirai
   ceyyumpaṭi aṉuppiṉaṉ; appāl ivaḷ kaṅkaiyāṭi imayamalaiyil ceṉṟu iraṇṭu
   varuṭam aṅkē taṅki āṇṭuḷḷa periyōrāl camaya vikaṟpaṅkaḷai aṟintu
   kaṉṉiyā kumariyil nīrāṭuvataṟku yāttiraiyāḷarōṭu aṅkiruntu puṟappaṭṭu
   uñcai nakarukku vantapoḻutu, ivaḷuṭaiya kalvi, aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟai
   aṟinta piraccōtaṉaṉ taṉ paṭṭattut tēviyākiya srīmatikku nītikaḷ
   mutaliyavaṟṟaic collumpaṭi ivaḷai niyamittaṉaṉ; ivaḷ ataṉac
   ceytuvarukaiyil oru pirāyattiṉaḷāka irunta vācavatattai ivaḷiṭattu aṉpu
   vaittatai aṟinta paṭṭattēvi avaḷukkuc cevilit tāyāyirukkumpaṭi vēṇṭa
   ivaḷ aṅṅaṉamē iruntu vantaṉaḷ. cāṅkiya camayattai mēṟkoṇṭamaiyālum
   vācavatattaikku cevilittāyāka iruntamaiyālum cāṅkiyattāyeṉṟu ivaḷ
   vaḻaṅkappaṭuvāḷ; vācavatattaipāl ivaḷukkirunta aṉpiṟku ellaiyillai; taṉ
   uyiraik kāppāṟṟiyatupaṟṟi utayaṇaṉpāl ivaḷukku mikka aṉpuṇṭu;
   vācavatattaiyaiyum utayaṇaṉaiyum avaṉaic cārntavarkaḷaiyum
   kāppāṟṟutaṟku avvap poḻutu taṉ varuttataip pārāṭṭāmal ivaḷ ceytuvanta
   muyaṟcikaḷ mikappala; tāyaik kāṭṭilum aṉpuvaittu utayaṇaṉ ivaḷai
   ātarittu vantāṉ; tāyeṉavum, ceviliyeṉavum, cāṅkiya mutumakaḷ eṉavum
   ivaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaḷ.
   cāṅkiyamuṉi: ivaṉ pala māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ tīrtta yāttirai ceytukoṇ ṭirunta
   oru paṇṭitaṉ; cāṅkiyattāykkup pala camayak koḷkaikaḷai aṟivuṟutti
   avaḷai nalvaḻippaṭuttiyavaṉ.
   cāṇṭiyaṉ: kāntāra nāṭṭil irattiṉa purattilirunta ōr antaṇaṉ. itu
   paṭaittukkōṭ peyar. antaṇa vaṭivamuṟṟu māṇakaṉeṉṉum peyar koṇṭu
   irācakiriyattil iruntapoḻutu utayaṇaṉ ip peyarai uṭaiyavaṉ makaṉ eṉṟu
   taṉṉaik kūṟikkoṇṭaṉaṉ.
   cākakaṉ: ivaṉuṭaiya ūr kōcampi nakaram. cātiyāl kuyavaṉ; utayaṇaṉ
   ciṟaippaṭṭuc ceṉṟapoḻutu atu poṟāmal vēṟu vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu tāṉum uṭaṉceṉṟu
   uñcainakariṉ pakkattil uḷḷatōr ūril oru vīṭṭil iruntu utayaṇaṉukku
   vēṇṭiya anukūlaṅkaḷaip piṟar aṟiyāmal ceytuvantavaṉ. maṟaintirunta
   yūkikku uyir naṭpāḷarkaḷāy avaṉuṭaṉ irunta vīrar patiṉmaruḷ
   oruvaṉākavum iruntavaṉ. irāca vicuvācattil ciṟantavaṉ.
   vācavatattataiyaip piṭimītu ēṟṟi utayaṇaṉ puṟappaṭṭa piṉpu cāṅkiyattāy
